Result: 在多个推理分割基准上评估。与VisionReasoner基线相比，LIRSeg在ReasonSeg上gIoU绝对提升4.9%，MUSE上提升7.1%，MMR上提升4.7%；同时推理token数量减少约16倍。结果表明该方法在分割精度与推理效率两方面均取得一致的改进。代码见补充材料。（摘要未给出更细粒度的消融、数据集规模或具体数值指标。）

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：提出以紧凑可学习潜在token完全取代显式CoT的推理分割框架LIRSeg，解决显式文本推理带来的注意力干扰与效率问题；结合两阶段训练（空间对齐 + GRPO）与三种信息论启发的优化机制，防止紧凑表征坍缩并提升信息量。方法在ReasonSeg、MUSE、MMR等基准上同时提升精度与效率，token削减约16倍，对多模态推理分割的实时化、轻量化具有借鉴意义；作为隐式推理范式，其可解释性相对显式CoT有所下降。局限方面，摘要未披露所依赖的MLLM骨干、训练数据规模及消融细节，其泛化性与在不同任务上的适用性有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Reasoning segmentation aims to interpret implicit textual queries and enable fine-grained visual perception, which is critical for applications such as human-computer interaction and embodied agents. Existing methods typically generate explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT) by multimodal large language models (MLLMs) before localizing the target. Although intuitive, such explicit verbal reasoning introduces substantial attention interference: redundant textual tokens disrupt attention during perception-token generation and also increase the effective distance between visual tokens. To address this issue, we propose LIRSeg, which fully replaces explicit CoT with a compact set of learnable latent tokens for reasoning segmentation. LIRSeg is trained in two stages: spatial alignment grounds the latent tokens in object-relevant visual evidence, and GRPO further optimizes them with segmentation rewards. To make these compact latent tokens more informative, we introduce three complementary mechanisms from an information perspective: extreme-advantage sampling for selecting informative training signals, decoupled exploration-stability updates for learning complementary representations, and latent diversity amplification for preventing representational collapse. Extensive experiments on benchmarks demonstrate that LIRSeg consistently improves both segmentation accuracy and reasoning efficiency. Compared with the VisionReasoner baseline, LIRSeg achieves absolute gIoU improvements of 4.9% on ReasonSeg, 7.1% on MUSE, and 4.7% on MMR, while achieving a approximately 16x reduction in reasoning tokens. Code is available in supplementary materials.

</details>


### [29] [Motion Style Slider: Endpoint-Supervised Continuous Style Control for Human Motion Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30795)
*Chen-Chieh Liao,Yichen Peng,Yiyi Cai,Yûi Ono,Hiroki Hanaoka,Erwin Wu,Hideki Koike,Shuichi Kurabayashi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Motion Style Slider，一个用于运动到运动风格迁移的框架，通过在学习到的运动风格嵌入空间中构造风格方向，并用标量强度条件化扩散生成，实现端点监督的连续风格强度控制。训练结合扩散去噪与潜在强度正则化，在无需中间强度真值运动的情况下，鼓励平滑、单调的风格缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人体运动扩散方法生成质量高，风格迁移模型也能注入目标风格线索，但对风格强度的细粒度、连续控制仍研究不足。在动画制作等实际场景中，风格强度具有主观性，不同美术师和导演的判定标准不同，因此真正需要的是一个可靠的单调控制轴，而非绝对的通用强度单位。论文关注的核心问题是：如何在只有弱/端点监督（缺少中间强度标注）的条件下，实现对风格强度的连续、可控、可外推的调节。

Method: 方法为运动到运动（motion-to-motion）的风格迁移框架。给定内容运动与风格运动，在学得的运动风格嵌入空间中构造一个风格方向（style direction），并用一个标量强度对扩散生成过程进行条件化。训练目标由两部分组成：扩散去噪损失与潜在强度正则化（latent intensity regularization），后者用于促使风格强度呈现平滑且单调的缩放行为，从而无需中间强度的真值运动作为监督。该框架可与预训练的运动扩散骨干网络兼容，并支持异构风格数据集（包括多角色风格运动数据集）。为测试超出训练范围（out-of-range）的可用性，作者额外引入一个小型真实采集的过激（over-reaction）扩展集，用未见过的目标来评估大强度行为。评估涵盖可控性、插值与外推行为、内容保持以及运动真实性，并对方向构造方式与损失设计进行消融。

Result: 论文在运动生成/风格迁移的常见设定上开展实验，评估维度包括：风格强度可控性、插值/外推行为、内容保持（content preservation）以及运动真实性（motion realism）。数据集方面支持异构风格数据集，涵盖多角色风格运动数据集；并构建了小型真实采集的过激扩展集用于测试超出范围的大强度外推。消融实验针对风格方向构造方式与损失设计（扩散去噪 + 潜在强度正则化）。摘要未给出具体数值指标，主要结论为：所提潜在强度正则化可在缺少中间强度真值的情况下实现平滑、单调的风格缩放，并具备一定的外推能力。

Conclusion: 主要贡献包括：(1) 提出 Motion Style Slider 框架，在学习到的运动风格嵌入空间中通过方向与标量强度实现对风格强度的连续控制；(2) 设计端点监督的训练目标，将扩散去噪与潜在强度正则化结合，避免对中间强度真值运动的依赖；(3) 与预训练扩散骨干兼容并支持异构风格数据集，同时引入真实采集的外推测试集评估大强度行为。意义在于为风格强度提供一个可靠单调的控制轴，契合实际生产中强度主观、需要连续调节的需求。可能的局限：摘要未报告定量结果与具体基线对比，效果强度尚需实验数据支撑；外推测试集规模较小，外推能力的泛化性有待进一步验证；风格方向与强度解耦的稳定性可能受嵌入空间质量影响。

Abstract: Existing human motion diffusion methods provide strong motion generation quality, and recent style transfer models can inject target style cues, but fine-grained continuous control of style intensity remains underexplored. In production, style intensity is subjective across artists and directors, so the practical requirement is not a universal absolute unit, but a reliable monotonic control axis. We propose Motion Style Slider, a motion-to-motion style transfer framework for endpoint-supervised continuous control. Given a content motion and a style motion, we construct a style direction in a learned motion-style embedding space and condition diffusion generation with a scalar intensity. The training objective combines diffusion denoising with latent intensity regularization to encourage smooth and monotonic style scaling without requiring intermediate-intensity ground-truth motions. Our framework is compatible with pretrained motion diffusion backbones and supports heterogeneous style datasets, including the multi-actor style motion dataset. To test out-of-range usability, we additionally introduce a small real-capture over-reaction extension and evaluate large-intensity behavior against these unseen targets. Experiments measure controllability, interpolation/extrapolation behavior, content preservation, and motion realism, with ablations on direction construction and loss design.

</details>


### [30] [MDSkin-Net: Multi-Task Skin Lesion Analysis Driven by Pattern Analysis Priors and Spatial Alignment Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30855)
*Yijian Li,Saad Bedros,Paul Bigliardi,Mei Bigliardi Qi,Vassilios Morellas,Nikolaos Papanikolopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MDSkin-Net 提出将皮肤镜诊断的"模式分析"(Pattern Analysis)临床先验注入混合 CNN-Transformer 多任务框架，通过模式分析引导注意力模块 (PAGAM) 与多尺度空间对齐正则 (MSAR) 联合进行皮肤病变分割与分类，在零样本跨数据集评估中展现出较强的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤镜计算机辅助诊断中，病变分割与分类相互关联。现有方法存在两类不足：(1) 多任务框架仅在架构层面耦合两个任务，未引入临床知识；(2) 注入知识的方案依赖宏观的 ABCD 规则，而 ABCD 规则是为肉眼观察设计，并非为皮肤镜这种显微(microscopic)成像模式设计。皮肤镜诊断的真正知识基础是"模式分析"——一种围绕皮肤镜特征 (dermoscopic features) 组织的显微框架。作者希望用符合皮肤镜本身语义的先验来引导网络，从而提升分割与分类的可靠性及跨队列泛化能力。

Method: 核心是混合 CNN-Transformer 架构，关键创新为模式分析引导注意力模块 (PAGAM)，包含三个受不同皮肤镜线索启发的先验：(1) 改进的高效通道注意力 (iECA)；(2) 多尺度空间注意力 (MSSA)；(3) 偏置非对称注意力 (BAA)。此外提出多尺度空间对齐正则 (MSAR)，将分割真值掩码作为分层软监督，约束分类头仅关注病变局部证据，并通过共享空间先验耦合分割与分类两条任务路径。

Result: 仅在 ISIC 2017 训练集上训练（不使用外部皮肤镜数据），集成模型在零样本迁移评估中表现稳健：PH2 上 DSC 达 92.38%、黑色素瘤 AUC 达 97.84%；ISIC 2018 Task 1 测试集 DSC 为 88.92%。域内 ISIC 2017 基准上，集成模型在两个分类任务（黑色素瘤、脂溢性角化病 vs 其余）的平均 AUC 为 91.60%，分割 DSC 为 84.72%。分类性能与基线相当；域内分割弱于单任务专用模型，但所提先验与对齐正则得到的表征在不同规模的队列间具有一致的泛化性。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于将符合皮肤镜语义的模式分析先验（而非 ABCD 规则）以注意力先验和软监督正则的形式注入多任务网络，并通过共享空间先验耦合分割与分类，显著提升了跨数据集零样本泛化能力。局限性方面：ISIC 2017 域内分割精度不及单任务专用模型，分类仅与基线持平，说明先验注入对域内峰值性能的提升有限；验证主要依赖公开皮肤镜数据集，尚未涉及其他成像模态或更广泛临床场景。

Abstract: Reliable skin lesion segmentation and classification are central to dermoscopic computer-aided diagnosis. Existing multi-task frameworks couple the two tasks architecturally without clinical knowledge, while knowledge-injecting approaches rely on the macroscopic ABCD rule, which was not designed for dermoscopy. Dermoscopic diagnosis is grounded in Pattern Analysis, a microscopic framework structured around dermoscopic features. We propose MDSkin-Net, which incorporates cue-level Pattern Analysis priors into a hybrid CNN-Transformer architecture. At its core is a Pattern Analysis-Guided Attention Module (PAGAM) comprising three priors motivated by distinct dermoscopic cues: an improved Efficient Channel Attention (iECA), a Multi-Scale Spatial Attention (MSSA), and a Biased Asymmetry Attention (BAA). We further introduce a multi-scale spatial alignment regularization (MSAR) that uses the segmentation ground-truth mask as hierarchical soft supervision, confining the classification head to lesion-localized evidence and coupling both task pathways through a shared spatial prior. Trained exclusively on the ISIC 2017 training split without external dermoscopy data, the MDSkin-Net ensemble transfers robustly under zero-shot evaluation, reaching a Dice Similarity Coefficient (DSC) of 92.38% and a melanoma AUC of 97.84%on PH2, and a DSC of 88.92% on the ISIC 2018 Task 1 test set. On the in-domain ISIC 2017 benchmark, the ensemble attains a mean Area Under the Curve (AUC) of 91.60% across the two classification tasks (melanoma and seborrheic keratosis vs. rest), and a DSC of 84.72% for segmentation. Classification remains competitive with baselines; in-domain segmentation trails single-task specialists, yet the proposed priors and alignment regularization yield representations that generalize consistently across cohorts of different scales.

</details>


### [31] [Reliability-Regulated Trajectory Optimization for Progressive COLMAP-Free 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30865)
*Zijian Wu,Jinliang Wang,Zidian Lin,Ying Song,Ziqian Lu,Hanjie Ma,Zhen Ye,Mingfeng Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种统一可靠性调节的轨迹优化框架（RRTO-CF3DGS），用于无COLMAP的渐进式3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）。其核心是自监督的双向循环一致性机制，通过一个在线可靠性信号同时调控前向运动传播与回溯轨迹校正，从而在无需外部神经先验和离线预处理的情况下缓解渐进式相机位姿跟踪中的误差累积与漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无COLMAP（COLMAP-free）的3DGS方法避免了昂贵的SfM流程，但其渐进式相机位姿跟踪存在根本性缺陷——误差累积。早期的成对（pairwise）跟踪误差会污染后续帧的初始化，并且这些错误会永久冻结在被优化后的场景表示中，难以纠正。现有方法要么依赖重量级的外部神经先验，要么仅通过孤立的启发式修补来处理渐进式跟踪问题，缺乏统一、自包含的方案。因此，如何在不使用外部先验与离线预处理的前提下抑制渐进式漂移、提升位姿与渲染质量，是该问题的关键所在。

Method: 论文提出统一可靠性调节的轨迹优化框架，核心为内在的、自监督的双向循环一致性（bidirectional cycle-consistency）机制，并作用于两个互补的时间尺度：（1）前向运动传播（Forward Motion Propagation）：利用在线可靠性信号对刚性运动的一阶运动学热启动（kinematic warm-start）进行自适应门控，将其注入后续成对配准中，提供有信息量的方向性搜索先验，同时安全地拦截不可信的位姿转移；（2）回溯轨迹校正（Retrospective Trajectory Correction）：使用同一可靠性信号对滑动窗口内相邻相机位姿之间的相对位姿一致性约束进行动态加权，实现对轨迹的回溯性巩固。通过统一的可靠性调节器同时治理前瞻性状态初始化与回溯性轨迹整合，该自包含框架在无需外部先验和离线预处理的情况下解决渐进式漂移。

Result: 论文在Tanks and Temples和CO3D-V2两个基准上进行了大量评估，主要评测相机轨迹精度与新视角渲染质量。实验结果表明，所提方法显著提升了位姿估计精度和渲染质量，在性能上优于现有的无位姿（unposed）基线方法。代码已开源（https://github.com/Zijian1026/RRTO-CF3DGS）。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于提出一个统一、自包含的可靠性调节轨迹优化框架，通过自监督双向循环一致性机制，同时约束前向运动传播与回溯轨迹校正，从而在无COLMAP的渐进式3DGS中有效缓解误差累积与漂移，并在轨迹精度和渲染质量上超越无位姿基线。其意义在于无需外部神经先验或离线预处理即可实现稳健的渐进式位姿跟踪。潜在局限可能在于：方法依赖可靠性信号的设计与阈值/加权策略，在剧烈运动或退化场景下的鲁棒性、以及滑动窗口机制带来的计算与内存开销等，仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: COLMAP-free 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) bypasses computationally expensive structure-from-motion (SfM) pipelines, yet progressive camera pose tracking remains fundamentally vulnerable to error compounding---early pairwise tracking inaccuracies both corrupt subsequent frame initializations and remain permanently frozen in the scene representation. Rather than relying on heavyweight external neural priors or treating progressive tracking through isolated heuristic fixes, we propose a unified reliability-regulated trajectory optimization framework for progressive COLMAP-free 3DGS. At its core, our framework establishes an intrinsic, self-supervised bidirectional cycle-consistency mechanism that systematically regulates progressive camera trajectory estimation across two complementary temporal horizons: (1) Forward Motion Propagation, where the online reliability signal adaptively gates first-order kinematic warm-starts of rigid motion into upcoming pairwise registrations, supplying informed directional search priors while safely intercepting untrusted transitions; and (2) Retrospective Trajectory Correction, where the same reliability signal dynamically weights relative-pose consistency constraints within a sliding window of neighboring camera poses. By governing both prospective state initialization and retrospective trajectory consolidation through a unified reliability regulator, our self-contained framework resolves progressive drift without external priors or offline preprocessing. Extensive evaluations on Tanks and Temples and CO3D-V2 benchmarks show that our method substantially improves camera trajectory accuracy and novel-view rendering quality, outperforming existing unposed baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/Zijian1026/RRTO-CF3DGS.

</details>


### [32] [UltraG-Bench: A Multi-task Benchmark for assessing Large Vision-Language Models on Pixel-level Evidence Grounding in Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30928)
*Quanhao Zhu,Bo Xu,Rui Lin,Chenyuan Wang,Yu Shao,Boling Zhu,Jiuyan Sun,Liang Zhao,Hongfei Lin,Feng Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 UltraG-Bench——一个面向超声图像像素级证据定位（evidence grounding）的大规模多任务基准，并在此基础上提出 UltraG-Agent，将通用 VLM 的语义推理能力与超声专用分割模型 UltraSAM3 相结合，显著提升了语义预测与像素级视觉定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声是最广泛使用的医学影像模态之一，近期大型视觉语言模型（VLM）在超声图像理解中的语义能力不断增强。然而，这些模型在给出语义预测时无法提供与之对齐的像素级视觉证据（即无法说明"依据图像的哪个区域"得出结论），其在超声领域的细粒度定位（grounding）能力也尚不明确。核心动机是：超声诊断高度依赖对具体病灶/解剖结构区域的视觉证据，仅凭语义输出难以满足临床可解释性与可信度需求，因此需要一个系统的评测框架来量化这一能力缺口。

Method: 1) 构建 UltraG-Bench：通过标注 40 个公开超声分割数据集（覆盖 13 个解剖类别）构建大规模多任务基准，包含三个递进任务——指令引导分割、证据接地 VQA（evidence-grounded VQA）、证据接地报告生成（evidence-grounded report generation），分别含 331,125、666,779、138,832 条标注。2) 评测 14 个 SOTA 模型，揭示语义理解与细粒度像素级定位之间存在显著鸿沟。3) 提出 UltraG-Agent：将 VLM 的语义推理能力与超声专用分割模型 UltraSAM3 的像素级分割能力相耦合，实现语义预测与视觉接地的联合优化。

Result: 在 UltraG-Bench 上对 14 个 SOTA 模型进行了全面评测，覆盖指令引导分割、证据接地 VQA 和证据接地报告生成三类任务，结果显示现有模型在语义理解与像素级定位之间存在明显差距。实验表明，UltraG-Agent 在语义预测和像素级视觉定位两方面均取得显著提升。数据集与代码已开源（https://github.com/zhuqh19/UltraG-Bench）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：首次系统构建面向超声像素级证据接地的大规模多任务基准 UltraG-Bench（整合 40 个数据集、13 类解剖结构、三类递进任务），量化了现有 VLM 在细粒度定位上的能力缺口，并提出 VLM+专用分割模型协同的 UltraG-Agent 框架以缓解该问题。意义在于推动医学 VLM 从"语义可用"走向"证据可信"的可解释性方向。潜在局限：依赖现有分割数据集的标注质量与类别覆盖，代理框架的性能可能受限于底层分割模型（UltraSAM3）在超声域的表现，摘要中未给出具体数值结果与泛化性验证细节。

Abstract: Ultrasound is one of the most widely used medical imaging modalities, and recent large vision-language models(VLMs) have shown increasing capabilities in ultrasound image understanding. However, these models fail to provide pixel-level visual evidence aligned with their semantic predictions, and their fine-grained grounding capability in ultrasound remains largely unclear. We introduce UltraG-Bench, a large-scale multi-task benchmark for evaluating pixel-level evidence grounding in ultrasound. UltraG-Bench is built by annotating 40 public ultrasound segmentation datasets spanning 13 anatomical categories, and comprises three progressive tasks: instruction-guided segmentation, evidence-grounded VQA, and evidence-grounded report generation, with 331125, 666779, and 138832 annotations, respectively. Comprehensive evaluation of 14 state-of-the-art models reveals a substantial gap between semantic understanding and fine-grained pixel-level localization. We further propose UltraG-Agent, which combines the semantic reasoning capabilities of a VLM with the ultrasound-specific segmentation capability of UltraSAM3. Experiments show that UltraG-Agent substantially improves both semantic prediction and pixel-level visual grounding. Our dataset and code are available at https://github.com/zhuqh19/UltraG-Bench.

</details>


### [33] [ManiVid: Unified and Explainable Forensic Analysis of Manipulated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30934)
*Hengrui Kang,Zhonghao Yan,Yuxuan Yang,Ruoyan Jing,Yuncheng Guo,Hao Chen,Kongming Liang,Zhanyu Ma,Conghui He,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出面向"篡改视频"（manipulated video）的统一取证分析任务 ManiVid，涵盖伪造检测、伪影定位与异常解释三部分；构建首个大规模配对数据集 ManiVid-38K（约19K对真实-伪造视频、含真伪标签/伪造掩码/异常解释）及评测集 ManiVidBench，并提出可解释统一框架 ManiVidLens，通过 Forensic Evidence Router 与 Prompt Distill Module 将低层取证证据与多模态推理、视频分割相耦合，在伪影定位与异常解释上取得显著相对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成视频技术的快速进步放大了欺骗性视频操纵的风险。与完全合成的视频不同，篡改视频保留了绝大部分源内容，仅改动局部区域，使取证分析（尤其是精确定位被改区域并给出解释）极具挑战。现有研究存在两方面不足：（1）针对"篡改视频"的高质量数据集与基准稀缺；（2）多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）虽把取证从二分类扩展到更丰富的分析，但难以有效利用低层取证线索，且无法给出像素级定位。因此需要一个统一的任务定义、配套数据基准，以及能融合低层取证证据与高层多模态推理的模型框架。

Method: 1）任务定义：提出 ManiVid 统一取证分析任务，包含伪造检测（detection）、伪影定位（artifact grounding）、异常解释（anomaly explanation）三个子任务。2）数据集：构建 ManiVid-38K，首个将"开放词汇的局部篡改"与真伪标签、伪造掩码、异常解释相结合的配对数据集；包含约19K人工核验的真实-伪造视频对，多为1080P，覆盖2种生成范式、15个强生成模型；并采样1K对构成 ManiVidBench，在六类操纵类型与各生成模型间均衡以保证公平评测。3）模型：提出 ManiVidLens 统一框架——Forensic Evidence Router 为多模态推理与视频分割提供共享的低层取证证据；Prompt Distill Module 将定位状态转化为语义与几何提示，并为掩码解码及全视频传播蒸馏空间先验。

Result: 在 ManiVidBench 上评测：ManiVidLens 在伪影定位上相较最强对比方法取得 +21.1% mIoU、+21.3% J&F 的相对提升；在异常解释上取得 +131.3% ROUGE-L、+9.9% CSS 的相对提升；伪造检测性能与专用分类器相当（0.914 Acc、0.913 F1）。评测涵盖六类操纵类型并跨生成模型进行，数据集多为1080P高分辨率，兼顾真伪标签、掩码与解释三类监督。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：（1）提出统一的篡改视频取证任务 ManiVid；（2）构建首个结合配对开放词汇局部篡改与真伪标签、伪造掩码、异常解释的大规模数据集 ManiVid-38K 与均衡评测基准 ManiVidBench；（3）提出 ManiVidLens 框架，通过低层取证证据路由与提示蒸馏实现可解释的检测、定位与解释一体化，在定位与解释指标上大幅超越对比方法，同时检测精度与专用分类器持平。局限在于：篡改仅限定为局部区域改动，对全局重合成等其它类型可能覆盖不足；检测层面未超越专用分类器；数据与生成模型分布可能影响跨域泛化。

Abstract: Rapid advances in AI-generated video (AIGV) have increased the risks posed by deceptive video manipulation. Unlike fully synthetic videos, manipulated videos retain most source content and alter only localized regions, making forensic analysis particularly challenging. Existing video forgery research faces two limitations in both data and methodology: (1) High-quality datasets and benchmarks tailored for manipulated videos remain scarce. (2) Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) extend forgery analysis beyond binary classification but struggle to use low-level forensic cues and provide precise pixel-level grounding. Specifically, we introduce ManiVid, a unified forensic analysis task covering forgery detection, artifact grounding, and anomaly explanation for manipulated videos. We construct ManiVid-38K, the first dataset to combine paired, open-vocabulary localized manipulations of general videos with authenticity labels, forgery masks, and anomaly explanations. It comprises about 19K manually verified real-fake video pairs, mostly at 1080P resolution, generated under 2 paradigms with 15 powerful generation models. We sample 1K pairs for ManiVidBench, balanced across six manipulation types and generation models for fair evaluation. We further propose ManiVidLens, a unified framework for explainable video forgery analysis. Its Forensic Evidence Router supplies shared low-level forensic evidence for multimodal reasoning and video segmentation. Its Prompt Distill Module converts grounding states into semantic and geometric prompts and distills spatial priors for mask decoding and full-video propagation. ManiVidLens achieves relative gains over the strongest comparison methods in artifact grounding (+21.1% mIoU; +21.3% J&F) and anomaly explanation (+131.3% ROUGE-L; +9.9% CSS). Its forgery detection remains comparable to dedicated classifiers (0.914 Acc; 0.913 F1).

</details>


### [34] [Spackle: Completing Large View Single Image NVS with Adaptive Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30941)
*Xuanzhi Liu,Yuhe Zhou,Xinyi Wu,Zhenyao Wu,Jinghao Chen,Ruize Han,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Spackle是一个轻量级的残差学习框架，用于单图像新视角合成（NVS）。它通过为重建较差的区域学习残差3DGS，缓解混合式前馈3DGS+扩散框架中的"容量竞争"问题，在保持高效率的同时实现了大幅度视角偏差下最先进的渲染性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图像NVS需要同时具备两个关键能力：对遮挡区域的鲁棒重建以及高推理效率。混合解耦框架（前馈3DGS+扩散模型）在大视角偏差场景下很有前景，但存在"容量竞争"（capacity competition）问题：高斯数量固定，当目标视角显著偏离输入时，资源会从已可见区域转移到新出现的遮挡区域，导致原场景保真度下降。这一问题限制了该类方法在大的视角变化下的鲁棒性与实用价值。

Method: Spackle是一个轻量级残差学习框架，不破坏原有框架效率。其流程分三阶段：（1）从给定视角预测基础3DGS属性；（2）自动识别重建质量较差的区域；（3）仅针对这些区域学习残差3DGS进行专门优化。推理时，将基线高斯与增强（残差）高斯相结合以完成新视角渲染。核心思想是通过残差建模隔离"新增遮挡区域"的优化，避免与原有可见区域的容量争夺。

Result: 论文在单图像新视角合成设置下进行了综合实验，重点评估大幅度视角偏差（large-view-deviation）场景，结果显示Spackle达到了最先进的性能。摘要未给出具体数据集、评价指标（如PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS）或定量数值，也未提供效率指标的具体数据，需查阅正文确认。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出Spackle——一种缓解混合式NVS框架容量竞争问题的轻量级残差3DGS学习策略，通过"识别差区域+残差优化"实现大视角偏差下的高质量重建，同时保持高效推理。潜在局限：依赖自动识别差区域的质量；残差高斯会增加一定内存/计算开销；具体量化结果与泛化性需正文进一步验证。（注：本文与事件相机、SNN、UAV、小目标检测等主题无直接关联。）

Abstract: Single-image novel view synthesis (NVS) enables photorealistic rendering of un- observed viewpoints from a single input. Practical NVS systems require two key capabilities: robust reconstruction of occluded regions and high inference effi- ciency. While hybrid decoupled frameworks combining feedforward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) and diffusion models show promise for large-view-deviation NVS, they suffer from capacity competition: a fixed number of Gaussians forces resource shifts from visible to newly disoccluded areas, degrading original scene fidelity when the target view deviates significantly from the input. To address this, we propose Spackle, a lightweight residual learning framework that mit- igates capacity competition without sacrificing efficiency. Spackle operates in three stages: predicting base 3DGS attributes from given views, automatically identifying poorly reconstructed regions, and learning a residual 3DGS optimized exclusively for these areas. At inference, we combine the baseline and aug- mented Gaussians for NVS. We conduct comprehensive experiments and show that Spackle achieves state-of-the-art performance on large-view-deviation cases.

</details>


### [35] [OneWorld: Learning Consistent Physics Across Actions in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30946)
*Ke He,Yichen Ding,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OneWorld 是一个"共享机制"反事实视频世界模型生成框架，通过在同一潜在物理机制下联合建模多条动作条件下的未来演化，提升跨干预（cross-intervention）的物理一致性，同时保持单次 rollout 的预测质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件视频世界模型需要预测不同动作下场景的演化，这是动态环境中规划、决策与交互的基础能力。然而，从同一初始场景独立生成的多个未来虽然各自看似合理，却可能隐含互不相容的物理属性（如摩擦系数、质量）。这种跨干预的不一致会导致预测相互矛盾，使模型无法维持对底层世界的一致理解，从而削弱其在规划与决策中的可靠性。核心问题在于：如何保证多条动作-结果分支能被同一套物理参数共同解释。

Method: 论文提出 OneWorld，一个共享机制的反事实生成框架。关键设计包括：(1) 物理机制解释器（physical mechanism interpreter）从每条动作-结果分支中推断潜在机制的分布；(2) 将这些分布聚合为"共享世界证据"（shared-world evidence），该证据既刻画各分支是否可被共同物理解释，又能对信息量较低的分支的不确定性进行折中处理；(3) 用该证据约束流（flow）训练并引导采样，从而在保持不同动作导致的不同结果的同时，促使底层物理机制趋于一致。此外，论文提出了一套多干预评估协议，用于检验生成的多条未来能否被同一组物理参数共同解释。

Result: 实验在受控环境中进行，交互设置沿用 ACWM-Phys。评估包含两类指标：一是标准单次 rollout 预测质量指标，二是新提出的多干预评估协议，用于衡量生成未来是否可被同一物理参数共同解释。结果表明，OneWorld 在提升跨干预物理一致性的同时，保持了有竞争力的单次 rollout 预测质量。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：将动作条件视频世界模型的物理一致性显式建模为跨分支共享潜在机制推断与聚合问题，并提出相应的多干预评估协议。意义在于提升世界模型在规划与决策中的可靠性和可解释性。潜在局限包括：实验主要在受控环境与 ACWM-Phys 交互设置下验证，向真实复杂场景的泛化性、以及"共享世界证据"对更复杂物理机制的表达能力仍待进一步检验。

Abstract: Action-conditioned video world models aim to predict scene evolution under different actions, a capability that is essential for reliable planning, decision-making, and interaction in dynamic environments. However, futures generated independently from the same initial scene may each appear plausible while implying incompatible physical properties, such as friction or mass. This inconsistency can lead to contradictory predictions across interventions, making it difficult for the model to maintain a coherent understanding of the underlying world and limiting its reliability for planning and decision-making. To address these issues, we propose OneWorld, a shared-mechanism counterfactual generation framework that jointly models multiple action-conditioned futures under a common latent physical mechanism. A physical mechanism interpreter first infers a distribution over latent mechanisms from each action-outcome branch. These distributions are then aggregated into shared-world evidence, which captures whether the branches admit a common physical explanation while accounting for uncertainty in less informative branches. This evidence constrains flow training and guides sampling, encouraging consistency in the underlying physical mechanism while preserving the distinct outcomes induced by different actions. We further introduce a multi-intervention evaluation protocol in controlled environments, following the interaction settings of ACWM-Phys, to assess whether generated futures can be jointly explained by the same physical parameters, alongside standard measures of single-rollout prediction quality. Experiments in these environments show that OneWorld improves cross-intervention physical consistency while maintaining competitive single-rollout prediction quality.

</details>


### [36] [DAPEVO: Deep Adaptive Patch Frame-Event Visual Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30947)
*Luca Gandolfi,Simone Nascivera,Roberto Pellerito,Rong Zou,Chiara Plizzari,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DAPEVO是一个学习式的视觉里程计系统，在共享patch位置上独立估计图像与事件对应关系，并在运动细化前融合两种模态的关联证据。通过逐patch-frame边的标量门控融合模态特定相关性嵌入，再经共享循环细化与BA更新，实现对RGB帧稀疏或缺失场景的鲁棒跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPS拒止环境下的自主导航依赖视觉里程计，但纯RGB方法对运动模糊、极端光照和丢帧敏感。事件相机具备高时间分辨率与高动态范围，可弥补RGB不足，但其异步测量使对应关系估计困难。现有方法（DPVO、RAMP-VO等）在RGB输入退化时性能急剧下降，而纯事件方法（DEVO）精度仍有限。因此需要一种能在RGB稀疏甚至缺失时仍保持稳健定位的多模态融合VO方案。

Method: DAPEVO在每个被跟踪patch处维护图像描述子与事件描述子，独立地在共享patch位置估计图像与事件两种对应关系。对每个patch-frame边，使用一个学习到的标量门将模态特定的相关性嵌入进行加权融合，随后送入共享的循环细化模块与bundle adjustment更新进行运动估计。系统支持event-only观测，使RGB帧稀疏或不可用时仍可继续跟踪；同时引入模态感知的关键帧剔除（modality-aware keyframe culling），以保留稀缺的帧约束。

Result: 在UZH-FPV数据集上仅保留1/6 RGB帧时，DAPEVO平均绝对轨迹误差（ATE）仅从1.00m升至1.36m（增加36%），而DPVO与RAMP-VO的ATE分别上升3.7倍和3.1倍。在TartanEvent上以3Hz RGB输入时，DAPEVO维持低于1m的ATE，而DPVO与RAMP-VO超过9m。在TartanEvent退化RGB输入下，DAPEVO达到0.60m ATE，而DPVO与RAMP-VO均超过4m，同时优于纯事件方法DEVO的0.87m。

Conclusion: DAPEVO通过共享patch上的双模态对应估计与学习式门控融合，显著提升了RGB退化（稀疏帧、低帧率、光照/运动退化）条件下的VO鲁棒性，并支持event-only模式延续跟踪。其优势在于多模态证据的细粒度逐边融合与模态感知关键帧管理，实验证明其ATE在低帧率与退化RGB输入下大幅优于DPVO、RAMP-VO，并超越纯事件DEVO。潜在局限包括依赖事件与图像描述子的联合训练质量、门控机制在大尺度或长期漂移场景下的泛化性，以及论文摘要未提供的计算开销与实时性评估。

Abstract: Visual odometry is essential for autonomous navigation in GPS-denied environments, yet RGB-based methods remain vulnerable to motion blur, challenging illumination, and dropped frames. Event cameras complement conventional cameras with high temporal resolution and dynamic range, but their asynchronous measurements complicate reliable correspondence estimation. We present DAPEVO, a learned visual odometry system that estimates image and event correspondences independently at shared patch locations and fuses their correlation evidence before motion refinement. Each tracked patch maintains image and event descriptors, and a learned scalar gate combines modality-specific correlation embeddings for each patch--frame edge before a shared recurrent refinement and bundle-adjustment update. DAPEVO also supports event-only observations, enabling continued tracking when RGB frames are sparse or unavailable, while modality-aware keyframe culling preserves scarce frame constraints. On UZH-FPV, when retaining only one in six RGB frames, DAPEVO's mean absolute trajectory error (ATE) increases by only 36%, from 1.00 to 1.36m, whereas the ATE of DPVO and RAMP-VO rises by factors of $3.7\times$ and $3.1\times$, respectively. On TartanEvent, DAPEVO similarly remains below 1m ATE at 3Hz RGB input, while DPVO and RAMP-VO exceed 9m. Under degraded RGB input on TartanEvent, DAPEVO achieves an ATE of 0.60m, compared with more than 4m for both DPVO and RAMP-VO, while also outperforming event-only DEVO at 0.87m.

</details>


### [37] [MVVBench: Benchmarking 4D Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30952)
*Hyungjin Chung,Byeongjun Park,Joonseok Lee,Hojun Kim,Jaeho Choi,Byung-Hoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MVVBench 是一个基于真实多相机数据集构建的多视角视频推理基准，其问题设计为"单视角歧义"（单视图不可答、多数问题单时刻亦不可答），只在跨视角、跨时间联合推理时才唯一可解。论文系统分析了当前 VLM 在多视角 4D 推理上的失败模式，并提出推理时提示策略与 RLVR 训练方法以激发潜在能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角视频理解需要跨多个（常常不重叠的）相机流整合空间与时间证据：跟踪实体在视角间的转移、跨时间对齐事件、并推理潜在的 4D 连续性，而非依赖任一单帧可见信息。然而现有多视角基准往往允许通过单视图或单时刻推断出答案，无法真正检验模型是否具备跨视角、跨时间的联合 4D 推理能力。作者认为，要推动可靠的具身感知（embodied perception），必须有一个严格排除捷径的评测工具，同时需要厘清当前视觉语言模型（VLM）在多视角推理上成功或失败的具体原因。

Method: 论文的核心方法包含构建基准与能力诊断两部分。基准构建方面：MVVBench 从真实世界多相机数据集派生，问题经过 "monocular-ambiguous"（单目歧义）约束筛选——每个问题在指定输入集内的任一单视角下均不可回答，且大多数问题在任一单时刻下也不可回答，因此只有在跨视角与跨时间联合推理后才唯一可解。任务覆盖六类能力：隐式/显式属性识别、隐式/显式相对距离、相对相机位姿、组合式计数（compositional counting）；所有 QA 由人工撰写并经严格验证。分析方面：论文刻画了三类典型错误——时间定位错误（temporal mis-localization）、跨视角身份断裂（cross-view identity breaks）、以及脆弱的多跳推理。在此基础上，论文研究推理时激发（inference-time elicitation）策略，包括任务特定链式思维（chain-of-thought）脚手架与结构化跨视角证据聚合，无需重训练即可释放潜藏的多视角能力。最后，论文提供初步证据表明，带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）可在基础模型中激发部分潜在的多视角能力。

Result: 论文构建并使用了 MVVBench 这一由真实多相机数据集派生、包含多类动态场景与六种推理能力的验证型基准，评价体系基于人工撰写与严格核验的 QA。主要发现包括：(1) 当前 VLM 在多视角 4D 推理上存在系统性问题，错误可归因于时间定位错误、跨视角身份断裂和脆弱的多跳推理；(2) 采用任务特定 chain-of-thought 脚手架与结构化跨视角证据聚合等推理时策略，可在不重训练的情况下取得显著性能提升；(3) 初步证据显示，使用可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）能够激发基础模型的部分潜在多视角能力，提示训练时方法是有前景的未来方向。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：（1）提出 MVVBench，一个通过"单视角歧义"设计严格排除单视图/单时刻捷径的真实多相机 4D 推理基准，覆盖六类能力并配有验证 QA；（2）系统刻画当前 VLM 在多视角推理中的失败模式（时间误定位、跨视角身份断裂、多跳推理脆弱）；（3）证明推理时激发策略与 RLVR 均可释放潜在的多视角能力。其意义在于为可靠的具身感知与 4D 多视角推理提供了严格评测基础与改进方向。潜在局限在于：论文对 RLVR 仅给出"初步证据"，尚不足以确证该训练时方法的可扩展性与稳定性；基准的规模、场景与相机配置多样性，以及推理时提示策略的泛化性等，仍需后续工作进一步验证。此外，该工作面向通用多视角视频理解，未涉及事件相机、脉冲神经网络等神经形态或高效边缘部署场景。

Abstract: Multi-view video understanding requires integrating spatial and temporal evidence across multiple, often non-overlapping camera streams: tracking entities as they transition between viewpoints, aligning events across time, and reasoning about latent 4D continuity rather than any single visible frame. We introduce MVVBench, a benchmark for multi-view video reasoning built from real world multi camera datasets. Questions are curated to be monocular-ambiguous along both the view and the temporal axis: each question is unanswerable from any single view in the designated input set, and the majority are further unanswerable from any single moment. Each question becomes uniquely solvable only by jointly reasoning across views and across time. MVVBench spans diverse dynamic scenes and probes six capabilities: implicit/explicit attribute identification, implicit/explicit relative distance, relative camera pose, and compositional counting, with human-authored QA and rigorous verification. Beyond benchmarking, we provide an extensive analysis of when and why current vision language models succeed or fail, characterizing errors due to temporal mis-localization, cross-view identity breaks, and brittle multi-hop reasoning. We then study inference-time elicitation strategies that unlock latent multi-view competence---task-specific chain-of-thought scaffolds and structured cross-view evidence aggregation---yielding substantial gains without retraining. Finally, we present preliminary evidence that reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards can elicit some latent multi-view competence in the base model, pointing to training-time approaches as a promising direction for future work. Together, MVVBench offers a rigorous evaluation of 4D multi-view reasoning and a foundation for future progress toward reliable embodied perception.

</details>


### [38] [IDM-Net: A Lightweight Illumination-Decoupled Modulation Network for Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30962)
*Cheng-Yen Hsiao,Jing-Ming Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IDM-Net 是一种轻量级"光照解耦调制"网络，通过将亮度（Y）通道作为显式引导先验注入解码器，在不显著增加计算量的前提下兼顾低光增强的亮度恢复与色彩保真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光图像增强（LLIE）对轻量模型而言十分困难：在 RGB 空间中，光照恢复与色彩保真难以同时优化。近期一些"颜色解耦"方法虽将亮度与色度分离，但通常仅把亮度当作增强目标（优化对象），而忽视了亮度作为**显式引导先验**在特征重建阶段的价值。该文旨在挖掘解耦亮度作为先验信号、引导特征重建的潜力，以解决轻量模型下亮度与色彩难以兼顾的矛盾。

Method: 提出 IDM-Net，采用双编码器架构：(1) 结构编码器从 RGB 图像提取多尺度外观（结构）特征；(2) 轻量光照编码器从解耦的亮度（Y）通道学习光照先验。核心创新为 Illumination-Guided Modulation（IGM）模块，通过空间自适应仿射调制（spatially adaptive affine modulation）将多尺度光照线索注入解码器，从而在保持自然色彩一致性的同时实现准确的亮度恢复。此外设计了轻量的 Feature Refinement Block（FRB），在重建过程中逐步抑制退化伪影并恢复细粒度纹理细节。

Result: 在多个标准低光图像增强基准数据集（benchmark）上进行了大量实验。结果表明，IDM-Net 在轻量级 LLIE 方法中取得有竞争力的性能，并在恢复质量与计算效率之间保持了良好平衡（兼顾增强效果与轻量化）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献为：以解耦亮度作为显式引导先验的 IGM 调制机制、支持该机制的双编码器架构，以及轻量 FRB 细化模块，共同在轻量设定下改善亮度恢复与色彩保真。其意义在于为轻量 LLIE 提供了一条"亮度先验调制特征重建"的高效路径。潜在局限：摘要未给出具体量化指标、参数量/FLOPs 与推理速度，也未报告真实低光场景或跨域泛化、噪声极端条件下的鲁棒性，且对 Y 通道解耦质量及彩度保持的边界条件缺乏细节说明。

Abstract: Low-light image enhancement (LLIE) remains challenging for lightweight models because illumination restoration and color fidelity are difficult to optimize simultaneously in the RGB color space. Although recent color-decoupled methods separate luminance and chrominance representations, they primarily optimize luminance as an enhancement target, leaving its potential as an explicit guidance prior largely unexplored during feature reconstruction. To address this limitation, we propose IDM-Net, a lightweight Illumination-Decoupled Modulation Network for low-light image enhancement. IDM-Net adopts a dual-encoder architecture consisting of a structure encoder that extracts multi-scale appearance features from the RGB image and a lightweight illumination encoder that learns illumination priors from the decoupled luminance (Y) channel. To effectively exploit these priors, we introduce an Illumination-Guided Modulation (IGM) module that injects multi-scale illumination cues into the decoder through spatially adaptive affine modulation, enabling accurate brightness restoration while preserving natural color consistency. Furthermore, we design a lightweight Feature Refinement Block (FRB) to progressively suppress degradation artifacts and recover fine-grained image details during reconstruction. Extensive experiments on multiple standard low-light image enhancement benchmarks demonstrate that IDM-Net achieves competitive performance among lightweight LLIE methods while maintaining an excellent balance between restoration quality and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [39] [Where and When to Force: Routed Forcing for Streaming Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30963)
*Zihan Su,Siwen Lu,Junhao Zhuang,Zeyue Xue,Haoyang Huang,Guanghao Li,Xiaofeng Tan,Chun Yuan,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Routed Forcing，针对音频驱动实时虚拟人生成中 Self Forcing（基于 DMD 蒸馏）导致动态与多样性坍塌的问题，通过按语义区域与噪声阶段路由蒸馏目标（DFD/DMD 混合）来恢复运动多样性与动态性，同时保持视觉质量与口型同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频驱动的流式虚拟人生成要求实时合成与语音同步、且具备动态和多样运动的视频。Self Forcing 使用分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）将双向视频扩散模型蒸馏为因果、少步生成器以实现实时流式推理。然而 DMD 最小化的是反向 KL 散度，本质上属于 mode-seeking（模式寻求），会促使学生模型丢弃高动态模式、坍缩到静态输出，从而同时压缩生成视频的动态性与多样性。作者进一步发现这种坍塌是区域异质的：包含姿态与手势变化的人物区域多样性损失最大，音频驱动的嘴部区域损失较小，背景几乎保持稳定。这一现象说明统一的蒸馏目标并非最优，需要针对不同区域的坍塌程度采用差异化监督。

Method: 提出 Routed Forcing，核心思想是按语义区域与噪声阶段对蒸馏目标进行路由，以在保持视觉质量的同时提升动态性与多样性。具体包含两个维度：(1) Where to Force —— 语义区域路由（Semantic-Region Routing）：在多样性坍塌最严重的 person 区域使用 Data-Forcing Distillation（DFD），即用真实视频对学生模型进行监督；而在嘴部与背景区域保留 DMD，以维持唇形同步与场景稳定性。(2) When to Force —— 噪声阶段路由（Noise-Stage Routing）：在高噪声阶段激活 DFD，此时真实视频能作为有效监督注入多样化且动态的运动模式；在低噪声阶段使用 DMD 进行细节精修，避免因真实视频与学生生成视频之间的空间差异导致的模糊和伪影。该方案在因果少步流式生成器框架下对 DMD 蒸馏目标进行区域与阶段的混合路由。

Result: 实验表明，相较于 Self Forcing，Routed Forcing 将动态性提升最高达 45%，多样性提升 7–25%，同时保持视频质量与唇形同步。结果说明通过在语义区域与噪声阶段层面进行差异化路由监督，可有效缓解 DMD 反向 KL 导致的模式坍塌，且针对嘴部与背景继续使用 DMD 有助于维持同步与稳定性。（摘要未提供具体数据集名称与评测指标细节，上述数值为相对提升。）

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：揭示了 Self Forcing/DMD 蒸馏在音频驱动虚拟人生成中的动态-多样性坍塌具有区域异质性，并提出 Routed Forcing，通过语义区域路由与噪声阶段路由将 DFD 与 DMD 组合使用，在提升动态性与多样性的同时保持视觉质量与唇形同步。其意义在于为流式实时虚拟人蒸馏提供了更精细的监督分配策略。潜在局限：摘要未给出具体数据集、评测指标与定量基线的详细对比，DFD 在高噪声阶段依赖真实视频监督、低噪声阶段切换回 DMD，路由策略的设计与超参（区域划分、噪声阶段阈值）的敏感性及其泛化性尚未明确说明。

Abstract: Audio-driven streaming avatar generation requires real-time synthesis of speech-synchronized videos with dynamic and diverse motion. Self Forcing uses Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) to distill bidirectional video diffusion models into causal, few-step generators for real-time streaming. However, DMD minimizes a reverse KL divergence, which is inherently mode-seeking: it causes the student to discard high-dynamic modes and collapse onto static outputs, compressing both dynamics and diversity of generated videos. We find that this collapse is region-heterogeneous: person regions involving pose and gesture variations suffer the largest diversity loss, the audio-driven mouth region shows a small loss, and the background remains nearly stable. Based on this observation, we propose Routed Forcing, which routes the distillation objective by semantic region and noise stage to improve dynamics and diversity while preserving visual quality. Specifically, (1) Where to Force: Semantic-Region Routing applies Data-Forcing Distillation (DFD), which supervises the student with real videos, to the person region where diversity collapse is most severe, while retaining DMD for the mouth and background to preserve lip synchronization and scene stability. (2) When to Force: Noise-Stage Routing activates DFD at high noise stages, where real video serves as effective supervision to inject diverse and dynamic motion patterns. At low noise stages, DMD is used to refine details, avoiding blur and artifacts from spatial differences between real video and student-generated video. Experiments show that Routed Forcing improves dynamics by up to 45% and diversity by 7-25% over Self Forcing, while preserving video quality and lip synchronization.

</details>


### [40] [CCRV-Bench: Constraint-Based Evaluation of Causal Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30979)
*Linyuan Gao,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 CCRV-Bench，一个面向单图像物理场景的"约束驱动"视觉因果推理基准，通过正交化任务维度与多类约束，区分 VLM 是基于视觉证据进行因果推理还是依赖统计捷径，并系统揭示模型在约束下的性能退化模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）在各类视觉任务上表现优异，但其"视觉因果推理"能力缺乏可靠评估。现有评测难以区分模型是真正基于视觉证据做因果推断，还是利用数据集中的统计相关性进行捷径学习（shortcut learning），从而系统性高估模型的实际能力。这一问题在物理场景理解、机器人感知等需要真实因果判断的任务中尤为关键。作者因此需要一个能在受控约束下诊断图像接地（image-grounded）因果推理的标准化评测框架。

Method: 论文构建了 CCRV-Bench，其核心是一个"正交框架"（orthogonal framework），将因果推理分解为四个评测维度：因果关系发现（causal relation discovery）、状态预测（state prediction）、因果诊断（causal diagnosis）与干预（intervention）。为降低捷径线索、同时保留任务所需的物理常识，作者引入四类约束：实体符号化（entity symbolization）、空间接地（spatial grounding）、事实对抗约束（factual adversarial constraint）以及极简输出约束（minimalist output constraints）。通过对比模型在无约束与受约束条件下的表现差异（即约束敏感性），诊断其推理的鲁棒性与失败类型，而非仅依赖单一聚合分数。

Result: 作者在 15 个多模态模型上进行了评测，主要发现包括：(1) 约束敏感性具有任务依赖性与模型依赖性；(2) 在四个因果任务中，"干预"任务的平均有效性能退化（effective degradation）最大；(3) 平均而言，空间接地约束对模型性能的破坏最严重；(4) 事实对抗约束对提高 DCR（degradation/因果相关指标）对所有被测模型均有正向作用。结论表明，无约束下的性能并不能决定受约束条件下的鲁棒性，单一聚合分数会掩盖模型在因果识别、空间接地与约束合规表达上的差异性失败。

Conclusion: CCRV-Bench 的贡献在于提供了一个受控约束下诊断图像接地因果推理的标准化基准，并通过正交任务设计与多约束组合，揭示了 VLM 视觉因果推理的脆弱性与失败模式，对评估模型是否真正进行因果推理具有重要意义。可能的局限在于：任务限定于单图像物理场景，未涉及视频/时序或事件流等动态推理；约束设计可能引入人为偏置，且 15 个模型的覆盖面与 DCR 等指标的普适性仍有待进一步验证。代码已开源于 GitHub。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have demonstrated excellent performance in visual tasks, but their visual causal reasoning capabilities still lack reliable evaluation. Existing evaluations struggle to distinguish whether a model is performing causal reasoning based on visual evidence or relying on statistical correlations for shortcut learning, thereby potentially overestimating their actual capabilities. This paper proposes CCRV-Bench, a constraint-driven visual causal reasoning benchmark for single-image physical scenarios. We construct an orthogonal framework that evaluates four causal task dimensions: causal relation discovery, state prediction, causal diagnosis, and intervention. We further introduce entity symbolization, spatial grounding, the factual adversarial constraint, and minimalist output constraints to reduce shortcut cues while preserving the physical commonsense required by the task. Experiments across 15 multimodal models show that constraint sensitivity is task- and model-dependent: intervention has the largest average effective degradation among the four causal tasks, spatial grounding is the most damaging constraint on average, and the factual adversarial constraint improves DCR for all evaluated models. These results show that unconstrained performance does not determine constrained robustness and that a single aggregate score can obscure distinct failures in causal identification, spatial grounding, and constraint-compliant expression. CCRV-Bench provides a standardized framework for diagnosing image-grounded causal reasoning under controlled constraints. The code is available at https://github.com/0815linyuan/CCRV-Bench-Constraint-Based-Evaluation-of-Causal-Reasoning-in-Vision-Language-Models

</details>


### [41] [STORM-Bench: Evaluating Online Video QA under Evolving and Incomplete Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30981)
*Siru Zhong,Shenghan Tan,Rihong Yan,Xiaohui Lv,Yuzheng Zhuang,Shuai Tao,Wulong Liu,Haohuan Fu,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 STORM-Bench，一个面向"在线视频问答"的可靠性评测基准，强调在演化且证据不完整的视频流中同时追踪状态转移与在证据不足时选择性弃答（abstention）。基准包含 5,736 个问题、630 个变化密集的紧凑 episode，覆盖 5 个第一人称域（STORM-Real）与 2 个受控仿真子集（STORM-Sim），并提出联合衡量答案与弃答状态正确性的调和指标 STORM-BR 及不确定性归因指标 STORM-BR-ATTR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的在线视频问答不仅要求在证据充足时给出正确答案，还要求在视频状态持续演化、且当前时刻视觉证据不足以支撑回答时能够"选择性弃答"，避免过度自信的错误输出。现有基准多聚焦静态识别或长程检索，很少在"证据随时间演化且不完整"的条件下联合评测这两项耦合能力，导致任务准确率可能掩盖模型在认知可靠性（epistemic reliability）与状态追踪上的缺陷。

Method: 作者构建 STORM-Bench：含 630 个紧凑、变化密集的 episode，共 5,736 个问题，采样率 1 FPS；分为 STORM-Real（5 个第一人称域）和 STORM-Sim（2 个受控仿真子集）。问题按两个维度分层：以累积变化强度为代理指标的 Low/Medium/High，以及查询时刻的可回答性 Known/Uncertain。评测指标方面，提出 STORM-BR——对"答案正确性 + 弃答状态正确性"的联合正确性取调和平均，用以暴露被聚合准确率掩盖的弃答失败；并提出 STORM-BR-ATTR 用于不确定性归因。

Result: 在 14 个视频大语言模型（video LLM）上进行评测。模型在线准确率最高仅 60.3%（均值 51.7%），但 STORM-BR 仅在 5.7%–35.6% 区间（均值 18.8%），差距显著，主要源于模型在 Uncertain 查询上普遍过度自信。实验表明任务准确率会掩盖认知可靠性与状态追踪方面的实际缺口。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出一个联合评估在线状态追踪与选择性弃答可靠性的基准 STORM-Bench，以及能揭示弃答失败的调和指标 STORM-BR 与不确定性归因指标 STORM-BR-ATTR，并通过 14 个 video LLM 的实验量化了模型在认知可靠性上的显著不足。局限可能在于 1 FPS 采样与基于代理指标的变化强度分层，以及主要面向 video LLM 的评测范围。

Abstract: Reliable online video question answering requires tracking state transitions while selectively abstaining when visual evidence is insufficient. Existing benchmarks focus on static recognition or long-range retrieval, rarely evaluating these coupled capabilities under evolving and incomplete evidence. We present STORM-Bench, comprising 5,736 questions across 630 compact, change-dense episodes spanning five egocentric domains (STORM-Real) and two controlled simulation subsets (STORM-Sim) at 1 FPS. Questions are stratified by a proxy for accumulated change intensity (Low, Medium, High) and query-time answerability (Known, Uncertain). To measure reliability, we introduce STORM-BR, a harmonic metric over joint answer-status correctness that exposes abstention failures masked by aggregate accuracy, alongside STORM-BR-ATTR for uncertainty attribution. Across 14 video LLMs, online accuracy peaks at 60.3\% (mean 51.7\%), whereas STORM-BR ranges from 5.7\% to 35.6\% (mean 18.8\%), driven by pervasive overconfidence on uncertain queries. STORM-Bench shows that task accuracy masks these gaps in epistemic reliability and state tracking. Benchmark and code are available at https://github.com/siruzhong/STORM-Bench.

</details>


### [42] [FARE: Forensic Acceptance Region Estimation for Catching Bait-and-Switch Image Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30982)
*Kai Yao,Marc Juarez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 FARE（Forensic Acceptance Region Estimation），一种面向以不透明 API 形式部署的图像生成器的"部署时完整性审计"方法：通过对认证生成器采样的图像进行训练，建立生成器特定的"接受域"，从而仅凭单张生成图像即可判断其是否来自已认证的生成器，用于检测提供方在认证后静默切换为更廉价、低质模型的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 AI 图像生成器多以黑盒 API 形式交付，客户可查询服务却无法检查模型权重或架构，因此存在治理漏洞：提供方可能用高质量模型通过认证，随后在实际部署中偷偷替换为更便宜、质量更低的模型。这种行为会破坏公众信任，在高风险领域中甚至带来安全隐患。现有的模型归属/取证方法多针对权重可得的白盒场景，难以应对仅有输入-输出访问权限的部署时审计需求，因此需要一种仅依赖生成图像本身、可在部署阶段执行的完整性审计手段。

Method: FARE 的核心是"取证接受域估计"（Forensic Acceptance Region Estimation）。注册阶段（enrollment）从被认证的生成器中采样图像并训练 FARE，使其学习到该生成器的判别边界，即接受域。检测阶段仅输入单张待测生成图像，判断其是否与已注册生成器一致，无需访问模型权重或架构。特征层面，FARE 基于针对图像生成器特有的取证伪影（generator-specific artifacts）构建，这些伪影此前已被用于图像取证任务。训练上，方法通过挖掘困难样本（hard samples）来放大这些特征的作用，从而收紧接受域并提升对认证生成器细微变化的敏感度。该方法支持在严格的运行点（strict operating points）上进行判别。

Result: 论文在多种生成器替换场景下进行评估，包括替换为相似模型版本以及模型变体（model variants）的情形。结果显示，FARE 能够有效检测生成器替换，在严格运行点下持续优于现有基线方法。此外，在论文所评估的精确模型攻击（exact-model attack）与仅决策攻击（decision-only attack）下，FARE 仍保持有效。摘要未给出具体数据集名称、评价指标数值或定量对比表。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出并验证了"部署时生成器完整性审计"这一问题设定，以及相应的 FARE 方法：通过生成器特定取证伪影构建接受域，实现仅凭单张图像判断生成器一致性的黑盒审计，并在多种替换场景与攻击下优于基线。其意义在于为不透明生成 API 的治理认证提供了可落地的检测机制。潜在局限包括：摘要未说明接受域在生成器发生重大架构变化、后处理扰动或分布外输入下的鲁棒性，也未报告数据集规模与误报/漏报的定量结果；同时方法的泛化性与计算成本尚未在摘要中明确。

Abstract: Modern AI image generators are increasingly deployed as opaque APIs, where customers can query the deployed service, but cannot inspect model weights or architecture. This creates a practical challenge: a provider may pass governance certification with one generator and later silently switch to a cheaper and lower-quality one for deployment, compromising public trust or even safety in high-stakes domains. We study integrity auditing at deployment time and propose FARE (Forensic Acceptance Region Estimation). A certified generator is enrolled by training FARE on images sampled from that generator. After deployment, FARE can determine whether a generated image is consistent with the enrolled generator---using only that image. FARE's features are based on image generator-specific artifacts that have been proposed for forensic applications. FARE amplifies these features during training by finding hard samples that tighten the acceptance region and increase sensitivity to subtle changes in the certified generator. Across generator swaps, including substitutions with similar model versions and model variants, FARE is effective at detecting swaps, consistently outperforming existing baselines at strict operating points, and remains effective under the exact-model and decision-only attacks evaluated in this work.

</details>


### [43] [Self-Supervised Perceptually Interpretable Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30987)
*Zain Ul Abidin,George Dimas,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 PIMDE，一种可感知解释的自监督单目深度估计（MDE）框架。它不直接在 RGB 输入上回归深度，而是将图像分解为若干编码特定视觉线索的感知特征图（PFM），由独立分支分别预测深度（PIDE），再通过显式融合策略合并，从而可分析每种感知线索对深度预测的贡献，并在 KITTI 上取得与主流自监督 MDE 方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督单目深度估计无需真值深度，适合大规模和真实场景应用，精度也在持续提升。但现有方法多直接基于 RGB 表征回归深度，RGB 是多种视觉线索（如纹理、边缘、阴影、透视等）的纠缠混合，导致单个感知成分对深度预测的影响被掩盖，模型缺乏透明度。这种不透明性使得难以系统分析失败案例，也降低了在安全关键场景（如自动驾驶、机器人感知）中的可信度。作者希望在不牺牲精度的前提下，将深度预测与输入图像中可区分的感知成分建立对应关系，从而提升可解释性。

Method: 核心思路是"感知分解 + 分分支预测 + 显式融合"。首先将每张输入图像分解为一组感知特征图（Perceptual Feature Maps, PFM），每个 PFM 编码一种特定的视觉线索。随后采用多个彼此独立的深度估计分支，分别处理各个 PFM，得到对应的感知解释深度估计（Perceptual Interpretable Depth Estimates, PIDE）。最后通过一个显式的融合策略将这些 PIDE 组合为最终深度图。由于分支之间相互独立且融合是显式的，可以直接考察每种感知线索对最终深度预测的贡献，实现从 RGB 黑箱到可归因、可分析的深度估计流程的转变。整体仍保持自监督训练范式。

Result: 在 KITTI 基准数据集上进行实验评估。结果表明，PIMDE 在深度估计精度上与既有自监督 MDE 方法相当（comparable performance），说明感知分解并未以精度下降为代价；同时该方法额外提供了关于不同感知线索如何影响深度估计的洞察，便于分析各线索的贡献与失败模式。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出 PIMDE 框架，通过将图像分解为感知特征图、独立分支预测并显式融合，把可解释性引入自监督单目深度估计，并在 KITTI 上验证了"可解释性可与精度共存"。其意义在于支持对深度估计失败案例的系统分析，提升安全关键场景下的可信度。潜在局限：摘要仅报告与现有方法"相当"而非更优的性能；感知特征图的具体定义、分解方式、融合策略的细节及计算开销未在摘要中说明；可解释性主要基于线索归因，是否等价于因果解释仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Self-supervised monocular depth estimation (MDE) enables depth prediction from monocular images without requiring ground-truth supervision, making it attractive for large-scale and real-world applications. Despite steady improvements in accuracy, most existing methods remain difficult to interpret, as depth is inferred from RGB representations that obscure the impact of individual perceptual image components. This lack of transparency limits systematic analysis of failure cases and reduces confidence in safety-critical settings. This paper presents a self-supervised framework for perceptually interpretable monocular depth estimation (PIMDE), designed to associate depth predictions with distinct perceptual components of the input image. Rather than operating directly on RGB inputs, the proposed method decomposes each image into a set of perceptual feature maps (PFMs), each encoding a specific visual cue. Distinct depth estimation branches process these PFMs independently to produce depth estimates (PIDEs), which are subsequently combined through an explicit fusion strategy. This formulation allows us to examine directly the contribution of each perceptual cue to the final depth prediction. Experiments conducted on the KITTI benchmark dataset demonstrate that PIMDE achieves performance comparable to established self-supervised MDE methods while providing additional insight into how different perceptual cues influence depth estimation. These results indicate that perceptual decomposition can support interpretability without sacrificing depth estimation accuracy.

</details>


### [44] [PhoenixSR: Generative Heterogeneous Distillation Unleashes Efficient Models for Real-World Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30988)
*Xin Di,Mingyu Shi,Yuanfei Bao,Long Peng,Yue Zhao,Jiaming Guo,Renjing Pei,Xueyang Fu,Yang Cao,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhoenixSR 提出一种生成式异构蒸馏框架，将预训练扩散模型的生成先验通过基于分数的分布匹配迁移到独立的、无扩散组件的前馈超分网络中。训练后移除所有扩散相关部件，推理时保持原学生网络的架构与计算成本不变。方法引入异构分布自适应与方向可靠性加权两项技术，在三个 SR 基准和六个前馈骨干上实现一致的感知质量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界图像超分辨（Real-world SR）需要在复杂低分辨率观测下重建感知逼真且内容忠实的高分辨率图像。现有方法存在两难：基于扩散的 SR 借助强生成先验能产生逼真高频细节，但推理时计算开销巨大（多步采样）；而前馈 CNN 与 Transformer SR 模型效率高，却难以恢复现实的高频纹理细节。因此核心问题是：能否在不引入推理期扩散组件的前提下，把扩散先验迁移到已有的无扩散 SR 网络中？该问题对实时、高效的高质量图像恢复具有重要意义。

Method: 提出 PhoenixSR，一种生成式异构蒸馏框架，通过基于分数的分布匹配（score-based distribution matching）将扩散先验迁移到独立设计的前馈 SR 网络。与对齐异构特征或模仿采样得到的扩散输出不同，该框架把预训练扩散模型用作分布级监督，同时用成对的 SR 监督保持重建保真度。为让分布匹配适配对保真度敏感的 SR 任务，引入异构分布自适应（Heterogeneous Distribution Adaptation）：将目标分数自适应到 SR 域、改善对不断演化的学生分布的跟踪，并用成对监督锚定训练。此外提出方向可靠性加权（Directional Reliability Weighting），一种基于残差一致性的轻量级重加权策略，用于抑制不稳定的分布引导。训练完成后，所有扩散相关组件被移除，学生网络架构与推理成本保持不变。

Result: 在三个 SR 基准数据集上进行实验，覆盖六个前馈骨干网络，包括 SwinIR、HAT、Real-ESRGAN 和 SeeMoRe。结果表明，PhoenixSR 在保持重建保真度大体不变的同时，带来一致的感知质量提升。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出了一种无需推理期扩散组件的生成先验蒸馏范式：以分布级分数匹配替代特征对齐或输出模仿，配合异构分布自适应与方向可靠性加权，使高效前馈 SR 网络获得扩散级生成先验。方法在多种骨干和基准上验证了通用性与感知收益，且不增加推理成本。潜在局限在于：训练阶段依赖预训练扩散模型，蒸馏过程与分布匹配的稳定性/超参（如重加权）仍需调优，且实验结果主要报告感知指标提升，保真度仅"大体保持"，定量细节与泛化边界有待正文验证。

Abstract: Real-world image super-resolution (SR) requires recovering perceptually realistic high-resolution images from complex low-resolution observations while preserving faithful content. Diffusion-based SR benefits from strong generative priors but incurs substantial computational overhead, whereas feed-forward CNN and Transformer SR models are efficient yet often struggle to recover realistic high-frequency details. This motivates a natural question: can diffusion priors be transferred to existing diffusion-free SR networks without introducing diffusion components at inference time? To this end, we propose PhoenixSR, a generative heterogeneous distillation framework that transfers diffusion priors to independently designed feed-forward SR networks through score-based distribution matching. Rather than aligning heterogeneous features or imitating sampled diffusion outputs, PhoenixSR uses the pretrained diffusion model as distribution-level supervision, while paired SR supervision preserves reconstruction fidelity. To make distribution matching effective for fidelity-sensitive SR, we introduce Heterogeneous Distribution Adaptation, which adapts the target score to the SR domain, improves tracking of the evolving student distribution, and anchors training with paired supervision. We further employ Directional Reliability Weighting, a lightweight residual-consistency-based reweighting strategy that reduces unstable distributional guidance. All diffusion-related components are removed after training, leaving the original student architecture and inference cost unchanged. Experiments on three SR benchmarks and six feed-forward backbones, including SwinIR, HAT, Real-ESRGAN, and SeeMoRe, show consistent perceptual improvements with largely preserved reconstruction fidelity.

</details>


### [45] [PICO: Projection-Informed Consistency Optimisation for 6DoF Surgical Tool Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30989)
*Lucy Fothergill,Pietro Valdastri,Dominic Jones,Duygu Sarikaya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 PICO，一个端到端可训练的 6DoF 手术器械位姿估计模型，通过多任务学习同时预测分割图、深度图以及平移与旋转参数，并引入投影损失与点到点损失两个几何一致性代理任务，在 SurgRIPE 数据集上取得有竞争力的旋转与平移精度，尤其在遮挡场景下表现稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术器械的精确 6DoF 位姿估计对手术自动化、机器人本体感知（proprioception）以及器械与组织的安全交互至关重要。现有方法存在两类局限：一是基于运动学的方法，由于机械臂的缆线驱动（cable-driven）特性会产生累积误差；二是视觉方法，往往依赖外部标记或跟踪器。近期提出的视觉两阶段位姿估计方法虽有所改进，但因误差累积与计算开销大，难以满足实时性与鲁棒性要求，尤其在遮挡等挑战性场景下表现不足。

Method: PICO 是一个端到端可训练的多任务学习架构，输出包括分割图（segmentation）、深度图（depth map）以及平移（translation）与旋转（rotation）参数回归。方法的核心创新在于定义了两个代理任务（proxy tasks），分别在 2D 和 3D 空间中施加几何一致性约束：一是投影损失（projection loss），用于强化 2D 投影一致性；二是点到点损失（point-to-point loss），用于强化 3D 空间中的几何一致性。这两个损失函数作为多任务学习的辅助监督信号，提升位姿估计的精度与鲁棒性，避免了传统两阶段流程中的级联误差与额外计算开销。

Result: 在 SurgRIPE 数据集上进行评估，采用标准 6DoF 位姿估计指标与当前先进方法（state-of-the-art）进行对比。结果显示，PICO 在该数据集的全部四个子集上均保持稳定强表现，旋转估计排名第二，且在遮挡条件下依然稳健；平移估计性能相当，具有竞争力，特别是在遮挡场景中表现良好。

Conclusion: PICO 验证了多任务学习与几何感知代理任务在鲁棒、可靠的手术器械位姿估计中的有效性，尤其适用于遮挡场景，表明其未来在手术自动化与机器人感知中的应用潜力。可能的局限性在于：评估仅基于 SurgRIPE 单一数据集，尚未在更广泛或真实临床场景中验证泛化能力；旋转性能排名第二而非最优；论文摘要未给出具体数值指标与实时性（推理时延）的定量分析。

Abstract: Purpose: Accurate 6 DoF pose estimation of surgical tools is critical for automa- tion, robotic proprioception, and safe interaction with the tissue operated on. Kinematics-based approaches suffer from accumulated errors due to the cable- driven nature of robotic arms, while vision-based methods often rely on external markers or trackers. Although more recent vision-based advances have been pro- posed, these two-stage pose estimation methods often lack real-time robustness due to accumulated errors and computational overhead. Methods: We propose a novel end-to-end trainable model, PICO. Our model employs a multi-task learning architecture to predict segmentation and depth maps, alongside regression of translation and rotation parameters. We define two proxy tasks that enforce geometric consistency in both 2D and 3D spaces, improving accuracy and robustness. For this, we propose a projection loss, and a point-to-point loss. Results: We evaluate our method on the SurgRIPE dataset, benchmarking its performance against state-of-the-art approaches using standard 6DoF pose esti- mation metrics. Our results demonstrate consistently strong performance across all four subsets, specifically in rotation, ranking second even under occlusion. It also demonstrates comparable translational performance, remaining competitive, especially in occluded cases. Conclusion: PICO demonstrates the effectiveness of multi-task learning and geometry-aware proxy tasks for robust and reliable surgical tool pose estimation, especially in occluded scenarios, highlighting potential for future applications.

</details>


### [46] [FLIP: Final Layer Inference-Time Probing for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30993)
*Drandreb Earl O. Juanico,Rowel O. Atienza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FLIP 是一种作用于视觉语言模型（VLM）最终归一化隐状态、面向 logit 的推理时探针（inference-time probe）。它通过对 logit 计算前的归一化隐状态施加逐元素下限截断（elementwise flooring），在不改变模型参数、提示词和解码方式的前提下，测试某个面向 logit 的干预位点是否承载结构化、与任务挂钩的计算，而非单纯的扰动。在受控检测/计数任务上，扫描干预强度会呈现三个区域，其中存在一个可实现检测召回率提升、计数误差下降的有界内部区间，并据此提出四准则的"探针-扫描"验证协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放权重视觉语言模型的机制可解释性研究中，对模型内部施加干预并观察行为变化是一种常见手段，但这种行为变化本身具有机制层面的歧义性：它可能反映模型对视觉证据的更有效利用，也可能只是输出不稳定或性能退化。论文旨在解决"如何区分有意义的任务相关计算与普通扰动"这一核心问题，提出一种可在推理时执行的验证步骤，用以判定某个面向 logit 的干预位点是否支持结构化、与任务相关联的计算。该问题对可靠地开展基于干预的可解释性研究具有重要意义。

Method: FLIP 的核心操作是在 logit 计算之前，对最终归一化后的隐状态施加逐元素下限截断（flooring），保持参数、提示词和解码过程完全不变，因此该位点即"面向 logit"的规范实例。方法上采用"强度扫描"策略：逐步改变干预强度（非定向 flooring 扫描），观察行为响应。论文形式化了一个四准则的"探针-扫描"协议以规范对干预效果的解读，包括：（1）区域结构（regime structure），即是否存在可分辨的行为区域；（2）grounding 代理对齐，即改善是否与视觉证据利用的提升一致；（3）特征一致性依赖（feature-coherence dependence）；（4）在基于性能的负控制上无法复现同一正向区域。作者据此区分出三个强度区域：可忽略变化区、有界内部区域（检测召回率 $R_{50}$ 提升且容忍计数误差 $\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{count}}$ 下降），以及过度抑制区。

Result: 在受控的检测/计数探针任务上，对干预强度进行扫描可观察到三个区域：可忽略变化、有界内部改善区以及过度抑制区。在内部改善区中，IoU 0.50 下的检测召回率 $R_{50}$ 提升，同时容忍计数误差 $\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{count}}$ 下降。消融与控制实验显示：原始解码层干预（包括最终归一化前的最后一个 block 输出）以及单例对左右控制（singleton-pair left/right control）均无法复现 Final-site 的特征签名；而同一位点上的算子（same-site operators）以及多个不同的 VLM 均能复现该签名。这表明该正向区域是最终归一化后位点特有的、可跨算子与跨模型复现的现象。

Conclusion: 论文提出的 FLIP 定位为一种面向干预式机制可解释性的验证步骤（validation step），而非一种引导（steering）方法。其主要贡献在于：引入面向 logit 的最终归一化隐状态位点作为探针实例，并提出四准则的"探针-扫描"协议，用以纪律化对干预效果的解读，从而区分结构化的任务相关计算与普通扰动或不稳定。意义在于为 VLM 可解释性研究提供更严谨的实验规范。局限方面，论文本身强调其并非引导方法，评估主要基于受控的检测/计数探针，其对更广泛任务与模型类型的适用性需进一步验证。

Abstract: We present FLIP, a final-layer inference-time probe for testing whether a logit-facing intervention site in an open-weight vision-language model (VLM) supports structured, task-linked computation rather than generic perturbation. Behavioral change under internal intervention is otherwise mechanistically ambiguous: it may reflect improved use of visual evidence, generic output instability, or outright degradation. FLIP applies elementwise flooring to the final normalized hidden state before logit computation, leaving parameters, prompts, and decoding unchanged. On a controlled detection/counting probe, sweeping intervention strength reveals three regions: negligible change, a bounded interior regime in which detection recall at IoU 0.50 ($R_{50}$) improves while tolerant counting error ($\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{count}}$) falls, and over-suppression. We formalize a four-criterion probe-and-sweep protocol for disciplining the interpretation of intervention effects: regime structure, grounding-proxy alignment, feature-coherence dependence, and failure to reproduce the same positive regime on a performance-based negative control. The post-normalization state passed to the output head is the logit-facing instantiation of this test; under a non-targeted flooring sweep it satisfies the full protocol. Raw decoder-layer interventions, including the last-block output before final normalization, and the singleton-pair left/right control fail to reproduce the Final-site signature, while same-site operators and multiple VLMs replicate it. FLIP is therefore a validation step for intervention-based mechanistic interpretability, not a steering method.

</details>


### [47] [TRACKGRAPH: Online Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Graphs via Image-Space Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31005)
*Peder Borge Hellesylt,Albert Gassol Puigjaner,Kostas Alexis,Annette Stahl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRACKGRAPH 是一个在线开放词汇 3D 建图系统，核心思想是在图像流中先维持短期 2D 掩码身份（跟踪），再融合为 3D 片段，从而减少昂贵的视觉-语言（VL）推理与重复分割，实现更快、更省显存的开放词汇 3D 语义地图构建与检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇的 3D 地图让机器人能够用自然语言推理此前未知的环境，对自主导航、场景图构建与目标搜索很重要。但现有系统通常对每一帧输入图像都做分割、将检测结果与持久 3D 片段关联，并频繁执行代价高昂的 Vision-Language（如 CLIP）推理，导致计算与显存开销大，难以在线、机载运行。论文希望解决这种"逐帧全量分割+频繁 VL 推理"的低效问题，在保证开放词汇分割与检索质量的同时提升实时性与资源效率。

Method: TRACKGRAPH 将短期 2D 掩码身份维持与 3D 融合解耦：(1) 在稀疏关键帧上计算 FastSAM 掩码与 CLIP 特征；(2) 在关键帧之间使用密集的 DINOv3 特征以高频率传播掩码，从而在图像流中保持掩码的时序一致（短期跟踪），避免对每帧都做分割与 VL 推理；(3) 将跟踪得到的掩码融合进分层场景图中的类无关 3D 片段层，3D 关联模块负责处理跟踪中断与长期重访；(4) 使用紧凑的多视角 CLIP 嵌入支持开放词汇检索。

Result: 在 Replica、ScanNet++ 和 HM3D 上评测，TRACKGRAPH 在开放词汇分割与检索任务上取得了与当前最优建图方法相当的性能，其中在 Replica 上同义词频率（synonym frequency）最高，为 0.50。在相同的 NVIDIA A100 上，相比 ViT-H OVI-MAP 快 1.7 倍，GPU 显存占用减少 3.3 倍。真实四足机器人部署验证了机载场景图构建与物体搜索，运行频率达 7.5Hz；同时使用录制的无人机数据测试了空中视角下的表现。

Conclusion: TRACKGRAPH 的主要贡献在于提出以"图像流中的 2D 掩码跟踪 + 稀疏关键帧 VL 推理"为核心的在线开放词汇 3D 建图框架，结合分层场景图与 3D 关联来处理跟踪中断和长期重访，在精度、速度与显存效率之间取得良好平衡，并展示了四足与无人机平台上的实用性。潜在局限在于：依赖 FastSAM/CLIP/DINOv3 等外部模型的表现；对快速运动或剧烈视角变化下掩码传播的鲁棒性、以及无人机等空中视角下的定量结果在摘要中未充分给出。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D maps enable robots to reason about previously unknown environments using natural language. However, existing systems typically segment every incoming image, associate detections with persistent 3D segments, and frequently perform costly Vision-Language (VL) inference. We present TRACKGRAPH, an online open-vocabulary system that maintains short-term 2D mask identity directly in the image stream before fusing segments into 3D. FastSAM masks and CLIP features are computed at sparse keyframes, while dense DINOv3 features are used to propagate masks at a high rate in between. The resulting tracked masks are fused into a class-agnostic 3D segment layer within a hierarchical scene graph, with 3D association handling tracking interruptions and long-term revisits. Compact multi-view CLIP embeddings enable open-vocabulary retrieval. Across Replica, ScanNet++, and HM3D, TRACKGRAPH achieves competitive open-vocabulary segmentation and retrieval against state-of-the-art mapping methods, including the highest synonym frequency on Replica (0.50). On the same NVIDIA A100, it is 1.7x faster and uses 3.3x less GPU memory than ViT-H OVI-MAP. Real-world quadruped deployments demonstrate onboard scene graph construction and object search at 7.5Hz, while recorded drone data is used to test the method under aerial viewpoints.

</details>


### [48] [Refining Cytology Predictions with Conditional Random Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31028)
*Manon Dausort,Tiffanie Godelaine,Karim El Khoury,Maxime Zanella,Christophe De Vleeschouwer,Benoît Macq*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CytoCRF，将条件随机场（CRF）适配到细胞学图像，通过面向染色质与细胞学染色特性的成对势项设计，并融合多骨干网络的邻域信息，在十个细胞学数据集上大幅超越现有 CRF 框架与零样本 VLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）在组织学（histology）图像上的零样本分类表现优异，但在细胞学（cytology）图像上明显退化——两者在染色方案与细胞形态上差异显著。已有 CRF 框架是针对组织病理学设计的，无法迁移到以"独立 patch 池、跨多种染色协议"形式发布的细胞学数据集，因此需要一种专门面向细胞学的标签传播/预测精修机制。

Method: 提出 CytoCRF：核心是对 CRF 的成对势项（pairwise terms）进行细胞学适配，显式针对染色质（chromatin）特征与细胞学特有的染色模式建模；同时对每个势项（potential term）的邻域进行扩充，通过组合多个骨干网络（backbones）的特征来丰富邻域构建。论文的核心观点是：邻域拓扑（neighborhood topology）比在邻域上计算的成对势本身更重要。整体框架仍是在 VLM 初始预测基础上做 CRF 式信息传播与精修。

Result: 在十个细胞学数据集上进行评估，覆盖多个标注预算（annotation budget）。CytoCRF 在每个标注预算下均优于现有 CRF 框架：相对最佳基线最高提升 +13.6 个百分点；在仅 50 个标注时，相对零样本 VLM 提升 +33.7 个百分点。进一步融合多个骨干网络的信息带来额外增益，验证了邻域拓扑的关键作用。主要指标为分类准确率。

Conclusion: 贡献在于将 CRF 精修机制从组织病理学成功迁移并改造到细胞学领域，通过染色质/染色相关的成对势设计与多骨干邻域构建，显著降低了对标注量的需求，并指出了邻域拓扑优于成对势这一反直觉发现。潜在局限：方法依赖 VLM 初始预测质量与多骨干特征，且针对细胞学染色协议定制，向其他模态或跨染色协议的泛化能力仍待验证。与事件相机、脉冲神经网络、UAV 感知等方向无直接关联。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong zero-shot (ZS) classification on histology images but do not perform as well on cytology, whose stains and cell morphology differ markedly compared to histology. Conditional random fields (CRFs) can refine noisy VLM predictions by propagating information across patches, but existing CRF frameworks were designed for histopathology and do not transfer to cytology datasets, released as independent patch pools spanning multiple staining protocols. We introduce CytoCRF, which adapts the pairwise terms to cytology by targeting chromatin and cytology-specific staining, and further enrich the neighborhood of each potential term by combining multiple backbones. Across ten cytology datasets, CytoCRF outperforms existing CRF frameworks at every annotation budget, reaching +13.6 percentage points over the best baseline and +33.7 over ZS with only 50 annotations. Combining information from multiple backbones brings further gains, showing that the neighborhood topology matters more than the pairwise potential computed over it.

</details>


### [49] [Exploiting Spatial Structure for Transductive Few-Shot Classification of Whole-Slide Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31040)
*Tiffanie Godelaine,Manon Dausort,Karim El Khoury,Benoît Gérin,Benoît Macq,Christophe De Vleeschouwer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SlideTIM，将近期转导式（transductive）方法 LC-TIM 适配到全切片图像（WSI）分析，通过结合空间-隐空间正则项与 patch 类别分布先验，在无标注条件下显著提升零样本 patch 级分类的细粒度精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化分析全切片图像（WSI）是癌症诊断的关键步骤，可减轻病理学家负担并提升诊断准确性。近年视觉-语言模型在无需标注的 patch 级分类上表现良好，但其零样本（ZS）预测在细粒度任务上噪声较大，需要进一步精炼。一个有前景的方向是对所有预测进行联合精炼，即转导式方法，但现有方法大多并非为 WSI 设计，难以应对 WSI 复杂的空间组织与强烈的类别不平衡问题。

Method: SlideTIM 将近期转导式方法 LC-TIM 适配到 WSI 场景，核心是引入两项机制：1）组合的空间-隐空间正则项（combined spatial–latent regularizer），强制空间上相邻且语义上相近的 patch 获得一致的预测，从而利用 WSI 中 patch 的空间邻近性与语义相似性；2）关于 patch 类别分布的先验（prior），用于校准预测的类别比例，缓解 WSI 中强烈的类别不平衡。二者共同应对 WSI 的复杂空间组织与类别分布偏斜问题，在转导式框架下对零样本预测进行联合精炼。

Result: 在四个组织学（histology）数据集上进行评估，使用 macro-F1 作为主要指标。SlideTIM 一致优于所有 TIM 变体：在 1-shot 设置下，相比最佳竞争基线提升 +8.1 个百分点（pp）；相比零样本（ZS）预测，在 1-shot 下 macro-F1 提升 +19.4pp。论文声明代码将在投稿后公开。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于将转导式精炼范式定制化地迁移到 WSI 分析，通过空间-隐空间正则与类别分布先验解决 WSI 的空间组织复杂性与类别不平衡问题，在四个组织学数据集上显著超越现有 TIM 变体与零样本基线。意义在于提升了无标注条件下 WSI 细粒度 patch 分类的可靠性，有助于减轻病理学家工作量。潜在局限包括：仅验证于组织学数据，方法的具体泛化性、对 patch 邻接与隐空间近邻定义方式的敏感性，以及依赖类别分布先验的可得性等未在摘要中充分说明。

Abstract: Automating the analysis of whole-slide images (WSIs), a key step in cancer diagnosis, has high clinical value, as it can reduce pathologist's workload while improving diagnosis accuracy. Recently, vision-language models have shown promising performance for patch-level classification without requiring any annotation, yet these zero-shot (ZS) predictions remain noisy on fine-grained tasks and must be further refined. A promising direction is to refine all predictions jointly, i.e., a transductive approach. However, most existing methods are not tailored to WSIs. We thus propose SlideTIM, an adaptation to WSIs of the recent transductive approach LC-TIM, which introduces a combined spatial--latent regularizer together with a prior on the patch class distribution. The former enforces spatially and semantically close patches to receive the same predictions, while the prior calibrates the predicted class proportions. Together, they address the complex spatial organization and the strong class imbalance of WSIs. Evaluated on four histology datasets, SlideTIM consistently outperforms all TIM variants, improving the macro-F1 by +8.1pp over the best competing baseline at 1 shot. Compared to the ZS, it raises the macro-F1 by +19.4pp at 1 shot. The code will be made available after submission.

</details>


### [50] [Where Compute Matters: Heterogeneous Attention for Efficient Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31050)
*Olga Zatsarynna,Denis Korzhenkov,Juergen Gall,Amir Habibian,Mohsen Ghafoorian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HetA-DiT 提出一种异构注意力机制，根据 token 的去噪难度自适应地分配计算资源：用轻量级不确定性分支预测每个 token 的去噪难度，将高不确定性 token 路由到稠密全局注意力，其余 token 使用高效局部注意力，从而在视频生成中大幅降低注意力开销同时保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成中，自注意力对长时空 token 序列的计算代价随序列长度呈二次增长，成为效率瓶颈。现有高效注意力方法对所有 token 施加相同的计算模式，忽略了视频不同区域的去噪难度差异显著、且这种难度会随生成步动态演变这一事实。因此，对所有 token 一视同仁会造成计算浪费：简单区域本可用廉价局部注意力处理，而困难区域才真正需要全局上下文。

Method: 核心是异构注意力（heterogeneous attention）与难度感知路由。一个轻量级不确定性分支对每个 token 预测去噪难度估计，据此进行路由：不确定性高的 token 走稠密全局注意力以保留全局上下文，更可靠的 token 走高效局部注意力。该路由同时依赖内容与时间步（content- and timestep-adaptive），并提供单一超参数控制质量-效率权衡。方法兼容少步分布匹配蒸馏，且在推理时通过复用前一个去噪步的不确定性估计，不额外引入 Transformer 评估开销。实验基于 DMD 蒸馏的 Wan2.2-5B 与 Wan2.1-1.3B 模型。

Result: 在 DMD 蒸馏的 Wan2.2-5B 和 Wan2.1-1.3B 上评估，使用 VBench、VBench-2.0 以及人类偏好评测。结果显示：仅将约 20% 的 token 路由到稠密注意力，HetA-DiT 仍能维持有竞争力的生成质量，说明在保持质量的前提下显著减少了稠密注意力的计算量，且通过复用上一步不确定性估计避免了推理时的额外 Transformer 评估。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出内容与时间步自适应的异构注意力机制，通过 token 级去噪难度路由实现计算资源的按需分配，并可与少步蒸馏结合、推理零额外 Transformer 开销。意义在于把高效注意力的设计从"统一模式"推进到"难度感知的异构分配"。可能的局限：摘要未给出与最强基线在效率指标上的量化对比细节，未报告绝对画质或具体加速比，且方法在非蒸馏或少步以外的生成范式上的泛化性有待验证。

Abstract: Efficient video generation requires reducing the quadratic cost of self-attention over long spatio-temporal token sequences. Existing efficient-attention methods typically apply the same computation pattern to every token, even though denoising difficulty varies substantially across video regions and evolves throughout the generation process. We introduce HetA-DiT, a heterogeneous attention mechanism that adaptively allocates computation according to token difficulty. A lightweight uncertainty branch predicts a token-wise estimate of denoising difficulty, which is used to route uncertain tokens through dense global attention while processing more reliable tokens with efficient local attention. The resulting routing is content- and timestep-adaptive, retains global context where it matters most, and provides a single parameter for controlling the quality-efficiency trade-off. HetA-DiT is compatible with few-step distribution-matching distillation and introduces no additional Transformer evaluation at inference time by reusing uncertainty estimates from the preceding denoising step. We evaluate the method on DMD-distilled Wan2.2-5B and Wan2.1-1.3B models. Across VBench, VBench-2.0, and human preference evaluation, HetA-DiT maintains competitive generation quality while routing only approximately 20% of tokens through dense attention.

</details>


### [51] [Band-Selection Stability and Semantic Segmentation Performance: A Study on Hyperspectral City](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31074)
*Jiarong Li,Imad Ali Shah,Enda Ward,Martin Glavin,Edward Jones,Brian Deegan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了六种波段选择方法在高光谱语义分割中的稳定性与下游性能，通过十个独立采样的类别均衡ROI集合生成60个波段子集，发现在Hyperspectral City V2数据集上，波段选择方法的内部稳定性（如Sim-LP最高）并不能可靠预测语义分割模型（SSM）的性能，且Top-K子集在K=9时可达2.01 mIoU与1.72 mF1提升并实现18–22倍CPU推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱成像（HSI）提供丰富的光谱信息，对自动驾驶等自主感知任务有价值，但高维波段导致存储、计算和推理成本高昂，因此常用波段选择来降维。然而，现有波段选择方法对采样数据敏感（依赖具体的ROI样本），且其与语义分割模型（SSM）之间的关联机制尚不清晰。若无法理解这种敏感性和稳定性—性能关系，实际部署中的波段选择可能不稳定、不可复现，也难以保证下游分割精度。该文正是为了填补"波段选择稳定性是否可预测分割性能"这一空白。

Method: 研究在Hyperspectral City V2数据集（128波段，450–950nm）上构建十个独立采样、类别均衡的ROI集合。对六种波段选择方法分别在每个ROI集合上选出Top-25波段，共得到60个波段子集。采用成对Jaccard相似度度量方法内部稳定性（intra-method stability）。从前三组ROI中提取Top-K波段（K∈{3,5,…,13}），输入三种语义分割模型，与全128波段基线对比。核心设计包括：(1) 重复采样的ROI协议以量化采样敏感性；(2) 稳定性指标与分割性能的关联分析；(3) 多K值、多SSM的联合评估。

Result: 实验表明，方法内部稳定性因方法而异：Sim-LP稳定性最高，且Sim-LP与JMIM+CSNR共同取得最佳分割结果。基于Top-K的SSM整体具有竞争力，最高提升约2.01 mIoU和1.72 mF1；当K=9时，CPU推理速度提升18–22倍。但性能并不随K单调提升，且稳定性与SSM性能之间没有一致关联。评估指标包括mIoU与mF1、成对Jaccard相似度，以及推理效率。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是首次在重复ROI采样、多子集规模和多SSM的条件下系统评估波段选择方法，揭示内部稳定性虽具信息量但不能可靠指示下游分割性能。这提示研究者和工程实践需跨重复采样、不同K值和多种分割模型进行评估，而非仅依赖稳定性指标。局限在于实验仅基于单一数据集（Hyperspectral City V2）和有限的K范围与三种SSM，结论的泛化性仍待在其他高光谱场景（如无人机/机器人感知）中验证。

Abstract: Resource constraints make high-dimensional hyperspectral imaging challenging in autonomous perception, motivating the use of band selection methods. However, the sensitivity of band-selection methods to sampled data and their relationship to semantic segmentation models (SSMs) remain underexplored. This study evaluates six band selection methods on ten independently sampled, class-balanced region-of-interest (ROI) sets, yielding 60 top-25 band subsets from the Hyperspectral City V2 (128 bands: 450-950nm) dataset. Top-$K$ bands ($K\in\{3,5, ... 13\}$) from the first three ROI sets are evaluated with three SSMs against the corresponding 128-band baseline. Experiments show that intra-method stability is method-dependent: Sim-LP shows the highest stability (pairwise Jaccard similarity) and, together with JMIM+CSNR, yields the best segmentation results. Top-$K$ based SSMs remain competitive with baselines, with gains of up to 2.01 mIoU and 1.72 mF1 points, and 18-22x faster CPU inference for $K=9$. However, performance does not improve monotonically with $K$, and stability shows no consistent association with SSM performance. These findings suggest that intra-method stability is informative but an unreliable indicator of downstream segmentation performance, highlighting the need to evaluate band-selection methods across repeated samples, subset sizes, and SSMs.

</details>


### [52] [DepthEvidence: Unifying Metric Depth Prediction and Geometric Reasoning in Multimodal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31103)
*Jiangning Wei,Yuan Yao,Miaomiao Cui,Mingsheng Li,Humen Zhong,Shuai Bai,Zhibo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DepthEvidence 是一个 4B 参数的多模态模型，将自身预测的密集度量深度（metric depth）作为"对象锚定的几何证据"注入语言推理，从而在语言生成中保留数值与几何内容，实现带度量约束的空间推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具备度量约束的空间推理需要把对象与几何测量值关联起来，并在语言推理过程中保持其数值内容不被"语言化"而失真。现有 VLM 在把连续几何量（如深度）转成文本 token 时容易丢失精度，难以支持绝对测量、相对比较以及"空间+数值"复合约束的决策。该工作旨在让模型能基于自身预测的度量深度做对象级的定量空间推理。

Method: 提出 DepthEvidence（4B）：(1) 相机条件化解码器（camera-conditioned decoder）利用多尺度视觉特征与高分辨率 RGB 精修，预测全分辨率度量深度；(2) 密集到语言接口（dense-to-language interface）将预测深度和解码器特征转换为"对象对齐的连续几何 token"，并锚定到对象标识符（object identifiers），使几何信息以连续量而非离散文本形式进入语言上下文；(3) 采用几何监督，要求度量信息在语言上下文交互前后均可恢复（recoverable）；(4) 指令微调支持对象测量与组合式推理。此外构建了 Depth-VQA 基准。

Result: 在九个数据集上评测：DepthEvidence 在评估方法中取得最高平均密集 δ1，与专用深度估计器相当；在实例级度量深度估计上领先；在 Depth-VQA 基准（对象深度查询、相对比较、空间+数值约束决策）的相对推理与度量推理两条赛道上均取得最高总体准确率；同时基本保持通用 VQA 性能，并相对基座模型提升了空间理解能力。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于将自预测的密集度量深度作为对象级几何证据直接接入语言推理，并通过几何监督与连续几何 token 缓解数值信息在语言交互中的损失，配套提出 Depth-VQA 评估协议，在度量/相对深度推理上超越同类方法。局限方面：体积为 4B、计算成本高于轻量模型；性能依赖自身深度预测质量（误差可能传播）；目前验证集中于深度这一几何模态，对更广泛的 3D 几何（如点云、法向）与实时/边缘部署场景的适用性尚未充分验证。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning with metric constraints requires linking objects to geometric measurements and preserving their numerical content during language reasoning. We present DepthEvidence, a 4B model that uses its own dense metric predictions as object-grounded evidence for language generation. A camera-conditioned decoder predicts full-resolution metric depth using multi-scale visual features and high-resolution RGB refinement. A dense-to-language interface converts predicted depths and decoder features into object-aligned continuous geometry tokens anchored to object identifiers. Geometric supervision encourages metric information to remain recoverable before and after language-context interaction, while instruction tuning supports object measurement and compositional reasoning. We introduce a Depth-VQA benchmark evaluating object-depth queries, relative comparisons, and decisions combining spatial and numerical constraints. Across nine datasets, DepthEvidence achieves the highest average dense $δ_1$ among evaluated methods, competitive with specialized estimators. It also leads the evaluated methods in instance-level metric depth estimation and overall accuracy on both relative and metric reasoning tracks, while broadly preserving general VQA performance and improving spatial understanding relative to the base model.

</details>


### [53] [Double-stream registration with pyramid fusion for HDR video with alternating exposures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31108)
*Onofre Martorell,Ivan Pereira-Sánchez,Antoni Fuentes,Antoni Buades*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于双流配准与金字塔融合的HDR视频重建框架：以三帧连续交替曝光序列为输入，直接计算光流并以"中点位移"策略补充处理严重过曝情形，最后将辐照度图与LDR图像在金字塔融合阶段合成为HDR输出，实验上持续优于现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从交替曝光（alternating-exposure）序列重建HDR视频是计算摄影的经典难题，在亮度变化极端的区域尤其困难。交替曝光会在相邻帧间产生大范围亮度跳变，使基于亮度一致性的运动估计（光流）失效；而在严重过曝区域，纹理与梯度信息缺失，进一步破坏帧间配准的可靠性，导致鬼影（ghosting）、结构错位等伪影。因此，如何在极端亮度差异下同时保证精确配准与高质量辐射域融合，是该任务的核心问题。

Method: 方法由两个关键阶段组成：(1) 双流配准（dual-stream registration）：给定三帧连续图像，以中央帧为参考，第一流直接计算光流进行帧间对齐；针对严重过曝导致直接配准失效的情况，引入互补的中点位移（midpoint displacement）策略作为第二条配准路径，以更鲁棒地估计曝光差异较大帧之间的对应关系。(2) 金字塔融合（accurate pyramid fusion）：将配准后得到的辐照度（radiance）图像与LDR图像在金字塔多尺度框架下融合，输出最终HDR结果，从而在不同尺度上抑制错位伪影并保留细节。整体流程强调"配准—融合"解耦，用互补配准流覆盖不同曝光质量区间。

Result: 摘要仅给出概括性结论：实验表明该方法在HDR视频重建任务上持续优于当前最先进方法（state-of-the-art）。由于摘要未提供具体数据集名称（如典型交替曝光HDR视频基准）、评价指标（如PSNR、HDR-VDP）以及定量数值，无法对训练/测试设置和具体提升幅度做出确切陈述，相关细节需参考正文。

Conclusion: 贡献在于提出了一种面向交替曝光序列的HDR视频重建框架，其核心是双流配准（直接光流 + 中点位移互补策略）与金字塔融合的组合，旨在解决极端亮度变化下的配准失效与融合伪影问题，并在实验中取得优于现有方法的性能。局限方面：摘要未提供定量结果、数据集规模与运行效率（实时性）信息；双流配准与金字塔融合引入了额外计算开销，且中点位移策略的适用范围与对更复杂运动/遮挡场景的鲁棒性仍有待验证。

Abstract: High dynamic range (HDR) video reconstruction from al\-ter\-na\-ting-exposure sequences remains challenging, especially in regions with extreme luminance variation. We propose a novel HDR reconstruction framework based on dual-stream registration and accurate pyramid fusion. Given three consecutive frames, our method computes optical flow directly with the central frame, while introducing a complementary midpoint displacement strategy to handle cases with severe overexposition. A pyramid fusion stage then merges the resulting radiance and LDR images into a final HDR output. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [54] [Pocket-STVG: lightweight architecture for Spatio-Temporal Video Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31135)
*Alberto Presta,Michal Byra,Grzegorz Stefański,Karol Szurkowski,Eryk Kołodziejczyk,Krzysztof Arendt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Pocket-STVG（P-STVG）提出一种轻量级级联式时空视频定位（STVG）框架，通过组合高效的预训练组件（MobileViCLIP时序编码器、MDETR空间编解码器、共享对齐文本编码器）替代大型端到端模型或MLLM，用不到90M参数在弱监督与零样本STVG任务上达到与弱监督方法相当、优于早期零样本方法的性能，并实现查询无关的预计算特征与索引友好推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空视频定位（STVG）需要根据自然语言查询在视频中定位三维时空管道（空间框+时间区间）。现有方法虽在全集监督、弱监督、零样本设定下取得较好性能，但普遍依赖计算开销大的架构、复杂训练流程或多模态大语言模型（MLLM），导致内存与算力成本高，难以规模化部署于大规模视频检索场景。论文动机是在不牺牲定位性能的前提下，构建高计算效率、低显存占用的STVG方案，并统一支持弱监督与零样本两种设定。

Method: P-STVG采用轻量级级联（cascade）架构，而非端到端大模型，核心设计包括：（1）时序感知视频编码器，基于MobileViCLIP构建，用于提取时空视频表示；（2）空间编码器-解码器，派生自MDETR，用于在给定文本条件下定位目标空间区域；（3）共享对齐的文本编码器。时序定位通过两种可选模块实现——轻量级1D U-Net或简单阈值化策略，使同一框架可同时适配弱监督与零样本设定。关键创新在于将视频特征表示与查询解耦、独立预计算，从而形成索引友好（indexing-friendly）的流水线，支持高效推理与大规模视频集合处理。

Result: 论文以参数量（<90M）、内存与计算开销作为效率指标，与弱监督及零样本STVG方法进行对比。结果显示：P-STVG在参数量与显存/算力成本大幅降低的条件下，性能与弱监督方法相当，并优于早期零样本方法，形成了有利的性能-效率折中（performance-efficiency trade-off）。摘要未披露具体数据集（如VidSTG等）与定量指标数值，仅给出上述总体结论。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出紧凑的级联式STVG框架P-STVG，证明通过组合高效预训练组件即可在弱监督与零样本STVG上获得与重型方法相当的性能，同时显著降低参数与计算成本，并支持视频表示预计算与索引式推理，利于大规模视频检索。局限在于：摘要未报告具体数据集、评测指标与定量数值，也未说明级联组件间的误差累积影响与阈值化策略的敏感度；级联结构在复杂查询与长时视频上的泛化能力仍需实验验证。该方法与事件相机、脉冲神经网络、UAV感知、小目标检测等方向无直接关联，其价值主要体现在高效视频理解与实时/大规模视觉检索系统。

Abstract: Spatio-Temporal Video Grounding (STVG) aims to localize the spatio-temporal tube in a video corresponding to a natural language query. While recent methods achieve strong performance in fully supervised, weakly supervised, and zero-shot settings, they typically rely on computationally expensive architectures, complex training pipelines, or multimodal large language models. We present Pocket-STVG (P-STVG), a lightweight cascade architecture that addresses STVG by combining efficient pre-trained components instead of large end-to-end models. P-STVG integrates a temporal-aware video encoder based on MobileViCLIP, a spatial encoder-decoder derived from MDETR, and a shared aligned text encoder. Temporal localization is performed through either a lightweight 1D U-Net or a simple thresholding strategy, enabling the same framework to operate in both weakly supervised and zero-shot settings. Furthermore, video representations are precomputed independently of the query, yielding an indexing-friendly pipeline for efficient inference and large-scale video collections. Despite requiring fewer than 90M parameters, P-STVG performs on par with weakly supervised methods and improves on earlier zero-shot approaches at a fraction of their memory and computational cost, establishing a favorable performance-efficiency trade-off for STVG.

</details>


### [55] [Seeing Semantic Shift: Difference-Aware Sentence-Level Temporal Segmentation of Sign Language Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31148)
*Bowen Guo,Shiwei Gan,Yafeng Yin,Xiao Liu,Kuizhuang Liu,Zhiwei Jiang,Lei Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SignShift：一种差分感知的句级手语分割框架（Vis-SSLS），通过显式建模帧间特征变化作为语义线索，将连续手语视频切分为非重叠的句级片段，无需字幕辅助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语理解方法在短句、单句视频上表现优异，但在长时连续手语视频上性能急剧下降，主要原因在于缺乏句级切分这一前置步骤。论文聚焦 Visual-only Sentence-level Sign Language Segmentation（Vis-SSLS）：在无任何字幕/文本辅助下，把连续手语视频划分为互不重叠的句级片段，作为下游识别与翻译任务的关键前提。难点在于手语句子之间的过渡往往平滑且视觉上含糊，缺少显式停顿或姿态重置，因此静态帧表示难以捕捉指示句子边界的细微时间变化。

Method: 提出 SignShift 差分感知分割框架，核心思想是把帧间特征变化显式建模为句边界检测的语义线索，包含两个模块：(1) Temporal Difference Module（时序差分模块）——融合全帧、面部与手部三类线索，采用帧间差分（inter-frame differencing）学习多尺度时间变化，以同时捕获细粒度局部运动学与全局语义过渡；(2) Segment Count Prediction 模块——预测句子数量，用该数量约束/引导边界选择，从而缓解过分割与欠分割问题。

Result: 论文在多个基准数据集上进行大量实验，SignShift 相比现有方法取得显著提升，验证了差分建模与句数预测引导策略在句级手语分割任务上的有效性（摘要未给出具体数据集名称、评价指标 F1/IoU 及数值结果）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：(1) 提出更具现实挑战性的 Vis-SSLS 设定，即在无字幕条件下进行句级手语分割；(2) 以帧间差分显式建模句子边界处的细微时间变化，并联合面/手/全帧多线索；(3) 引入句数预测以抑制过分割与欠分割。其意义在于为长时连续手语的识别与翻译提供可靠前置切分。潜在局限：句数预测的不准确可能引入误差传播，且依赖差分特征对边界视觉显著性要求较高；摘要中未报告定量结果与误差分析。

Abstract: Recent advances in sign language understanding have achieved impressive success on short, single-sentence videos, yet their performance drops sharply when applied to long, continuous sign language videos. To bridge this gap, we focus on a challenging and realistic setting: Visual-only Sentence-level Sign Language Segmentation (Vis-SSLS), which aims to partition continuous sign language videos into non-overlapping sentence-level segments without any caption assistance, serving as a crucial prerequisite for downstream recognition and translation tasks. However, sentence transitions in sign language are often smooth and visually ambiguous, lacking explicit pauses or posture resets. As a result, static frame representations may fail to capture the subtle temporal changes that indicate sentence boundaries.
