To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{SignShift}, a difference-aware segmentation framework that explicitly models frame-to-frame feature variation as semantic cues for sentence boundary detection. First, to model the feature variation, we design a Temporal Difference Module, which incorporates full-frame, facial, and hand cues, and employs inter-frame differencing to learn multi-scale temporal variations that capture both fine-grained local kinematics and global semantic transitions. Second, to mitigate over- and under-segmentation issues, we design a Segment Count Prediction module, which predicts the number of sentences to guide boundary selection. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that SignShift substantially outperforms existing methods, validating its effectiveness.

</details>


### [56] [FedHisto-PAST: Parameter-Efficient Stain-Aware Federated Learning for Cross-Site Lung Histopathology Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31150)
*Muhammad Muhtasim Shahriar,M. M. Golam Hafiz,Saad Aloteibi,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FedHisto-PAST v2 是一个面向跨站点肺组织病理三分类（ACA/Normal/SCC）的联邦学习框架，将冻结的 HIBOU-B 基础模型与参数高效适配、染色条件化的成对视图预测与特征一致性、可靠性感知原型学习以及自适应联邦聚合相结合，并以仅在客户端本地保留原始数据、非 IID 的五客户端仿真进行验证。外部队列 LungHist700 上取得 Macro-F1 0.728560、平衡准确率 0.730454，仅更新 1.253841% 的模型参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨站点肺组织病理分类面临多重现实挑战：不同中心的染色差异（stain variation）造成域偏移；各客户端数据分布非独立同分布（non-IID）且存在类别缺失（missing classes）；同时，直接微调大型病理基础编码器（如病理 foundation model）计算与存储成本高昂，且医学数据受隐私约束无法集中。论文动机在于：在原始数据不出本地的前提下，实现染色鲁棒的参数高效联邦适配，从而在通信受限、类别不均衡的多中心场景中完成 ACA、Normal、SCC 三分类。

Method: 框架由四个核心组件构成：（1）以冻结的 HIBOU-B 病理基础模型为主干，仅通过参数高效适配（PEFT）进行调优，从而把可训练参数限制在约 1.25%；（2）染色条件化的成对视图预测（stain-conditioned paired-view prediction）与特征一致性约束，即对同一图像构造染色扰动视图并施加预测层与特征层的一致性；（3）可靠性感知的原型学习（reliability-aware prototype learning），客户端维护类原型并以可靠性加权进行表征对齐与正则化；（4）自适应联邦聚合（adaptive federated aggregation），按客户端可靠性／贡献度调整聚合权重。整体在五客户端、非 IID、原始数据本地化的仿真下训练，并配套客户端级分析、组件消融与通信开销核算。

Result: 实验采用五客户端、非 IID、原始数据保留本地的联邦仿真，设置固定内部评估划分、客户端级分析、组件消融、通信量核算，并额外引入受开发过程影响的探索性队列 LungHist700。在内部固定划分上，所有主要方法均达到接近饱和（near-ceiling）的性能，因而无法有效区分方法优劣。在 LungHist700 上，FedHisto-PAST v2 的 Macro-F1 为 0.728560、平衡准确率为 0.730454；模型对 Normal 与 SCC 的识别较好，但 ACA 召回率偏低，且概率校准仍不理想。外部分析的消融表明，只有预测级一致性（prediction-level consistency）具有明确可支持的独立贡献；特征一致性与原型正则化在 Macro-F1 上未见结论性的独立增益。

Conclusion: 该工作的主要贡献是提出并初步验证了一个染色感知、参数高效的联邦病理分类框架，可用极少可训练参数（1.253841%）在非 IID 多客户端设置下完成三分类，并提供了跨数据集的探索性证据。其局限性在于：证据属于探索性而非正式验证；未建立形式化隐私保证、患者级独立性、前瞻性部署评估或临床验证；内部划分性能饱和削弱了方法区分的说服力；外部队列受开发过程影响；且类别间性能不均衡（ACA 召回低）与校准不佳仍需解决。

Abstract: Cross-site lung histopathology classification must account for stain variation, non-IID client data, missing classes, and the cost of adapting large pathology encoders. This study evaluates FedHisto-PAST v2 for three-way classification of adenocarcinoma (ACA), Normal, and squamous cell carcinoma (SCC). FedHisto-PAST v2 combines a frozen HIBOU-B foundation model with parameter-efficient adaptation, stain-conditioned paired-view prediction and feature consistency, reliability-aware prototype learning, and adaptive federated aggregation. Experiments used a five-client, non-IID, raw-data-local simulation with fixed internal evaluation, client-level analysis, component ablations, communication accounting, and a development-influenced exploratory LungHist700 cohort. All principal methods achieved near- ceiling internal performance, which limited discrimination on the fixed split. On LungHist700, FedHisto- PAST v2 achieved a Macro-F1 of 0.728560 and a balanced accuracy of 0.730454. Higher recognition of Normal and SCC was accompanied by lower ACA recall, and calibration remained imperfect. Prediction-level consistency was the only component with a clearly supported independent contribution in the external ablation analysis. Feature consistency and prototype regularization showed no conclusive independent overall gains in Macro-F1. The framework updated 1.253841% of the model parameters. The results provide exploratory cross-dataset evidence for stain-aware, parameter-efficient federation; they do not establish formal privacy, patient-level independence, prospective deployment, or clinical validation.

</details>


### [57] [HyperErase: Scale-Calibrated Hypernetwork for Multi-Concept Erasure in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31154)
*Yi Sun,Xinhao Zhong,Zhiqi Zhang,Yimin Zhou,Junhao Li,Yuxia Qiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HyperErase 提出了一种基于超网络（hypernetwork）驱动的、由提示词条件化生成参数的文本到图像（T2I）概念擦除框架，将概念擦除重新表述为"提示词条件化的参数摊还"（prompt-conditioned parameter amortization），并引入解耦校正策略（decoupled rectification）以提升合成适配器的稳定性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期 T2I 生成模型的视觉合成能力显著提升，但随之带来了严重的安全隐患，即可能生成有害或不当内容（如特定概念、敏感对象等）。现有的概念擦除方法大多遵循"静态权重范式"，即训练一个单一的、冻结的适配器（adapter）。这种静态适配器存在两大缺陷：(1) 难以适应多样化的提示词变化（prompt variations），泛化能力不足；(2) 在扩展到多个概念时会出现参数干扰（parameter interference），导致擦除效果与图像质量下降。因此，如何在多概念、多提示词的场景下实现灵活、稳定且精确的概念擦除，是一个重要的安全问题。

Method: 1) 问题重述：作者将概念擦除重新定义为"提示词条件化的参数摊还"，即不再为每个提示词单独做梯度优化，而是学习一个从文本描述到提示词特定参数更新的映射。2) 超网络驱动合成：训练一个超网络，将文本描述映射为提示词特定的 LoRA 更新（prompt-specific LoRA updates），从而消除逐提示词梯度优化的需求，也无需人工合并 LoRA。3) 解耦校正策略（decoupled rectification）：为提升合成适配器的稳定性与精度，将 LoRA token 解耦为"模式子空间"（pattern subspace）与"尺度子空间"（scale subspace）；对尺度部分施加平方根变换（square-root transform）以抑制乘法式过缩放（multiplicative over-scaling）；并利用教师模型导出的规范化先验（canonical priors）进行推理时校正。4) 推理效率：模型在单次前向传播中即可为每个输入提示词变体生成专门的 LoRA，推理阶段无需任何梯度更新。

Result: 论文在主要概念类别（major concept categories）上进行了广泛的实验。评估维度涵盖概念的擦除有效性（erasure effectiveness）、生成图像质量（image quality）以及语义对齐（semantic alignment）三者的权衡。结果表明，HyperErase 在这三个维度上均持续改善其权衡关系，性能可与"金标准"的单概念基线（gold-standard single-concept baselines）相媲美。此外，所得模型能够在单次前向传播中为每个输入提示词变体提供专用 LoRA，且推理时无需梯度更新。摘要未提供具体数据集名称、定量指标数值（如 FID、CLIP score、erasure accuracy 等）或消融实验细节。

Conclusion: 主要贡献包括：(1) 提出 HyperErase，将概念擦除重新表述为提示词条件化的参数摊还范式；(2) 设计超网络以从文本描述直接合成提示词特定 LoRA，避免逐提示词优化与人工合并；(3) 引入解耦校正策略（模式/尺度子空间解耦、平方根变换、教师先验校正）提升适配器稳定性和精度；(4) 实现推理阶段单次前向、无梯度的灵活擦除。意义在于为 T2I 模型的概念擦除提供了新的范式，兼顾擦除效果、画质与语义对齐。潜在局限：摘要未说明其在极端多概念组合下的可扩展性边界、超网络训练成本，以及与现有微调类方法在计算开销上的直接对比。

Abstract: Recent advances in text-to-image (T2I) generation have substantially improved visual synthesis, but have also raised increasing safety concerns due to their potential to generate harmful or undesirable content. Existing concept erasure methods predominantly follow a static weight paradigm, producing a single frozen adapter that struggles to adapt to diverse prompt variations and suffers from parameter interference when scaling to multiple concepts. We propose \textbf{HyperErase}, a framework for concept erasure based on hypernetwork-driven prompt-conditioned parameter synthesis. Our approach first reframes concept erasure as prompt-conditioned parameter amortization and trains a hypernetwork to map textual descriptions to prompt-specific LoRA updates, eliminating the need for per-prompt gradient optimization or manual LoRA merging. To further improve the stability and precision of synthesized adapters, we develop a decoupled rectification strategy, which disentangles LoRA tokens into pattern and scale subspaces, applies a square-root transform to curb multiplicative over-scaling, and leverages teacher-derived canonical priors for inference-time correction. Extensive experiments across major concept categories demonstrate that HyperErase consistently improves the trade-off between erasure effectiveness, image quality, and semantic alignment, achieving performance comparable to gold-standard single-concept baselines. Furthermore, the resulting models can provide specialized LoRAs for each input prompt variation in a single forward pass without requiring gradient updates during inference. These principled and flexible framework offers a new paradigm for concept erasure in T2I models.

</details>


### [58] [ReG-SAM: Reference Graph-Driven SAM for 2D Foundational Vessel Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31160)
*Donghang Lyu,Zichen Zhang,Oleh Dzyubachyk,Marius Staring*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ReG-SAM，一个基于 SAM 的 2D 血管分割框架，通过"参考图集合（reference graph set）"构建图提示嵌入（GPEs）与血管原型嵌入（VPEs）两种模态感知表示，并借助模态级血管数据库与两种参考图引导的表示学习方案在推理阶段无需 ground-truth 掩码即可估计这两类嵌入，在 19 个数据集上超越现有基线（包括使用人工提示的方法），尤其在细血管上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2D 医学图像血管分割对诊断与治疗规划至关重要，但血管形态复杂、成像条件多样，现有深度学习方法大多针对单一解剖结构或成像模态设计，缺乏跨解剖、跨模态的泛化能力。SAM 虽在通用分割上表现优异，但其原始设计未针对血管形态优化，且对细粒度血管结构（thin vessels）处理不佳，导致性能次优。因此，作者希望构建一个可跨解剖与跨模态泛化的通用血管分割器，并充分挖掘血管的形态学与模态特异性特征。

Method: ReG-SAM 在 SAM 框架基础上引入"参考图集合"以增强血管表示，核心是两类模态感知表示：(1) 图提示嵌入（Graph Prompt Embeddings, GPEs）——从图中编码全局空间特征，作为提示输入引导分割；(2) 血管原型嵌入（Vascular Prototype Embeddings, VPEs）——从多尺度特征图与血管掩码中捕获细粒度、模态特异的血管特征。由于 GPEs 与 VPEs 的构造原本依赖推理时不可获得的血管掩码，作者构建了模态级血管数据库（modality-wise vascular database），并设计两种参考图引导的表示学习方案（reference graph-guided representation learning schemes），利用数据库样本而非真值掩码来估计 GPEs 与 VPEs。整体设计使模型在推理时无需 GT 掩码，同时保留模态感知的血管特征表示能力。

Result: 在 19 个血管分割数据集上进行大量实验，覆盖多种解剖结构与成像模态。结果显示 ReG-SAM 一致优于现有基线方法，包括那些依赖人工提示（manual prompts，如点/框提示）的方法；在具有挑战性的细血管（thin vessels）场景中提升尤为显著，验证了参考图引导的模态感知表示对细粒度血管结构的有效性。摘要未给出具体量化指标（如 Dice/ clDice 数值或消融细节），但强调了跨数据集的一致性与细血管上的优势。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：将 SAM 适配为面向 2D 血管分割的通用框架，通过参考图集合引入 GPEs 与 VPEs 两种互补表示，配合模态级血管数据库与参考图引导表示学习，在不使用推理期 GT 掩码的前提下实现跨解剖、跨模态泛化，并在 19 个数据集上全面超越基线，特别是细血管分割。意义在于为构建通用医学血管分割器提供了可扩展的模态感知表示学习范式。潜在局限：摘要未披露模型参数量与推理效率（对实时/边缘部署的影响不明），依赖构建的模态级血管数据库（规模、覆盖度与构建成本未详述），且作为 SAM 派生框架其计算开销可能较大，跨未见模态的泛化能力仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Vessel segmentation in medical images is essential for many clinical tasks, ranging from diagnosis to treatment planning. However, it remains challenging due to complex vascular morphology and diverse imaging conditions. Existing deep learning methods rarely aim at building a generalizable vessel segmentor across anatomies and modalities. While the Seg- ment Anything Model (SAM) has shown promise for med- ical image segmentation, its original design does not fully exploit vascular morphology and struggles with fine-grained vascular structures, leading to suboptimal performance. In this paper, we propose ReG-SAM, a SAM-based framework tailored to 2D vessel segmentation that leverages reference graph set for enhancing vascular representations. Specifically, we introduce two modality-aware representations derived from the reference masks: graph prompt embeddings (GPEs) that encode global spatial features from graphs, and vascu- lar prototype embeddings (VPEs) that capture fine-grained modality-specific vessel characteristics from multi-scale fea- ture maps and vascular masks. Since both require vascular masks that are unavailable during inference and require robust modality-aware vascular feature representations, we construct a modality-wise vascular database and develop two reference graph-guided representation learning schemes for estimating GPEs and VPEs using samples from the database rather than ground-truth masks. Extensive experiments across 19 datasets demonstrate that ReG-SAM consistently outperforms existing baselines, even those using manual prompts, particularly on challenging thin vessels.

</details>


### [59] [TaskIR: Task-Driven Image Restoration via Degradation Adaptation and Task Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31170)
*Yanjie Tu,Qingsen Yan,Axi Niu,Wenxuan Cai,Tao Hu,Wei Dong,Haokui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TaskIR 是一个两阶段、面向下游任务的统一图像复原框架：阶段一通过退化表征模块（DRM）提取退化表示，并由退化引导 Transformer 块（DGTB）进行自适应调制复原；阶段二通过任务到复原反馈生成模块（TRFG）将异构任务特征转化为复原反馈，并由选择性任务反馈精化模块（STFR）评估反馈相关性后选择性精化中间复原特征，从而在多种退化类型与多个下游任务上同时取得有竞争力的复原质量与任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务驱动图像复原的目标是同时提升图像质量与下游任务性能，但现有方法主要针对单一退化类型，难以应对真实场景中的多样化退化。不同退化对复原的要求不同，复原不足会残留退化与伪影，破坏目标边界与语义线索，从而损害下游任务；而过度或不当复原同样可能干扰已恢复良好的内容。因此需要一个能同时处理多退化类型、并根据任务需求自适应调整复原强度的统一框架。

Method: TaskIR 采用两阶段设计。阶段一为退化自适应复原：退化表征模块（DRM）显式提取退化表示，退化引导 Transformer 块（DGTB）据此动态调制特征变换，实现与退化类型/程度匹配的自适应复原。阶段二为任务反馈精化：任务到复原反馈生成模块（TRFG）将异构任务特征（如检测、分割等不同任务的特征）转换为复原反馈，其关键在于建模“任务表征与当前复原状态之间的差异”；随后选择性任务反馈精化模块（STFR）评估该反馈的相关性，只对需要改进的中间复原特征进行选择性精化，以避免破坏已复原良好的内容、减少任务反馈带来的干扰。

Result: 论文声称进行了大量实验，验证 TaskIR 在多种退化类型与多个下游任务上均能取得有竞争力的复原质量和下游任务性能。摘要中未给出具体数据集、评价指标数值或消融细节，仅强调跨退化类型与跨任务的泛化表现，体现其统一框架相对单退化复原方法的优势。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出统一的任务驱动图像复原框架 TaskIR，通过退化自适应复原（DRM + DGTB）与任务反馈精化（TRFG + STFR）两阶段协同，缓解多退化场景下复原不足或过度复原对下游任务的负面影响，并具备跨任务、跨退化的通用性。潜在局限在于：摘要未披露具体数据集、定量指标与计算开销，两阶段设计可能引入额外推理成本，且任务反馈精化的有效性对任务类型与反馈相关性评估的准确性可能较为敏感。

Abstract: Task-driven image restoration aims to improve both image quality and downstream task performance. However, existing methods predominantly focus on single degradation type and struggle to handle the diverse degradations encountered in real-world scenarios. Different degradations impose distinct restoration demands, and insufficient restoration may leave residual degradations and artifacts that impair object boundaries and semantic cues, thereby compromising downstream task performance. To address these challenges, we propose TaskIR, a two-stage task-driven unified image restoration framework that integrates degradation-adaptive restoration with task feedback refinement. In Stage I, a Degradation Representation Module (DRM) extracts degradation representations, enabling a Degradation-Guided Transformer Block (DGTB) to dynamically modulate feature transformations for adaptive restoration. In Stage II, a Task-to-Restoration Feedback Generation module (TRFG) transforms heterogeneous task features into restoration feedback by modeling task-representation discrepancies associated with the current restoration. Subsequently, a Selective Task Feedback Refinement module (STFR) assesses feedback relevance and selectively refines intermediate restoration features to mitigate interference with well-restored content. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TaskIR achieves competitive restoration quality and downstream task performance across diverse degradations and tasks.

</details>


### [60] [Who Says What: Symbolic Trimodal Binding Mechanisms in Audio-Visual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31193)
*Jihoo Jung,Youngjoon Jang,Joon Son Chung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了音视频大语言模型（AVLLMs）在多说话人对话视频中如何实现"文本-音频-视觉"三模态绑定（trimodal binding），发现模型涌现出基于模态特定符号变量的符号化三模态绑定机制，并提出一种免训练的音频-视觉提示方法（借助现成ASD模型）显著提升对话理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AVLLMs在处理包含多说话人对话的视频时表现不佳，核心难点在于需要解决"谁说了什么"（who says what）的问题，这要求模型将文本、音频、视觉三种模态进行联合绑定。然而，AVLLMs内部如何实现这种三模态绑定、以及绑定失败时的根本原因尚不清楚。厘清这些机理对于提升对话导向的音视频理解、以及设计更有效的多模态推理策略具有重要意义。

Method: 论文首先对AVLLMs中三模态绑定的实现机制进行系统性探究，识别出一种"涌现式符号化三模态绑定"机制：模型将听觉信息编码为符号变量（捕捉时间上的话语序列），将视觉信息编码为符号变量（捕捉空间上的实体坐标），并在此抽象符号空间内建立跨模态链接。其次，作者定位绑定失败的主要来源——音视频连接错位（misaligned audio-visual connections）。为此提出一种音频-视觉提示方法：利用现成的Active Speaker Detection（ASD）模型检测活跃说话人，并仅在活跃说话人上叠加视觉边界框（bounding boxes）作为提示。该方法为免训练（training-free）方案，无需修改模型参数即可施加显式音视频对应先验。

Result: 在四个以对话为中心的基准（conversation-centric benchmarks）上，该ASD叠加边界框的免训练提示方法带来了即时的性能提升。此外，在ASD提示视频上进行少于300步的轻量微调后，性能增益可进一步扩展到三个通用音视频基准（general AV benchmarks），表明该提示方法具有一定的泛化能力。实验结果同时验证了音频-视觉连接错位是三模态绑定失败的主导因素这一机制性假设。

Conclusion: 主要贡献包括：(1) 揭示了AVLLMs中涌现的符号化三模态绑定机制，即通过模态特定符号变量在抽象空间完成跨模态链接；(2) 指出音视频连接错位是三模态绑定失败的主要瓶颈；(3) 提出基于ASD模型的免训练音视频提示方法，在多个对话基准上取得即时增益，并通过极少量微调泛化到通用AV基准。该工作为多说话人对话场景的音视频理解提供了机制性洞察与轻量高效的改进手段。潜在局限在于：方法依赖现成ASD模型的检测质量，且所提出的绑定机制结论主要建立在经验性分析之上，其理论普适性与在其他AVLLM架构上的适用性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Current Audio-Visual LLMs (AVLLMs) struggle with reasoning over videos featuring multi-speaker dialogues. In such videos, resolving "who says what" is crucial, which necessitates trimodal (text-audio-visual) binding. Motivated by these challenges, we systematically investigate how this trimodal binding is achieved in AVLLMs. Specifically, we identify emergent symbolic trimodal binding mechanisms in AVLLMs that utilize modality-specific symbolic variables. By encoding auditory and visual components into symbolic variables-capturing temporal utterance sequences and spatial entity coordinates, respectively-the model establishes cross-modal linking within this abstract space. Crucially, we reveal that when trimodal binding fails, the breakdown predominantly stems from misaligned audio-visual connections. To overcome this bottleneck, we introduce an audio-visual prompting method utilizing an off-the-shelf Active Speaker Detection (ASD) model. By simply overlaying visual bounding boxes on active speakers, this training-free approach yields immediate performance gains across four conversation-centric benchmarks. Moreover, lightweight fine-tuning of fewer than 300 steps on these ASD-prompted-videos extends these gains to three general AV benchmarks, suggesting the generalizability of our method.

</details>


### [61] [Light Field Primitive for Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31198)
*Liang Chen,Jiahui Ning,Xun Jiang,Xing Xu,Jimmy Ren,Fenglei Fan,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LFP（Light Field Primitives）提出了一种新的新视角合成（NVS）范式：用一组紧凑的可微基元替代稠密的射线数据库，每个基元以经典双平面参数化（two-plane parameterization）驻留于4D射线空间，通过"查询射线对基元的归属度"来决定响应，渲染时对所有响应进行合成，从而实现可实时渲染、且在光学/外观效应上高度统一的表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新视角合成的主流方法（如基于隐式辐射场或稠密光线数据库的表示）通常把大量计算或存储开销花在稠密射线采样上，且各类光学与外观效应（抗锯齿、散焦、鱼眼畸变、透明与折射）往往需要为每种效应单独设计专门的渲染机制。作者希望找到一种"结构上更本质"的表示：让基元直接生活在4D射线空间中，使原本是光场算子的光学效应自然成为同一个共享渲染器的行为，从而在不牺牲性能的前提下简化并统一各种成像效应的建模。

Method: 核心是把光场用紧凑的可微基元集合而非稠密射线集合表示。每个基元压缩（condense）一组光线为一条可学习的记录，并以其参数化位置定义该组的覆盖范围；对任意查询射线的响应由"该查询属于该组的程度"（相似度/归属度）决定，因此对一条光线渲染即等价于合成所有基元对其产生的响应——即一个可微的"响应求和/合成"过程。场景可直接从带位姿的图像中优化，训练完成后可用光线实时渲染。由于基元位于4D射线空间，原本定义在光场上的操作（如多尺度滤波、散焦、折射方向变换）可直接作用于单个统一渲染器；作者仅需对渲染器做最小修改，即支持多尺度抗锯齿、散焦去模糊与重聚焦、鱼眼相机渲染，甚至利用射线折射进行透明物体重建。

Result: 论文在标准新视角合成基准上取得了有竞争力的表现（据摘要所述为"competitive performance"），同时强调其主要优势在于结构统一性：通过与为各效应专门设计大量机制的专用框架相比，LFP 用同一个共享渲染器（仅做最小改动）即可在抗锯齿、散焦去模糊/重聚焦、鱼眼渲染和透明物体+折射重建等任务上达到相当效果，并支持实时基于光线的渲染。

Conclusion: LFP 的主要贡献在于提出了一种把基元直接嵌入4D射线空间的新型光场表示，用一个统一的渲染器同时覆盖渲染与多种光学/外观效应，兼顾实时性与实用性。其意义在于将"效应建模"从框架级的专门机制下放到表示/渲染层面的统一算子。可能的局限（摘要未展开）包括：该方法主要面向基于带位姿图像的光场/新视角合成任务，属于被动成像范式，尚未涉及事件相机、动态场景、非刚性形变或时序视频等更复杂情形；此外，摘要未给出具体定量指标，各效应下相对于专用框架的具体性能差距仍需以正文实验为准。

Abstract: We present Light Field Primitives (LFP), a formulation for novel view synthesis that replaces the dense ray database with a compact set of differentiable primitives in the classical two-plane parameterization. Each primitive condenses a group of rays into one learned record, and its response to a query is governed by how closely that query belongs to the group. Rendering a camera ray then reduces to compositing all responses it elicits, and a scene can be optimized directly from posed images and rendered in real time with rays. Beyond its competitive performance on standard benchmarks, the main advantage of LFP is structural: its primitives reside directly in the 4D ray space, so optical and appearance effects that are already operations on the light field become behaviors of a single shared renderer. With minimal changes to that renderer, LFP supports multi-scale anti-aliasing, defocus deblurring with refocusing, rendering for fisheye cameras, and even transparent object reconstruction with ray refraction, matching specialized frameworks that devote substantial machinery to these effects.

</details>


### [62] [Preserve-and-Compose Training for Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31202)
*Sehyun Kwon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PACT（Preserve-and-Compose Training）提出一种零样本组合图像检索（ZS-CIR）训练范式：在仅使用目标caption监督的基础上，引入源图像的视觉证据作为补充监督，从image–text–text（ITT）三元组中学习组合查询表示；并配套提出Chord打分机制，将目标相似度与源相对方向一致性结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合图像检索（CIR）要求检索既满足用户指定修改、又保留参考图中相关视觉内容的图像。传统CIR需要收集目标图像作为监督，代价高昂，因此出现以目标caption替代目标图像的零样本CIR（ZS-CIR）方法。但问题在于：目标caption往往只描述修改后的内容，会遗漏那些应被保留的源图细节，导致监督信号与"保留源信息"这一检索目标不一致。论文的动机正是弥补这一监督缺口——在caption监督之外，显式地从源图像中提取"应被保留"的视觉证据。

Method: 核心方法包含两部分：1）Preserve-and-Compose Training（PACT）：使用image–text–text（ITT）三元组（源图像、源caption、目标caption）进行训练，无需目标图像、也无需对gallery进行更新。训练目标同时优化两路监督——将组合查询对齐到目标caption（compose，负责语义修改），以及通过视觉监督保留源图像中的相关证据（preserve）。2）Chord scoring：一种组合式打分策略，在冻结的图像特征空间中同时衡量（a）组合查询与候选图像的目标相似度，以及（b）相对于源图像的方向一致性（source-relative directional agreement），从而在检索阶段联合考虑"修改满足度"与"源内容保留度"。

Result: 在四个ZS-CIR基准上进行了实验评估，结果显示：将目标caption监督与源图像视觉证据相结合，能够在不同数据集、不同骨干网络规模（backbone scales）以及外部gallery设置下均取得较强的检索性能。结果表明该"caption+源图证据"的联合监督范式具有良好的泛化性和插拔性。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：(1) 指出目标caption监督在ZS-CIR中会遗漏应保留的源信息，并提出利用源图像视觉证据进行补充监督；(2) 提出PACT训练框架，仅依赖ITT三元组即可学习组合查询，且不需目标图像或gallery更新；(3) 提出Chord打分，将目标相似度与源相对方向一致性融合。其意义在于为ZS-CIR提供了一种更贴合"保留+组合"任务本质的监督与检索方案。可能的局限：仍需依赖caption质量与预训练视觉-语言特征空间，打分中方向一致性的假设在复杂修改或分布外场景下的稳健性尚待进一步验证。

Abstract: Composed image retrieval (CIR) aims to retrieve images that satisfy a user-specified modification while preserving relevant visual content from a reference image. Collecting target images for this purpose is costly, motivating zero-shot CIR methods that instead use target captions as supervision. However, target captions may omit source details that should be preserved. We therefore propose, Preserve-and-Compose Training, which complements target-caption supervision with visual evidence from the source image. PACT learns from image--text--text (ITT) triplets without target images or gallery updates, aligning composed queries with target captions while preserving source evidence through visual supervision. We further introduce Chord scoring, which combines target similarity with source-relative directional agreement in the frozen image space. Results across four ZS-CIR benchmarks show that combining target-caption supervision with source-image evidence leads to strong retrieval performance across datasets, backbone scales, and external galleries. The code is available on https://github.com/sehyunkwon/PACT.

</details>


### [63] [WeaveAgent: A Two-Stage Tool-Routing Agent for Ultra-High-Resolution Remote Sensing Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31234)
*Zhongyu Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WeaveAgent 是一种两阶段「工具路由智能体」，将路由决策与视觉感知解耦：先用对齐 SFT 与 GRPO 训练模型可靠地「发射」工具调用，再按查询类型分支执行（内在查询走视觉问答，外在查询在原始全分辨率影像上调用工具并在第二轮基于工具观测作答）。论文同时给出一个诊断性结论：训练后的 2B 系统整体上并未超越零样本 8B 基线（0.263 vs 0.250）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对超高清（UHR）遥感场景中「用户意图模糊」的两大瓶颈：(1) 视觉 token 开销过高，难以对大尺寸全分辨率影像做端到端感知；(2) 工具调用必须格式可靠，而预训练模型在零样本下几乎不产生工具调用（文中为 0% 外在路由），导致外在查询无法被正确处理。该问题重要在于：UHR 遥感下的模糊意图既需要保留全分辨率细节以支持小目标/精细证据，又受制于上下文预算与调用格式的稳定性。

Method: 提出两阶段工具路由智能体 WeaveAgent：(A) 路由优先（routing-first）——工具调用的发射能力通过训练而非提示诱导获得；(B) 条件执行（conditional execution）——内在查询进入视觉问答路径，使用全场景缩略图，并可选用 WeaveEarth 风格「证据板」（evidence board）作为固定预算约 5k token 的压缩接口；外在查询直接在原始全分辨率影像上执行工具调用，并在第二轮「观测掩蔽（observation-masked）」条件下依据工具观测作答。训练流程为对齐 SFT，随后在奖励 R_WA2 下进行 GRPO。关键设计包括：路由与感知解耦、观测掩蔽的两轮式执行、以及以固定 token 预算压缩超高清证据。

Result: 数据与评测使用 VagueUHR 语料（5,000 / 3,273 / 1,000 条记录；其中 600 条内在查询 + 400 条需工具查询；base 数据集仅用于合成种子，不参与优化）。对齐 SFT 将外在路由从 0% 提升至 80.75%（323/400）；GRPO 抑制了 9 次内在查询误发射，且工具选择未发生变化。整体上训练后的 2B 系统未超过零样本 8B 基线（0.263 vs 0.250），作为诊断性贡献呈现。Oracle 归因分离出两项修复要素：将观测载入上下文使外在回答准确率在无标记跨模态返回下由 0.025 升至 0.425；两轮 SFT 阶段再额外提升 9.3 个点至 0.518，代价是路由性能小幅下降。±图像消融表明发射抑制具有视觉依据；查询注册矩阵显示 LLM 改写查询会使训练后检查点损失 2–11 个点。单次证据构建在 RTX 4090 上耗时 7.31 秒/图。代码、数据与评测协议将开源。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：将 UHR 遥感智能体的「工具调用格式可靠性」与「高成本视觉感知」显式解耦，验证了「发射靠训练而非诱导」的有效性，并通过 Oracle 归因量化了上下文载入与两轮式 SFT 各自的收益。其意义在于为超高清、模糊意图下的工具使用型视觉智能体提供了可复现的评测协议与诊断框架。局限包括：小模型（2B）在整体指标上仍未超越更大的零样本基线，说明路由训练并未转化为普遍的能力提升；证据构建 7.31 s/图偏慢，难以满足实时性需求；性能对查询表述敏感（LLM 改写导致 2–11 点下降），且 5k token 压缩接口带来信息损失。

Abstract: Problem. Ultra-high-resolution (UHR) remote sensing with vague user intents has two bottlenecks: visual tokens are expensive, and tool calling must be format-reliable (pretrained models emit zero tool calls zero-shot). Method. WeaveAgent, a two-stage tool-routing agent, decouples routing from visual perception. Stage A is routing-first: emission is trained, not elicited. Stage B executes conditionally: intrinsic queries enter visual answering (full-scene thumbnail; a WeaveEarth-style evidence board as an optional fixed-budget, approx. 5k-token compression interface); extrinsic queries execute tool call on original full-resolution imagery, answering from tool observations in a second, observation-masked round. Training: alignment SFT, then GRPO under reward R_WA2. Results. Alignment SFT lifts extrinsic routing from 0% to 80.75% (323/400); GRPO suppresses 9 intrinsic mis-emissions while tool selection is unchanged. The trained 2B system does not beat the zero-shot 8B baseline overall (0.263 vs. 0.250), a diagnostic contribution. Oracle attribution separates two repair ingredients: loading the observation into context lifts extrinsic answer accuracy from 0.025 to 0.425 under marker-free cross-mode returns, and the two-turn SFT stage adds a further +9.3 points to 0.518 at a small routing cost. A +/- image ablation shows emission suppression is visually grounded, and a query-register matrix shows LLM-rewritten queries cost trained checkpoints 2-11 points. Scope. All training and evaluation use the 5,000 / 3,273 / 1,000-record VagueUHR corpus (600 intrinsic + 400 tool-requiring; the base seeds synthesis and is not used for optimization). Single-pass evidence construction runs at 7.31 s per image on an RTX 4090. Code, data, and evaluation protocols will be released.

</details>


### [64] [Geometric Inconsistency Localization in Multi-View Image Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31247)
*Xander Staelens,Albéric Loos,Bert Ramlot,Hannes Mareen,Peter Lambert,Glenn Van Wallendael*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出宽基线多视角数据集 DeformView（含像素级几何不一致标注），并在此基础上提出轻量级学习式分类器 DEFECt3R，通过跨视角特征关系实现像素级的几何不一致定位，用于多媒体取证。研究表明现有多视角一致性评分方法在该取证任务上迁移效果差，而显式监督（含"几何一致但变形"困难负样本）能显著提升定位性能并降低误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新视角合成（NVS）模型虽能生成逼真的新视角图像，但生成的不同视角之间往往缺乏几何一致性。多视角一致性已被用作评估 NVS 模型的工具，但其在多媒体取证中的潜力尚未被充分挖掘，尤其缺乏在宽基线图像对上定位几何不一致的能力。该问题的意义在于：几何不一致可作为检测合成/篡改图像的取证信号，而现有方法缺少数据集与针对性方法。为此作者构建 DeformView 数据集以支持该方向研究，并指出为 NVS 评估设计的 MV 一致性评分方法难以直接迁移到取证定位任务。

Method: 1) 数据集：DeformView，宽基线多视角数据集，提供像素级几何不一致标注，用于支持取证定位研究。2) 基线评估：在 DeformView 上评测当前最先进的多视角一致性评分方法，揭示其从 NVS 评估向取证定位迁移的不足。3) 提出 DEFECt3R：轻量级、基于学习的分类器，利用跨视角特征关系（cross-view feature relationships）对几何不一致进行像素级定位。4) 训练策略：采用显式监督，引入来自"几何一致但发生形变"视角的困难负样本（hard negatives），以提升定位性能并显著降低误报。5) 消融实验分析特征表示（feature representations）与对应关系质量（correspondence quality）对定位性能的贡献。

Result: 实验在自建的 DeformView 宽基线多视角数据集上进行，评价任务为像素级几何不一致定位（含误报控制）。主要发现：为 NVS 评估开发的现有多视角一致性评分方法在该取证定位任务上迁移效果较差；提出的 DEFECt3R 通过显式监督与困难负样本，相比现有一致性评分方法提升了定位性能并大幅减少误报。消融实验表明特征表示与对应关系质量二者均对定位性能有贡献。代码与数据集已开源（https://github.com/IDLabMedia/DeformView-DEFECt3R）。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献包括：构建并开源了首个（面向该任务的）宽基线多视角几何不一致像素级标注数据集 DeformView；系统评测并揭示了现有 MV 一致性评分方法在取证定位任务上的迁移局限；提出轻量级学习式方法 DEFECt3R，借助跨视角特征关系与困难负样本监督实现像素级几何不一致定位，建立该方向的基准与基线。意义在于表明多视角几何一致性是多媒体取证中一个有前景但尚待深入挖掘的信号。可能的局限：论文摘要未给出具体数据集规模、评价指标数值与跨数据集泛化能力，方法依赖显式监督与跨视角对应关系质量，在无标注或对应关系退化场景下的适用性有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Novel view synthesis (NVS) models can produce realistic new views of the same scene from different viewpoints. However, these generated views are not always geometrically consistent with one another. Multi-view (MV) consistency has shown promise as a tool for evaluating these NVS models. Its potential for multimedia forensics, however, remains largely unexplored, particularly for localizing geometric inconsistencies across wide-baseline image pairs. To enable research in this direction, we introduce DeformView, a wide-baseline MV dataset with pixel-level annotations of geometric inconsistencies. Using DeformView, we evaluate state-of-the-art MV consistency-scoring methods and show that approaches developed for NVS evaluation transfer poorly to the forensic task of geometric inconsistency localization. To address this limitation, we propose DEFECt3R, a lightweight learning-based classifier that uses cross-view feature relationships to localize geometric inconsistencies at the pixel level. By learning from explicit supervision, including hard negatives from geometrically consistent yet deformed views, DEFECt3R improves localization performance and substantially reduces false positives compared to existing consistency-scoring methods. Ablation experiments further show that both feature representations and correspondence quality contribute to localization performance. Overall, our findings demonstrate that MV geometric consistency is a promising yet underexplored signal for multimedia forensics and establish a benchmark and baseline for geometric inconsistency localization in wide-baseline MV image pairs. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/IDLabMedia/DeformView-DEFECt3R

</details>


### [65] [Gauss What You Need: Compact Gaussian Splatting Across Scene Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31248)
*Afif Boudaoud,Jiayi Liu,Alexandru Calotoiu,Torsten Hoefler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TangoGS 提出一种将"捕获派生模型定规模"与"训练反馈定最终规模"结合的 3D Gaussian Splatting 训练框架：训练前依据 capture 的像素量（扣除重复观测同一场景点的视图）设定高斯原语数量的增长预算，训练中根据训练视图重建质量动态增删高斯，从而在无需重新调参的情况下跨场景规模实现质量—体积的均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D Gaussian Splatting 将场景表示为大量高斯原语，原语数量直接决定重建质量、存储开销和渲染成本。然而如何自动确定该数量、并在不同捕获规模（capture scale）间自适配仍未解决：在标准基准上有效的配置，用于大尺度捕获时往往高斯数量不足，难以恢复细节。作者的关键观察是：待表示表面由捕获的覆盖范围与分辨率决定，因此其规模在训练前已知；而内容复杂度只有在训练过程中、通过训练视图的重建质量才能显现。这一"先验可知规模 + 训练中可知复杂度"的不对称性构成了方法的出发点。

Method: TangoGS 采用两阶段/双层自适配机制：
1）捕获派生的学习预算（capture-derived learning allowance）：在训练前从 capture 的总像素数出发估计模型可增长的学习额度（允许生成的高斯数量上限），并对重复观测同一场景点的视图进行折扣，避免因视角冗余而高估所需容量，从而得到一个与捕获尺度相称的规模尺度（scale）。
2）训练质量引导的密度控制（training-quality guided density control）：在训练过程中，以训练视图上的重建质量作为反馈信号，动态决定新增（add）和移除（remove）多少高斯原语，使最终模型大小落在该尺度内的合适位置。
整体思路是将"规模尺度"由数据先验固定，而"尺度内的具体大小"交由训练反馈决定，因此同一配置可跨场景尺度使用而无需针对每个场景重新调参。

Result: 实验覆盖两类场景：13 个标准基准场景与 8 个大规模捕获。评估指标以 PSNR 为主，同时比较高斯数量（模型大小）。
- 标准基准：TangoGS 在平均 PSNR 上达到所评估基线中表现最佳的 LeGS 的水平，但高斯数量减少 48%，即同等质量下模型显著更紧凑。
- 大尺度捕获：同一配置自动扩展为更大的模型，在评估方法中取得最高平均 PSNR，比第二名高 0.54 dB，同时使用了 2.3× 的高斯数量（用于换取该质量提升）。
结果表明，捕获派生的学习预算与训练质量引导的密度控制结合，可在不同场景尺度上实现最优的质量—体积折中，且无需重新调参。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出一种跨捕获尺度自适配的 Gaussian Splatting 模型规模控制方案：用捕获像素量与视角冗余折扣先验地确定模型尺度，再用训练重建质量在线决定最终高斯数量，从而在标准基准上以更少高斯匹配最佳基线 PSNR，在大尺度捕获上自动扩容并取得最高 PSNR。其意义在于缓解了 GS 训练中"每个场景/尺度都需重新调参"的工程负担，并提供了一条以数据先验与训练反馈联合确定模型容量的通用思路（与神经渲染/3D 重建中自适应容量分配问题相关）。
可能的局限（据摘要可推断）：摘要仅以 PSNR 报告结果，未提及对存储、训练时长、渲染 FPS 等其他成本维度以及 SSIM/LPIPS 等感知指标的完整评估；"最佳基线 LeGS"等比较范围有限；对视角冗余折扣的估计依赖位姿已知的捕获；跨尺度泛化能力是否适用于极端尺度差异或动态/稀疏视角情形尚不明确。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstructs a scene as a collection of Gaussian primitives from a set of posed photographs called the capture. The number of primitives used to represent the scene affects reconstruction quality, storage, and rendering cost. How to select this number automatically across capture scales remains unresolved: configurations effective on standard benchmarks can leave larger captures with too few Gaussians to reconstruct fine details. We observe that the surface to represent, given by the capture's extent and resolution, is known before training, whereas its content complexity becomes apparent during training, through the reconstruction quality on the training views. We introduce TangoGS, which combines capture-derived model sizing with training-based adaptation: the capture determines the scale of the model, and training feedback determines its final size within that scale. Before training, TangoGS derives a learning allowance for model growth from the capture's total pixels after discounting views that re-observe the same scene points. During training, reconstruction quality guides how many Gaussians to add and remove. On 13 standard benchmark scenes, TangoGS matches the mean PSNR of the best-performing evaluated baseline, LeGS, with $48\%$ fewer Gaussians. On eight large captures, the same configuration automatically scales to larger models when necessary, achieving the highest mean PSNR among evaluated methods: $0.54$ dB above the runner-up with $2.3\times$ as many Gaussians. Together, capture-derived learning allowances and training-quality guided density control enable a state-of-the-art quality--size compromise across scene scales without retuning.

</details>


### [66] [MoTop: Motion-Topological Model For Micro AU Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31285)
*Huai-Qian Khor,Mengting Wei,Yante Li,Chu Kiong Loo,Guoying Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoTop 是一种"运动-拓扑"模型，用于微表情中的面部动作单元（AU）检测。它通过可学习的运动上下文生成区域性软引导，并结合面部关键点捕捉微 AU 的细尺度拓扑变化，在 CD6ME 协议的微 AU 检测任务上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面部微表情是自发的、短暂的、细微的面部运动，能在高风险场景下揭示被压抑的情绪。相比经典的表情分析，检测动作单元（Action Unit, AU）可提供更精细的面部运动表示，是定义表情类别及下游任务的前置步骤，因此 AU 检测是面部分析中的关键上游任务，其精度提升能直接改善整体面部分析。然而 AU 检测具有挑战性，原因是 AU 激活区域本身收缩性强（constrictive），导致不同 AU 之间出现混淆，即所谓的"AU 歧义"（AU ambiguity）。论文的动机正是要对这种细尺度变化进行建模以缓解 AU 歧义。

Method: 论文提出 MoTop（Motion-Topological model），一个运动-拓扑模型，核心由以下组件构成：
1. 可学习的运动上下文：为面部活动生成区域性的"软引导"（regional soft guidance），引导模型关注 AU 相关的局部区域。
2. 面部关键点拓扑建模：利用面部关键点捕捉微 AU 的细尺度拓扑变化。
3. 关键点过渡增强：通过线性外推（linear extrapolation）放大编码后的面部关键点过渡，从而增大关键点的空间邻近性并增强低强度的关键点动态。
4. 解剖学面部聚类（anatomical facial clusters）：增强分层表示，促进面部几何的多尺度建模，改进微拓扑表示。
整体思路是把运动上下文与拓扑几何表示结合，以多尺度方式刻画细微的面部几何变化。

Result: 论文在 CD6ME 协议的微 AU 检测任务上进行评估，并报告取得了 state-of-the-art 性能。摘要未提供具体的评价指标数值、数据集细节或消融实验结果。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是提出 MoTop 模型，通过可学习运动上下文、面部关键点拓扑建模、线性外推增强关键点动态以及解剖学面部聚类等设计，缓解 AU 歧义问题，并在 CD6ME 微 AU 检测协议上达到最优性能，为面部微表情分析的上游任务提供了更精细的表示。潜在局限在于：现有材料未给出量化结果与消融分析，也未说明模型的计算开销、实时性以及在更广泛数据集上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Facial micro-expressions are spontaneous, brief, and subtle facial movements that reveal suppressed emotions in high-stakes environments. In contrast to classic expression analysis, detecting action unit (AU) yields a finer representation of facial movements, serving as a preliminary step before defining expression classes and other downstream tasks. Therefore, it represents a crucial upstream task in facial analysis, and improving an AU detection module increases the precision of facial analysis. Despite that, detecting AU is challenging because of the constrictive nature of the AU activation regions, leading to confusion among different AUs known as AU ambiguity. To model the fine-scale changes, we propose \textbf{MoTop}, a motion-topological model that is augmented with a learnable motion context, yielding regional soft guidance for facial activity, followed by facial landmarks that capture the fine-scale topological changes of micro AUs. To increase the micro facial landmark representations, we amplify the encoded facial landmark transitions via linear extrapolation, thereby increasing the spatial proximity of landmarks and enhancing the low-intensity landmark dynamics. In addition, we design anatomical facial clusters that enhance the hierarchical representation, facilitating multi-scale modelling of facial geometry and improving micro-topological representations. With these contributions, we have achieved state-of-the-art performance on the CD6ME protocol for the micro AU detection task.

</details>


### [67] [UniAR: A Unified Framework for Autism Recognition Enhanced by Multi-View Prompt Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31298)
*Lei Xin,Zeheng Wang,Jiayin Zhu,Shihong Huang,Fanhu Zeng,Changjiang Jiang,Dengbo He,Yutao Yue,Zhenglun Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniAR是一个面向自闭症谱系障碍(ASD)识别的统一框架，通过多粒度提示学习和多尺度语义-视觉对齐，在临床文本稀缺条件下实现稳健识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASD是复杂的神经发育障碍，早期准确诊断对改善长期发育结果至关重要。现有ASD识别方法受限于诊断文本数据稀缺，主要依赖视觉分析，无法建模具有临床意义的语义推理，且难以应对异质数据变化。

Method: 提出UniAR统一框架。首先利用大型多模态模型生成词、短语、句子三个层级的层次化诊断描述，弥补配对临床报告的缺失。随后设计基于混合专家(Mixture-of-Experts)的多尺度对齐模块，将向量量化后的视觉原型与对应粒度的语义表示进行动态匹配，实现语义与视觉证据的对齐。

Result: 在涵盖脑部MRI和面部表情两类场景的四个基准上进行了大量实验。UniAR持续优于现有SOTA方法，MRI基准平均准确率达75.9%，面部基准达91.6%；相比基线，MRI基准平均准确率提升1.5个百分点，面部基准提升1.2个百分点。

Conclusion: UniAR通过多粒度提示学习与多尺度语义-视觉对齐，在语义稀缺条件下提供了稳健且可解释的ASD筛查框架，在MRI和面部基准上均取得领先性能。局限在于各基准的绝对准确率（尤其MRI约75.9%）仍有提升空间，且对大型多模态模型生成语义的质量存在依赖。

Abstract: Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD) is a complex neurodevelopmental disorder for which early and accurate diagnosis is critical to improving long-term developmental outcomes. However, existing ASD recognition methods are often constrained by the scarcity of diagnostic text data, forcing them to rely mainly on visual analysis and limiting their ability to model clinically meaningful semantic reasoning. To address this challenge, we propose UniAR, a unified framework enhanced by multi-granularity prompt learning for robust ASD recognition under heterogeneous data variations. Specifically, UniAR leverages a large multimodal model to generate hierarchical diagnostic descriptions at the word, phrase, and sentence levels, compensating for the lack of paired clinical reports. To align the generated semantics with visual evidence, we further design a Mixture-of-Experts-based Multi-Scale Alignment Module, which dynamically matches vector-quantized visual prototypes with semantic representations at corresponding granularities. Extensive experiments on four benchmarks covering brain MRI and facial expression scenarios show that UniAR consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods, achieving average accuracies of 75.9\% on MRI benchmarks and 91.6\% on facial benchmarks, while improving average Accuracy on MRI benchmarks by 1.5 percentage points and average Accuracy on facial benchmarks by 1.2 percentage points over baselines. These results demonstrate that UniAR offers a robust and interpretable framework for ASD screening under semantic scarcity.

</details>


### [68] [CytoSPM: Open-Vocabulary Cytopathology Detection with Structured Prompt Bank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31314)
*Wenjie Li,Zishan Xu,Jinyang Huang,Zhengxin Nie,Shichao Kan,Yixiong Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PentaCyto——覆盖宫颈、泌尿、呼吸、浆液、甲状腺五个细胞学域的多域开放词汇细胞病理学检测基准（24个基类+9个新类，每类带结构化细胞形态学提示），以及高效解耦两阶段检测器CytoSPM，通过类无关视觉表征提取与类感知结构提示匹配实现新类与开放词汇检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞病理学检测中，细胞类别具有细粒度、长尾分布以及随器官系统不断演化的特点，因此需要开放词汇（open-vocabulary）识别能力，以便在推理时泛化到训练中未见过的新类别。然而现有细胞学检测器大多局限于单域、闭集设定，且缺乏统一的开放词汇细胞病理检测评测基准，导致方法间难以公平比较，也限制了在临床多器官场景下的部署。

Method: 工作包含基准与模型两部分。基准PentaCyto：覆盖宫颈、泌尿、呼吸、浆液（serous fluid）、甲状腺五大细胞学域，包含24个基类与9个留出的新类（held-out novel）；每个类别配有结构化细胞形态学提示（cytomorphology prompts），用于描述诊断相关的形态学属性，注入具有临床依据的文本知识。模型CytoSPM：采用解耦的两阶段设计，第一阶段提取可复用的类无关（class-agnostic）视觉表征，第二阶段以类名与细胞形态学提示进行类感知的结构化提示匹配（structural prompt matching），从而实现开放词汇检测。该解耦设计使视觉特征抽取与文本/类别侧匹配相互独立，兼顾泛化性与推理效率。

Result: 在自建的多域基准PentaCyto上进行评测，评价维度包括新类检测与开放词汇检测性能，并关注推理效率。实验结果显示CytoSPM在新类检测与开放词汇检测上均优于现有方法，同时保持高效的推理速度。摘要未给出具体数值指标（如AP/mAP）、未说明因域划分的跨域评测协议细节，也未报告与其他方法在效率上的定量对比数值。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出首个覆盖五个细胞学域的多域开放词汇细胞病理检测统一基准PentaCyto，并引入带临床形态学知识的结构化提示；同时提出解耦两阶段的CytoSPM检测器，在新类与开放词汇检测上取得优越性能并保持高效推理。意义在于推动细胞学检测从单域闭集走向多域开放词汇，并强调结构化文本知识对细粒度、长尾类别识别的作用。局限在于：基准规模有限（24+9类）、新类数量较少；摘要未提供量化结果与效率对比，结构化提示的构造成本与依赖专家知识的可扩展性、以及跨域泛化与临床真实分布偏移下的鲁棒性仍待验证。

Abstract: Cytopathology detection requires open-vocabulary recognition because cellular categories are fine-grained, long-tailed, and continuously evolving across different organ systems. However, existing cytology detectors are mostly single-domain and closed-set, and there is still no unified benchmark for evaluating open-vocabulary cytopathology detection. We present PentaCyto, a multi-domain benchmark covering cervical, urinary, respiratory, serous fluid, and thyroid cytology, with 24 base categories and 9 held-out novel categories. Each category is associated with structured cytomorphology prompts that describe diagnostic morphological attributes and provide clinically grounded textual knowledge. We further propose CytoSPM, an efficient detector based on a decoupled two-stage design. It first extracts reusable class-agnostic visual representations, and then performs class-aware structural prompt matching with class names and cytomorphology prompts. On PentaCyto, CytoSPM outperforms existing methods in novel-category detection and open-vocabulary detection while maintaining efficient inference.

</details>


### [69] [CG-HAF: An Interpretable Global-Local Lesion-Burden Fusion Framework for Ordinal Acne Severity Grading in Agentic Skincare Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31326)
*Muhammad Muhtasim Shahriar,Md. Naimur Asif Borno,Saad Aloteibi,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 CG-HAF，一种全局-局部融合框架用于有序痤疮严重度分级：将独立训练的全局分类器输出的整体严重度概率，与目标检测器提取的病变负担描述子（病变数量、检测置信度、病变面积）显式融合，再由轻量可解释分类器输出最终等级。实验表明融合优于纯全局证据基线，且跨数据集时分级标准不一致是主要迁移障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有序痤疮分级需要区分视觉上高度相似的相邻等级，同时兼顾面部整体外观与局部病灶负担这两类互补证据。现有方法通常将两类信息压缩进单一不透明的表示中，导致证据不可分解、可解释性差，也难以定位错误来源。作者认为将全局外观证据与局部病灶证据保持显式分离，能提升分级性能与决策透明度。

Method: CG-HAF 采用全局-局部双分支融合：1）全局分支使用独立训练的多个分类器输出平均化的整体严重度概率（holistic severity probabilities）；2）局部分支使用目标检测器提取结构化病变负担描述子，包括病变数量、检测置信度、病变面积；3）两类特征组成紧凑表示，输入一个轻量、可解释的分类器（如线性/浅层模型）产生最终等级。关键创新在于保持证据显式且结构化，而非端到端黑箱融合，从而支持后续诊断与解释。

Result: 在一个广泛使用的基准数据集上，全局-局部融合相较仅用全局证据的基线取得明显且具统计显著性的提升，在最严重等级样本上增益最大。在采用不同分级标准的独立数据集上测试时，数据集内强性能并不能自动迁移，后续诊断分析将该差距主要归因于分级标准不匹配，而非单纯的检测失败。结果同时展示了严重度信号可用于透明、非诊断性的护肤应用决策参考。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出可解释的全局-局部融合策略用于有序痤疮分级，验证了显式融合病灶负担描述子的有效性，并识别出分级标准对齐是跨数据集可移植性的关键因素。意义在于提升分级性能同时保持决策透明度。局限方面：跨数据集泛化受制于标注标准差异，需数据集特定验证；聚焦皮肤痤疮领域，尚需更多数据集和对比方法验证普适性。

Abstract: Ordinal acne severity grading requires distinguishing visually similar neighboring grades while jointly weighing holistic facial appearance and localized lesion burden - evidence that most existing approaches collapse into a single opaque representation. We introduce CG-HAF, a global-local fusion framework that instead keeps this evidence explicit: averaged holistic severity probabilities from independently trained classifiers are combined with structured lesion-burden descriptors from an object detector (lesion count, detection confidence, lesion area) into a compact representation, from which a lightweight, interpretable classifier produces the final grade. On a widely used benchmark, this fusion yields a clear, statistically supported improvement over global-evidence-only baselines, with the largest gains on the most severe cases. Testing on an independent dataset with a different grading standard shows that strong within-dataset performance does not transfer automatically, and a follow-up diagnostic attributes much of this gap to mismatched grading criteria rather than detection failure alone. These findings support interpretable global-local fusion as an effective strategy for ordinal acne grading while highlighting criterion alignment as key to cross-dataset portability, with a further illustration of how the resulting severity signal can support transparent, non-diagnostic decision-making in skincare applications.

</details>


### [70] [DyMD: Preserving Interaction Dynamics through Distribution Matching Distillation in Few-Step Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31349)
*Haojun Xu,Jie Huang,Xin Lu,Mingchen Zhong,Zihao Fan,Linjiang Huang,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DyMD 是一个自适应分布匹配蒸馏（Distribution Matching Distillation, DMD）框架，通过让教师监督与判别器拟合同时随学生的演化而调整，将 14B 视频扩散教师蒸馏为四步 1.3B 学生模型（推理时无辅助模块），在保持视觉质量的同时显著恢复机器人-物体交互动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视频扩散模型为具身预测与学习提供了强大的表达能力，但其多步采样在交互式下游应用中代价过高。DMD 能实现少步视频生成，但在蒸馏过程中往往会抑制机器人-物体的运动，仅保留视觉质量。作者通过分析 DMD 的教师信号与 fake-score 信号发现两个问题：(1) 弱重加噪使教师后验集中在缺乏运动的 rollout 附近，限制了恢复运动的引导；(2) 运动更强的 rollout 通常带来更大的 fake-score 拟合误差，从而阻碍生成器学习交互动态。因此需要一种能够同时适配动态变化学生状态的自适应蒸馏方案，以兼顾运动恢复与外观精修，这对具身视频生成及下游动作规划至关重要。

Method: 提出 DyMD，核心是让教师监督与 critic 拟合都随学生演化而自适应：(1) 时间亲和度条件重加噪采样（Temporal affinity-conditioned re-noise sampling）：根据每个 rollout 当前的交互保真度自适应调整时间步分布，将基础调度与一个由局部后验变化动机引导的教师先验混合，从而平衡运动恢复与外观精修。(2) 动态引导的 fake-score 追踪（dynamics-guided fake-score tracking）：使用噪声条件预测器从潜在时序动态中估计噪声相关的拟合难度，并在 critic 损失中对预测为困难的 rollout 加权，从而更好地跟踪运动更强的 rollout。推理阶段无任何辅助模块，仅使用四步 1.3B 学生模型。

Result: 在具身视频基准上，相比 Base DMD，学生模型在 R-Bench 任务遵循度上提升 9.6 个百分点，PAI-Bench-G 的 Domain 分数提升 5.1 分，同时保持可比的视觉质量。作为下游动作规划的骨干，学生在两个 WorldArena 任务上取得 34% 平均成功率，而 Base DMD 为 16%。

Conclusion: DyMD 的主要贡献在于识别并缓解了DMD蒸馏中运动被抑制的两个信号层面的根因，通过自适应教师监督与 critic 拟合实现了几步蒸馏下的交互动态恢复，并在具身视频生成与动作规划上验证了有效性。其意义在于提升了大型视频扩散模型在交互式具身任务中的实用性。局限可能包括：仅评估于具身视频基准与 WorldArena 任务，泛化性有待验证；仍依赖 14B 教师进行蒸馏；对更细粒度运动指标与长时域稳定性的评估描述有限。

Abstract: Large video diffusion models offer expressive priors for embodied prediction and learning, yet their many-step sampling remains costly for interactive downstream use. Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) enables few-step video generation, but can suppress robot--object motion while preserving visual quality. Examining DMD's teacher and fake-score signals, we find that weak re-noising keeps the teacher posterior concentrated near motion-deficient rollouts, limiting motion-restoring guidance. Meanwhile, stronger-motion rollouts tend to incur larger fake-score fitting errors, which can hinder the generator's learning of interaction dynamics. We propose DyMD, a DMD framework that adapts both teacher supervision and critic fitting to the evolving student. Temporal affinity--conditioned re-noise sampling adapts the timestep distribution to each rollout's current interaction fidelity by mixing the base schedule with a teacher prior motivated by local posterior variation, thereby balancing motion recovery and appearance refinement. To better track stronger-motion rollouts, dynamics-guided fake-score tracking uses a noise-conditioned predictor to estimate noise-relative fitting difficulty from latent temporal dynamics, then upweights predicted-hard rollouts in the critic loss. Using DyMD, we distill a 14B teacher into a four-step 1.3B student with no auxiliary modules at inference. On embodied-video benchmarks, the student improves R-Bench task adherence by $9.6$ percentage points and PAI-Bench-G Domain score by $5.1$ points over Base DMD while maintaining comparable visual quality. As a backbone for downstream action planning, our student achieves 34% mean success across two WorldArena tasks, compared with 16% for Base DMD.

</details>


### [71] [Open Vocabulary Domain Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31356)
*Sumanth Udupa,Mehrtash Harandi,Yadan Luo,Mahsa Baktashmotlagh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"开放词汇域遗忘"（Open-Vocabulary Domain Unlearning, OVDU）新范式，指出现有近似域遗忘（ADU）方法受限于封闭词汇假设，仅在已见类别上评估遗忘效果，实为对"类别-域"配对过拟合而非真正抹除域。作者提出基于参数编辑的框架：Fisher信息掩码定位域敏感权重以保护零样本泛化，配合Targeted Manifold Scattering（TMS）目标散射遗忘域的风格几何结构。方法在PACS、OfficeHome、DomainNet上显著提升开放词汇泛化，并以仅4个样本超越8样本基线的峰值表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）虽具备强大的零样本泛化能力，却会编码不良或危险的风格域，例如医学AI中的理想化教科书示意图、自动驾驶中的卡通车辆，构成安全隐患。近似域遗忘（ADU）旨在选择性抹除模型对特定目标视觉域的识别，同时保留其余域的准确率。然而，现有ADU方法建立在有缺陷的封闭词汇假设之上：仅在遗忘微调阶段所见的特定物体类别上评估遗忘效果。因此这些方法并未真正遗忘"域"本身，而是过拟合到已见的类别-域配对，对未见类别而言该域仍易于识别，从而造成"已移除"的假象。作者主张真正的域抹除必须是类别无关的，这一问题的解决对模型安全、合规部署与可靠性至关重要。

Method: 作者形式化了OVDU协议，要求域遗忘必须迁移到留出类别（held-out classes）上。为此提出外科手术式参数编辑框架，包含两个核心组件：其一，Fisher Information掩码用于隔离对域敏感的权重，在数学上保护模型的基础零样本泛化能力，避免全参数微调带来的灾难性遗忘；其二，Targeted Manifold Scattering（TMS）目标函数，通过基于偏好的挖掘（preference-based mining）策略，在局部散射遗忘域的风格几何结构，从而破坏该域在特征流形上的可识别性。整体方法以参数编辑为指导，而非简单的全参数微调，兼顾域遗忘与跨类别泛化保持。

Result: 方法在PACS、OfficeHome与DomainNet三个域泛化基准数据集上进行评估，重点考察开放词汇（类别无关）泛化能力。实验表明，相比现有ADU基线，本文方法在开放词汇泛化上取得大幅提升。尤为关键的是样本效率极佳：仅用4个样本（4-shot）即超越基线在8-shot设定下的峰值结果。评价维度涵盖对留出类别上遗忘域的可识别性抑制与剩余域准确率的保持。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献在于：指出ADU封闭词汇假设的根本缺陷，形式化OVDU协议并要求遗忘迁移到未见类别；提出结合Fisher信息掩码与TMS目标的参数编辑框架，实现类别无关的真正域抹除。其意义在于为VLM安全与合规部署提供了更严谨的评估范式与高效方法。可能局限在于：域遗忘与泛化保持间的权衡边界、风格几何散射对复杂多域场景的可扩展性，以及Fisher掩码对超参（如阈值、样本数）的敏感性仍待进一步检验。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) exhibit remarkable zero-shot generalization, yet they often encode unwanted or hazardous stylistic domains such as idealized textbook diagrams in medical AI or cartoon vehicles in autonomous driving. Approximate Domain Unlearning (ADU) aims to selectively erase a model's recognition of a target visual domain while preserving accuracy on the remaining domains. However, existing ADU methods operate under a flawed closed-vocabulary assumption: they evaluate unlearning solely on the specific object classes seen during the unlearning fine-tuning phase. Consequently, these methods do not unlearn the domain itself; they merely overfit to seen class-domain pairs, leaving the domain easily recognizable for unseen classes and providing a false sense of removal. We argue that true domain erasure must be class-agnostic. To address this, we formalize Open-Vocabulary Domain Unlearning (OVDU), a rigorous protocol that mandates domain forgetting must transfer to held-out classes. To solve the OVDU challenge, we propose a surgical parameter-editing framework. First, a Fisher Information mask isolates domain-sensitive weights, mathematically protecting foundational zero-shot generalization. Second, our Targeted Manifold Scattering (TMS) objective uses preference-based mining to locally scatter the forget domain's stylistic geometry. Evaluated across PACS, OfficeHome, and DomainNet, our method vastly improves open-vocabulary generalization over existing baselines. Crucially, it delivers exceptional sample efficiency, outperforming peak 8-shot baseline results with only 4 shots.

</details>


### [72] [OpenVAM: Open-World Visual Attention Modeling with VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31364)
*Kiana Hooshanfar,Amirhossein Kazerouni,Alireza Hosseini,Michael Brudno,Babak Taati*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OpenVAM 是一个面向开放世界的统一视觉注意力建模框架，将稠密显著图定位与"what/why"语义解释解耦但协同地结合，通过指令跟随的 VLM 语义头实现跨自然图像、商业图像与 UI/Web 布局的域泛化与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类注视预测（gaze prediction / saliency modeling）在 Web/UI 设计分析、机器人、人机交互等场景中具有重要价值。然而现有方法大多只输出稠密显著图（dense saliency map），无法直接支撑下游行动：实践者需要将注意力峰值关联到场景中的离散元素（what），并理解这些峰值在上下文中的成因（why）。此外，跨自然图像、商业内容和 UI/Web 布局存在显著域偏移（domain shift），现有模型缺乏统一性与鲁棒性。因此，论文的核心动机是同时解决"通用性（universality）"与"可解释性（explainability）"两个挑战，并跨异构领域与多种监督模态进行统一建模。

Method: 论文提出 OpenVAM（Open-world Visual Attention Modeling with VLMs），采用"解耦但对齐（decoupled-but-aligned）"的设计：

1. 双路径架构：一条专用的稠密视觉路径（dense visual pathway）负责稳定、空间精确的显著位置定位；一条指令跟随的视觉-语言语义头（instruction-following vision-language semantic head）在同一图像与数据类型提示（data-type prompts）条件下生成有依据（grounded）的 what/why 解释。

2. 三阶段训练策略：先保留强定位先验（localization priors），再逐步引入语言 grounding，并通过参数高效适配（parameter-efficient adaptation）提升解释对齐度，且不扰动显著图分支。

3. 数据与标注流水线：提出可扩展的流水线，为多域（自然、商业、UI/Web）生成显著-理由（saliency-reason）联合标注，用于训练与系统性评测。

Result: 在两个核心方面进行评估：跨多域数据集的鲁棒性，以及解释与图像内容的贴合程度。实验结果表明，OpenVAM 在域偏移（domain shift）条件下提升了鲁棒性，同时生成的图像 grounded 解释使显著预测更具可解释性。（摘要未给出具体数据集名称、评价指标数值或消融结果，仅提供定性/相对性结论。）

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：(1) 提出统一框架 OpenVAM，首次在一个系统中同时追求跨异构领域的通用性与 what/why 可解释性；(2) 解耦但对齐的架构设计与三阶段训练策略，在保留强定位能力的同时引入语言 grounding；(3) 可扩展的多域显著-理由标注流水线，支持训练与系统评测。

意义：将显著图预测从"仅输出热图"推进到"输出可操作、可解释的结构化注意力理解"，对 UI/Web 设计分析、机器人与 HCI 具有实用价值。

可能局限：摘要中未披露具体数据集规模、量化指标、计算开销，也未说明参数高效适配与 VLM 语义头在实时性/边缘部署上的可行性；what/why 解释的忠实性与评测客观性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Predicting human gaze is a core capability for applications ranging from web/UI design analysis to robotics and human-computer interaction. Yet, most visual attention modeling methods output only a dense saliency map, which is often insufficient for action: practitioners need to connect attention peaks to discrete elements in the scene (what) and understand the drivers of those peaks in context (why), while remaining robust to domain shift across natural images, commercial content, and UI/web layouts. We, therefore, introduce OpenVAM (Open-world Visual Attention Modeling with VLMs), a unified framework that jointly addresses universality and explainability across heterogeneous domains (natural scenes, commercial imagery, and UI/web layouts) and supervision modalities. OpenVAM adopts a decoupled-but-aligned design: a dedicated dense visual pathway provides stable, spatially precise localization, while an instruction-following vision--language semantic head generates grounded what/why explanations conditioned on the same image and data-type prompts. A three-stage training strategy preserves strong localization priors while progressively introducing language grounding and improving explanation alignment via parameter-efficient adaptation without perturbing the saliency branch. We further propose a scalable pipeline to generate multi-domain saliency-reason annotations for training and systematic evaluation. Experiments across diverse datasets show that OpenVAM improves robustness under domain shift while producing image-grounded explanations that make saliency predictions more interpretable.

</details>


### [73] [ContraFM-S2O: Flow Matching-Based One-step SAR-to-Optical Image Translation Model with Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31378)
*Mingqian Yu,Wei-kuan Chiang,Qiurui Wang,Peilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ContraFM-S2O 是一种基于流匹配（Flow Matching）的 SAR 到光学图像翻译模型，通过预测速度场并以 ODE 求解替代扩散模型的 SDE 采样，同时引入平均速度实现一步生成，并结合对比学习提升生成图像质量，在 SAR2Opt 与 QXS 数据集上取得 SOTA 并显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAR 到光学图像翻译在遥感解译中具有重要价值，但现有主流方法（扩散模型与 GAN）存在明显缺陷：扩散模型推理延迟高（需多步迭代采样），GAN 训练不稳定；此外生成的光学图像细节保真度低，常出现边缘模糊与细纹理丢失的问题。因此需要一种兼具高生成质量、高细节保真度与低推理延迟的翻译方法。

Method: 提出 ContraFM-S2O 流匹配框架：1）与扩散模型不同，训练阶段学习预测速度场（velocity field），推理阶段求解 ODE 而非 SDE，从而提升采样效率；2）核心创新在于用插值路径上的平均速度（average velocity）替代瞬时速度（instantaneous velocity），实现 SAR 到光学图像的一步翻译；3）引入对比学习（contrastive learning）约束以提升生成光学图像的质量与细节保真度。

Result: 在 SAR2Opt 和 QXS 两个数据集上进行实验，模型均达到当前最优（state-of-the-art）性能，优于所对比的基线方法；同时通过一步生成机制有效降低了推理延迟，兼顾了生成质量与效率。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于将流匹配范式引入 SAR 到光学图像翻译任务，通过平均速度实现一步生成并借助对比学习增强图像质量，在性能与效率上均优于扩散与 GAN 方法。可能的局限包括：抽象未给出定量指标（如 FID、PSNR、SSIM）与具体延迟数值，一步生成在复杂场景下的细节保持能力、以及跨数据集泛化性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: In recent years, diffusion models and GAN-based models have become the mainstream approaches for SAR-to-optical image translation, owing to their advantages, such as high-quality generation and stable training. However, they have shortcomings such as high inference latency and the generated optical images suffer from low detail fidelity, often resulting in blurred edges and loss of fine textures. Thus, we propose ContraFM-S2O, which is a flow matching-based model for SAR-to-optical image translation. Unlike conventional diffusion models, ContraFM-S2O learns to predict the velocity field in training and solves ODE instead of SDE during inference to improve the sampling efficiency. In addition, ContraFM-S2O replaces instantaneous velocity with average velocity along the interpolation path to realize one-step SAR-to-optical image translation and uses contrastive learning to improve the quality of the generated optical images. Experiments show our model achieves state-of-the-art on SAR2Opt and QXS datasets, outperforming baselines, and reduces inference latency via one-step generation.

</details>


### [74] [AxonSynth: Domain-Randomized Synthetic Data for Zero-Shot 3D Axon Segmentation in Light-Sheet Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31431)
*Edward Gaibor,Kyriaki-Margarita Bintsi,Carmen Luz Leiva Ureta,Zayneb Bellatif,Chiara Maffei,Wenze Li,Elizabeth Hillman,Yaël Balbastre,Anastasia Yendiki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AxonSynth，一个基于域随机化的合成数据框架，无需真实标注体数据即可训练 3D 轴突分割模型，实现合成到真实的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D 显微成像中的轴突精确分割对白质组织结构分析至关重要，但稠密真值标注成本极高。现有监督方法依赖目标域标注，当组织类型、物种、成像模态或采集条件变化时泛化能力差、易失效。因此需要一种能减少对人工轴突标注依赖的分割方案。

Method: AxonSynth 通过域随机化生成稠密合成轴突标签：引入反映真实纤维构型的朝向先验（orientation priors），并对密度、对比度、偏置场（bias fields）、模糊和噪声等成像因素进行随机化渲染，以模拟多样的采集条件。训练一个三分类 3D U-Net，预测背景、轴突鞘（axon sheath）与轴突内空间（intra-axonal space）。推理阶段为合成到真实的零样本迁移。

Result: 在 10 个留出的光片显微镜（LSM）图像块上评估，样本来自猕猴与人脑组织，使用三种轴突标记之一。对比基线为校准阈值分割和 Frangi 滤波，评价指标包括重叠度、校正检测、假阳性与拓扑指标。猕猴样本上 AxonSynth 取得最佳校正 Dice 与校正精度（0.826 与 0.851），优于阈值法（0.765、0.754）与 Frangi（0.685、0.762）。人脑样本上校正 Dice 与阈值法相当（0.857 vs 0.868），但连通分量计数误差从 22,504 降至 3,377。所有留出图像块中，AxonSynth 在 10/10 上降低连通分量误差，在 8/10 上降低欧拉示性数误差。

Conclusion: AxonSynth 表明，合成标签配合域随机化可显著降低对手工轴突标注的依赖，并支持合成到真实的 3D 分割迁移，尤其在拓扑一致性（连通分量、欧拉示性数）方面提升明显。其主要贡献在于无需真实训练标注的域随机化框架及拓扑友好的评估结果；局限在于仅在 LSM 模态的少量留出块上做零样本评估，未在真实数据上微调，且人脑样本的校正 Dice 未超过阈值法，泛化边界仍待验证。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of axons in 3D microscopy data is important for analyzing white-matter organization, but dense ground truth labels are expensive to obtain. Existing supervised axon segmentation methods rely on target-domain annotations and can be brittle when tissue type, species, modality, or acquisition conditions change. We present AxonSynth, a domain-randomized synthetic-data framework for training 3D axon segmentation models without manually annotated real training volumes. AxonSynth generates dense synthetic axon labels with orientation priors that reflect realistic fiber configurations and renders them with randomized density, contrast, bias fields, blur, and noise. A three-class 3D U-Net is trained to predict background, axon sheath and intra-axonal space. We evaluate zero-shot transfer on 10 held-out light-sheet microscopy (LSM) patches from macaque and human brain samples labeled with one of three axonal markers, comparing against calibrated thresholding and Frangi filtering using overlap, corrected detection, false-positive, and topology metrics. On macaque samples, AxonSynth achieved the best corrected Dice and corrected precision (0.826 and 0.851), compared with 0.765 and 0.754 for thresholding and 0.685 and 0.762 for Frangi. On human samples, corrected Dice was comparable to thresholding (0.857 vs. 0.868), while component-count error decreased from 22,504 to 3,377. Across all held-out patches, AxonSynth reduced component-count error in 10/10 patches and Euler-characteristic error in 8/10. These results show that synthetic-label domain randomization can reduce dependence on manual axon annotation while supporting synthetic-to-real 3D segmentation.

</details>


### [75] [Implicit Neural Representation for Hyperspectral Video Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31435)
*Alfredo Scalera,Paul Murray,Jaime Zabalza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于隐式神经表示（INR）的高光谱视频压缩方法，通过在现有RGB视频压缩模型基础上进行扩展，显著提升压缩质量与下游跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着快照式相机的发展，高光谱视频数据获取日益便捷，应用需求增长导致数据集规模不断扩大。然而，高光谱视频压缩研究仍处于早期阶段。传统做法（如高光谱图像压缩方法逐帧处理、基于PCA+JPEG2000）未能充分利用高光谱视频在光谱与时空维度上的冗余，压缩效率和下游任务可用性受限。因此需要探索更适合高光谱视频特性的压缩范式。

Method: 作者采用隐式神经表示（Implicit Neural Representation, INR）作为压缩候选方案，将视频/光谱数据建模为连续函数表示，而非离散像素存储。核心贡献是对一个已有的RGB视频压缩模型进行新颖扩展，使其适配高光谱数据（多光谱通道）。该方法旨在联合利用高光谱视频的空间、时间与光谱冗余，进行端到端的表示学习与压缩。

Result: 实验以HOT2026数据集为对象。在重建质量上，相较传统逐帧高光谱图像压缩方法，取得Bj&oslash;ntegaard Delta PSNR +4.99 dB 与 Bj&oslash;ntegaard Delta rate -88.88% 的提升。在低数据量条件下，相较基于PCA与JPEG2000的压缩方法，本方法在目标跟踪任务上取得更好的下游性能：跟踪AUC（area under the curve）最高提升23.42%，距离精度（distance precision）最高提升35.56%。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统探索将INR用于高光谱视频压缩，通过对RGB视频压缩模型的扩展，在率失真性能和下游目标跟踪任务上均取得显著提升，表明压缩质量与任务可用性可同时受益。局限在于方法继承自RGB模型、对具体基线选择与数据集（HOT2026）依赖较强，未来可探索更专用的光谱-时空联合表示与更广泛的评测。

Abstract: With the advent of snapshot cameras, hyperspectral video is becoming more readily available. In recent years, new applications have emerged which have led to increasingly larger datasets. However, hyperspectral video compression remains in the early stages. In this study, we explore the use of implicit neural representation as a candidate solution. We propose a novel extension of an existing RGB video compression model, achieving Bjøntegaard Delta PSNR gains of +4.99 dB and Bjøntegaard Delta rate of -88.88% compared to traditional hyperspectral image compression methods applied frame-by-frame. In addition to reconstruction quality, the effects on downstream task performance are measured in the form of object tracking success. Compared to video compressed with methods based on principal component analysis and JPEG2000 in low data regimes, our proposed method improves tracking area under the curve by up to 23.42% and distance precision by up to 35.56% on examples from the HOT2026 dataset.

</details>


### [76] [From Reward Signal to Visual Utility: A Controlled Audit of Medical VLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31450)
*Wang Jingxin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文对医学视觉语言模型（VLM）的后训练过程进行了实证与测量层面的审计，在PMC-VQA上用Qwen2.5-VL-3B受控实验，比较SFT、LM-only LoRA、扩展多模态适配、标准答案型GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）以及反事实证据（counterfactual evidence）目标，揭示"答案准确率提升"与"图像条件化决策/视觉证据利用"之间存在明显脱节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学VLM后训练通常仅以答案准确率（answer accuracy）作为评价标准，但准确率无法反映模型是否真正利用了图像证据做决策。论文动机是探究训练目标与准确率变化如何影响图像条件化（image-conditioned）决策行为，即模型是否因看到图像而改变回答（视觉获益/视觉敏感度），从而识别"优化活动、目标获取"与"有价值的留出集视觉行为"之间的差距。

Method: 在PMC-VQA上以Qwen2.5-VL-3B进行受控研究，比较四类后训练策略：(1) 仅语言模型部分的LoRA有监督微调（LM LoRA SFT）；(2) 扩展多模态适配范围（broader adaptation scopes）；(3) 标准答案型GRPO（group relative policy optimization，仅答案监督）；(4) 反事实证据目标（counterfactual evidence objective）。评测采用配对bootstrap置信区间、视觉获益事件（visual-benefit events，即正确图像下答对但错误图像下答错的样本）增减、图像敏感度（image sensitivity）变化，并做生成审计（generation audit），对比"规范选项打分路径"与"贪心生成路径"下token路径差异，从而在匹配训练剂量下比较证据目标与标准GRPO。

Result: 在2,000道干净测试题上：LM LoRA SFT使正确图像准确率变化+1.10个百分点（95%配对bootstrap CI：-0.85至+3.05），但同时视觉获益事件下降2.40个百分点、图像敏感度下降5.60个百分点；配对记录显示获得155个、丢失203个视觉获益事件。扩展适配范围的正确图像准确率低于LM LoRA SFT。标准GRPO产生混合奖励组与参数更新，但干净测试准确率变化不确定。生成审计发现规范选项分数（canonical option scores）可能沿与生成答案不同的token路径；改用贪心生成路径打分后，证据目标在训练集上改善，但其相对标准GRPO的增益在验证集上、匹配训练剂量下并不一致。样本级分析刻画了证据分数、决策间隔与生成答案在后训练中的变化。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是对医学VLM后训练进行实证与测量审计，指出答案准确率、优化活动与真正有用的留出集视觉行为之间存在系统性偏差，并揭示答案打分路径与生成路径的错配问题。其意义在于提示仅用准确率评估医学VLM后训练可能产生误导，需引入图像条件化/视觉证据利用层面的度量。局限包括：仅单一模型（Qwen2.5-VL-3B）与单一数据集（PMC-VQA），部分准确率差异置信区间跨越零、证据目标相对GRPO的增益在验证集上不一致，结论的普适性有待更广泛验证。

Abstract: Medical vision-language model (VLM) post-training is commonly evaluated through answer accuracy. We examine how changes in accuracy and training objectives relate to image-conditioned decisions in a controlled Qwen2.5-VL-3B study on PMC-VQA. We compare supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with low-rank adaptation (LoRA) restricted to the language model, expanded multimodal adaptation scopes, standard answer-only Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), and a counterfactual evidence objective. On 2,000 clean-test questions, language model LoRA SFT changes correct-image accuracy by +1.10 percentage points (95% paired bootstrap CI:-0.85 to +3.05), while visual-benefit events decrease by 2.40 points and image sensitivity decreases by 5.60 points. Paired records reveal 155 acquired and 203 lost visual-benefit events. Broader adaptation yields lower correct-image accuracy than language-model LoRA SFT. Standard GRPO produces mixed-reward groups and parameter updates, with an uncertain clean test accuracy change. A generation audit reveals that canonical option scores can follow a different token path from generated answers. With scores taken along the greedy generation path, the evidence target improves on the training set; its gains over standard GRPO remain inconsistent on validation data at matched training doses. Sample-level analyses trace how evidence scores, decision margins, and generated answers change during post-training. This empirical and measurement audit identifies gaps between optimization activity, target acquisition, and useful held-out visual behavior.

</details>


### [77] [Diagnosing the Sources of Compositional Failure in Vision-Language Models: A Controlled Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31456)
*Mona Gandhi,Cenk Merih Olcay,Kuan-Chieh Lo,Santiago Castro,Christopher W. Myers,Srinivasan Parthasarathy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 COMPASS（COMPositional Analysis of SkillS）评测框架，通过将组合式图像-标题与"分解后"的对应形式进行对比，直接量化视觉语言模型（VLM）在组合推理中的整合成本，并在技能层面（物体检测、属性绑定、关系推理）系统分析组合退化的多重成因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 VLM 在组合推理任务上表现不佳，社区普遍假设原因是模型难以整合多个组成成分（compositional binding），并据此设计训练干预；但这一假设从未被直接量化。现有组合性基准仅评估组合后的标题，无法将"联合推理的成本"与"在负载增加下识别单个成分的成本"分离开来，因而无法定位退化的真正来源。该问题对理解并改进视觉-语言模型的组合泛化能力至关重要。

Method: 提出 COMPASS 这一受控评测框架：核心手段是构建"组合标题 vs. 分解标题"的配对对照，在保持相同成分的前提下分离出组合整合的边际成本。分析分两层：1）粗粒度层，在 87K 图像-标题对上量化组合整合成本；2）细粒度层，将行为拆解为三项独立技能——物体检测（object detection）、属性绑定（attribute binding）、关系推理（relation reasoning），并使用面向技能的扰动（skill-targeted perturbations）在 274K 图像-标题对上分别施加负载变化，区分"自负载"（同类型基元数量增加）与"交叉负载"（其他类型基元数量增加）对性能的影响。评测覆盖标准对比式编码器、显式训练组合推理的模型以及非对比式架构。

Result: 在 87K 图像-标题对上，组合形式与分解形式之间的性能差确实存在，但仅能解释观察到退化的一部分，说明联合推理并非唯一成因。在 274K 图像-标题对上的技能级扰动实验揭示了高度一致的规律：每种技能主要随其自身基元类型数量（self-load）增加而退化，而跨类型负载（cross-load）效应以正向为主。这表明不同类型的基元（物体、属性、关系）彼此提供有用的语义定位上下文，而非相互干扰。该模式在标准对比编码器、组合推理专用模型和非对比架构中普遍成立。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：提出可分离归因的受控评测框架 COMPASS，首次直接量化组合整合成本，并证明组合退化由多个可分离因素构成，不能被简化为"联合推理"单一原因。其意义在于修正了社区对组合失败的传统假设，为后续诊断与改进提供了技能级分析工具。局限在于：分析基于图像-标题对与扰动式评测，属于行为层面的归因，未触及模型内部表征机制；结论的普适性仍受所评测的 VLM 集合与技能定义粒度限制。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) often struggle with compositional reasoning tasks, but the reasons for this underperformance remain unclear. A common hypothesis is that models struggle to integrate multiple components, leading to training interventions to improve compositional binding. However, this assumption has never been directly quantified. Existing benchmarks evaluate captions only in their composed form, making it impossible to separate the cost of joint reasoning from the cost of recognizing individual components under increasing load. We introduce COMPASS (COMPositional Analysis of SkillS), a controlled evaluation framework designed to isolate and measure the distinct factors underlying compositional failure. By comparing performance on composed captions with their decomposed counterparts , we directly quantify the cost of compositional integration across 87K image-caption pairs. Across multiple VLMs, this gap is real but partial, accounting for only part of the observed degradation. This motivates a finer-grained investigation into what additional factors govern model behavior. We analyze performance at the level of individual skills: object detection, attribute binding, and relation reasoning, using skill-targeted perturbations across 274K image-caption pairs. We find a consistent skill-specific pattern: each skill degrades primarily with the count of its own primitive type (self-load), while cross-load effects are predominantly positive, suggesting that primitives of different types provide useful grounding context. This pattern holds across standard contrastive encoders, explicitly trained compositional reasoning models, and non-contrastive architectures. These findings show that compositional degradation reflects multiple separable factors that cannot be reduced to joint reasoning alone.

</details>


### [78] [KneePreM: Towards 3D Knee MRI Foundation Models via Large-Scale Unlabeled Pretraining and Label-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31461)
*Xinxin Wang,Liam Hazan,Jing Li,Simona Rabinovici-Cohen,Xiaojuan Li,Mingrui Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KneePreM 是一个面向膝关节 MRI 的 3D 自监督预训练模型（基于 3D U-Net 的掩码自编码器 MAE），在 19,011 例未标注 OAI MRI 序列上预训练，通过下游微调在分类与分割任务上同时提升迁移性能与标签效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开膝关节 MRI 数据（如 OAI）中存在大量未标注影像，但未被充分利用。医学影像标注成本高、专家稀缺，导致下游分类/分割任务常面临标注数据不足的问题。已有通用医学自监督模型（如 SuPreM）并非针对膝关节解剖结构设计，迁移到膝关节任务时性能与标签效率有限。因此作者希望构建膝关节特异性的 3D 自监督模型，在少标注场景下仍能取得接近甚至超越全量标注基线的表现。

Method: 核心是 KneePreM，一个膝关节专用的 3D 自监督模型：(1) 预训练：采用 3D U-Net 结构的掩码自编码器（masked autoencoder），在来自 4,791 名受试者的 19,011 条未标注 OAI MRI 序列上进行自监督预训练，学习膝关节三维解剖表征。(2) 下游适配：对分类任务（fastMRI+ 二标签、APM 八目标）和分割任务（SKM-TEA、APM）进行微调，并系统评估全量数据与缩减数据（如 50%、70%、75%）下的迁移性能，考察标签效率。(3) 部署：实现基于 Model Context Protocol（MCP）接口的部署工作流，便于模型在下游应用中被调用。(4) 对比基线：随机初始化（random initialization）与通用医学自监督模型 SuPreM。

Result: 数据集与任务：预训练用 OAI 未标注 19,011 序列；下游包括 fastMRI+ 二标签分类（1,172 例检查）、APM 八目标分类（1,716 例检查）、SKM-TEA 分割（155 例检查）、APM 分割（25 例检查）。评价指标：balanced accuracy、F1、ROC AUC、PR AUC、Dice；统计上用 bootstrap 置信区间与配对 bootstrap 检验（分类）、Wilcoxon 符号秩检验（分割）。主要结果：KneePreM 在全量数据下对 fastMRI+ 与 APM 分类的宏 ROC AUC 均显著高于两基线（p < .001）。标签效率上，fastMRI+ 仅用 50% 数据即达 ROC AUC 0.722，超过两基线的全量数据表现；APM 用 70% 数据达 ROC AUC 0.740，追平全量随机初始化基线并优于 SuPreM。分割方面，SKM-TEA 用 70% 数据 Dice 0.838，超过全量随机基线（0.835）及同预算对比方法；APM 分割用 75% 数据 Dice 0.746，超过全量随机基线（0.731）及同预算对比方法。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出膝关节特异性 3D 自监督预训练模型 KneePreM，证明领域特异的 3D MAE 预训练能在分类与分割任务上同时提升迁移性能与标签效率，尤其在标注数据受限时优势明显（部分任务以 50%–75% 数据超越全量标注基线），并通过 MCP 接口给出可用的部署方案。意义在于为医学影像中未标注数据的利用与低标注成本建模提供了有效范式。局限方面：预训练与评估仅基于 OAI 单一来源数据与有限的下游检查量（如 APM 分割仅 25 例），跨中心、跨扫描仪泛化性尚不明确；模型面向膝关节，通用性有限；摘要未报告推理效率与计算开销等部署性能指标。

Abstract: Background: Large volumes of unlabeled knee MRI scans are available across repositories but remain insufficiently leveraged. We developed KneePreM, a knee-specific 3D self-supervised model, and evaluated transfer and label efficiency for classification and segmentation. Methods: A 3D U-Net masked autoencoder was pretrained on 19,011 unlabeled Osteoarthritis Initiative (OAI) MRI series from 4,791 participants. Downstream fine-tuning used full and reduced training sets for fastMRI+ two-label classification (1,172 examinations), Arthroscopic Partial Meniscectomy (APM) eight-target classification (1,716 examinations), SKM-TEA segmentation (155 examinations), and APM segmentation (25 examinations). Baselines were random initialization and SuPreM. Deployment workflow was implemented with a Model Context Protocol interface. Evaluation metrics included balanced accuracy, F1 score, ROC AUC, PR AUC, and Dice score. Statistical analysis used bootstrap confidence intervals and paired bootstrap tests for classification and Wilcoxon signed-rank tests for segmentation. Results: KneePreM achieved higher full-data macro ROC AUC than both baselines for fastMRI+ and APM (all p < .001). For fastMRI+ classification, KneePreM achieved a ROC AUC of 0.722 using 50% of the training data, exceeding both full-data baselines. In APM classification, KneePreM reached a ROC AUC of 0.740 with 70% of the data, matching the full-data random baseline and outperforming SuPreM. For SKM-TEA segmentation, its 70%-data Dice of 0.838 exceeded the full-data random baseline (0.835) and both same-budget comparators. In APM segmentation, its 75%-data Dice of 0.746 exceeded the full-data random baseline (0.731) and both same-budget comparators. Conclusion: KneePreM improves transfer performance and label efficiency across knee MRI classification and segmentation tasks, particularly when labeled training data are limited.

</details>


### [79] [SatNav: A Scalable Benchmark for Long-Horizon UAV Vision-Language Navigation from Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31507)
*Jiajun Jiang,Chunliang Hua,Zichun Chen,Yanxing Wu,Zeyuan Yang,Jie Song,Xiao Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SatNav是一个基于高分辨率卫星影像构建的可扩展、长时程城市级无人机视觉语言导航（UAV VLN）基准，包含18个城市59个场景的11.8万条episode，并提出可切换记忆模块的模块化框架SwiftVLN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市UAV视觉语言导航要求智能体在广阔城市空间中跟随指令，天然需要长时程记忆与地理空间定位能力。然而现有基准依赖昂贵的重建三维资产，导致地理多样性受限、episode规模难以扩展，从而制约了长时程VLN的研究。该工作旨在用低成本、易获取的高分辨率卫星影像替代3D重建，构建可大规模扩展的城市级长时程导航基准。

Method: 该工作提出SatNav基准：以高分辨率卫星影像裁片近似无人机正下视（nadir）视图作为视觉观测，面向城市级导航任务。通过自动化的“线索到episode（cue-to-episode）”流水线，从18个城市、59个场景构建11.8万条episode，平均轨迹长度379米。为测试长时程记忆与地理空间推理，定义三类任务族：Boundary（环路进度跟踪）、Landmark（基于地标的空间定位）、Route（带计数线索的路径跟随）。此外提出SwiftVLN——一个具有可切换记忆组件的模块化框架，用于系统性记忆设计消融实验。

Result: 在SatNav上对经典VLN智能体与基于大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）的近期智能体进行评测，结果显示城市级导航仍具挑战性。通过SwiftVLN进行系统性的记忆设计消融实验，分析了不同记忆组件的作用。卫星到无人机的迁移实验表明，在卫星数据上训练的导航模型能够在真实飞行UAV观测上运行，验证了SatNav的实际相关性。项目页面：https://eku127.github.io/SatNav/。

Conclusion: 主要贡献为：1）提出基于卫星影像的可扩展城市级长时程UAV VLN基准SatNav，大幅降低成本并提升地理多样性与规模；2）定义三类压力测试长时程记忆与地理推理的任务族；3）提出模块化框架SwiftVLN并开展记忆设计消融；4）验证卫星训练到真实UAV飞行的迁移可行性。意义在于为城市级VLN提供了低成本、可扩展的评测平台。潜在局限可能包括：卫星正下视裁片与真实UAV视角之间存在域差异（如视角畸变、动态场景、光照变化），以及城市级导航在现有智能体上性能仍不足。

Abstract: Urban uncrewed aerial vehicle (UAV) vision-language navigation (VLN) requires agents to follow instructions across extended urban spaces, inherently demanding long-term memory and geospatial grounding. However, scaling existing benchmarks remains difficult because of their reliance on costly reconstructed 3D assets, limiting geographic diversity and episode scale. To address this, we introduce SatNav, a scalable, long-horizon UAV VLN benchmark built from high-resolution satellite imagery. SatNav targets city-level navigation missions and uses satellite crops as approximations of UAV nadir views for visual observations. Through an automated cue-to-episode pipeline, SatNav constructs 118K episodes from 59 scenes across 18 cities, with an average trajectory length of 379 m. To stress-test long-horizon memory and geospatial reasoning, SatNav defines three task families: Boundary, Landmark, and Route, targeting loop progress tracking, landmark-based spatial grounding, and route following with counting cues. Benchmarking classical VLN agents and recent agents based on large vision-language models (LVLMs) on SatNav shows that city-scale navigation remains challenging. We further introduce SwiftVLN, a modular framework with switchable memory components, and conduct systematic memory-design ablations. Finally, satellite-to-UAV transfer experiments show that satellite-trained navigation models can operate on real-flight UAV observations, showing the practical relevance of SatNav. Our project page: https://eku127.github.io/SatNav/

</details>


### [80] [ClearGS: Reliability-Aware Gaussian Splatting from Handheld Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31509)
*Xuanzhi Liu,Xinyi Wu,Hang Pan,Wensi Huang,Zhenyao Wu,Ruize Han,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ClearGS 提出一种针对手持视频的 3D 高斯泼溅（3DGS）重建框架，通过"可靠性感知视角分配（RVA）"进行分级监督加权、并利用"渲染引导的视频内修复（RIVR）"恢复模糊/失真细节，在无配对清晰监督与无干净参考条件下实现 SOTA 重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手持视频重建 3DGS 面临两大核心痛点：（1）视角覆盖不均匀，部分轨迹区域帧稀疏或质量差；（2）帧质量参差不齐，存在运动模糊、失真等退化。现有方法多采用"二元决策"（选帧/弃帧），要么丢失轨迹覆盖，要么引入退化监督，导致重建出现伪影、细节缺失和几何不稳定。该问题的重要性在于：实际采集场景往往无法获得干净的配对参考（sharp GT）或匹配的理想视角，因此需要一种能容忍退化、同时保持覆盖度的鲁棒监督与修复机制。

Method: 方法包含三个核心模块：
1) **Reliability-aware View Allocation (RVA)**：不做二元选帧，而是为每帧分配"分级"的原始监督权重，依据三方面因素——外观可靠性（appearance reliability）、退化风险（degradation risk）、几何效用（geometric utility）；同时"弱激活"被抑制但轨迹上有用的帧，以维持轨迹覆盖度。
2) **Render-Guided In-Video Restoration (RIVR)**：由于加权无法恢复模糊/失真丢失的细节，利用当前 3DGS 渲染图作为"位姿对齐的结构候选"，配合一个冻结的无参考修复专家（no-reference restoration expert）对对应原始视频观测进行修复，全程不需要干净参考图；再用无参考感知分数在"渲染图/修复观测/高频融合候选"三者中选择。
3) **Full-Trajectory Repair Consolidation**：对已接受的修复进行全轨迹回访与整合，防止早期引入的细节在后续优化中被稀释或丢失。
整体形成"分级加权监督 + 渲染引导修复 + 全局整合"的闭环优化流程。

Result: 在 GS2E 与 GSOTM 两个数据集上进行评估，ClearGS 取得 SOTA 整体性能；在多数退化设置下 CLIP-IQA 与 MUSIQ 指标一致提升，LPIPS 一致降低。关键在于：该方法无需成对的清晰监督（paired sharp supervision），也不需要匹配的干净参考图，仍能达到上述效果，验证了其在真实退化手持视频场景中的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：（1）提出分级视角分配替代二元选帧，兼顾轨迹覆盖与监督质量；（2）提出无参考、渲染引导的视频内修复流程，摆脱对干净参考的依赖；（3）通过全轨迹修复整合保持细节一致性，在多个基准上取得 SOTA。潜在局限：无参考感知分数本身存在不确定性，可能影响修复候选选择；方法依赖预训练修复专家质量，泛化性待验证；在极端退化或大范围视角缺失时几何一致性仍可能受限。

Abstract: We present ClearGS for 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) from handheld videos with uneven viewpoint coverage and mixed frame quality. Rather than selecting frames with binary decisions, ClearGS uses Reliability-aware View Allocation (RVA) to assign graded raw-supervision weights based on appearance reliability, degradation risk, and geometric utility, while weakly reactivating useful suppressed frames to maintain trajectory coverage. Since weighting cannot restore details lost to blur or distortion, ClearGS further introduces Render-Guided In-Video Restoration (RIVR). The current 3DGS render provides a pose-aligned structural candidate, a frozen no-reference restoration expert restores the corresponding raw video observation without any clean reference image, and no-reference perceptual scores select among the render, restored observation, and high-frequency fused candidate. ClearGS then applies Full-Trajectory Repair Consolidation to revisit accepted repairs and preserve details introduced early. On GS2E and GSOTM, ClearGS achieves state-of-the-art overall performance, with consistent CLIP-IQA and MUSIQ gains and LPIPS reductions in most degradation settings, without paired sharp supervision or matched clean references.

</details>


### [81] [Forensic Twins: Self-Supervised Residual Learning for AI-Generated Image Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31514)
*Javier Muñoz-Haro,Ruben Tolosana,Ruben Vera-Rodriguez,Aythami Morales,Julian Fierrez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: "Forensic Twins"：一种自监督残差学习（SSRL）框架，通过在冻结的现成取证残差提取器上生成空间不相交的双视图，将冗余消减目标（如自蒸馏/对比学习）导向图像采集流程中的稳定指纹（相机/成像管道噪声），从而完全在真实图像上训练、无需见到任何AI生成样本，即可实现跨未见生成器的零样本AI图像检测与来源归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测器通常需要在训练时见过所有目标生成架构样本，导致每当新生成模型（GAN、扩散模型、商用系统）出现时性能急剧下降，泛化性差。近期尝试以自监督预训练缓解该问题，但标准自监督框架与取证任务存在内在冲突：常规数据增强（裁剪、颜色抖动、模糊、翻转等）会破坏图像成像过程中的微观统计特征（micro-statistics/图像形成指纹），而这些微弱痕迹恰恰是区分真实与生成图像的关键。因此需要一个自监督预训练范式，使其 pretext task 天然适配取证信号而非语义信号。

Method: 核心思想是"抑制宏观内容可得性、迫使模型学习成像管道共性信号"。具体：每张图像先经一个冻结的、现成的取证残差提取器映射，得到残差表示（剥离语义内容、保留成像痕迹）；随后从该残差图中采样两个空间上互不重叠（无共享像素）的裁剪视图。由于两视图无像素重叠，语义结构极其有限，无法依靠语义对齐，冗余消减目标的主导公共信号只剩下来自图像采集管道的稳态指纹（stationary fingerprint）。模型据此学习残差表示。关键设计：(1) 使用残差域而非RGB域做自监督；(2) 双视图空间不相交以消除语义捷径；(3) 训练集仅含真实图像，全程不接触任何AI生成图像。检测阶段更进一步：仅用真实图像嵌入离线拟合一个高斯混合模型（GMM），即可将其转化为零样本检测器，无需任何生成样本或微调。

Result: 所提方法在AI生成来源归因任务上达到56.61%准确率，较此前最优零样本方法高6.13%，同时延迟降低约375倍，体现极高效率。更关键的是，将 Forensic Twins 提取的真实图像嵌入离线拟合 GMM 后，作为零样本检测器在27个未见AI生成器（涵盖GAN、扩散模型及商用系统）上取得97.99% AUC，达到当时的state-of-the-art水平。评估以准确率与AUC为主要指标，强调跨生成器泛化与推理效率。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出一种面向取证任务的自监督残差学习范式，通过残差域、空间不相交双视图及仅真实图像训练，绕开了标准自监督增强破坏微观统计的问题，并借助轻量GMM实现强零样本检测。其意义在于：无需预先掌握目标生成器即可泛化至未见架构，且延迟极低，利于实际部署。潜在局限包括：检测/归因依赖成像管道指纹，若生成图像经后处理或压缩、或训练真实图像与测试域分布差异较大时可能削弱效果；56.61%的归因准确率仍有较大提升空间；对残差提取器选择的敏感性需进一步验证（论文称将公开代码、权重与数据划分）。

Abstract: Detectors of AI-generated images are typically trained using samples from all Generative AI architectures they must catch, and struggle as soon as a new architecture emerges. Recent approaches have explored self-supervised pre-training as an alternative solution, yet standard frameworks work against the forensic task, e.g., their augmentations overwrite the micro-statistics of image formation. This paper introduces Forensic Twins, a Self-Supervised Residual Learning (SSRL) framework whose pretext task suppresses macroscopic content availability. Each image is mapped through a frozen, off-the-shelf forensic residual extractor, from which two spatially disjoint crops are drawn. Sharing no pixel, the two views retain minimal semantic structure to align, leaving a redundancy-reduction objective with a predominant common signal: the stationary fingerprint of the image acquisition pipeline. Additionally, Forensic Twins is trained exclusively on real images; no AI-generated image is observed at any stage. Experiments show that Forensic Twins attributes AI generator sources with 56.61% accuracy, i.e., 6.13% above the previous state-of-the-art zero-shot method at 375x lower latency. We also demonstrate that fitting a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) offline using only the real image embeddings extracted from Forensic Twins turns it into a state-of-the-art zero-shot detector, reaching 97.99% AUC across 27 unseen AI generators, including GANs, diffusion models and commercial systems. Code, weights and exact splits will be made publicly available

</details>


### [82] [Structured Reasoning Agentic Framework for Interpretable Critical View of Safety Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31524)
*Qing Xu,Yuxiang Luo,Zhen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReasonCVS，一个基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的结构化推理智能体框架，将腹腔镜胆囊切除术中的"关键安全视野"（CVS）评估从整体黑箱预测重构为细粒度解剖学验证任务。该框架通过解剖场景图抽象（ASGA）组织解剖实体及其空间关系，并借助经理由蒸馏微调的LLM作为中心决策者，调用VLM子准则验证器逐项评估，在Endoscapes-CVS201基准上取得68.1% mAP，并输出可追溯的临床推理链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腹腔镜胆囊切除术中，肝胆囊三角解剖结构复杂，胆管损伤风险高，因此CVS的准确评估对计算机辅助干预至关重要。现有方法多将CVS评估视为端到端的整体预测任务，直接将视觉特征映射到准则级标签，属于黑箱范式，缺乏对解剖关系的显式推理能力，导致可解释性差、组合泛化能力受限。如何让模型像外科医生一样进行结构化的解剖学推理，而非单纯模式匹配，是本文的核心动机。

Method: ReasonCVS是一个结构化推理的智能体框架，核心包含三部分：（1）解剖场景图抽象（ASGA）：将解剖实体及其空间关系组织为结构化图表示，为显式推理提供符号化中介；（2）理由感知推理智能体（Rationale-Aware Reasoning Agent）：以经理由蒸馏（rationale distillation）微调的LLM作为严格中心决策者，负责解析场景图、协调验证流程并综合证据；（3）VLM驱动的子准则验证器（Sub-criterion Verifier）：作为专用感知工具，对单个CVS子准则独立评估，将感知结果回传给决策者。智能体通过校准的软推理（calibrated soft reasoning）融合分布式的工具观测结果，最终给出判定及可追溯的临床理由，实现"感知-推理-决策"的解耦。

Result: 在Endoscapes-CVS201基准数据集上进行实验，ReasonCVS取得68.1% mAP，优于现有最先进方法，同时能够提供准则级别的可解释解释。实验结果表明，将CVS评估分解为显式解剖验证并结合场景图结构化表示与LLM推理，可同时提升评估准确性和可解释性，为可靠的手术评估提供支持。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于提出结构化推理智能体框架ReasonCVS，通过ASGA场景图抽象、理由蒸馏微调的LLM决策者和VLM子准则验证器，将CVS评估从黑箱整体预测转化为可解释的细粒度解剖验证，并输出可追溯临床理由，在Endoscapes-CVS201上达到68.1% mAP。其意义在于提升手术评估的可解释性与组合泛化能力。可能的局限包括：依赖多个VLM/LLM组件的级联带来推理成本与延迟，场景图构建质量对最终性能敏感，以及仅在单一CVS任务与数据集上验证，跨术式与跨中心的泛化性尚待评估。

Abstract: Surgical scene understanding is critical for computer-assisted intervention, yet laparoscopic cholecystectomy remains challenged by the complex anatomy of the hepatocystic triangle and the risk of bile duct injury.
