Existing methods for Critical View of Safety (CVS) assessment typically treat it as a holistic prediction task, mapping visual features directly to criterion-level labels. This black-box paradigm lacks explicit reasoning about anatomical relationships, limiting both interpretability and compositional generalization. To address this, we propose ReasonCVS, a structured reasoning agentic framework empowered by Vision-Language Models (VLMs) that decomposes CVS assessment into explicit, fine-grained anatomical verification. Specifically, we devise an Anatomical Scene Graph Abstraction (ASGA) that organizes anatomical entities and their spatial relationships into a structured representation. To operationalize this, we introduce a Rationale-Aware Reasoning Agent, powered by a Large Language Model (LLM) fine-tuned via rationale distillation. Functioning as a strict central decision-maker, it invokes VLM-driven Sub-criterion Verifier as a specialized perceptual tool to parse the graph and independently evaluate individual sub-criteria. Through calibrated soft reasoning, this agent synthesizes the tool-gathered distributed observations, yielding a final verdict alongside a traceable clinical rationale. Extensive experiments on the Endoscapes-CVS201 benchmark demonstrate that ReasonCVS achieves superior performance (68.1\% mAP) over state-of-the-art while providing interpretable, criterion-level explanations for reliable surgical assessment.

</details>


### [83] [Region-Level Black-Box Defense Against Stealthy Embedding-Space Backdoors in CLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31558)
*Ahmed Abdelnaby,Mohamed Elmahallawy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLIPGuard 是一种轻量级、完全黑盒的防御方法，用于缓解 CLIP 视觉编码器中的"嵌入空间后门攻击"。它通过度量图像分段的嵌入扰动来定位恶意区域，并仅对可疑分段进行语义修复（inpainting），在保持干净精度的同时将攻击成功率降至最低 1.05%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP 因强大的迁移性和零样本能力成为主流视觉主干，但其联合嵌入空间易受后门攻击。与操纵分类 logits 的传统后门不同，嵌入空间后门直接污染表示，仅需极低毒化比例（poisoning ratio）即可植入隐蔽触发，难以检测且极具破坏性。现有防御方法大多依赖模型参数、梯度、logits 或干净验证集，这些在真实黑盒部署中往往无法获取；而已有的黑盒方法在定位小型或分布外（OOD）触发时精度不足。因此作者提出一种无需访问模型内部信息的黑盒防御方案。

Method: CLIPGuard 采用完全黑盒设置，仅依赖对 CLIP 编码器的输入输出查询。核心思路是：将输入图像划分为若干分段（segment），逐一度量各分段对整体嵌入表示的扰动（segment-wise embedding perturbation），从而定位潜在的恶意触发区域。随后仅对判定为可疑的分段进行语义修复（semantic inpainting），用与周围语义一致的生成内容替换可疑区域，而保留其余良性视觉内容。这种"选择性净化"策略避免了对整幅图像的重建，从而在去除触发的同时维持了干净的视觉信息与图文对齐质量。方法强调轻量化，无需参数、梯度、logits 或干净验证数据。

Result: 在 STL-10 和 ImageNet 数据集上，针对多种触发家族（包括 BadCLIP、BadNets、blended、patch-based 以及 typographic 攻击）进行了广泛实验。主要指标为攻击成功率（ASR）与干净准确率（clean accuracy）。结果显示 CLIPGuard 将 ASR 降至最低 1.05%，同时保持干净准确率高达 86.34%，持续优于现有黑盒防御方法（如 CleanCLIP 与 CleanerCLIP）。代码已开源。

Conclusion: CLIPGuard 提出了一种轻量、无需白盒访问的黑盒防御框架，通过分段嵌入扰动检测与语义修复，有效缓解 CLIP 嵌入空间后门攻击，并在多种触发类型与数据集上取得优于现有方法的性能。其意义在于贴合现实黑盒部署场景，同时兼顾防御效果与干净精度。潜在局限包括对语义修复生成质量的依赖，以及分段扰动度量在更复杂或自适应攻击下的鲁棒性有待进一步验证（论文摘要未明确说明）。

Abstract: Contrastive Language--Image Pretraining (CLIP) has emerged as a dominant vision backbone due to its strong transferability and zero-shot capabilities. However, recent studies reveal a critical vulnerability: embedding-space backdoor attacks. By poisoning only a tiny fraction of image--text pairs, adversaries can implant stealthy triggers that induce targeted shifts in CLIP's joint embedding space. Unlike conventional backdoors that manipulate classifier logits, these attacks corrupt representations directly, making them highly effective under extremely low poisoning ratios and difficult to detect. Existing defenses require access to model parameters, gradients, logits, or clean validation data---assumptions that rarely hold in realistic black-box deployments. Moreover, current black-box methods struggle to accurately localize small or out-of-distribution triggers. We propose CLIPGuard, a lightweight and fully black-box defense specifically designed to mitigate embedding-space backdoors in CLIP encoders. CLIPGuard identifies malicious regions by measuring segment-wise embedding perturbations and selectively purifies only suspicious segments via semantic inpainting, preserving benign visual content and alignment quality. Extensive experiments on STL-10, ImageNet, and diverse trigger families---including BadCLIP, BadNets, blended, patch-based, and typographic attacks---demonstrate that CLIPGuard reduces attack success rates to as low as 1.05% while maintaining clean accuracy up to 86.34%, consistently outperforming existing black-box defenses, including CleanCLIP and CleanerCLIP. Our code is available https://github.com/wsu-cyber-security-lab-ai/CLIPGuard.git

</details>


### [84] [OC-GS: Gaussian Splatting for Irregular Turntable Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31572)
*Jae Joong Lee,Bedrich Benes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OC-GS（Orbit-Consistent Gaussian Splatting）提出一种以物体为中心的高斯泼溅方法，在转台稀疏、不规则拍摄条件下，同时优化每张图像的视角角度与共享的相机-旋转轴-枢轴运动模型，实现对不规则转台捕捉的高质量物体重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转台（turntable）拍摄是物体重建的常见方式，但实际中存在两大问题：旋转速度不均匀（uneven rotation）和丢帧（dropped frames），使得"等角度间隔"这一常规假设失效。此外稀疏捕获下可用的视角数量很少。现有无位姿（pose-free）高斯泼溅方法依赖图像估计角度或置固定角度的假设，在不规则捕获时误差被放大，导致重建质量下降。因此需要一种能够在共享运动模型约束下、对不确定的每帧角度进行联合优化来重建物体的方法。

Method: OC-GS 的核心是以物体为中心的高斯泼溅框架，维护一个共享的相机、旋转轴与枢轴（pivot）组成的运动模型。关键创新是"轨道一致性精化"（orbit-consistent refinement）：在训练中联合优化图像导出的几何与各图像的角度，而非将图像估计的角度固定。角度先验由图像派生初始化，随后在共享运动模型约束下被细化。方法通过消融验证了图像导出的角度初始化与共享运动模型两个组件的贡献，并在共享训练器（shared trainer）下对比"精化角度"与"固定估计角度"的效果。

Result: 在渲染物体上，采用 12、8、6 个不规则间隔视角时，OC-GS 的平均前景 PSNR 分别为 21.26、19.36、15.83 dB，在每种设置下均超过所评估的四个无位姿高斯泼溅基线。在共享训练器下，细化图像估计角度相比固定这些估计值，平均前景 PSNR 提升 7.88 dB。消融研究表明图像导出角度初始化与共享运动模型均有贡献。在真实捕捉数据上，OC-GS 的精化使平均前景 PSNR 提升 0.70 dB。

Conclusion: OC-GS 证明在共享运动模型内对不确定角度进行精化能够显著改善稀疏、不规则转台捕获的物体重建，并在多种稀疏视角下优于现有无位姿高斯泼溅基线。其意义在于放宽了转台重建对等角度假设的依赖，提升了真实场景下的鲁棒性。局限方面：评估主要基于前景 PSNR 指标，真实数据增益相对较小（0.70 dB），且方法依赖转台（单轴旋转）捕获假设，对更一般的自由视角或复杂运动场景的适用性未在摘要中说明。

Abstract: Uneven rotation and dropped frames make equal-angle assumptions unreliable for turntable reconstruction. We present OC-GS, an object-centric Gaussian splatting that refines each image's angle while maintaining a shared camera, rotation axis, and pivot. This orbit-consistent refinement jointly optimizes image-derived geometry and angles to reconstruct objects from sparse, irregular captures. On rendered objects with 12, 8, and 6 irregularly spaced views, OC-GS achieves mean foreground PSNR scores of 21.26, 19.36, and 15.83dB, respectively, exceeding all four evaluated pose-free Gaussian splatting baselines in each condition. Under a shared trainer, refining image-estimated angles improves mean foreground PSNR by 7.88dB over keeping those estimates fixed. An ablation study shows that both image-derived angle initialization and the shared motion model contribute to the improvement. On real captures, OC-GS's refinement increases mean foreground PSNR by 0.70dB. Results show that refining uncertain angles within a shared motion model improves reconstruction from sparse, irregular turntable captures.

</details>


### [85] [How Far Can INRs Go? Cross-Domain Parameter-efficient INR-Based Semantic Segmentation for Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31573)
*Ziyao Shang,Pouya Sadeghi,Letian Jiang,Alexander Wong,Sirisha Rambhatla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了隐式神经表示（INR）用于脑MRI跨域分割的机理、参数缩放行为与泛化能力，发现INR优势主要体现在低参数与弱数据增强场景，并提出分层聚合多层特征的HierINRSeg架构，在域内和域外均显著超越MetaSeg基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学图像分割在实际部署中面临标注量有限、内存受限和跨中心分布偏移等挑战。近期INR作为轻量级语义分割替代方案，能以远少于传统架构的参数取得有竞争力的性能，但其工作机制、参数缩放规律以及域泛化能力尚缺乏深入理解。论文以跨域脑MRI分割为背景，旨在厘清INR分割何时有效、为何有效，为模型选择与后续研究提供实证指导。

Method: 作者在低参数区间内对INR分割与传统流程进行对比研究，涵盖域内与域外设置，并改变参数量与数据增强强度以观察性能缩放行为。在表示分析方面，他们检查INR隐藏特征中语义信息的编码方式，发现与分割相关的互补结构分散在多个INR层中。基于这一发现提出HierINRSeg：一种分层INR架构，通过聚合多层表示来增强鲁棒性与泛化能力。实验以MetaSeg这一近期强INR分割基线作为主要对比对象。

Result: 实验在跨域脑MRI分割任务上开展，评价指标为Dice。结果显示：INR模型并非随参数预算增加而单调变好，其优势在低参数和有限增强设置下最为明显；而基于U-Net的模型则更受益于更大容量与标准增强。HierINRSeg持续优于MetaSeg，域内测试集Dice平均提升5.6个百分点，域外平均提升8.2个百分点，表明多层特征聚合有效改善了鲁棒性与跨域泛化。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于：系统刻画了INR分割的参数缩放与域泛化特性，揭示了语义信息在多INR层中分布式编码的现象，并据此提出多层聚合的HierINRSeg架构，在跨域脑MRI分割上取得显著提升。其意义在于明确了INR分割的适用条件并为模型选择提供指导。局限在于研究范围集中于脑MRI与特定INR/MetaSeg基线，结论在其他模态、任务与更大参数规模下的普适性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Biomedical image segmentation is central to medical image analysis, but practical deployment often faces limited annotations, memory constraints, and cross-site distribution shifts. Implicit Neural Representations (INRs) have recently emerged as a lightweight alternative for semantic segmentation, achieving competitive performance with substantially fewer parameters than conventional architectures. However, the mechanisms, scaling behavior, and domain generalization abilities of INR-based segmentation remain insufficiently understood. In this work, we study these questions in the context of cross-domain brain MRI segmentation. We analyze INR-based segmentation across low-parameter regimes, comparing it with conventional pipelines in both in-domain and out-of-domain settings. Surprisingly, we find that INR-based models do not simply improve with increasing parameter budget. Their advantage is most pronounced under low-parameter and limited-augmentation settings, while U-Net-based models benefit more from larger capacity and standard augmentation. We also investigate how INRs encode semantic information in their hidden features and show that complementary segmentation-relevant structure is distributed across multiple INR layers. Building on this insight, we introduce HierINRSeg, a hierarchical INR-based architecture that aggregates multi-layer representations for improved robustness and generalization. Extensive experiments show that HierINRSeg consistently outperforms MetaSeg, a strong recent INR-based segmentation baseline, with an average improvement of 5.6 percentage points in Dice for the in-domain test set and 8.2 percentage points out-of-domain. Overall, our analysis identifies the conditions under which INR-based segmentation is most effective, providing concrete guidance for model selection and future research.

</details>


### [86] [GraphWrit3R: End-to-End 3D Scene Graph Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31595)
*Luka Milivojevic,Nikola Popovic,Sayan Deb Sarkar,Sebastian Koch,Iro Armeni,Luc Van Gool,Danda Pani Paudel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GraphWrit3R 提出一种端到端的 3D 场景图生成方法,直接以 3D 点云、Gaussian Splats 或两者组合为输入,输出结构化 JSON 格式的完整场景图。方法将两种模态投影到共享体素网格,通过新颖的逐体素对比对齐损失融合,并由大语言模型解码,在 3DSSG 基准上取得对象类别、谓词和三重组召回率的最优性能,且推理时无需 ground-truth 物体标注,同时支持开放词汇查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D 场景图通过编码物体、其语义属性以及物体间的空间与功能关系,为复杂环境提供结构化表示,对机器人感知、具身智能等任务具有重要价值。然而现有 3D 场景图生成方法存在若干根本性缺陷:(1) 依赖复杂的多阶段流水线与显式中间表示,系统脆弱且易发生误差累积传播;(2) 在推理阶段假定可获取 ground-truth 物体标注,与真实场景不符;(3) 要么依赖专有模型难以开源部署,要么推理速度过慢。作者希望提出一种简单、端到端、可开源且无需 GT 标注的方法来规避上述限制。

Method: GraphWrit3R 是一个端到端方法,输入为 3D 点云、Gaussian Splats 或二者组合,直接输出结构化的 JSON 场景图脚本,列出所有物体、语义属性及其相互关系。多模态输入的设计目的是提升通用性,使单一套权重可应对多样场景:点云输入经 Sonata 编码,Gaussian Splat 输入经 Chorus 编码,两种模态被投影到共享的体素网格,并通过一种新颖的逐体素对比对齐损失(per-voxel contrastive alignment loss)进行融合,随后由大语言模型(LLM)解码生成场景图。得益于 LLM 的使用,方法天然支持开放词汇(open-vocabulary)查询。作者还分析了不同输入模态配置、对比损失形式以及 token 融合策略的影响。

Result: 方法在 3DSSG 基准上进行评估,在对象类别(object class)、谓词(predicate)和三重组(triplet)召回率三项指标上均达到 state-of-the-art,且性能超过了那些在推理阶段依赖 ground-truth 物体标注的方法。作者进一步提供了定性结果,并分析了不同输入模态配置、对比损失公式与 token 融合策略的效果。整体而言,论文重点在于召回率类指标上的优越性与端到端、免 GT 标注的特性。

Conclusion: GraphWrit3R 的主要贡献在于:(1) 提出一个简单、端到端的 3D 场景图生成框架,直接输出结构化 JSON 场景图;(2) 通过 Sonata/Chorus 编码与共享体素网格上的逐体素对比对齐损失,实现点云与 Gaussian Splat 的多模态融合,单一权重适配多种输入;(3) 借助 LLM 解码器支持开放词汇查询,并摆脱推理阶段的 ground-truth 物体标注需求;(4) 在 3DSSG 上取得对象类别、谓词与三重组召回率的 SOTA。其意义在于简化了此前脆弱的多阶段流水线,提升了实际部署可行性。潜在局限可能包括:对 LLM 解码的依赖带来计算开销、评估仅限于 3DSSG 单一基准、以及多模态融合与对比损失超参配置的敏感性(作者已通过对多种配置的分析部分涉及)。

Abstract: 3D scene graphs provide a structured representation of complex environments by encoding objects, their semantic attributes, and the spatial and functional relationships between them. Current approaches for 3D scene graph generation suffer from several fundamental limitations. They rely on complex multi-stage pipelines with explicit intermediate representations, making systems fragile and prone to error propagation. They assume access to ground-truth object annotations during inference, which deviates from real-world scenarios. They depend on proprietary models, hindering open-source deployment, or incur prohibitively slow inference. We present GraphWrit3R, a simple end-to-end method that takes a 3D point cloud, Gaussian Splats, or a combination of both as input, and directly outputs a complete scene graph as a structured JSON script. The graph lists all objects, their semantic attributes, and the relationships between them, while avoiding all of the above mentioned limitations. The choice of multiple input modalities is purely for versatility, allowing a single set of weights to handle diverse scenarios. Point cloud inputs are encoded via Sonata and Gaussian Splat inputs via Chorus, with both modalities projected onto a shared voxel grid and fused through a novel per-voxel contrastive alignment loss before being decoded by a large language model. As a natural consequence of the LLM, GraphWrit3R also supports open-vocabulary querying. On the 3DSSG benchmark, our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on object class, predicate, and triplet recall, outperforming methods that rely on ground-truth object annotations during inference. We further provide qualitative results and analyze different input modality configurations, contrastive loss formulations, and token fusion strategies.

</details>


### [87] [FuseReg: Regularizing Layer Fusion Mitigates the Reconstruction-Generation Gap in Representation Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31620)
*Hongyang Du,Yunfei Xie,Junjie Ye,Jiawei Yang,Xiaoyan Cong,Haodong Zhang,Yongchao Huang,Haiyu Wu,Zongxia Li,Shihang Gui,Dawei Liu,Runhao Li,Jingcheng Ni,Chen Wei,Randall Balestriero,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FuseReg 提出用"训练时对编码器层随机子集采样"替代 RAE 中启发式固定层融合，从而解耦重建与生成对隐空间的不同需求，用单一解码器同时支持全层/稀疏/单层融合，并在不改动预训练编码器的前提下显著提升重建 PSNR 与生成 gFID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表征自编码器（RAE）复用预训练视觉编码器的特征，同时作为重建 latent 与扩散 latent，把强视觉表征引入图像生成。但关键超参——哪些编码器层构成共享隐空间——存在固有矛盾：浅层更利于保留像素细节（重建友好），深层更利于生成质量（语义友好）。固定启发式层融合把这两个目标不同的阶段强行绑定，导致重建与生成之间出现难以弥合的 gap。论文动机即用可训练、对层选择鲁棒的机制取代人工启发式，且不修改预训练编码器。

Method: 核心方法 FuseReg：训练时对编码器层进行随机子集采样（subset sampling），让解码器/生成器在不同层组合下都保持一致输出，而非固定某一层或某种加权融合。论文给出理论分析，指出子集采样显式惩罚了模型对跨层不一致性（cross-layer disagreement）的敏感性，起正则化作用。基于此，一个单一 FuseReg 解码器即可处理全层、稀疏、单层融合而无需重训练。该正则原则进一步推广到扩散训练，对重建与生成两个阶段联合施加层融合鲁棒性正则。整个流程不修改预训练编码器，只训练下游解码器与生成器。

Result: 在 ImageNet-256 上，以 DINOv3-L 为编码器，单个 FuseReg 解码器在全层/稀疏/单层三种融合下均无需重训练即可重建，PSNR 高于专为固定融合定制的解码器。生成方面：仅替换解码器（RAEv2 DiT-XL 生成器保持不变）即把 unguided gFID 降低 27%；在 DiT-Base 上对两阶段联合正则进一步将 unguided gFID 降低 29%。结果表明面向层融合鲁棒性的下游训练可缩小重建-生成 gap。

Conclusion: 主要贡献：用随机层子集采样正则（FuseReg）替代启发式层融合，提供理论解释，并证明其能统一重建与生成 latent 的需求，同时提升 PSNR 与 gFID 且不动预训练编码器。意义在于将"层选择"这一设计难题转化为可训练的鲁棒性问题，对复用预训练表征的生成建模有实用价值。局限（基于摘要）：实验集中于 ImageNet-256 与 DINOv3-L/DiT 系列，未展示其他分辨率、编码器家族或视频/多模态任务；27%/29% 的具体基准数值及稀疏/单层设置细节需查阅正文验证。

Abstract: Representation autoencoders (RAEs) reuse features from a pretrained visual encoder as reconstruction and diffusion latents, integrating strong visual representations into image generation. However, RAEs still need to decide which encoder layers form the shared latent space for the generator and pixel decoder. This choice involves a trade-off. Shallower layers tend to preserve fine pixel details better, while deeper layers tend to yield better generation metrics. A fixed heuristic layer fusion therefore couples two stages that benefit from different information. We introduce FuseReg, which replaces heuristic feature selection with training over random subsets of encoder layers. We theoretically analyze the underlying mechanism: subset sampling explicitly penalizes sensitivity to cross-layer disagreement. On ImageNet-256 with DINOv3-L, a single FuseReg decoder reconstructs from full, sparse, and single-layer fusions without retraining, achieving higher PSNR than decoders specialized to fixed fusions. This flexibility also benefits generation: decoder replacement alone reduces unguided gFID by 27% with an unchanged RAEv2 DiT-XL generator. The same regularization principle extends to diffusion training, with joint regularization of both stages reducing unguided gFID by 29% on DiT-Base. These results show that training downstream models for layer-fusion robustness narrows the reconstruction-generation gap without modifying the pretrained encoder.

</details>

### [88] [Structured Bayesian Modeling of Dynamic Receptive1 Fields in Salamander Retinal Ganglion Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30731)
*Alokesh Manna*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该论文将动态感受野（receptive field）估计建模为高维贝叶斯问题，用空间高斯马尔可夫随机场（GMRF）+ 时间自回归过程的泊松模型，使估计出的感受野在空间上连贯、在时间上平滑，从而避免 LASSO 等稀疏正则化导致的碎片化与不连续问题，并在火蜥蜴视网膜神经节细胞记录上完成单神经元场估计与基于功能聚类的群体分型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉系统神经元对特定时空刺激特征具有选择性，其刻画依赖感受野估计。该问题本质是高维的：需要对每个像素、每个时间 bin 估计一个系数，而实验可用的 trial 数很少（few trials），因此必须引入正则化。稀疏正则化（如 LASSO）虽能应对维度问题，但它在每个时间点独立地选择像素，缺乏保持空间区域连贯性与时间平滑性的机制，因此即使真实响应是平滑演化的，估计结果也可能出现碎片化或不连续重组——而这种时空演化模式恰恰是感受野估计应当捕捉的核心对象。因此，需要一种能够显式编码空间相干性与时间平滑性的建模框架。

Method: 作者把动态感受野估计形式化为高维贝叶斯推断问题，构建泊松观测模型：空间维度上使用高斯马尔可夫随机场（GMRF）先验以鼓励相邻像素的连贯性，时间维度上使用自回归（autoregressive）过程以刻画平滑的时间演化，二者结合构成时空结构化先验，从而在 few-trial 条件下进行正则化估计。估计按神经元逐个独立拟合。在得到每个神经元的感受野"曲面"后，进一步用空间平均的时间响应曲线对该神经元进行概括，并采用基于模型的功能聚类（model-based functional clustering）对曲线聚类，用 BIC 选择聚类数。论文另设计已知 ground truth 的仿真实验，与无正则化的泊松 GLM、LASSO 和 elastic net 在恢复与估计精度上进行比较。

Result: 实验使用 155 个火蜥蜴视网膜神经节细胞（salamander retinal ganglion cells）的记录。逐神经元独立拟合该模型可恢复出连贯的感受野曲面，而像素级 Poisson-LASSO 对比方法给出的是碎片化曲面。将每个神经元的曲面用空间平均时间响应曲线概括并对这些曲线做基于模型的聚类后，BIC 选出三类平衡的时间响应表型（分别含 85、32、38 个神经元）；相比之下，直接对原始感受野曲面聚类得到的是退化的分组。已知 ground truth 的仿真研究复现了相同规律：所提模型在恢复与估计精度上优于无正则化泊松 GLM、LASSO 与 elastic net，不过 LASSO 在控制假阳性（false positive）方面表现更好。

Conclusion: 论文的贡献包括三部分：逐神经元的动态感受野场辨识、其与 LASSO 的对比（体现结构化时空先验相对逐点稀疏正则的优势与代价），以及基于功能聚类的群体表型分型。方法意义在于为 few-trial、高维的神经时空响应估计提供了显式编码空间连贯与时间平滑的贝叶斯框架，并把单细胞层面的估计与群体层面的功能性分型联系起来。局限方面：聚类结果的可靠性依赖空间平均曲线这一概括方式，且未经独立验证；LASSO 在假阳性控制上仍更优，说明本模型在特异性/稀疏性上存在权衡；评估主要基于单一物种（火蜥蜴视网膜）的 155 个细胞记录及仿真，向其他脑区、模态或物种的泛化性尚未验证。该工作属于神经科学计算建模方向，未涉及事件相机或脉冲神经网络等工程感知系统。

Abstract: Neurons in the visual system are selective for specific spatial and temporal stimulus features, described by their \emph{receptive field}. Estimating one means a coefficient per pixel per time bin from few trials -- a high-dimensional problem requiring regularization. Sparse regularizers such as the LASSO handle the dimension but select pixels independently at each time point, with nothing to keep the region coherent in space or smooth in time; it can fragment or reorganize discontinuously even when the true response evolves smoothly, a failure since this evolving pattern is what a receptive-field estimate should capture. We formulate dynamic receptive-field estimation as a high-dimensional Bayesian problem: a Poisson model combining a Gaussian Markov random field in space with an autoregressive process in time, so the estimated field is smooth and coherent across space and time. On recordings from $155$ salamander retinal ganglion cells, fitting this model independently per neuron recovers a coherent surface, where a pixel-level Poisson-LASSO comparison instead returns a fragmented one. Summarizing each neuron's surface by its space-averaged temporal response and clustering these curves with a model-based functional-clustering procedure, BIC selects three balanced temporal-response phenotypes ($85$, $32$, $38$ neurons), against a degenerate grouping from clustering the raw surfaces. A simulation study with known ground truth confirms the same pattern, with the model beating an unregularized Poisson GLM, LASSO, and the elastic net on recovery and estimation accuracy, though LASSO controls false positives better. The per-neuron field identification, its contrast with LASSO, and the functional-clustering population typing constitute this paper's contribution.

</details>


### [89] [Landscape Limits of Quantum-Inspired Evolutionary Optimization across 256 continuous functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30938)
*Rishi Govind,Ferdin Sagai Don Bosco,Kasturi Venkata Srikanth,Aman Mittal,Abhishek Singh,Aditya Singh,Abhishek Chopra*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文对量子启发式进化优化（QIEO）进行了大规模基准评测：将设计变量编码为量子比特振幅对，并通过向每代精英旋转振幅进行更新；作者从既有文献中选取508个连续函数（256个未平移+252个平移，代表11种景观特征），系统比较三种QIEO变体、两种遗传算法（GA）编码以及CMA-ES，从计算代价、求解精度和景观特征专属性三个维度揭示QIEO的适用边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QIEO以量子比特振幅对表示设计变量，通过向单代精英旋转实现廉价、近乎无参数、易并行化的搜索，已在工程、设计与规划中被广泛采用，并常被认为在定性效果与计算效率上优于经典GA。然而其核心缺陷在于将设计变量视为相互独立的概率分量，无法利用局部曲率、各向异性以及变量耦合结构。本文旨在通过覆盖多种景观特征的系统性基准，明确QIEO何时具有竞争力、独立性表示在何处成为瓶颈，以及不同变体是否对特定景观特征具有优势，从而为方法的合理使用与改进提供依据。

Method: 研究采用统一的实验框架：从既有工作中选取508个连续测试函数（256个未平移+252个平移，另有4个无法平移），覆盖11种景观特征——连续性、可微性、可分性、可扩展性、多峰性、凸性、条件数、对称性、最大维度、维度依赖性与变量耦合模式。对比算法包括三种QIEO变体、两种GA编码方式以及Hansen的CMA-ES。评估侧重于三方面：计算代价、解的精度，以及算法在不同景观特征上的专属性表现。（注：摘要未给出QIEO各变体的具体构造差异与旋转/更新算子的数学细节。）

Result: 实验在508个连续函数上完成，涵盖上述11类景观特征，以计算代价与求解精度为主要指标，并考察各算法对不同景观特征的专属性。结果显示：研究识别出QIEO具备竞争力的具体条件，明确了其以独立变量表示为核心的限制在何种景观（如存在耦合、各向异性或强条件数的情形）下显现，并判定了特定QIEO变体是否在特定景观特征上具有优势。摘要未给出具体的数值结果、精度提升幅度或统计显著性数据。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是构建了一个覆盖11种景观特征的508函数基准，对QIEO、GA与CMA-ES进行横向评测，从而厘清QIEO的竞争性区间与其独立变量表示导致的能力上限，并为不同变体的适用场景提供经验依据。其意义在于纠正“QIEO普遍优于GA”的笼统认知，为连续优化中的算法选择提供更细致的指导。局限在于：核心结论依赖所选函数集合的覆盖度，且未针对独立性表示的缺陷提出结构性改进方案（摘要未提及新的算法设计），因而更多是诊断性而非建设性的工作。

Abstract: Quantum-inspired evolutionary optimization (QIEO) represents design variables as a set of qubits and searches a continuous, multi-dimensional landscape through rotation of the qubit's amplitude pair. Every generation rotates those amplitudes toward a single elite, which corresponds to that generation's best. The update is cheap, almost parameter-free, and well-suited for massive parallel implementation, which has encouraged its adoption in engineering, design, and planning applications. However, there are critical issues with this formulation, principally, the treatment of design variables as independent probability components which make it incapable of exploiting local curvature, anisotropy, or variable coupling. Despite this, QIEO is believed to hold promise, and has been used extensively to solve real-world problems, with significant qualitative and computational advantage over its classical counterpart, Genetic Algorithm (GA).
  A collection of 256 (actually 508; 256 unshifted + 252 shifted, 4 could not be shifted) continuous function are selected from the prior works, in such a way that they represent eleven landscape characteristics, namely continuity, differentiability, separability, scalability, modality, convexity, conditioning, symmetry, maximum dimensionality, dimension dependency, and the coupling pattern of the design variables. These functions are then solved by three QIEO variants, two GA encodings and Hansen's Covariance Matrix Adaptation Evolution Strategy (CMA-ES).
  The results are evaluated in terms of computational cost, solution precision, and specialization across landscape characteristics. They identify the conditions under which QIEO provides competitive performance, clarify where its independent-variable representation becomes limiting, and establish whether particular QIEO variants offer advantages for specific landscape characteristics.

</details>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [TinyCVIO: A Constellation-Aided Visual-Inertial Odometry System for Nanodrones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30358)
*Derin Ozturk,Kaan Akan,Irwin Wang,Christopher Batten*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TinyCVIO 是一套面向纳米无人机（nanodrone）的视觉-惯性里程计（VIO）系统，通过与商品级双核微控制器（520 kB SRAM）协同设计微型传感、视觉前端与状态估计模块，利用低成本 LED 星座实现板载实时、厘米级精度的位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米无人机在严重受限的感知与算力条件下仍需要准确、实时的状态估计。传统 VIO 通常依赖较强的算力平台和较重的视觉特征处理，难以部署在具备 520 kB SRAM 级别的微控制器上；同时，外部动捕系统或已知标定位置的信标方案又引入了部署成本与限制。因此，需要一种在极低资源下仍能稳定运行、且不依赖预先测量位姿的信标感知-估计方案。

Method: 系统采用感知、视觉处理与估计的协同设计。用轻量级 LED 星座提供已知几何结构，无需预先测量位置或偏航角，仅假设 LED 布置在同一水平面上。毫米级相机以 29.2 FPS 运行流式视觉前端，跟踪 LED 观测。关键创新是刚性板（rigid-board）测量模型，保留 LED 之间的相对约束（inter-LED constraints），而非将 LED 当作独立平面点；同时使用流式 QR 分解对估计工作空间进行边界约束，使滤波器状态维度保持固定，适配微控制器的内存限制。

Result: 在 19 个手持硬件在环（hardware-in-the-loop）数据集上，刚性板模型相比平面点模型将平均绝对轨迹误差（ATE）降低 27%。完整系统在 Crazyflie 上板载运行，完成三种速度下的 9 次飞行，平均绝对轨迹误差为 3.5–3.7 cm，10 m 段上的相对位姿误差为 0.50–0.60%，平均估计延迟为 15.7–16.3 ms。

Conclusion: TinyCVIO 展示了在商品级双核微控制器（520 kB SRAM）上实现板载 VIO 的可行性，通过 LED 星座几何先验与刚性板约束、流式 QR 边界控制，实现了厘米级精度与约 16 ms 延迟的实时估计，验证了纳米无人机在无外部定位系统下的自主状态估计能力。局限在于依赖同一水平面布置的 LED 星座、实验规模较小（9 次飞行）且为受控室内场景，尚需验证在更复杂光照、运动与户外环境下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Nanodrones require accurate, real-time state estimation under severe sensing and computational constraints. We present TinyCVIO, a visual-inertial odometry system that co-designs miniature sensing, visual processing, and estimation for a commodity dual-core microcontroller with 520 kB SRAM. Lightweight LED constellations provide known geometry without surveyed positions or yaw angles, assuming placement on a common level plane. A streaming visual frontend tracks LED observations from a millimeter-scale camera at 29.2 FPS, while a rigid-board measurement model retains inter-LED constraints and streaming QR bounds estimation workspace for a fixed filter-state size. Across 19 hand-held hardware-in-the-loop datasets, the rigid-board model reduces mean absolute trajectory error by 27% relative to planar points. The complete system runs onboard a Crazyflie across nine flights at three speeds, achieving 3.5-3.7 cm mean absolute trajectory error and 0.50-0.60% relative pose error over 10 m segments, with mean estimate latency of 15.7-16.3 ms.

</details>


### [91] [POIL: Point-based One-Shot Imitation Learning with Stable Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30404)
*Sang Min Kim,Jinwoo Seo,Hyeongjun Heo,Junho Lee,Yonghyeon Lee,Young Min Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: POIL 是一个基于点集表示的一次性模仿学习框架，将"轨迹迁移"与"闭环执行"统一到物体功能部件上的 3D 点集这一共享表示中。它借助多模态大模型（MLLM）定位功能部件、通过点对应完成跨视角/位姿/类别的轨迹迁移，并以 Point-set BCSDM 将点集速度投影到单一刚体 twist 上实现稳定闭环控制，无需已知 3D 模型或位姿估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一次性模仿学习虽能免去大量示教采集，但成功的机器人操作不仅要把示教轨迹迁移到新物体上，还需在场景变化、抓取构型改变和外部扰动下稳健执行。现有方法通常将"迁移"与"执行"分立处理：轨迹迁移依赖物体级位姿或 CAD 模型，而闭环控制又依赖精确的位姿估计，二者在类别变化、遮挡和扰动场景下都不够鲁棒。POIL 的动机是寻找一个同时适用于迁移与执行的共享中间表示，从而在缺乏已知 3D 模型和位姿估计器的条件下兼顾泛化性与稳定性。

Method: 1) 共享表示：选取物体"功能部件"上的一组 3D 点，同时用于轨迹迁移与闭环执行。2) 功能部件 grounding：用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在物体上定位功能部件，获得语义上可跨类别对应的区域。3) 一次性迁移：利用点对应关系，将示教轨迹映射到新物体，支持跨视角、跨位姿、跨物体类别的迁移。4) 在线执行：通过多视图跟踪在线观测同一组点；提出 Point-set BCSDM（基于点集的 BCSDM），由被跟踪点自身计算单一刚体 twist，并将每个点的速度投影到该 twist 上，形成闭环驱动。该方法将稳定动力学模型从 SE(3) 位姿扩展到点集，无需已知 3D 模型或位姿估计器。5) 理论分析：证明在目标位置处控制器退化为经典 SO(3) 势函数上的梯度流，因此在刚体假设下终端阶段继承了该势函数几乎全局收敛的性质。

Result: 论文在仿真与真实机器人平台上开展实验，验证 POIL 能够用单次示教在物体类别、抓取位姿和目标几何形状发生变化时完成轨迹迁移，并在执行过程中从外部扰动中恢复。评估关注跨类别/位姿/目标的迁移能力与扰动恢复能力（具体数据集、量化指标与数值在摘要中未给出）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：提出以功能部件点集为共享表示的"迁移+闭环执行"统一框架；借助 MLLM grounding 实现跨类别一次性迁移；提出 Point-set BCSDM 将稳定动力学从 SE(3) 位姿推广到点集，并给出终端阶段近乎全局收敛的理论依据；在仿真与真机上验证了泛化性与扰动鲁棒性。潜在局限：摘要未提供定量结果与失败案例分析，收敛性结论依赖于刚体假设，且性能可能受 MLLM grounding 与多视图点跟踪精度的制约。

Abstract: We present POIL, a point-based one-shot imitation learning framework with stable dynamical systems. While one-shot imitation avoids collecting extensive demonstrations, successful one-shot manipulation requires not only transferring a demonstrated trajectory to a novel object but also executing it robustly under changing scene conditions, grasp configurations, and external disturbances. POIL addresses both problems through a shared representation: a set of 3D points on the object's functional part, used jointly for trajectory transfer and closed-loop execution. The one-shot transfer from the demonstrated trajectory is enabled with point correspondences. POIL grounds the shared functional part with a multi-modal large language model, and transfers the trajectory across viewpoint, pose, and object category changes. During execution, multi-view tracking observes the same points online, and Point-set BCSDM drives them in closed loop by projecting per-point velocities onto a single rigid-body twist computed from the tracked points alone. This extends stable dynamical models from an SE(3) pose to a point set without requiring a known 3D model or pose estimator. We show that at the goal the controller becomes a gradient flow on the classical SO(3) potential, so its terminal phase inherits the almost-global convergence of that potential under a rigid-object assumption. Across simulation and real-robot experiments, POIL transfers a single demonstration across object category, grasp pose, and goal geometry, while recovering from external disturbances during execution. Project page: https://sangminkim-99.github.io/poil

</details>


### [92] [Actively Resolving Contextual Uncertainty for Underspecified Tasks in Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30428)
*Zachary Ravichandran,Jonathan Diller,Fernando Cladera,Varun Murali,George J. Pappas,Vijay Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CLUE是一个面向"欠指定"自然语言任务的机器人框架，利用LLM生成任务相关概念与候选规划假设，并在线构建语言嵌入地图将假设落地为动作，通过闭环环境交互顺序地评估与修正假设，从而主动消解上下文不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言条件策略（language-conditioned policies）大多假设目标是明确且完整指定的，并依赖预先给定的先验地图来提供任务相关信息。然而在陌生环境中面对欠指定任务时，存在高度的上下文不确定性：机器人必须同时推断"什么算任务成功""什么信息是相关的""该信息在哪（或是否存在）"。这种联合不确定性使得传统"先建图再查询"的开环范式失效，亟需一种能主动探索并消解上下文不确定性的方法。

Method: 论文提出CLUE（Closed-Loop contextual Uncertainty rEsolution）框架。核心由两部分组成：(1) 一个由LLM导出的策略，用于假设任务相关的概念（concepts）与可能的规划（plans）；(2) 一个在线构建的语言嵌入地图（language-embedded map），用于将上述语言层面的假设接地（ground）为具体动作。CLUE的关键创新在于闭环：策略在环境中顺序执行并评估各假设，随着获取新信息不断精炼其规划，形成"假设—接地—执行—重规划"的迭代回路，而非一次性依赖静态先验地图。

Result: CLUE部署于Boston Dynamics Spot四足机器人，在3个真实室内外环境中评测，覆盖15个需要物体消歧（object disambiguation）、功能推断（functional inference）与遮挡推理（occlusion reasoning）的任务。结果显示：CLUE成功率与oracle策略相差在7个百分点以内；相比不带闭环反馈的LLM-enabled planner提升约4倍。消融/对比实验表明，单纯"先构建再查询语言增强地图"的方式不足以解决复杂上下文规划任务，其成功率约为CLUE的三分之一，且消耗的VLM token超过10倍。相关材料见 https://zacravichandran.github.io/CLUE。

Conclusion: CLUE将语言条件下的机器人规划从"开环、先验地图驱动"推进到"闭环、不确定性主动消解"范式，证明了闭环交互反馈对欠指定任务的关键作用，并在真实四足平台上取得接近oracle的成功率且显著提升token效率。潜在局限包括：任务规模（3环境15任务）与场景多样性仍有限，依赖LLM/VLM的基础能力与感知可靠性，闭环探索在时间与能耗上的代价，以及对语言嵌入地图质量的敏感性等，尚需在更大规模与更复杂动态环境中验证。

Abstract: Foundation models provide robots with the ability to interpret natural language and reason about environmental context, yet most language-conditioned policies assume that goals are well-specified and that task-relevant information is provided upfront via a prior map. Operating in unfamiliar environments with underspecified tasks entails high contextual uncertainty: the robot must jointly infer what constitutes task success, what constitutes relevant information, and where (or whether) that information exists. We address these limitations via CLUE (Closed-Loop contextual Uncertainty rEsolution), a framework for actively resolving contextual uncertainty given underspecified tasks in natural language. CLUE uses an LLM-derived policy to hypothesize task-relevant concepts and potential plans. It then uses a language-embedded map, which is constructed online, to ground these hypotheses into actions. The policy sequentially evaluates hypotheses via closed-loop environment interaction and refines its plans as it gathers new information. We deploy CLUE on a Boston Dynamics Spot across three real indoor and outdoor environments spanning 15 tasks that require object disambiguation, functional inference, and occlusion reasoning. CLUE achieves a success rate within 7 percentage points of an oracle policy and outperforms an LLM-enabled planner without closed-loop feedback by a 4x margin. Supporting experiments demonstrate that simply building and then querying a language-enriched map is insufficient to resolve complex contextual planning tasks; these approaches achieve roughly one third the success rate of CLUE while requiring over 10x more VLM tokens. We provide additional information at https://zacravichandran.github.io/CLUE.

</details>


### [93] [WALT: Learning World-Model-Aligned Latent Trajectories for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30436)
*Mingkai Jia,Jiaxin Guo,Zhijian Shu,Jiawei Xu,Mingxiao Li,Jintao Cheng,Ping Tan,Wei Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 WALT（World-Model Alignment for Latent Trajectories），通过将原始几何轨迹映射到紧凑的生成式轨迹隐空间，并从冻结的预训练驾驶世界模型中迁移语义知识，从而对齐视觉世界状态与轨迹表征，提升基于世界模型的轨迹规划性能，同时显著降低规划器计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶世界模型能从视觉观测中学习到丰富的预测性环境表示，但视觉预测精度高并不必然带来有效的轨迹规划。作者认为核心瓶颈在于视觉世界状态（world state）与原始几何轨迹（raw geometric trajectories / waypoints）之间存在表征层面的不匹配，导致规划器难以充分利用世界模型中编码的、与动作相关的语义信息。因此需要一种能把世界模型的语义知识有效迁移到轨迹空间的接口，以弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: WALT 不改动已冻结的预训练驾驶世界模型，而是将轨迹表征学习视为从世界模型向轨迹空间的语义知识迁移问题。具体地：(1) 设计双分支轨迹自编码器（dual-branch trajectory autoencoder），把原始 waypoints 映射到紧凑的生成式隐空间，而非直接生成原始轨迹点；(2) 通过特征对齐的方式，将冻结视觉世界模型中的语义知识迁移进该轨迹隐空间，使学习到的动作表征能够捕获与未来运动、规划相关的场景级线索。此外，作者系统性地研究了基于 JEPA（Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures）的隐空间学习，以及借鉴 REPA（Representation Alignment）的特征对齐策略，以探究仅基于轨迹的表征学习如何影响下游规划任务。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 基准上进行评估。相较于直接生成原始 waypoints 的基线方法，WALT 在 NAVSIMv1 上将 PDMS 从 89.4 提升至 89.8，在 NAVSIMv2 上将 EPDMS 从 87.3 提升至 87.9，同时将轨迹规划器的 FLOPs 降低了 30.5%。结果表明，在保持世界模型表征不变的前提下提取动作相关信息，可作为世界模型驱动轨迹规划的有效接口。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：(1) 提出 WALT，通过轨迹隐空间对齐将冻结驾驶世界模型的语义知识迁移到动作表征，缓解视觉世界状态与几何轨迹间的表征错配；(2) 系统性比较了 JEPA 式隐空间学习与 REPA 式特征对齐对下游规划的影响，为轨迹表征学习提供了实证参考。方法在 NAVSIMv1/v2 上取得性能与效率的双重提升（PDMS/EPDMS 增益且规划器 FLOPs 降低约 30.5%），说明其对世界模型驱动规划具有实用价值。潜在局限在于性能提升幅度较小（PDMS +0.4、EPDMS +0.6），且依赖冻结的预训练世界模型质量，泛化能力与在其他数据集/场景下的有效性有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Driving world models learn rich predictive representations of the surrounding environment from visual observations, yet accurate visual prediction does not necessarily translate into effective trajectory planning. We argue that a key bottleneck lies in the mismatch between visual world states and raw geometric trajectories, which may limit the planner's ability to exploit action-relevant semantics encoded by the world model. To address this issue, we propose World-Model Alignment for Latent Trajectories (WALT), which learns a compact generative trajectory latent space by transferring information from a frozen pretrained driving world model without modifying the world model itself. Rather than directly generating raw waypoints, WALT maps them into compact representations through a dual-branch trajectory autoencoder and transfers semantic knowledge from the frozen visual world model into this trajectory space, encouraging the learned action representation to capture scene-level cues relevant to future motion and planning. Beyond our proposed formulation, we systematically study latent learning based on Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPA) and feature alignment following Representation Alignment (REPA) to investigate how trajectory-only representation learning affects downstream planning. We evaluate WALT on the NAVSIM benchmarks. Relative to the raw-waypoint baseline, WALT improves PDMS from 89.4 to 89.8 on NAVSIMv1 and EPDMS from 87.3 to 87.9 on NAVSIMv2 while reducing trajectory planner FLOPs by 30.5%. These results suggest that preserving world representations while extracting action-relevant information provides an effective interface for world-model-based trajectory planning.

</details>


### [94] [VkVIO: Cross-platform GPU Acceleration for Visual-Inertial Odometry with Vulkan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30459)
*Ole Hoffmann,Mateo de Mayo,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VkVIO 是首个基于 Vulkan API 的跨平台 GPU 加速视觉-惯性里程计（VIO）方法，在多种设备上实现实时、因果的位姿估计，并在相同硬件上超越基于 CUDA 的同类系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人、XR 等领域的感知依赖良好的状态估计，而 VIO/VI-SLAM 是低成本、高精度的实现途径。系统效率决定了设备能否做到更小、更凉、更轻。GPU 加速是降低延迟的自然选择，因为嵌入式计算机、手机、XR 头显普遍配备 GPU。然而已有工作大多局限于 CUDA，把部署选项绑定到单一厂商（NVIDIA），牺牲了跨平台性与部署灵活性。作者希望用厂商无关的 Vulkan API 打破这一限制。

Method: 核心是采用 Vulkan——一个最初为 3D 图形严格性能需求设计的、厂商无关的 GPU 计算 API——来加速 VIO 流水线，而非 CUDA。系统提供因果（causal）估计，即只依赖当前及历史数据，满足实时运行要求。该方法在 Vulkan 上实现了 VIO 所需的关键并行计算模块，使其可在不同 GPU 厂商的硬件上运行；摘要未披露具体的特征跟踪、惯性预积分或后端优化等模块细节，也未给出网络/架构层面的更多信息。

Result: VkVIO 部署在一个工作站、一台笔记本和一块极低价的单板计算机等多样化设备上，达到当前最优（state-of-the-art）的精度，并在相同硬件上性能超过基于 CUDA 的系统。评估强调跨平台部署能力与实时因果估计，但摘要未给出具体数据集、轨迹误差指标（如 ATE/RPE）、延迟或功耗的具体数值。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出首个跨平台 GPU 加速的 VIO 方法，用 Vulkan 替代 CUDA，实现跨厂商部署并在多样设备上保持 SOTA 精度且优于 CUDA 方案，为机器人/XR 中低延迟、低功耗、低成本的 VIO 提供可能。局限在于摘要未提供具体数据集与量化结果，CUDA 对比的具体设置和硬件细节也不明确。

Abstract: Perception in robotics and XR fundamentally relies on good state estimation. Visual-inertial odometry (VIO) and Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (VI-SLAM) are proven ways of achieving this goal in a cost-effective and accurate manner. Efficiency in these systems allows for smaller, cooler, and lighter devices. GPU acceleration is a natural approach for reducing latency, thanks to their wide availability in platforms like embedded computers, mobile phones, and XR headsets. However, previous works in the literature have limited themselves to the use of CUDA for this task, significantly reducing deployment options to a single vendor. We instead leverage the vendor-agnostic Vulkan API, originally designed for the strict performance requirements of 3D graphics applications. In this work, we present VkVIO, the first, to the best of our knowledge, cross-platform GPU-accelerated VIO method. We provide state-of-the-art accuracy with causal estimates required for real-time operation. We deploy VkVIO on a diverse range of devices spanning a workstation, a laptop, and an extremely inexpensive single-board computer, while outperforming CUDA-based systems on the same hardware. VkVIO enables possibilities for low-latency, low-power, and low-cost VIO in robotics and XR.

</details>


### [95] [Realizability Is Not Enough: Encoding, Liveness, and Auditing of Synthesized Robot Supervisors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30460)
*David C. Conner,Joshua Luzier,William J. Doyle,Emma R. Faith,Aubrie B. Kooiker,Andrew J. Farney,Sebastian Fox,Evangelina Grimes,Ian G. Conner,Kyle Bloom*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一套面向 ROS 2 FlexBE 监督器的开源流水线，将机器人能力建模为 GR(1) 规范，覆盖规范生成、合成前假设分析、策略审计、带行为保持证明的状态约简，以及可执行状态机导出；并在四组案例（含两个四旋翼硬件平台）上系统对比了枚举编码与 one-hot 编码、两种活性公式的实际可部署性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高层机器人监督器依据各能力的汇报结果决定下一步动作。反应式合成（reactive synthesis）虽能从形式化规范生成带保证的监督器，但"GR(1) 规范可实现（realizable）"并不等于"能在真实机器人上部署"：设计者需要显式建模易失败的能力、选择与重试意图相符的活性（liveness）假设、审计合成出的策略，并将其翻译为机器人软件。作者指出，命题数量与可实现性都无法衡量可部署性，这正是从形式化保证到实际 ROS 2 控制器之间的缺口。

Method: 流水线面向 ROS 2 FlexBE 监督器，关键技术环节包括：(1) 从能力（capability）出发自动生成 GR(1) 规范；(2) 在合成之前对假设进行分析，避免不可满足或设计意图不符的规范；(3) 对合成得到的策略进行审计，检测四类结构性缺陷（协议违背、死锁、有界失败违规、目标不可达陷阱），该审计器对这四类缺陷是 sound and complete 的，但并非通用活性验证器；(4) 带行为保持证明（behavior-preservation proof）的状态约简，在能力层面保持行为不变；(5) 导出可执行状态机。方法层面还系统考察了两种状态编码（enumerated vs. one-hot）与两种活性公式（System-Goal 不含 pending memory、Fair-Outcome）的组合。

Result: 实验包含四个案例研究、共六组对比，其中两组在四旋翼硬件平台上验证。主要发现：在测试所用后端下，枚举编码通常合成更快，但命题数更少并不能可靠地预示控制器更小或符号代价更低；"不含 pending memory 的 System-Goal"是唯一在两种编码、所报告网格下均被确认可产生可执行控制器的活性处理方式，而 Fair-Outcome 可能允许存在可实现但并非设计者所期望完成路径的循环。作者据此推荐：采用枚举编码 + System-Goal，并对每一个实际合成出的策略进行审计。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提供了一条从 GR(1) 可实现性到通过协议与结构进展检查的可部署 ROS 2 FlexBE 监督器的开放流水线，并以硬件案例验证了编码与活性公式选择的实践影响，明确指出命题数与可实现性并非可部署性的度量。局限在于：审计器仅对四类结构缺陷 sound and complete，不能替代通用活性验证；约简仅在能力层保持行为；结论主要针对本文测试的特定后端与模型，泛化性有待进一步验证。

Abstract: High-level robotic supervisors coordinate capabilities whose reported outcomes determine the robot's next action. Reactive synthesis can generate such supervisors with formal guarantees, but deployment requires more than proving a Generalized Reactivity (1) (GR(1)) specification realizable. Designers must encode failure-prone capabilities, choose liveness assumptions that match retry intent, audit strategies, and translate them into robot software. We present an open-source pipeline for Robot Operating System (ROS) 2 Flexible Behavior Engine (FlexBE) supervisors that generates capability-based GR(1) specifications, analyzes assumptions before synthesis, audits strategies, reduces states with a behavior-preservation proof, and emits executable state machines.
  Across four case studies (six comparisons), including hardware on two quadcopter platforms, we compare enumerated and one-hot encodings and two liveness formulations. Under the tested backend, enumerated encoding usually synthesizes faster, although fewer propositions do not reliably predict smaller controllers or lower symbolic cost. System-Goal without pending memory is the only liveness treatment confirmed to yield executable controllers under both encodings across the reported grid; Fair-Outcome can permit realizable cycles without designer-intended completion. For this backend and model, we recommend enumerated encoding with System-Goal and auditing every realized strategy, since proposition count and realizability do not measure deployability.
  The auditor is sound and complete for four structural defect classes (protocol violations, deadlocks, bounded-failure violations, goal-unreachable traps) but is not a general liveness verifier, and the reduction preserves capability-level behavior. Together, these stages narrow the gap between formal realizability and controllers that pass protocol and structural-progress checks.

</details>


### [96] [DGT-Map: Directional Global Traversability Mapping Utilizing Multi-Task Learning for Heterogeneous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30461)
*Jaskrit Singh,Kashif K. Noori,Jing Xiao,Constantinos Chamzas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DGT-MAP 提出一个自监督框架，从 RGB-D 观测与运动信号中学习全局、方向性且车辆条件化的越野可通行性代价地图，通过共享多任务骨干网络与车辆特定预测头实现跨车辆的地形表示共享，并输出可用于方向感知规划器的航向索引代价图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野可通行性本质上是方向相关且车辆相关的：同一位置的下坡可行而上坡不可行，同一障碍物对某些车辆可通过而对另一些不可通过。然而现有全局地图通常为每个位置分配单一的各向同性代价，忽略了方向依赖性；同时现有学习型估计器多为每种车辆独立训练，虽保留了平台特异性，却无法让车辆共享通用地形表示。这两点限制了越野导航在复杂地形上的可靠性与泛化能力。

Method: DGT-MAP 采用自监督学习框架，输入为 RGB-D 观测与运动（locomotion）信号，无需人工标注。架构上使用共享的多任务骨干网络学习跨训练车辆的通用地形特征，并为每辆车配备车辆特定的预测头以保留平台相关的响应差异。输出为航向索引（heading-indexed）的可通行性代价地图，即对每个位置沿不同航向给出方向相关的代价，可直接供方向感知规划器使用。整个流程整合进 Hybrid A* 导航栈中。

Result: 在仿真环境中进行评估，将 DGT-MAP 集成到 Hybrid A* 导航栈，以下游任务成功率作为核心指标。测试地形具有挑战性，包括只能下坡不能上坡的斜坡，以及部分车辆可通过而其他车辆不可通过的脊状障碍。与几何、二值以及学习型方向无关基线相比，DGT-MAP 在所评估任务上取得了最高或并列最高的导航成功率。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：提出方向相关、车辆条件化的可通行性建模，克服了传统各向同性代价图的局限；通过共享骨干加特定头的设计在保留平台特性的同时实现跨车辆表示共享；并以自监督方式从 RGB-D 与运动信号学习，输出可被方向感知规划器直接利用的代价图。局限在于实验仅在仿真中完成，尚未在真实越野平台与真实传感器噪声下验证，且对训练中未见的车型泛化能力有待进一步考察。

Abstract: Off-road traversability is direction-dependent and vehicle specific, yet most global maps assign a single isotropic cost to each location. Existing learned estimators are also commonly trained independently for each vehicle; this preserves vehicle-specific behavior but prevents vehicles from sharing common terrain representations. DGT-MAP addresses both limitations through a self-supervised framework that learns global, directional, and vehicle-conditioned traversability costmaps from RGB-D observations and locomotion signals. A shared multi-task backbone learns common terrain features across training vehicles while vehicle-specific prediction heads preserve platform-dependent responses. At inference, DGT-MAP produces a heading-indexed costmap that can be used by a direction-aware planner. We evaluate DGT-MAP in simulation by integrating it into a Hybrid A* navigation stack and measuring downstream task success on challenging terrains, including slopes that are traversable downhill but not uphill and a ridge obstacle that is traversable by some vehicles, but not by others. Across evaluated tasks, DGT-MAP achieves the highest or tied-highest navigation success rate when compared against geometric, binary, and learned direction-agnostic baselines.

</details>


### [97] [Policy-Calibrated DAgger: Offline Calibrated Noise Injection for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30462)
*Jenny Wang,George Kantor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: tldr: 提出 Policy-Calibrated DAgger，利用扩散（生成式）策略自身预测的动作分布，在离线状态下估计策略的噪声水平与闭环误差，从而自动标定 DAgger 采样所需的噪声幅度，避免在线探索带来的安全隐患与人工噪声扫描。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: motivation: 模仿学习策略在闭环执行中会累积误差，导致机器人偏离训练分布（covariate shift）。DAgger 及后续方法通过在策略失败处采集额外数据来缓解这一问题，但存在两个痛点：(1) 若让机器人主动探索失败区域，可能使机器人处于不安全状态；(2) 在带噪专家演示中采集数据需要人为选择一个合适的噪声分布/幅度，而最优噪声水平通常依赖经验扫描，代价高昂。作者希望在不进行在线探索、也不做噪声水平超参搜索的前提下，自动确定合适的噪声标定。

Method: method: 核心思路是利用近期扩散策略（diffusion policy）的生成特性来离线估计策略噪声。(1) 在专家轨迹上的各个观测处，测量扩散策略预测动作分布的"离散程度"（spread），作为策略不确定性的代理；(2) 将该离散度与策略沿专家轨迹闭环执行时相对录制轨迹的误差进行关联，从而在离线阶段推断出合适的噪声水平。(3) 针对多模态动作空间中误差度量困难的问题，提出在闭环控制时通过"部分去噪（partial denoising）"将策略引导至参考轨迹，并利用扩散模型的性质对测量到的误差进行"反归一化（unnormalize）"，还原为未引导情形下的等效误差。这样既能在引导下获得稳定的误差估计，又能保持对真实策略行为的忠实性。整体流程无需在线 rollout 采集失败数据，也无需对噪声水平做 sweep。

Result: result: 实验在两个环境中进行：一个为 3D 逼真仿真器，任务是让机器人在杂乱且狭窄的环境中到达发动机拉杆（engine lever）；另一个为 2D 平面 reacher 环境。评估比较对象包括：不做加噪的数据集聚合（dataset aggregation without noising）策略，以及"事后最优"噪声水平（best noise level in hindsight）的策略。结果显示，所提方法优于不加噪的数据集聚合方法，并且在不进行噪声水平扫描的情况下，性能可匹配事后最优噪声水平的效果。说明其离线标定的噪声估计能够有效替代人工噪声调参。

Conclusion: conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出一种基于生成式（扩散）策略自身动作分布特性的离线噪声标定方法，用于指导 DAgger 式数据聚合，规避了在线探索的安全风险与噪声分布选择的超参难题。方法在杂乱狭窄环境的到达任务与 2D reacher 任务上验证有效。潜在局限包括：方法依赖扩散/生成式策略的特定性质，对非生成式策略的适用性未明确；误差反归一化依赖扩散模型的建模假设；实验规模相对有限（两个任务、均为仿真），在真实机器人、多模态复杂操作任务上的泛化性、以及对专家轨迹质量与观测覆盖范围的敏感性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Policies trained with imitation learning can accumulate errors over time, causing the robot to drift outside the training distribution. Existing methods mitigate this covariate shift by collecting additional data where the policy fails or is likely to fail. The first places the robot in unsafe conditions and the second requires choosing an appropriate noise distribution to collect new expert demonstrations under that noise. We propose Policy-Calibrated DAgger, a method that makes use of the properties of recent generative policies to estimate the policy's noise offline by using its own predicted action distribution. We measure a diffusion policy's spread of predicted actions at observations along the expert trajectory and measure its closed-loop error relative to a recorded trajectory. To address issues with measuring error in a multimodal action space, we guide the policy towards the trajectory during closed-loop control through partial denoising, and use properties of a diffusion model to unnormalize the measured error as if we did not guide it. We experiment in a scenario where a robot is tasked to reach an engine lever in a cluttered and narrow environment and show results in a 3D photorealistic simulator and a 2D planar reacher environment. We show that our method surpasses policies trained with dataset aggregation without noising and matches the performance of the best noise level in hindsight, without requiring a sweep over noise levels.

</details>


### [98] [Learning-Based Pressure Predictive Control of a Vertebraic Soft Robotic Tail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30479)
*Wenjian Yang,Nan Huang,Yukang Nie,Fang Chen,Wanchao Chi,Jiansheng Dai,Sicong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于LSTM的压力预测控制方法（PPC），用于脊椎式软体机器人尾巴的运动控制及其与四足机器人的协调，实现非静态与准静态运动控制并提升实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人因安全的人机交互能力和柔顺性而备受关注，但其连续体结构和非线性材料特性使运动学建模复杂，尤其是在非静态运动下建模困难。软体尾巴要实现与环境交互及与四足机器人协调运动，需要准确且实时的控制模型，而传统纯逆运动学模型难以应对非线性与动态响应需求。

Method: 提出LSTM-based压力预测控制（PPC），由三部分组成：逆运动学（IK）模型、前向运动学（FK）模型和压力补偿（P-comp）模型。IK与FK建模软体尾巴的运动映射，P-comp模型基于LSTM对压力进行补偿与预测，从而同时支持非静态和准静态运动控制。在软尾-四足协调运动中使用预测数据集训练PPC，实现下一时刻动作预测。

Result: 相比仅使用IK的模型，PPC在执行目标轨迹时仿真轨迹的平均RMSE降低69.8%。在软尾-四足协调运动中，采用预测数据集训练的PPC可实现下一时刻动作预测，计算时间减少60.9%，使尾巴的实时响应与四足躯干运动速率相匹配。

Conclusion: PPC为软体尾巴在非静态与准静态运动控制下提供了一种简单有效的建模方法，并赋予软尾-四足系统与环境交互的功能。主要局限在于实验以仿真轨迹评估为主，真实物理平台的泛化性与长期稳定性尚需进一步验证。

Abstract: Soft robots have attracted much attention for their safe human-robot interaction and flexibility, but the typical continuum structure and nonlinear material behavior make the kinematics modelling complex, especially in non-static motions. In this work, we proposed an LSTM-based pressure predictive control (PPC) for the motion control of a vertebraic soft robotic tail and the coordination with a quadruped robot. The PPC consists of an inverse kinematics (IK) model, a forward kinematics (FK) model and a pressure compensation (P-comp) model, and achieves non-static and quasi-static motion control of the tail. Compared with the IK-only model, the average RMSE of the PPC's simulation trajectories reduces by 69.8%, when executing target trajectories. In the coordinated motions of the soft tail quadruped, using a prediction data set to train the PPC enables next-moment action prediction and reduces computation time by 60.9%, which enhances the real-time response of the tail to match the quadruped torso's moving rate. The PPC provides a simple and effective method to model the soft tail for both non-static and quasi-static motion control, and grants the soft tail quadruped with the functionality of interacting with the environment.

</details>


### [99] [Memory-Aware Multi-Sensor Perception for Efficient and Safe Navigation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30495)
*Jingshuo Li,Yifan Xue,Yifei Li,Shubhodeep Shiv Aditya,Nadia Figueroa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种具备记忆能力的多传感器自主导航框架，融合LiDAR与RGB感知、在线距离场表征学习以及阶段自适应调制控制障碍函数二次规划（MCBF-QP），实现对静态基础设施的持久表征与动态障碍物跟踪，从而利用历史观测几何提升导航效率与避障能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知环境中的自主导航需同时满足有效感知、可持续的环境表征、避障以及朝目标推进等要求。现有基于感知的方法通常依赖先验地图或仅利用短时程观测，难以利用此前已观测到的环境结构信息，导致在窄通道、动态障碍物等复杂场景下导航效率与目标到达能力受限。该问题对机器人在室内外复杂环境中的长时程自主运行具有重要意义。

Method: 提出记忆感知的多传感器导航框架，核心包含三部分：（1）LiDAR与RGB多模态感知融合，兼顾几何与视觉信息；（2）在线距离场表征学习，持续构建环境表征，其中对静态基础设施进行持久化建模，同时对动态障碍物进行跟踪，使系统能够复用已观测几何；（3）阶段自适应调制控制障碍函数二次规划（MCBF-QP）控制器，根据局部条件自适应调整安全约束与导引项，并将记忆中的几何信息用于障碍物绕行。该方法将感知记忆、表征学习与安全控制耦合为统一框架。

Result: 在复杂室内与室外环境中进行了实验评估，主要考察导航效率与目标到达性能，同时验证碰撞避免能力。实验结果表明，所提框架在窄通道与动态障碍物场景下能够在保持避障的同时提升导航效率与目标到达表现，体现出利用历史观测几何与阶段自适应安全约束的优势。（摘要未提供具体数据集名称、定量指标数值与基线对比细节。）

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于将多传感器感知、持久化环境记忆表征与阶段自适应MCBF-QP控制相结合，使机器人能够利用此前观测到的结构信息进行避障与导航，提升了复杂室内外环境中的导航效率与目标到达性能并保持碰撞避免。局限性方面，摘要未给出定量对比与消融结果，记忆机制在高度动态或大尺度环境中的可扩展性、在线距离场表征学习的计算开销以及实时性表现仍有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in previously unseen environments requires effective perception, persistent environmental representation, and collision avoidance while maintaining progress toward a goal. Existing perception-based methods often rely on prior maps or short-horizon observations, limiting their ability to exploit previously observed structure. We propose a memory-aware multi-sensor navigation framework that integrates LiDAR and RGB perception, online distance-field representation learning, and a stage-adaptive Modulated Control Barrier Function Quadratic Program (MCBF-QP). The framework persistently represents static infrastructure while tracking dynamic obstacles, enabling the MCBF-QP controller to exploit previously observed geometry for obstacle circumvention and adapt its safety constraints and guidance to local conditions. Experiments in complex indoor and outdoor environments demonstrate improved navigation efficiency and goal-reaching performance while maintaining collision avoidance in narrow passages and around dynamic obstacles.

</details>


### [100] [Tactile Sensing Array for Multi-Phalanx Sensing in Humanoid Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30506)
*Neel Adwani,Muhaiminul Islam Akash,Rituja Bhattacharya,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于Velostat压阻材料与导电胶带的低成本人形手指触觉阵列，覆盖指尖（含2×3矩阵包裹式接触点+尖端单点）及近端、中端指节，并通过3D打印柔性接触结构（带气隙与凸起）将释放后恢复时间缩短74%，验证了不同接触几何（平面、边缘、角点）产生可区分的激活模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形手抓取与操作需要覆盖整个指体的触觉反馈，而非仅指尖。视觉与本体感知在手指自遮挡相机视野时无法可靠提供抓取接触信息。现有方案多聚焦指尖或成本较高，作者希望以低成本、易制造的方式实现全指段的接触感知，解决抓取时的遮挡与接触状态缺失问题。

Method: 传感器使用Velostat压阻材料与导电胶带构成触觉阵列。指尖含7个接触点：一个跨越前、左、右三面的2×3矩阵，加一个尖端单独接触点；近端与中端指节各有一条前置接触线。通过在3D打印柔性接触结构中引入气隙（gap）与凸起（bump）来改善力学响应，该设计受既有工作启发。评估通过重复加载与按压-释放测试测量迟滞与释放后恢复时间，并对比平坦接触基线。

Result: 实验包括重复加载与按压-释放测试，测量传感器迟滞与释放后恢复时间。带气隙与凸起的柔性接触结构相较平坦接触基线将恢复时间降低74%。指尖在平面、边缘、角点三类接触几何下产生不同的激活模式；抓握过程中传感器在三个指节（指尖、近端、中端）均记录到接触。作者同时讨论了制造工艺修改的局限，并提出以软件补偿作为缓解残余迟滞的后续方向。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是低成本、可制造的全指段触觉阵列设计及其接触几何判别能力，并通过结构设计显著改善释放恢复时间。意义在于为受遮挡影响的人形手抓取提供指体级触觉反馈。局限在于残余迟滞尚未在硬件层面根本解决，仅部分通过结构优化缓解，作者建议未来依靠软件补偿。

Abstract: Humanoid hands require tactile feedback across the whole finger, not just the fingertip, to grasp and manipulate objects properly. Vision and proprioception alone cannot reliably provide this information, particularly when the hand's own fingers occlude the camera's view of the grasp. We present a low-cost tactile array for a humanoid finger, made from Velostat and conductive tape. The fingertip carries seven contact points including a 2x3 matrix wrapped across its front, left, and right faces, and a separate contact point at the tip. The proximal and middle phalanges each carry a single front-facing contact line. We measure the sensor's hysteresis and recovery time after release, through repeated loading and press-release tests. We also test a compliant, 3D-printed contact structure with a gap and a bump, inspired by similar designs in prior work, and show it cuts recovery time by 74% compared to a flush-contact baseline. We then show the sensor can produce distinct activation patterns for different contact geometries (flat, edge, corner) at the fingertip, and that it registers contact across all three phalanges during a grip. Finally, we discuss the limits of our fabrication changes and point to software-based compensation as a promising way to more directly fix the remaining hysteresis in the future.

</details>


### [101] [Aerial Manipulation in the Wild with Onboard Perception, Policy Learning, and Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30521)
*Yuanzhu Zhan,Yufei Jiang,Zemu Zhang,Junyi Geng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个真实户外空中操作框架，融合模仿学习（Diffusion Policy）、机载LiDAR-惯性状态估计与全身模型预测控制（MPC），摆脱对动作捕捉系统的依赖，在实体空中操作平台上完成户外操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 户外空中操作同时面临可靠状态估计、稳定空中运动与精确操作三大挑战，且需在外部扰动下保持鲁棒。既有工作多依赖室内动作捕捉系统提供状态，难以扩展到户外真实环境；而户外缺乏外部定位基础设施、存在扰动与平台-机械臂耦合问题，因此需要一套完全机载、可端到端执行的操作框架。这一问题对无人机自主作业、非结构化环境机器人感知与操作具有重要意义。

Method: 框架由三部分组成：(1) 基于演示的模仿学习：从操作演示中训练 Diffusion Policy，以机载观测为输入生成期望的末端执行器运动指令，利用扩散模型的生成式多模态特性建模操作行为分布；(2) 状态估计：采用机载 LiDAR-惯性里程计（LiDAR-inertial odometry）提供户外无外部动捕条件下的位姿估计；(3) 全身控制：使用 whole-body MPC 联合协调空中平台与机械臂，将学习到的末端执行器指令转化为期望轨迹并实时执行。核心思想是"学习生成指令 + 模型基全身控制执行"的分层解耦，从而兼顾学习策略的灵活性与模型控制的稳定性。

Result: 在实体空中操作平台（多旋翼 + 机械臂）上验证完整框架，评估指标为户外操作任务的成功执行情况（任务层面的成功率与任务完成）。实验表明，演示驱动的操作策略能够与机载状态估计及基于模型的全身控制有效集成，成功完成户外操作任务，证明该方案可超越受控室内环境而适用于户外场景。文中未给出具体任务数量、成功率数值或对比基线的细节。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于构建并实体验证了一套系统级户外空中操作框架，将扩散策略模仿学习、LiDAR-惯性机载状态估计与全身MPC统一整合，消除了对动作捕捉基础设施的依赖，推动了空中操作向真实户外环境扩展。局限在于：摘要未量化性能与对比基线，尚未明确展示其相对纯模型控制或纯学习方法的优势；Demo驱动的策略泛化性与对未见场景的鲁棒性、LiDAR-惯性里程计在剧烈机动下的漂移问题以及端到端实时性等仍待进一步验证。

Abstract: Aerial manipulation in outdoor environments remains challenging due to the simultaneous requirements of reliable state estimation, stable aerial motion, and precise manipulation under external disturbances. In this work, we present a real-world outdoor aerial manipulation framework that integrates imitation learning, onboard LiDAR-inertial state estimation, and whole-body model predictive control. A Diffusion Policy is trained from manipulation demonstrations to generate desired end-effector motions from onboard observations. These learned commands are executed by a whole-body MPC that jointly coordinates the aerial platform and manipulator to realize the desired end-effector trajectory. To eliminate reliance on external motion-capture infrastructure, the platform employs onboard LiDAR-inertial odometry for state estimation during outdoor operation. We validate the complete framework on a physical aerial manipulator and demonstrate successful execution of outdoor manipulation tasks. The experimental results show that demonstration-driven manipulation policies can be effectively integrated with onboard state estimation and model-based whole-body control to enable aerial manipulation beyond controlled indoor environments.

</details>


### [102] [Containing Behavioral Cascades from Manipulated Claims in LLM-Powered Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30523)
*Waleed Bin Khalid,Byung-Cheol Min*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对LLM驱动的多机器人系统易受语义操纵的问题，提出一种主动验证框架（Verify-Adapt-Hold，即验证-适应-保持），在成功操纵发生后遏制其级联物理后果。核心思想是将被妥协后的验证问题从"二元信任决策"重新表述为"团队级规划问题"，通过让部分机器人主动巡察关键区域、部分机器人临时适应、其余机器人保持可信计划，阻止错误世界状态声明在机器人车队中引发全局行为级联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的多机器人系统依赖语言模型进行任务规划与协作，但其对世界状态的语义理解易被注入虚假声明（如假障碍物声明）所操纵。单个被接受的错误世界状态声明可能触发车队级行为级联，导致不必要的重规划、路径成本上升、拥堵甚至任务看似不可行。现有方法通常将此类问题简化为对声明的二元信任/不信任判断，无法有效控制操纵在物理执行层面的传播。因此，需要在操纵已成功的前提下（conditioning on a successful manipulation），设计一种能在下游影响扩散前将其局部化的机制，这对多机器人协作的鲁棒性与可靠性具有重要意义。

Method: 论文提出主动验证框架，核心是一个专用验证模块，生成结构化的"Verify-Adapt-Hold"三阶段计划：1) Verify（验证）：选定部分机器人前往并检查具有后果性/关键性的区域，以核实被质疑的世界状态声明；2) Adapt（适应）：仅让有限的机器人子集在必要时临时调整其计划；3) Hold（保持）：其余机器人维持其原有可信计划不变。该设计将后妥协验证建模为团队级规划问题，而非对单个声明的二元信任决策，从而在局部化验证成本的同时限制级联传播。

Result: 论文在多机器人运输（multi-robot transportation）环境中进行评测，通过注入不同影响程度的虚假障碍物声明，并在不同团队规模下测试。评估指标包括：级联遏制（cascade containment）、总成本（Sum-of-Costs）、完工时间（makespan）和覆盖率（coverage ratio）。结果表明，将后妥协验证视为团队级规划问题能有效限制语义操纵带来的级联物理后果。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出主动验证框架及Verify-Adapt-Hold结构化计划，将被妥协后的验证从二元信任决策重构为团队级规划问题，有效遏制语义操纵在多机器人车队中的级联传播。其意义在于提升LLM驱动多机器人系统对语义操纵的鲁棒性。潜在局限包括：评测仅基于仿真运输环境与注入的虚假障碍物声明，未涉及真实硬件；虚假声明类型和攻击模型相对单一；框架对验证资源的分配与验证模块本身可能引入额外开销，其对更复杂任务与更广泛语义攻击的泛化能力仍有待验证。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-powered multi-robot systems are vulnerable to semantic manipulation: an accepted false world-state claim can trigger a fleet-wide behavioral cascade, causing unnecessary replanning, increased path costs, congestion, or apparent mission infeasibility. Conditioning on a successful manipulation, we propose an active verification framework that contains its downstream effects before they propagate across the fleet. A dedicated verification module generates a structured Verify-Adapt-Hold plan: selected robots inspect consequential regions, a limited subset provisionally adapts when necessary, and the remaining robots retain their trusted plans. We evaluate the framework in a multi-robot transportation environment using injected false obstacle claims across different impacts and team sizes. Evaluation measures cascade containment, Sum-of-Costs, makespan, and coverage ratio. Results show that treating post-compromise verification as a team-level planning problem, rather than a binary trust decision, effectively limits the cascading physical consequences of semantic manipulation.

</details>


### [103] [A Long-Legged, Direct-Drive Monopedal Robot Achieves Exceptional Jump Height](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30530)
*Gihyeok Na,Justin K. Yim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种由单条长腿、直接驱动传动驱动的跳跃机器人，无弹性储能即可实现7.6 m高、190 ms蹬地时间的快速跳跃，整机仅281 g，在同类型电力驱动、无弹簧系统中达到最高跳跃高度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跳跃是机器人越过障碍、快速机动的关键能力，但传统方案多依赖弹性储能（弹簧/储能腿）来放大功率，这带来结构复杂、能量释放时序难以控制、重复性受限等问题。如何在无弹性储能的条件下，让小功率电机驱动长腿实现高且快的跳跃，是可移动机器人机动性研究中的重要挑战；同时跳跃过程中的姿态控制（地面起跳与空中姿态调节）也难以在单腿结构上实现。

Method: 核心创新包括：（1）采用直接驱动（direct-drive）传动，去除弹性储能环节，由单条长腿完成整个蹬地行程；（2）提出新型"织物缠绕传动"（fabric-wrap transmission），可提供变化的机械增益（variable mechanical advantage），使小型电机在整个行程中接近峰值功率输出，同时对细长轻质腿提供抗屈曲（buckling）支撑；（3）在腿顶部安装平衡模块，利用小型螺旋桨控制腿在地面与空中的姿态，长腿结构为控制力矩提供了大反作用力臂，从而以较小推力实现姿态调节。

Result: 机器人总质量281 g，实现7.6 m跳跃高度和190 ms的蹬地时间。作者报告该指标为当前已报道的电力驱动、无弹簧系统中最高跳跃记录。在户外环境中完成了验证实验，包括垂直跳跃、地面与飞行中的姿态控制，以及倾斜地面上的跳跃。

Conclusion: 该工作证明通过变机械增益的织物缠绕传动，可以不依赖弹性储能而实现高、快跳跃，为轻量、简构型的高机动跳跃机器人提供了新思路，并借助螺旋桨平衡模块与长腿力臂解决姿态控制问题。可能的局限在于：单腿结构的地面移动能力（如行走/连续跳跃步态）未体现，负载能力与续航受限于281 g平台上的电机与螺旋桨，且对比的仅为无弹簧电力驱动同类系统。

Abstract: We demonstrate a jumping robot that reaches high (7.6 m) and fast (190 ms stance time) jumps from a single long leg driven by a direct-drive transmission, without elastic energy storage. At 281 g, it achieves the highest jump yet reported for an electrically actuated, spring-free system. The leg uses a new fabric-wrap transmission that provides a variable mechanical advantage, keeping a small electric motor near its peak power output through the stroke while bracing the long, lightweight leg against buckling. A balancing module at the top of the leg uses small propellers to control the leg's orientation on the ground and in the air, where the long leg provides a large moment arm for the control torques. The robot is validated outdoors with vertical jumps, attitude control on the ground and in flight, and tilted jumps.

</details>


### [104] [ST-pRRTC: Parallel Space-Time RRT-C with Adaptive Goal-Time Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30533)
*Duo Zhang,Jintong Li,Junshan Huang,Jingjin Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ST-pRRTC：面向动态障碍物轨迹已知但到达时间未指定的运动规划问题，提出GPU并行的时空RRT-Connect规划器。通过共享前向树与自适应后向目标时间森林（含区间根公式与根回收策略），在保证概率完备性和渐近到达时间最优性的同时，降低平均首次求解时间并提前最终到达时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境下的运动规划需要同时考虑空间与时间维度。当障碍物轨迹已知但机器人的到达时间未指定时，搜索多个可能的到达时间可以扩大时间覆盖范围，但固定的规划预算会被分散到更多的后向树上，导致单棵树的搜索效率下降。现有方法（如ST-RRT*、SI-RRT）在高维时空搜索中面临预算稀释与收敛缓慢的问题，因此需要一种在广泛到达时间范围内仍能高效利用计算资源的时空规划方法，这对机器人在移动障碍物（如无人机群）中安全导航具有重要意义。

Method: ST-pRRTC是一种GPU并行的时空RRT-Connect规划器，核心设计包括：（1）构建单棵共享前向树（shared forward tree）和一组自适应的后向目标时间树森林（adaptive forest of backward goal-time trees），避免为每个到达时间单独建立前向树造成预算稀释；（2）提出区间根公式（interval root formulation），以连续方式采样目标到达时间，并在给定假设与有界时间域下提供概率完备性与渐近到达时间最优性的理论保证；（3）提出实用的根回收策略（root recycling policy），在固定数量的后向树上进行自适应调整——当某个根不再有价值时用新根替换，同时保留已有搜索进展，但该策略不具备上述理论保证。整个方法利用GPU并行加速时空树的扩展与碰撞检测。

Result: 在三个动态基准测试（dynamic benchmarks）上进行评估，与ST-RRT*和SI-RRT对比。在所有方法均能求解的问题上，ST-pRRTC的两种变体（区间根公式与根回收策略）都取得更低的平均首次解时间（mean first-solution time）和更早的平均最终到达时间（mean final arrival）。进一步的实验表明，在广泛的到达时间范围内，根回收策略优于简单的广泛采样。此外，论文通过真实机器人演示验证了有效性：根回收版ST-pRRTC为UR5e机械臂在移动的Crazyflie四旋翼之间规划运动。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出一种GPU并行的时空RRT-Connect规划框架，用共享前向树加自适应后向树森林的结构解决了多到达时间搜索的预算稀释问题，并分别提供了理论保证（区间根公式）和实用高效的启发式（根回收策略）；在动态基准与真机实验中均优于现有方法。局限在于理论保证仅适用于区间根公式版本，根回收策略虽在实验中最有效但缺乏概率完备性与最优性保证；实验规模较小（三个基准、单一真机场景），泛化性与高动态、高维场景下的可扩展性仍待进一步验证。

Abstract: We propose ST-pRRTC, a GPU-parallel space- time RRT-Connect motion planner for problems with known obstacle trajectories and unspecified arrival time. Searching over many arrival times broadens temporal coverage but divides a finite planning budget among more backward trees. To address the challenge, ST-pRRTC builds a shared forward tree and an adaptive forest of backward goal-time trees. Its interval root formulation samples goal arrival times continuously and guarantees probabilistic completeness and asymptotic arrival- time optimality under the stated assumptions in a bounded time domain. The practical root recycling policy has no such guar- antees. It adapts a fixed number of backward trees, replacing later roots while retaining useful search progress. Experiments on three dynamic benchmarks show that both variants achieve lower mean first-solution times and earlier mean final arrivals than ST-RRT* and SI-RRT on problems solved by all compared methods. Further experiments demonstrate the benefit of recy- cling over broad arrival-time ranges. Real-robot demonstrations show root-recycling ST-pRRTC planning motions for a UR5e among moving Crazyflie quadrotors.

</details>


### [105] [GraspTwin: Zero-Shot Task-Oriented Grasp Optimization via a Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30543)
*Daniel J. Evans,Yinlong Dai,Simon Stepputtis,Dylan P. Losey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 GraspTwin，一个 real-to-sim-to-real 框架：基于单次 RGB-D 观测构建环境数字孪生，将大型基础模型（foundation model）给出的任务导向抓取建议视为语义先验，并通过贝叶斯优化（Thompson 采样）+ 域随机化物理仿真rollout 进行局部无梯度优化，从而在真实机器人上实现兼具任务语义与物理可行性的零样本抓取迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人从结构化工厂走向家庭环境，需与种类繁多、形态各异的物体交互，且抓取往往要服务于后续任务。例如"倒咖啡"任务中，机器人必须握住马克杯把手而非杯沿。现有方法存在两类不足：一是传统基于学习的抓取仅追求鲁棒、无碰撞，但基本与任务无关（可能抓杯沿）；二是直接使用基础模型提出"任务合适"的抓取位置，却缺乏细粒度物理接地（physical grounding），导致伸手去抓把手却抓空。因此需要一种既懂任务语义、又保证物理可行的抓取方案。

Method: 核心思路是把基础模型的抓取建议当作语义先验，作为局部无梯度优化的种子。
1) Real-to-Sim：由单张 RGB-D 观测重建环境数字孪生（digital twin）。
2) 语义先验：查询大型基础模型，依据物体 affordance 与任务描述生成任务导向的抓取候选。
3) 优化：采用贝叶斯优化，并用 Thompson 采样批量抽取邻近位姿（nearby poses），在域随机化（domain-randomized）物理仿真中进行并行 rollout 评估，以筛选出既鲁棒又物理合理的抓取。
4) Sim-to-Real：将优化结果直接部署到真实机械臂执行。整个流程为无需额外真机数据训练的零样本迁移。

Result: 实验在真实机器人任务导向抓取场景中进行。方法实现零样本 real-world 迁移，整个流程仅需数分钟，任务导向抓取成功率相较其他 SOTA 流程最高提升 33%。代码已开源（https://github.com/VT-Collab/GraspTwin/）。摘要未给出具体数据集名称与更细粒度指标，需参考原文。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出 real-to-sim-to-real 框架，将基础模型抓取建议重构为语义先验种子并结合 Thompson 采样贝叶斯优化与域随机化物理仿真，兼顾任务语义与物理可行性，实现快速零样本真机迁移并显著提升任务导向抓取成功率。潜在局限：依赖单视角 RGB-D 重建的数字孪生精度、物理仿真的域差距；基础模型先验错误可能影响优化初值；摘要未报告对多种任务/物体的泛化边界。

Abstract: As robots transition from structured factory settings into homes, they are required to interact with an ever-increasing variety of objects. Many tasks require grasping, and often it is not sufficient to just pick up the target object. Consider a task like "pouring coffee" --- to facilitate the subsequent pouring, the robot should grasp the mug by its handle. Existing learning-based approaches for grasping either find robust and collision-free grasps that are largely agnostic to the task (e.g., picking up the mug by its rim), or leverage foundation models to propose task-appropriate grasp locations that lack fine-grained physical grounding (e.g., reaching for and missing the handle). In this work, we bridge these approaches with a real-to-sim-to-real framework. Based on a single RGB-D observation, we construct a digital twin of the environment, query a large foundation model to propose grasps that align with the object's affordances and task description, and then optimize the proposals to ensure robustness and plausibility before executing the result on the real robot. Our key insight is that the grasp proposals of the foundation model should be regarded as semantic priors that serve as seeds for local, gradient-free optimization. We leverage Bayesian optimization with Thompson sampling to draw batches of nearby poses, which are subsequently evaluated in parallel under domain-randomized physics rollouts. The resulting grasp is both task-oriented and physically feasible for execution by the robot arm. Our full zero-shot real-world transfer only takes a few minutes and improves task-oriented grasping success by up to 33% as compared to other state-of-the-art pipelines. Our code is available here: https://github.com/VT-Collab/GraspTwin/

</details>


### [106] [Privacy-Preserving Prompted Policy Search for Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30554)
*Ali Irshayyid,Feng Lin,Chong Li,Jun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PP-ProPS 是一个隐私保护的 LLM 引导策略搜索框架，通过客户端秘密变换对策略参数和奖励进行编码，使云端 LLM API 仅能看到编码后的策略参数与缩放后的奖励信息，同时通过提供分项奖励和有界历史提升搜索效率，在 MuJoCo、经典控制、高速驾驶与机械臂任务上超过 Vanilla ProPS 及 PPO/SAC/TRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究显示，LLM 可作为强化学习中的上下文策略优化器，利用数值奖励信号与自然语言推理共同驱动策略搜索。但这类方法在落地时需将原始策略参数与奖励历史上传至云端 LLM API，导致专有控制策略暴露给第三方服务商，存在隐私泄露风险。此外，Vanilla ProPS 还要求向 LLM 披露真实最优回合回报，进一步加剧了信息暴露。因此，如何在保持 LLM 引导优化能力的前提下，避免策略与环境参数外泄，是一个具有实际部署价值的问题。

Method: 论文提出 Privacy-Preserving Prompted Policy Search（PP-ProPS）。其核心是：在客户端对策略参数与奖励值施加秘密变换（secret client-side transformations）后再写入每次 API 请求，LLM 提供方只能观察到编码后的策略参数和缩放后的奖励信息，无法还原真实控制策略。框架不要求 LLM 知晓真实的最优回合回报（区别于 Vanilla ProPS）。此外，PP-ProPS 做了两点搜索增强：一是向 LLM 提供各个独立的奖励分量而非单一总回报，使候选策略的反馈更具信息量；二是采用有界历史（bounded history）防止提示词无限增长，从而支持高维策略搜索并适配开放权重 LLM。

Result: PP-ProPS 在连续与离散控制任务上进行了评估，覆盖 MuJoCo 多关节接触动力学运动控制、经典控制、高速驾驶（highway driving）以及机械臂操作（robotic arm manipulation）。与 Vanilla ProPS 相比，PP-ProPS 在十个评测任务中的七个上表现更优；在六个任务中有五个超过了 PPO、SAC、TRPO 等传统 RL 方法。

Conclusion: 该工作的主要贡献在于将隐私保护机制引入 LLM 引导的策略搜索流程，通过客户端秘密编码隐藏策略参数与奖励信息，同时以分项奖励和有界历史改进搜索质量，并适配开放权重 LLM。其意义在于为云端 LLM 辅助强化学习提供了可部署的隐私方案。潜在局限方面，摘要未说明编码变换对 LLM 推理能力的具体影响、隐私保证的形式化程度，以及是否在真实网络/攻击场景下验证；此外评测任务规模有限（十项任务），泛化性尚需更多验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated promising capabilities as in-context policy optimizers for Reinforcement Learning (RL), enabling policy search driven by both numerical reward signals and natural language reasoning. However, deploying such methods in practice requires transmitting raw policy parameters and rewards history to cloud-based LLM APIs, exposing proprietary control strategies to third-party service providers. To address this issue, this paper introduces Privacy-Preserving Prompted Policy Search (PP-ProPS), a framework that enables LLM-guided policy optimization while keeping policy and environmental parameters confidential. PP-ProPS encodes policy parameters and reward values using secret client-side transformations before they are included in each API request, ensuring that the LLM provider observes only encoded policy parameters and scaled reward information. Furthermore, unlike Vanilla ProPS, the proposed framework does not require the true optimal episodic return to be known or disclosed to the LLM. Beyond protecting the optimization data, PP-ProPS improves the search process in two ways. First, it provides the LLM with individual reward components instead of only a single total return, offering more informative feedback about each candidate policy. Second, it uses a bounded history that prevents the prompt from growing indefinitely, improving search with high-dimensional policies and supporting the use of open-weight LLMs. The proposed PP-ProPS is evaluated on both continuous and discrete control problems spanning Multi-Joint dynamics with Contact (MuJoCo) locomotion, classic control, highway driving, and robotic arm manipulation. Compared to Vanilla ProPS, the proposed PP-ProPS outperforms ProPS in seven of the ten evaluated tasks, and surpasses conventional RL methods including PPO, SAC, and TRPO, in five of the six tasks.

</details>


### [107] [Auditing Latent-Space Monitors for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30557)
*Nikhil Kamalkumar Advani,Vishwajeet Shivaji Hogale,Saurav Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文系统审计了"利用模型内部隐表示进行运行时失效监测"这一策略在自动驾驶任务中的有效性。作者在车道线矢量化地图生成（LaneSegNet）与端到端规划（VAD）两个任务上构建帧级失效监测器，发现仅用可观测的输入输出即可达到甚至超过隐表示探针的性能，并在论文内部隐特征后无统计显著提升。据此，作者提出了一套用于检验"隐表示访问增量价值"的评估协议，并开源了逐帧失效端点标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运行时失效监测是保障自动驾驶等安全关键系统可靠性的重要手段。先前工作普遍假设模型内部隐表示携带了输出中不可见的额外信息，从而能够更早、更准地预测失效。然而，这种"隐表示优越性"是否真实存在，还是仅仅因为隐表示间接编码了可观测的输入输出信息，此前缺乏严格的对照验证。若隐表示并无增量价值，则监测系统可以设计得更简单、更易部署（无需访问模型内部），这对工程落地与可解释性评估均有重要意义。论文因此提出对隐监测策略进行系统审计。

Method: 论文采用"对照审计"式方法，在两个自动驾驶任务上构建并比较不同信息可及性的失效监测器：（1）车道线矢量化地图生成任务，使用 LaneSegNet，隐表示探针为监督式 latent probe，同时构建仅使用预测输出的基线监测器；（2）端到端规划任务，使用 VAD，隐表示探针为监督式 planning-latent probe，基线监测器仅使用自车状态、驾驶指令以及规划器预测轨迹。核心创新在于提出了一种评估协议，用于检验在已有可观测输入输出基线的条件下，隐表示访问是否带来统计显著的增量性能，即对 latent access 的边际价值进行假设检验。作者同时发布了逐帧失效端点标签以支持可复现研究。

Result: 实验在自动驾驶任务上以帧级失效预测为目标，采用 AUROC 作为核心指标。LaneSegNet 上，针对高 Chamfer 误差，监督式 latent probe 达到 AUROC 0.780，作者称这是首个面向在线矢量化地图生成的 post-hoc 帧级失效监测器；而仅用预测输出的监测器达到 AUROC 0.825，反超隐表示方案。VAD 上，针对 mean-ADE 失效，监督式 planning-latent probe 达到 AUROC 0.868；而仅使用自车状态、驾驶指令与预测轨迹的基线达到 0.924。在两种任务中，将隐特征加入可观测基线均未带来统计显著的性能提升。该结论在多种规划失效端点（包括依赖非基线可及几何信息的端点）上均保持一致。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献在于揭示：能够从模型内部隐表示预测失效，并不证明该隐表示提供了超越可观测输入输出的有用信息。这一发现对事件监测、可解释性与安全评估等领域具有警示意义，提示研究者必须设置强可观测基线以避免高估隐表示的贡献。作者提出的增量价值评估协议与公开的帧级失效标签为后续研究提供了规范工具。潜在局限包括：审计仅覆盖两个自动驾驶任务（LaneSegNet 与 VAD），结论是否可推广至事件相机感知、目标检测、机器人等更广泛任务尚待验证；且监测器采用监督式探针，其性能可能受探针设计与标注质量影响。

Abstract: Runtime failure monitors can use a model's internal representations to anticipate failures. We audit this monitoring strategy across two autonomous-driving tasks: online vectorized map generation with LaneSegNet and end-to-end planning with VAD. We find that frame-level errors are predictable at inference in both tasks. For LaneSegNet, a supervised latent probe reaches Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic curve (AUROC) 0.780 for high Chamfer error; to our knowledge, this is the first post-hoc frame-level failure monitor for online vectorized map generation. For VAD, a supervised planning-latent probe reaches AUROC 0.868 for mean-ADE failure.
  Our audit shows that internal access is not necessary for strong failure prediction. A monitor using only LaneSegNet's prediction outputs reaches AUROC 0.825, while for VAD, ego state, driving command, and the planner's predicted trajectory reach 0.924 on the same mean-ADE endpoint. Adding latent features to either baseline yields no statistically resolved improvement. This observation persists across a broad suite of planning failure endpoints, including endpoints whose labels depend on geometry unavailable to the non-latent baseline. Thus, predicting failure from an internal representation does not establish that the representation provides useful information beyond observable inputs and outputs. We propose an evaluation protocol for testing the incremental value of latent access and release our per-frame failure endpoint labels.

</details>


### [108] [SoGuDiff: Socially Guided Diffusion for Steerable, Norm-Grounded Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30560)
*Christian Schaible,Haoran Ji,Yash Vardhan Pant,Stephen L. Smith*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于扩散模型的社交导航框架，可在部署时通过连续风格轴（style axes）调节机器人的社交行为，并用可行性投影层将学到的社交行为与运动学可行性与避障解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交化机器人导航不仅需要避障，还需遵循随场景、文化、部署需求而变化的隐式社会规范（如绕行距离、让行方向、对群体让步程度）。传统导航策略（固定奖励的强化学习或人类示范模仿）只学到单一规范行为，缺乏运行时调节接口，难以适配多样的社交情境与部署要求。

Method: 核心是基于扩散模型的导航规划器，将期望的社交风格作为条件输入，从而在部署时调整行为。风格以连续轴（style axes）表示，可独立指定或组合成行为空间，而非离散的基元（primitive）规范；作者定义了如通过距离、让行侧向、对群体让步强度等语义轴。架构上引入"可行性投影层"，将学习到的社交行为与运动学可行性及碰撞避免分离，保证调节风格时不破坏安全约束。

Result: 通过单风格轴的扫描实验，得到一条风格-性能权衡曲线，该曲线在所评估的固定行为基线上严格占优（strictly dominates）。此外，风格差异在真实世界演示中得到复现，验证了框架在仿真与实机上的可迁移性与可调性。（摘要未披露具体数据集、指标数值基线细节。）

Conclusion: 贡献在于将社交导航从单一规范行为扩展为部署时可连续调节的行为空间，并将社交偏好与运动学/安全约束解耦。意义在于为机器人提供可定制的社会交互接口，兼顾安全与多样化社交规范。局限在于摘要未给出具体评估指标、基线细节及大规模实机验证，风格轴的可组合性与泛化性仍需更充分验证。

Abstract: Beyond collision avoidance, socially competent robot navigation requires adherence to implicit social conventions that vary across contexts, cultures, and deployment requirements. Many conventional navigation policies learn a single normative behavior, either through reinforcement learning against a fixed reward function or imitation of human demonstrations, exposing no interface for adjusting that conduct at runtime. We present a diffusion-based navigation framework whose social behavior can be tuned at deployment: a desired style is specified, such as how closely the robot passes, which side it yields to, or how much it defers to groups, and the planner adapts accordingly. Continuous style axes can be followed independently or composed, spanning a behavioral space rather than discrete, primitive-based specifications. A feasibility projection layer separates learned social behavior from kinematic feasibility and collision avoidance. A single-axis sweep illustrates a tradeoff curve that strictly dominates the evaluated fixed-behavior baseline configurations, and stylistic differences are replicated in real-world demonstrations.

</details>


### [109] [MOCHA: Multi-Objective Co-Design using Hypernetwork Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30570)
*Varun Madabushi,Neil Janwani,Maegan Tucker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MOCHA 是首个基于强化学习、用单一网络覆盖机器人设计空间并生成一族 Pareto 最优策略的方法，核心是多目标设计超网络（MDH），配合进化搜索生成设计 Pareto 集，并给出求解通用型机器人设计的高效方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人设计中形态参数与运动/控制策略相互耦合：不同结构尺寸下最优控制策略不同，而多目标（速度、能耗、稳定性等）之间又存在权衡，形成规模庞大的「设计—策略」联合空间。传统做法需为每个设计、每个目标偏好单独训练策略，计算代价高昂，无法在单一模型中表达随设计连续变化的策略族，也难以刻画跨目标的 Pareto 前沿。该问题对可定制机器人、协同设计（co-design）与多目标机器人优化至关重要。

Method: 核心思路是超网络（hypernetwork）架构：MDH 接收机器人设计参数与目标/偏好作为条件输入，输出主策略网络的权重，从而生成针对特定目标与设计定制的策略参数，实现用一个网络表示一族设计相关策略。策略训练采用强化学习。在获得 MDH 后，作者提出用进化搜索在已学习的策略网络上进行设计空间搜索，为每一种目标优先级生成对应的最优「设计—策略」组合，构建 Design Pareto 集。此外还提出一种高效计算通用型（generalist）机器人设计的方法，即在整个目标集合上取得最佳累积性能的设计。

Result: 在两个不同的机器人形态上验证，每种形态含六个设计维度，并在 2–3 个目标上进行实验。结果表明 MDH 能够表示复杂且依赖设计的策略族，进化搜索可有效生成覆盖不同目标优先级的 Design Pareto 集，所提方法同时能给出跨全部目标累积性能最优的通用设计。摘要未给出具体基线对比、量化指标或标准 benchmark 名称。

Conclusion: 贡献在于把超网络与强化学习结合，首次以单一网络表达机器人设计空间上的 Pareto 最优策略族，并提供生成 Design Pareto 集与通用设计的高效流程，把机器人协同设计问题从「逐设计重训练」转为「条件化一次训练 + 搜索」。潜在局限在于：设计维度固定为六维、形态与目标数量有限（2–3 个），进化搜索在大设计空间下的可扩展性与计算成本、以及向真实物理平台迁移的有效性尚待验证。

Abstract: In this work, we present MOCHA, the first, to our knowledge, reinforcement learning based approach to computing a family of Pareto-optimal policies across the design space of a robot using a single network. Specifically, MOCHA leverages the hypernetwork architecture to learn a network that produces specialized network parameters optimized for a given objective and parameterized robot design; we term this a multi-objective design hypernetwork (MDH). We demonstrate the capabilities of MDHs to represent a complex family of design-dependent strategies on two distinct robot morphologies, each with six design dimensions and across 2-3 objectives. Moreover, we propose an approach for efficiently producing a Design Pareto set using evolutionary search of the learned policy network, generating the optimal design-policy combination for each objective prioritization. Lastly, we provide an efficient method for computing generalist robot designs which achieve the best cumulative performance across the entire set of objectives.

</details>


### [110] [HuGo: LLMs as Whole-Body Policy Code Designers for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30594)
*Seoyeon Choi,Shizhao Ye,Nicholas Bui,Aayushi Shrivastava,Kanghyun Ryu,Dhruva Tirumala,Markus Wulfmeier,Negar Mehr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HuGo 提出一种分层式人形机器人全身运动-操作（loco-manipulation）框架：利用大语言模型（LLM）从任务描述直接生成可执行、闭环的高层策略代码，叠加在冻结的低层全身策略之上，并通过数值轨迹与选定视频帧的 rollout 反馈迭代精修代码，从而免除逐任务的奖励设计与示教采集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人要在日常环境中实用，必须完成大量耦合移动与操作的任务。现有方法通常依赖奖励工程或人类示教来获得 loco-manipulation 策略，再针对每个任务单独训练，成本高、难以扩展到新任务。该工作希望摆脱"每个任务都要重新设计奖励或收集示教"的瓶颈，把新任务的获取转化为可由语言描述驱动的代码生成问题。

Method: HuGo（Humanoid policy code Generation）采用分层架构。底层是一个冻结的全身策略（whole-body policy），负责低层运动与平衡控制；高层则由 LLM 根据给定任务描述、观测（observation）与命令（command）规范，生成可执行、闭环的高层策略代码。生成后进入精修（refinement）循环：利用策略 rollout 得到的数值轨迹和精选视频帧构成反馈信号，LLM 据此对代码进行有针对性的更新，迭代改进策略逻辑。关键创新在于以"代码即策略"的方式表达高层任务逻辑，并将多模态（数值 + 视频帧）rollout 反馈用于自动代码迭代。

Result: 在五个仿真任务上、使用两种不同的低层策略进行评测，HuGo 显著优于高层强化学习（RL）基线，并接近基于示教的基线性能。整个流程无需任务级别的奖励设计，也无需收集示教数据。作者进一步展示仿真生成的策略可零样本（zero-shot）迁移到真实硬件；并表明将同一精修循环应用于真实世界 rollout，可在无专家示教、不重新训练策略的情况下进一步提升迁移性能。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出一种无需逐任务奖励工程与示教的人形 loco-manipulation 策略获取范式，通过 LLM 代码生成与 rollout 反馈精修实现可扩展的任务适配，并在仿真与真机迁移上验证有效性。局限方面：摘要聚焦仿真任务与迁移实验，未明确给出真实世界任务的数量与覆盖范围；高层策略依赖底层冻结策略的能力边界，精修依赖 LLM 对多模态反馈的理解，稳健性与任务复杂度上限仍有待进一步验证。

Abstract: For humanoids to be useful in everyday environments, they must perform a wide range of tasks that couple locomotion and manipulation. Existing approaches commonly acquire a loco-manipulation policy through reward engineering or demonstrations followed by task-specific training, making it costly to scale to new tasks. In this work, we propose a hierarchical approach to humanoid loco-manipulation that eliminates these per-task requirements. HuGo, Humanoid policy code Generation, uses a Large Language Model (LLM) to generate executable, closed-loop high-level policy code from a task description on top of a frozen low-level whole-body policy. Given the task, observation, and command specifications, the LLM constructs the task logic in code. HuGo then refines the policy from its rollouts using numerical trajectories and selected video frames to produce feedback and targeted code updates. Across five simulation tasks, using two different low-level policies, HuGo substantially outperforms a high-level reinforcement learning baseline and approaches the performance of a demonstration-based baseline. We achieve this level of performance without task-specific reward design or demonstration collection. We further demonstrate zero-shot transfer of simulation-generated policies to hardware and show that applying the same refinement loop to real-world rollouts can further improve transfer performance without expert demonstrations or policy retraining. Project website is https://iconlab.negarmehr.com/HuGo/

</details>


### [111] [Learning-Accelerated Narrow-Phase Collision Detection via Check Ordering for Sampling-Based Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30599)
*Hao Jiang,Yinghan Wang,Jianping He,Xiaoming Duan*

Main category: cs.RO
