TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于学习的方法，通过优化phase-based碰撞检测中narrow phase的检测顺序来加速碰撞检测。作者首先推导出最小化narrow phase期望时间代价的最优检测排序准则，进而设计基于超网络（hypernetwork）的模型预测物体对碰撞概率以近似该最优顺序，从而在不替换底层几何碰撞检测器的前提下减少检测时间，并提升采样运动规划的效率与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碰撞检测对规划路径的安全性至关重要，但其计算开销对运动规划器而言不可忽略。在常用的phase-based（两阶段）碰撞检测中，broad phase利用层次结构快速剔除明显无碰撞的物体对，narrow phase则对剩余无法确定的物体对进行精细几何检查。虽然broad phase剪枝能有效减少精细检查数量，但narrow phase通常按broad phase返回的默认顺序执行，缺乏对检查顺序的显式优化。在杂乱（cluttered）环境中，broad phase后仍残留大量物体对，narrow phase可能占据总检测时间的显著比例，因此存在进一步加速空间。

Method: 核心思路是优化narrow phase中物体对的检查顺序。作者首先对narrow phase的期望时间代价进行建模，并推导出能够最小化该期望的最优检查排序准则（optimal check-ordering criterion）。由于该准则所需先验（如碰撞概率）难以事先获得，作者设计了一个基于超网络的模型来预测各物体对的碰撞概率，并用其近似最优检查顺序。该顺序用于指导narrow phase中精确网格（mesh）碰撞检查的执行次序，从而在保留原有几何碰撞检测器的基础上降低检测时间，属于对现有碰撞检测流程的加速封装而非替代。

Result: 论文报告了仿真实验结果。结果表明，所提方法能够有效加速phase-based碰撞检测，并提升基于采样的运动规划（sampling-based motion planning）的效率与成功率，尤其在杂乱环境中效果更为明显。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于：提出narrow phase期望时间代价的形式化建模并推导最优检查排序准则；设计超网络模型预测碰撞概率以近似最优顺序；在不替换底层几何碰撞检测器的情况下实现检测加速，并改善运动规划性能。局限性在于：其依赖碰撞概率预测的准确性，需额外学习开销，且摘要中未给出具体数据集、量化指标与大规模真实场景验证。

Abstract: Collision detection is critical for ensuring the safety of planned paths. However, it imposes a non-negligible computational burden on motion planners, motivating extensive studies on collision-detection acceleration. In commonly used phase-based collision-detection methods, the broad phase employs hierarchical structures to rapidly discard object pairs that are clearly collision-free, while the subsequent narrow phase performs detailed collision checks on the remaining object pairs whose collision status cannot be determined by the broad phase. Although these methods effectively reduce the number of detailed checks through broad-phase pruning, the narrow phase is usually executed in the default order returned by the broad phase, with little explicit optimization of the check order. This leaves room for further acceleration, especially in cluttered environments where many object pairs may remain after the broad phase and the narrow phase can account for a significant portion of the total detection time. In this work, we propose a learning-based method to accelerate phase-based collision detection by optimizing the check order in the narrow phase. We first formulate the expected time cost of the narrow phase and derive an optimal check-ordering criterion that minimizes this expectation. Since the priors required by this criterion are difficult to obtain in advance, we design a hypernetwork-based model to predict collision probabilities, which are then used to approximate the optimal check order. The resulting order guides the execution of exact mesh checks in the narrow phase, thereby reducing detection time without replacing the underlying geometric collision checker. Simulation results show that our method effectively accelerates phase-based collision detection and improves the efficiency and success rate of sampling-based motion planning, especially in cluttered environments.

</details>


### [112] [Audit Before You Commit: Locating Belief Failures in Active Identification for One-Shot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30608)
*Mohamed Abouagour,Byung-Cheol Min*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出"审计两条件"框架，将"少量探测后再执行不可逆动作"的决策分解为两个可分离条件：(1) 信念是否仍覆盖真正决定动作的那个坐标（coverage），(2) 用于评估动作的失败模型是否与真实发生的失败一致（failure-model calibration）。作者在真实部署的 probe-then-commit 流水线（粒子信念 + 场景失败评分 + 单次 commit）上分别离线、带真值地审计这两个条件，并据此定位到具体的建模误差（如 2.1 mm 的 tap 边界误差）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在执行不可逆动作前需要决定"证据是否足够"（例如插轴前先轻敲表面探测）。现有方法通常只用一个置信度阈值或统一的失败评分来决定是否 commit，但作者指出这样做混合了两类本质不同的失效来源：信念在关键坐标上已不覆盖真值，以及失败评分未反映实际失败。二者若不分开审计，即使增加探测次数或应用 conformal 校准，决策仍会出错（自信但错误的样本仍能通过 confidence gate）。

Method: 核心是一个可离线、带真值、逐条件审计的框架，作用于已部署的 probe-then-commit 流水线，该流水线由三部分构成：粒子滤波信念（particle belief）、场景失败评分（scenario failure score）、单次 commit 决策。审计分别度量：(i) 真值是否落在信念支撑集（support）之内，用"真值离开支撑"的episode比例量化；(ii) 失败评分与已实现失败（realized failure）之间的偏差（optimistic bias）。作者还引入 conformal calibration 作为对照，用于验证"仅恢复 coverage 并不修复决策"。审计产生的"签名"（signature）被用来反向定位误差来源，例如指向观测模型（observation model），并进一步通过 hand scan 发现 tap 边界处 2.1 mm 的误差；在另一引擎中同一审计则指向 execution-model mismatch。

Result: 在模拟 insertion 任务中：增加 tap 次数会锐化信念，但真值在 16.9% 的 episode 中离开信念支撑，失败评分乐观偏差达 0.31。Conformal calibration 能恢复 coverage 却不能修复决策——自信而错误的实例仍通过 confidence gate。修正 tap 边界那一个 2.1 mm 的误差后，未触碰过的实例上 failure 从 0.354 降至 0.112，并在不重新拟合的情况下迁移到第二个引擎；在第三个引擎上同一审计则提示执行模型不匹配。跨三个引擎、七个任务族，在固定执行器下少量探测即可降低 miss 或 failure。在物理机械臂将工具以触觉插入刚性孔（rigid pocket）的实验中，收益与两条件审计均可复现；将录制的 tap 数据在注入模型误差后回放，也能在真实数据上重现审计签名。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于把 probe-then-commit 的决策问题拆成"信念覆盖"与"失败模型一致性"两个可独立审计的条件，并证明分开审计能定位到比置信度阈值或 conformal 校准更根本的错误来源（观测模型的毫米级参数误差、执行模型不匹配），修正后显著降低失败率且具备跨引擎迁移性，并在真实机械臂的触觉插入任务上复现。意义在于为不可逆动作前的少量探测提供了可诊断、可定位的审计方法。潜在局限：整体仍是特定流水线（粒子信念 + 场景失败评分 + 单次 commit）的离线带真值审计，依赖可获得的 ground truth；"固定执行器"与部分实验为仿真，真实物理复现仅限单一刚性插入任务；边界误差修正的收益在不同引擎间表现不一致（第三个引擎指向执行模型而非观测模型），说明审计签名到具体修复之间的映射仍依任务/引擎而定。

Abstract: A robot that probes a few times before one irreversible action, such as tapping a surface before inserting a peg, must decide when the evidence is enough to commit. We argue that this decision rests on two conditions that existing methods do not separate: the belief must still cover the truth in the coordinate that decides the action, and the failure model that scores actions must track realized failure. We audit both conditions separately, offline and with ground truth, on a deployed probe-then-commit pipeline: a particle belief, a scenario failure score, and one commit. On simulated insertion, more taps sharpen the belief while the truth leaves its support on 16.9% of episodes and the failure score turns optimistic by 0.31. Conformal calibration restores coverage but not the decision: confidently wrong instances still pass a confidence gate. The audit's signatures instead point at the observation model, where a hand scan finds a 2.1 mm error in the tap boundary. Correcting that one number cuts failure from 0.354 to 0.112 on untouched instances and transfers unrefitted to a second engine, while in a third engine the same audit suggests an execution-model mismatch instead. Across seven task families in three engines, a few probes at a fixed executor reduce miss or failure. On a physical arm inserting a tool into a rigid pocket by touch, the gain and the audit's two conditions reproduce, and replaying the recorded taps under an injected model error shows the audit's signature on real data. Additional materials are available at https://sites.google.com/view/auditbeforeyoucommit.

</details>


### [113] [Frequency-Modulated Piezoelectric Haptic Display](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30626)
*Boyuan Liang,Lingfeng Sun,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于压电振动致动器的频率调制（FM）触觉显示方案，并设计了"共享源频率调制"（Shared-Source Frequency Modulation, SSFM）结构：多个触觉像素共享同一功率放大器，而通过频率而非振幅来编码触觉强度，从而缓解高密度、大面积可穿戴触觉显示在驱动系统复杂度和体积上的扩展瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉显示多采用振幅调制（AM）：即通过单个触觉像素的形变振幅来编码触觉强度。虽然 AM 方式可实现紧凑的触觉像素，但若要独立控制大量像素的形变振幅，就需要为每个像素配备独立的驱动/放大通路，导致驱动系统愈发复杂、笨重，难以扩展到高密度、大面积的可穿戴触觉显示。论文因此探索以振动频率编码触觉强度的 FM 设计原理，试图在保持像素紧凑的同时简化驱动架构。

Method: 论文研究将触觉强度通过振动频率进行编码的 FM 设计原理，而非传统的形变振幅编码。在此基础上提出 SSFM（Shared-Source Frequency Modulation）结构：多个触觉像素由同一个公共功率放大器供电，但每个像素的振动频谱可被单独控制，从而无需在每个像素处配置独立的高功率放大器，降低了驱动系统的复杂度与体积。作者构建了一个概念验证（proof-of-concept）的压电触觉显示原型，用于渲染空间与时间触觉图案。

Result: 作者通过志愿者实验对所构建的原型进行评估，考察其对空间触觉图案和时间触觉图案的渲染能力。结果显示，在所研究的工作范围内，被试能够可靠地区分由 FM 原理编码的空间图案与时间图案。这表明基于 FM 的分布式触觉渲染具有可行性。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出并验证了以频率调制编码触觉强度的设计思路，以及可让多像素共享功率放大器的 SSFM 架构，为未来高密度、大面积可穿戴触觉显示提供了一条更紧凑驱动架构的潜在路径。局限在于：工作仅基于概念验证原型和有限工作范围内的志愿者主观测试，尚未在大规模像素阵列、定量物理指标或实际可穿戴场景中得到验证；文中也未给出与 AM 方案的直接定量对比。

Abstract: We present a frequency-modulated (FM) haptic display based on piezoelectric vibrating actuators. Existing haptic displays commonly encode haptic intensity through the deformation amplitude of individual haptic pixels. Although amplitude-modulated (AM) approaches have enabled compact haptic pixels, independently controlling the deformation amplitude of a large number of pixels can require increasingly complex and bulky driving systems, posing challenges for scaling toward high-density, large-area wearable displays. To address this scaling challenge, we investigate an FM design principle in which haptic intensity is encoded through vibration frequency. We further develop a \textit{Shared-Source Frequency Modulation} (SSFM) structure in which multiple haptic pixels are powered by a common power amplifier while their vibration spectrum are controlled individually, reducing the need for independent high-power amplification at each pixel. A proof-of-concept piezoelectric haptic display was built and evaluated on rendering spatial and temporal haptic patterns through volunteer tests. The results show that participants reliably distinguished spatial and temporal patterns encoded using FM principles within the investigated operating range. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of FM-based distributed haptic rendering and suggest a potential pathway toward more compact driving architectures for future high-density, large-area wearable haptic displays.

</details>


### [114] [MR. POP: Multi-Robot Parallel Optimizing Planner for Almost-Surely Asymptotically Optimal Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30644)
*Chih H. Huang,Roy Xing,Brian Plancher,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MR. POP 是一种基于 GPU 的几乎必然渐近最优（a.s.a.o.）多机器人运动规划器，它结合 dRRT 与 AO-x 元算法，利用大规模 SIMT 并行同时执行数百次路线图构建与树搜索迭代，并并行化最近邻搜索与碰撞检测，从而在高达 35 自由度的多机器人系统中实现 100% 求解率并优于现有 a.s.a.o. 规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人运动规划中寻找全局最优路径仍是根本性难题。尽管基于 CPU 并行化的 a.s.a.o. 规划器在保证概率收敛的同时提升了计算性能，但它们仍难以扩展到多机器人场景。核心矛盾在于：既要维持渐近最优的概率收敛保证，又要具备足够的计算性能来处理多机器人系统带来的高维构型空间，而现有 CPU 并行方案在可扩展性上受限。

Method: MR. POP 以 dRRT（离散快速扩展随机树）和 AO-x 元算法为基础构建 GPU 加速的 a.s.a.o. 多机器人规划器。其关键技术在于利用 GPU 的大规模 SIMT（单指令多线程）并行性，同时运行数百次路线图构建与树搜索迭代；底层的最近邻搜索和碰撞检测操作也实现了并行化，以支撑高通量的采样与搜索流程。该方法本质上将原本串行/CPU 并行的 a.s.a.o. 搜索循环映射到 GPU 线程级并行之上。

Result: 论文报告 MR. POP 是唯一在高达 35-DOF 多机器人系统中达到 100% 求解率、同时速度快于当前最先进 a.s.a.o. 规划器的方法。此外，MR. POP 通过生成高质量、多样化的种子，提升了下游运动优化器的成功率，例如将成功率从 4% 提升到 72%，有助于避免陷入局部极小值。

Conclusion: MR. POP 的主要贡献是将 dRRT 与 AO-x 框架通过 GPU SIMT 并行实现高效多机器人 a.s.a.o. 规划，兼顾渐近最优的收敛保证与强计算性能，并可通过高质量种子改善下游优化。其意义在于推动 a.s.a.o. 规划器向高自由度多机器人系统的规模化应用。潜在局限在于方法高度依赖 GPU 并行资源，且作为规划工作主要面向运动规划任务，未涉及感知或不确定性建模等方向。

Abstract: Finding globally optimal paths remains a fundamental challenge in multi-robot motion planning. Despite acceleration of almost-surely asymptotically optimal (a.s.a.o.) planners via CPU-based parallelism, achieving both probabilistic convergence guarantees and strong computational performance, these algorithms still struggle to scale to multi-robot settings. As such, we introduce MR. POP, a GPU-based a.s.a.o. multi-robot planner based on dRRT and the AO-x meta-algorithm. MR. POP uses large-scale GPU-based SIMT-parallelism to simultaneously run hundreds of roadmap construction and tree search iterations with underlying parallel nearest neighbor search and collision checking operations. We show that this enables MR. POP to become the only planner achieving a 100% solve rate while being faster than state-of-the-art a.s.a.o. planners in multi-robot systems up to 35-DOF. MR. POP also raises the success rate of downstream motion optimizers (e.g., from 4% to 72%), by creating high-quality, diverse seeds that help avoid local minima.

</details>


### [115] [Can a Robot Read Braille? - Learning to Adapt Contact via Imitation Learning for Tactile Braille Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30676)
*Xi Chen,Yunlong Shan,Sihan Chen,Jun Hu,Zhongxuan Li,Shiyao Zhang,Sichao Liu,Zhong Zhao,Kosta Jovanovic,Peng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Paper proposes an adaptive-contact framework for robotic tactile Braille reading that assesses and physically corrects contact quality before recognition, using Multi-Head Policy Learning with expert-guided demonstrations.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: For blind people, touch (Braille) is essential for accessing written information. To give robots similar capability, they must not only recognize tactile patterns but also actively establish physical contact that makes patterns readable. Existing robotic Braille readers focus largely on recognition after contact, leaving the contact-establishment problem insufficiently addressed.

Method: The proposed adaptive-contact framework assesses contact quality and physically corrects unsuitable contact before recognition and reconstruction. Multi-Head Policy Learning uses expert-guided contact-adjustment demonstrations to jointly learn contact acceptability and pose corrections. During deployment, the robot iteratively evaluates and re-establishes contact, retaining reliable tactile observations for pose-aware fusion and Braille reconstruction.

Result: Experiments use 20 physical Braille plates for learning and evaluation. The approach achieves 94.0% tactile quality and 88.6% tactile reconstruction on the ten online-evaluation plates.

Conclusion: The work demonstrates the importance of actively establishing readable contact rather than relying solely on recognition under imperfect tactile observations for reliable robotic Braille reading. Limitations include reliance on physical plates and expert demonstrations, and evaluation confined to 20 plates.

Abstract: For people who are blind, touch provides an essen-tial channel for accessing written information through Braille. Bringing a similar capability to robots requires them not only to recognize tactile patterns, but also to actively establish physical contact that makes those patterns readable. Yet existing robotic Braille readers largely focus on recognition after contact, leaving contact establishment itself insufficiently addressed. We present an adaptive-contact framework for robotic tactile Braille reading that assesses contact quality and physically corrects unsuitable contact before recognition and reconstruc-tion. Multi-Head Policy Learning uses expert-guided contact-adjustment demonstrations to jointly learn contact acceptability and pose corrections. During deployment, the robot iteratively evaluates and re-establishes contact, retaining reliable tactile observations for pose-aware fusion and Braille reconstruction. Across 20 physical Braille plates used for learning and eval-uation, the proposed approach achieves 94.0% tactile quality and 88.6% tactile reconstruction on the ten online-evaluation plates. These results demonstrate the importance of actively establishing readable contact, rather than relying solely on recognition under imperfect tactile observations, for reliable robotic Braille reading.

</details>


### [116] [Multi-Objective Human-in-the-Loop Bayesian Optimization of a Lower-Limb Exoskeleton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30695)
*Neil Janwani,Matthew T. Lerner,Aaron J. Young,Maegan Tucker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 MO-HILBO（多目标人在回路贝叶斯优化），基于显式多目标贝叶斯优化，为可穿戴辅助设备推断个性化的 Pareto 最优控制器集合，而非仅优化单一目标或固定权重组合。作者在下肢外骨骼上以代谢消耗（效率）与序数化的人类反馈（舒适度）两个目标进行验证，并开源了 Python 工具包 mohilo。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人在回路优化（HILO）被广泛用于根据佩戴者独特的生物力学特征与主观偏好来优化辅助设备控制。然而，人的目标优先级会随时间变化（如环境、情绪、能量水平不同），现有 HILO 方法要么只考虑单一目标，要么在一组目标上强制使用固定权重，二者都无法刻画个体在多个目标之间的偏好。因此，需要一种能够高效推断个性化多目标偏好、并给出可权衡控制器集合的方法。

Method: 作者提出 MO-HILBO，将 HILO 建立在显式多目标贝叶斯优化（multi-objective Bayesian optimization）之上，用于高效推断一组个性化的 Pareto 最优控制器。与单目标 HILO 或固定加权方案不同，该方法显式建模多目标权衡，通过人类反馈与客观指标共同引导搜索，从而获得覆盖 Pareto 前沿的控制器集合，使用户可在效率与舒适度等目标间按情境取舍。

Result: 实验在下肢外骨骼上进行，涉及两个目标：代谢消耗（效率）与序数化的人类反馈（舒适度）。作者将 MO-HILBO 与已有的多目标 HILO 方法进行对比，结果显示：(1) MO-HILBO 能够发现 Pareto 最优控制器；(2) Pareto 前沿上的点对排序与验证试验结果一致，即推断出的前沿结构与实际偏好验证相符。作者同时开源了可在可穿戴设备上运行 HILO 与 MO-HILBO 的 Python 包 mohilo。

Conclusion: 该工作的主要贡献在于将显式多目标贝叶斯优化引入人在回路优化，使辅助设备控制器能够表征个体随时间变化的多目标偏好，并通过外骨骼实验验证了 Pareto 最优性与前沿排序的一致性，同时提供了开源工具。局限在于目前仅在单一任务（下肢外骨骼、两个目标）上验证，序数化人类反馈依赖主观性，偏好随时间动态变化的显式适应仍有待进一步研究。

Abstract: Human-in-the-loop optimization (HILO) is a common approach for optimizing the control of assistive devices to account for the wearer's unique biomechanics and subjective preferences. However, despite research suggesting that a person may have a different prioritization of objectives depending on time-varying factors such as the environment, their mood, or energy levels, existing HILO approaches only consider a single objective or enforce a fixed weighting on a set of objectives. Neither approach is capable of representing an individual's preferences over objectives. In this work, we propose Multi-Objective Human-in-the-loop Bayesian Optimization (MO-HILBO), which builds on explicit multi-objective Bayesian optimization to efficiently infer a personalized set of Pareto-optimal controllers. We compare our approach with an existing multi-objective HILO method and experimentally demonstrate MO-HILBO on a lower-limb exoskeleton across two objectives: metabolic cost (efficiency) and ordinal human feedback (comfort). We find that MO-HILBO (1) discovers Pareto-optimal controllers, and (2) that the pairwise ordering of points on the Pareto front itself is consistent with validation trials. Lastly, we open-source mohilo, a Python package for running both HILO and MO-HILBO on wearable devices: https://dynamicmobility.github.io/mohilo/.

</details>


### [117] [Learning Vision-Based Agile Gap Traversal: Differentiable Simulation with a Warm-Started Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30696)
*Nuthasith Gerdpratoom,Tianchen Sun,Yichao Gao,Lin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种两阶段强化学习框架，用于训练以自我中心视觉为输入的无人机穿越狭窄缝隙的视觉运动策略。框架利用可微仿真中的准解析策略梯度（QPG）避免对视觉渲染的反向传播，并通过critic热启动显著提升训练效率与穿越成功率，且无需在系统参数变化时重新训练专家actor。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主四旋翼穿越狭窄缝隙是一项高难度任务，尤其当控制指令直接来自高维视觉观测时。现有端到端方法主要依赖行为克隆或通过可微仿真进行完整rollout的时间反向传播（BPTT），前者限制策略性能上限，后者训练计算与内存开销巨大。同时，许多缝隙穿越方法需要沿优化后的参考轨迹重置智能体，且系统参数变化后需重新训练，泛化性差。因此，如何在保证策略性能的同时降低训练成本、提升样本效率与跨平台泛化能力，是亟需解决的问题。

Method: 提出两阶段强化学习框架：第一阶段使用包含缝隙几何等特权观测训练专家actor与critic，采用准解析策略梯度（QPG）通过可微仿真进行策略优化，避免对视觉渲染做反向传播，降低计算与内存成本并提升样本效率；且不同于以往方法，QPG训练无需沿优化参考轨迹重置智能体。第二阶段训练视觉策略，输入为两个自我中心相机生成的二值缝隙掩码及低维观测，其特权critic从第一阶段热启动，相比冷启动或完整rollout BPTT显著提升训练效率与成功率。框架在系统参数变化时无需重新训练专家actor，具备跨无人机平台的高效泛化能力。

Result: 摘要表明该框架在训练效率与穿越成功率上优于critic冷启动与完整rollout BPTT基线，并且相较基于动作监督的当前最优视觉缝隙穿越方法，在系统参数变化时无需重训练专家actor即可高效泛化到不同无人机平台。学习到的视觉策略还能泛化到未见形状的缝隙。大量真实世界实验验证了利用在线渲染二值掩码实现稳健的缝隙穿越。摘要未给出具体数据集、量化指标（如成功率数值）与消融细节。

Conclusion: 主要贡献为：提出基于QPG与critic热启动的两阶段RL框架，降低视觉运动策略训练成本、提升样本效率与穿越成功率；无需参考轨迹重置与专家重训练，实现跨平台与未见缝隙形状的泛化；并在真实无人机上验证有效性。意义在于为高维视觉输入下的高效机器人视觉运动学习提供了通用范式，可扩展到其他视觉运动任务。局限可能在于：依赖可微仿真与特权观测设计、二值掩码表示对复杂场景的适用性、真实实验规模与量化评估的充分性等在摘要中未充分说明。

Abstract: Traversing narrow gaps is challenging for autonomous quadrotors, especially when control commands come directly from high-dimensional visual observations. Existing end-to-end methods often rely on behavior cloning or full-rollout backpropagation through time (BPTT) via differentiable simulation, which can limit policy performance or incur high training costs. We propose a two-stage reinforcement learning framework for more efficient ego-centric visuomotor gap-traversal policy training, leveraging quasi-analytical policy gradients (QPG) via differentiable simulation and critic warm-starting. The framework utilizes QPG to avoid backpropagation through visual rendering, reducing computation and memory costs while improving sample efficiency. In the first stage, an expert actor and critic are trained using privileged observations, including gap geometry. Unlike prior gap-traversal approaches, our training utilizing QPG does not require resetting the agent along optimized reference trajectories. In the second stage, a visual policy is trained using binary gap masks from two ego-centric cameras and low-dimensional observations, while its privileged critic is warm-started from the first stage. This substantially improves training efficiency and traversal success compared with cold-starting the critic or using full-rollout BPTT. Our framework does not require retraining the expert actor when system parameters change, enabling more efficient generalization across drone platforms than state-of-the-art visual gap-traversal methods based on action supervision. The learned visual policy also generalizes to gaps with unseen shapes. Extensive real-world experiments further demonstrate robust gap traversal using binary masks rendered online. Beyond gap traversal, the proposed framework is generic and can be extended to other visuomotor robot learning tasks.

</details>


### [118] [From Visual Search to Movement Control: A Priority Field for Artificial Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30704)
*Han Zhang,Zhong Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文将人类视觉注意力的"优先级图（priority map）"概念从感知扩展到人工智能体的运动控制，提出一种统一的优先级计算框架：既能驱动轻量级视觉搜索模型，又能通过"优先级场（priority field）"控制智能体在动态环境中的运动决策，在样本效率与泛化能力上优于对比架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类空间注意常被解释为由整合感知显著性、当前目标和过往经验的优先级图所引导。然而，这种基于优先级的计算是否也能作为运动控制的通用基础，尚缺乏系统性验证。该研究动机在于：将注意力的优先级机制自视觉搜索延伸至运动规划与决策，检验其能否让智能体在动态、复杂场景中更高效地学习并具备更好的泛化性，同时重现人类行为特征（如眼动抑制、历史驱动的选择偏向）。

Method: 方法包含两部分：(1) 基于整合优先级图的轻量级视觉搜索模型，使用人类扫视（saccade）数据进行训练，用以复现注视抑制与历史驱动选择等行为模式；(2) 将该搜索模型扩展为带"优先级场"的人工智能体，用于reach-avoid任务（到达目标同时避开移动障碍）。优先级场作为统一的空间竞争性表征，将显著性、目标与记忆整合到同一计算结构中，并加入简单的记忆机制以产生历史驱动的预测效应，用于预估下一目标可能出现的位置。

Result: 实验分两类：视觉搜索模型在人类扫视数据上训练后，成功复现了包括眼动抑制与历史驱动选择在内的关键行为模式。运动控制方面，在需避开移动障碍到达目标的reach-avoid任务中，优先级场智能体与多种替代架构相比训练更高效，并在未见过的复杂场景中表现更好，即使仅从简单演示中学习也能保持优势。此外，加入简单记忆机制后，智能体在预测下一目标位置时表现出类人的、历史驱动的效应。研究以行为模式匹配度与任务成功率作为主要评价维度。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出并验证了"优先级计算"可作为人工智能体运动控制的统一基础，用轻量架构同时实现人类视觉搜索的行为拟合与动态环境下的高效运动决策。其意义在于联结认知科学与具身智能，为结合显著性、目标与记忆的统一控制机制提供了新思路，并暗示优先级机制在样本效率与泛化性上具有优势。潜在局限包括：任务设定相对受控（reach-avoid 类任务），记忆机制较为简单，尚未验证在真实机器人、复杂感知或大规模空地协同场景中的可扩展性，也未深入探讨与神经/脉冲实现的关联。

Abstract: Human spatial attention is widely conceptualized as being guided by a priority map that integrates perceptual salience, current goals, and past experiences. Here, we extend priority-based computation to movement control in artificial agents. We first introduce a lightweight model of visual search based on an integrated priority map. Trained on human saccades, it reproduced key behavioral patterns, including oculomotor suppression and history-driven selection. Extending the search model, we equipped an artificial agent with a priority field and evaluated its performance in a reach-avoid task that required reaching a goal destination while avoiding moving obstacles. Compared with alternative architectures, priority-field agents trained more efficiently and performed better in unseen, complex scenarios, even from simple demonstrations. Adding a simple memory mechanism also produced human-like, history-driven effects in anticipating the likely location of the upcoming goal. These findings suggest that priority-based computation may provide a promising foundation for movement control in artificial agents.

</details>


### [119] [RoboMonitor: Label-Efficient Runtime Monitoring of Robot Task Execution via Predictive Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30715)
*Abhiroop Ajith,Gokul Narayanan,Kyle Coelho,Tingji Zhao,Yash Shahapurkar,Brian Zhu,Melih Erdogan,Ted Krubasik,Constantinos Chamzas,Eugen Solowjow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboMonitor 是一个标签高效的视觉-语言机器人执行监控器：先在无监控标注的机器人策略学习数据集上通过自监督预训练（动作条件未来特征预测、逆动力学、掩码当前帧预测）学习视觉与上下文编码器，再迁移到因果监控器并用时序监督微调（Temporal SFT）完成阶段识别、失败检测与任务完成判定，仅需任务指令与相机观测即可部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略（policy）只输出动作，无法自证执行是否按预期推进；执行监控需要判断当前执行阶段、检测失败、识别任务完成，这些能力依赖执行过程中的观测。然而监控所需的阶段/失败/完成标注在面向策略学习采集的数据集中极度稀缺，导致直接训练监控器缺乏监督信号。因此需要一个能从现成的机器人策略学习数据中榨取监督、再以极少监控标注完成迁移的标签高效方案。

Method: 整体为"预训练 + 迁移 + 时序微调"三段式。(1) 预训练：在 25 小时、12 个操作任务、两种机器人本体（embodiment）的多相机轨迹上，使用三个自监督目标——动作条件未来特征预测、逆动力学（inverse dynamics）、掩码当前帧预测（masked-present prediction），学习视觉编码器与上下文编码器。(2) 迁移：将学到的视觉/上下文编码器接入一个因果（causal）监控器，使其在推理时依赖因果历史而非未来帧。(3) 时序监督微调（Temporal SFT）：在每个观测窗口内施加全程监督，并引入窗口内一致性目标与重叠窗口间的一致性目标，从而以少量监控标注获得密集时序监督。(3) 部署：仅需任务指令 + 相机观测，不依赖本体状态或动作输入。

Result: 在四任务监控基准上，RoboMonitor 仅用 52 条带标注 episode（两个微调随机种子平均）取得 93.1% 平均阶段准确率与 85.9% macro recall，超过在相同监控监督下训练的 Qwen3-VL 与 Robometer；其阶段准确率甚至超过用 100 条 episode 训练的 Qwen3-VL 与 Robometer。Qwen3-VL 消融显示 Temporal SFT 将平均虚假阶段切换率从 15.23% 降至 4.95%。闭环部署中，集成系统在仿真 Toolbox Sorting 完成 39/40 次试验，在真实 Reel Packing 完成 35/40 次试验，且未观察到错误的恢复触发（false recovery trigger）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：提出标签高效的机器人执行监控范式，通过自监督预训练 + Temporal SFT 把稀缺监控标注的利用率最大化，并给出完整闭环验证（仿真与真实世界）。意义在于监控器可复用策略学习数据、推理时仅需指令与相机，具有实际部署价值。局限在于评测规模有限（四任务基准、各 40 次闭环试验、单一监控标注量级），仅报告两个微调种子的结果，泛化性到更多任务、更多本体与更长时序任务仍有待验证。

Abstract: Learned robot policies produce actions, but their outputs alone do not establish whether execution is progressing as intended. Robot execution monitoring requires identifying the current execution phase, detecting failures, and recognizing task completion from observations available during execution. Training such monitors requires annotations that are scarce in datasets collected for robot-policy learning. We present RoboMonitor, a label-efficient vision--language execution monitor that learns from these datasets before introducing monitoring supervision. We pre-train on 25 hours of multi-camera trajectories spanning 12 manipulation tasks and two robot embodiments, using action-conditioned future-feature prediction, inverse dynamics, and masked-present prediction. We then transfer the learned visual and context encoders to a causal monitor and apply temporal supervised fine-tuning (Temporal SFT), which combines supervision throughout each observation window with consistency objectives within and across overlapping windows. At deployment, RoboMonitor requires only the task instruction and camera observations.
  On a four-task monitoring benchmark, RoboMonitor trained with 52 labeled episodes achieves 93.1% mean phase accuracy and 85.9% macro recall over two fine-tuning seeds, exceeding Qwen3-VL and Robometer trained with the same monitoring supervision. Its phase accuracy also exceeds that of both Qwen3-VL and Robometer trained with 100 episodes. A Qwen3-VL ablation shows that Temporal SFT reduces mean spurious phase switching from 15.23% to 4.95%. In closed-loop deployment, the integrated system completes 39 of 40 simulated Toolbox Sorting trials and 35 of 40 real-world Reel Packing trials, with no false recovery triggers observed.

</details>


### [120] [Praxis: Distilling Physical Interaction Priors from Egocentric Videos for Generalizable Whole-Body Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30735)
*Shuliang He,Ruiyan Xu,Bo Yue,Hengming Zhang,Huayi Zhou,Shuai Wang,Wei-Shi Zheng,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Praxis 是一个移动人形机器人全身操作框架，通过将单次自我中心（egocentric）人类演示视频中的物理交互先验与闭环姿态校准、在线视觉和触觉感知相结合，实现长时程、可由单个演示指定技能的操作行为，无需任务专属的策略重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动人形操作要求机器人既能到达可用工作空间，又能在物体位姿和接触条件变化时保持精确的手-物交互。从有限的任务专属数据中学习这类行为十分困难：传统模仿学习或强化学习通常需要大量任务特定演示或策略重训练，且难以应对物体位姿变化、场景配置改变和外部物理扰动。论文旨在弥合"开环重放演示"与"鲁棒闭环操作"之间的差距，使机器人能够以极少数据（每个技能仅一个演示）泛化到新场景并具备抗扰动恢复能力。

Method: Praxis 将物理交互先验（来自单次自我中心演示视频）与闭环姿态校准和在线感知相融合，协调三个阶段：(1) 视觉-语言引导的导航，驱动机器人接近目标物体；(2) 闭环姿态校准，对齐手臂-手部工作空间以保证可达性与操作精度；(3) 灵巧操作，同步控制上半身与下半身（即全身协同）。关键技术在于：在线视觉反馈在新物体位姿和场景配置下重新锚定（re-ground）演示中的交互几何，而触觉反馈则根据实际接触条件自适应调整手部动作。每个操作技能只需一个人类演示指定，且不进行任务专属的操纵策略重训练，从而实现了演示先验与在线反馈的互补。

Result: 论文在五个长时程（long-horizon）操作任务上进行了实验评估。结果显示该框架具备空间泛化、视觉泛化和跨物体泛化能力，并且在三个阶段（导航、姿态校准、灵巧操作）中均能从外部物理扰动中恢复。评估聚焦于泛化性与鲁棒性（抗扰动恢复），而非仅报告单一成功率。实验验证了单次演示即可指定技能、无需任务专属策略重训练的有效性。

Conclusion: Praxis 的主要贡献在于提出一个结合演示物理先验、闭环姿态校准与在线视觉-触觉感知的全身人形操作框架，以单次演示实现多任务长时程操作，并兼具空间、视觉与跨物体泛化及抗扰动恢复能力，显著降低了任务专属数据与策略重训练需求。潜在局限在于：摘要未给出量化的成功率、基线对比与失败案例分析；每个技能仍需一个人类演示；方法对视觉/触觉感知质量、姿态校准精度以及全身协调控制的稳定性较为依赖，其在不同硬件平台和更复杂接触任务上的可扩展性仍有待验证。

Abstract: Mobile humanoid manipulation requires both reaching a usable workspace and preserving precise hand-object interactions as object poses and contact conditions change. Learning these behaviors from limited task-specific data remains challenging. To bridge this gap, we introduce Praxis, a whole-body manipulation framework that combines physical interaction priors from one-shot egocentric video demonstrations with closed-loop posture calibration and online perception. The framework coordinates three stages: vision-language-guided navigation toward target objects, closed-loop posture calibration to align the arm-hand workspace, and dexterous manipulation with synchronized upper- and lower-body control. Online visual feedback re-grounds demonstrated interaction geometry under new object poses and scene configurations, while tactile feedback adapts hand motions to actual contact conditions. Each manipulation skill is specified by one human demonstration, without task-specific manipulation-policy retraining. Experiments across five long-horizon manipulation tasks demonstrate spatial, visual, and cross-object generalization, as well as recovery from external physical disturbances across all three stages.

</details>


### [121] [Anatomy-Aware Dexterity-Driven Design Optimization of Surgical Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30745)
*Tony Qin,Peter Connor,Khoa Dang,Carter Hatch,Caleb Rucker,Robert J. Webster,Ron Alterovitz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向医疗连续体机器人的几何设计优化方法，引入"可达体积化灵巧立体角"（RVDSA）度量与高效运动规划器，并结合模拟退火优化，在结肠息肉手术场景中将RVDSA平均提升78%（相比仅优化3D体素覆盖率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人执行复杂医疗手术时，其几何设计参数必须与特定解剖环境相匹配，机器人需在目标解剖结构内具备高灵巧性。然而传统设计优化往往只关注末端能否到达区域（3D体素覆盖率），忽略了"从多方向、经无碰撞路径到达目标点"的灵巧性，而这恰恰是精细手术操作的关键。因此需要一种同时兼顾灵巧性与解剖结构的设计优化方法。

Method: 作者提出RVDSA（Reachable Volumetric Dexterous Solid Angle）指标作为优化目标，衡量末端执行器从起始构型出发、通过无碰撞路径、能从不同方向到达目标体积内各点的能力，从而同时编码灵巧性与解剖约束。为高效计算该目标函数，设计了一个计算高效的运动规划器；随后采用渐近最优的模拟退火（simulated annealing）优化器搜索最优几何设计参数。

Result: 将该方法应用于优化"双臂灵巧鞘"（bimanual dexterous sheaths）机器人设计，用于在结肠解剖结构中执行癌性息肉手术。结果显示，与仅以3D体素覆盖率为目标的优化基线相比，所提方法平均获得高78%的RVDSA，表明其对灵巧性优化更有效。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：（1）提出RVDSA度量，将灵巧性与解剖结构统一纳入设计目标；（2）给出高效运动规划器以计算该指标；（3）结合模拟退火实现设计优化，并在结肠手术机器人上验证有效性。意义在于为医疗连续体机器人的解剖感知设计优化提供了新范式。局限可能包括：方法依赖运动规划器的计算效率与解剖建模精度，实验仅在结肠息肉场景验证，泛化性与真实手术环境的鲁棒性尚待进一步评估。

Abstract: Performing complex medical procedures with continuum robots requires careful selection of their geometric design parameters. The robot should have high dexterity in the specific anatomical environment of its procedure. This work presents a design optimization method that considers both dexterity and anatomy. We introduce the Reachable Volumetric Dexterous Solid Angle (RVDSA) metric as our objective, which measures the ability of a robot's end effector to reach the points in a goal volume from different directions via collision-free paths from a start configuration. We present a computationally efficient motion planner to compute this objective function for a given robotic design, and we use an asymptotically optimal simulated annealing optimizer to compute an optimized design. We applied our new method to optimize the design of a bimanual dexterous sheaths robot for performing procedures on cancerous polyps in colon anatomies, achieving a 78% higher RVDSA on average than optimizing for 3D voxel coverage alone.

</details>


### [122] [Design and Characterization of a Variable-Length Continuum Mechanism with Force Locking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30759)
*Katelyn King,Veronica Fish,Allison M. Okamura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于拮抗式伸展/收缩的杆驱动连续螺旋结构（rod-driven continuum helical structure），通过"变长度+力锁定"机制使同一机构兼具柔顺形变与刚性承载能力，从而提升连续体机构的作业空间与刚度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体/柔性机构虽具备灵活导航能力，但固有刚度低，在高负载操作场景中表现失效。核心问题在于柔顺性与刚度之间的固有矛盾：提升柔顺性通常以牺牲刚度为代价，反之亦然。因此需要一种可在"柔性模式"与"刚性模式"之间切换或连续调节的机构，以兼顾灵巧导航与高强度操作。

Method: 1) 机构设计：采用螺旋（helical）连续结构，兼具可变长度能力与力锁定（force locking）特性，通过杆驱动（rod-driven）方式实现。2) 驱动原理：利用驱动杆的拮抗式（antagonistic）伸展与收缩产生形变；杆的张力既可驱动弯曲，也可作为锁定载荷路径。3) 刚度增强机制：力锁定直接提升轴向刚度，并各向异性地间接增强弯曲刚度——沿轴向分量较大的载荷路径上弯曲刚度最高。4) 附加可调性：在力锁定状态下继续增加驱动杆张力，可进一步按比例提升弯曲刚度，提供额外的刚度调节自由度。论文给出了详细机构设计，并通过实验对弯曲能力与刚度进行表征。

Result: 通过弯曲与刚度量化实验评估该机构。(1) 运动范围：实现180°弯曲活动范围；(2) 定位精度：平均末端定位误差<10%，表明变长度驱动下仍具备可接受的位姿可控性；(3) 刚度特性：力锁定直接提高轴向刚度，并各向异性地提升弯曲刚度，最大弯曲刚度出现在轴向分量较大的载荷路径方向；(4) 张力调刚：在锁定状态下增大杆张力可成比例提升弯曲刚度，测得无量纲增益为0.56。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出并实验验证了一种通过拮抗伸缩与力锁定同时实现柔顺与刚性的连续螺旋机构，为高负载下的灵巧操作提供可行路径，并给出刚度各向异性与张力-刚度比例关系等定量表征。局限：工作聚焦机构设计与非视觉的弯曲/刚度表征，未涉及闭环控制、视觉感知或实际任务级操作验证；定位误差<10%在精细操作中可能仍偏高；张力增益0.56等指标依赖特定样机与实验条件，泛化性与耐久性（螺旋结构长期受力、杆张力驱动能耗）尚待评估。该工作与事件相机、脉冲神经网络、UAV感知等方向无直接关联。

Abstract: The utility of flexible continuum mechanisms for dexterous navigation is often impaired by their low stiffness, making them ineffective at manipulation in high-force scenarios. To address this challenge, we propose a novel continuum mechanism that achieves both flexible and rigid behavior by antagonistic extension and contraction of a rod-driven continuum helical structure. The helical design combines variable-length capacity with force locking for workspace and stiffness enhancement. In this article, we present the detailed design of the proposed mechanism and characterize its performance through experiments that quantify bending and stiffness. The results demonstrate 180 degree bending range of motion with an average distal positioning error of <10%. Further tests demonstrate that force locking directly improves axial stiffness and thus indirectly increases bending stiffness anisotropically, with maximum bending stiffness along load paths with a large axial component. Tensioning the driving rods provides additional stiffness tunability in the force-locked state, where increasing rod tension proportionally increases bending stiffness with a dimensionless gain of 0.56.

</details>


### [123] [NavGen: Visual Generative Models as a Scalable Data Engine for Embodied 3D Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30770)
*Xijie Huang,Yongyang Wan,Chengbin Dong,Zimo Ding,Mo Zhu,Yijin Wang,Zhiyang Liu,Fei Gao,Yuze Wu,Xin Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 NavGen，一种基于文本到视频（text-to-video）高保真视觉生成模型的具身 3D 导航数据生成流水线。NavGen 能合成跨室内外场景的多样化视觉-语言导航（VLN）情节，并配套提出风格多样化（style-diversification）方法来规模化扩展难以采集的长尾数据。最终数据集约含 40 万条导航情节，训练模型在真实世界飞行实验中达到 75% 成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人模型依赖大规模多样化数据，但具身 3D 导航领域面临根本性数据困境：仿真数据可大规模生成却存在视觉 sim-to-real 差距，真实飞行数据视觉逼真却采集成本高昂。该问题对 UAV 导航、机器人自主系统尤为重要——高质量、可扩展且视觉逼真的导航数据是训练可泛化导航策略的前提。论文探索第三条路径：将先进视觉生成模型作为可扩展的数据引擎，以缓解规模与真实感之间的取舍。

Method: 核心是 NavGen，一个 text-to-video 数据生成流水线，用于产出多样的视觉-语言导航情节，覆盖室内与室外场景。其关键组成包括：（1）利用高保真视觉生成模型合成导航视频；（2）提出风格多样化方法，专门扩展长尾（难以、昂贵采集）数据的规模；（3）生成数据成对提供视觉观测与语言指令，构成 VLN 训练样本。下游使用该数据训练导航模型，并采用 world-action-model（世界-动作模型）范式部署到真实飞行。方法强调生成多样性（场景、风格）与数据规模随生成能力提升而扩展。

Result: 构建数据集约 400K 条导航情节。评估方面，论文在多个指标上将该数据集与现有 UAV 导航数据集进行对比，发现基于 NavGen 数据训练的模型随规模扩大而性能持续提升，且总体优于在现有数据集上训练的模型（体现出更好或相当的 sim-to-real 泛化潜力）。真实世界验证上，将训练模型以 world-action-model 范式部署到真实飞行实验，最终模型在多种导航任务与环境下取得 75% 成功率。具体骨干网络结构、基线模型、评价指标细节及消融设置未在摘要中给出。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：提出以高保真视觉生成模型作为具身 3D 导航可扩展数据引擎的新方向；构建 NavGen text-to-video 生成流水线及风格多样化方法；发布约 400K 情节的大规模 VLN 数据集；并通过真实飞行实验验证 75% 成功率，证明生成数据可有效迁移到真实世界。其意义在于为 UAV/机器人导航提供兼顾规模与真实感的替代数据来源。潜在局限：依赖生成模型本身的保真度与物理一致性，长尾数据扩展的真实性需进一步验证；摘要未报告与真实数据集混合训练的对比、详细消融及生成多样性对下游性能的定量影响。

Abstract: General-purpose robot models increasingly rely on large and diverse datasets. For embodied 3D navigation, however, existing data sources face a fundamental trade-off: simulated data can be generated at scale but often suffer from the visual sim-to-real gap, whereas real-world flight data provide realistic observations but are costly to collect. This paper studies another direction: the use of high-fidelity visual generative models as scalable data engines for embodied 3D navigation. We introduce NavGen, a text-to-video data generation pipeline that produces diverse vision-language navigation (VLN) episodes across indoor and outdoor scenes. We also propose a style-diversification method that scales up long-tail data that are difficult and costly to collect. The resulting dataset contains approximately 400K navigation episodes. We evaluate our dataset against existing UAV navigation datasets across multiple metrics, and find that the model trained on our data generally improves with scale, outperforming those trained on existing datasets. To validate real-world transferability, we deploy the trained model in world-action-model paradigm to real-world flying experiments. The final model achieves a 75\% success rate across different navigation tasks and environments.

</details>


### [124] [Moving Horizon Estimation for Quadrotors: An $\mathcal{L}_1$ Adaptive Optimizer Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30777)
*Thinh Nguyen,Minkyung Kim,Sandeep Banik,Jinrae Kim,Naira Hovakimyan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将线性平滑移动视界估计（MHE）表述为稠密二次规划（QP）的方法，并设计了一个连续时间牛顿法结合 L1 自适应优化器（L1-AO）的时变求解器，通过利用问题的时序演化来跟踪最优解，从而在提升估计精度的同时降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MHE 基于有限视界优化，相比卡尔曼滤波类方法可获得更高精度，但计算代价显著增大。传统 MHE 将这一本质时变问题当作一系列独立、时不变的问题来处理，每个时间步都需调用迭代求解器，既不够精确又计算繁重。作者希望通过利用 MHE 问题的时序演化特性来同时改善精度与效率，并补偿实际中因传感器噪声和系统先验知识缺失导致的预测不准。

Method: 1) 将线性平滑 MHE 表述为一个稠密二次规划（QP）问题；2) 求解器采用连续时间牛顿法，并引入 L1 自适应优化器（L1-AO）进行增强；3) 与时不变求解器不同，该时变求解器通过跟踪问题随时间的演化，以更少的迭代次数逼近最优解，从而降低计算量；4) L1-AO 增强用于补偿预测误差，应对实际中噪声传感器与缺乏系统先验知识的情况。

Result: 在四旋翼（quadrotor）平台上的仿真结果表明：与基线时不变求解器相比，L1-AO 增强方法能更高效地求解 MHE 优化问题；在具有挑战性的条件下，该方法相较扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）和标准 MHE 均取得更高的估计精度。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出 MHE 的稠密 QP 表述及基于连续时间牛顿法与 L1-AO 的时变求解器，兼顾效率与鲁棒估计精度。其意义在于为计算受限且传感器噪声大的实时状态估计（如四旋翼/UAV 平台）提供了一种更具实用性、更高效的 MHE 方案。局限性方面，论文目前仅给出仿真结果，尚缺乏真实平台验证；同时方法针对线性平滑 MHE 表述，对非线性情形的适用性有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Moving Horizon Estimation (MHE) is a state estimation method based on finite-horizon optimization that can offer higher accuracy at the cost of increased computation compared to Kalman filter-based approaches. We present a linear smoothing MHE formulation as a dense Quadratic Program (QP), and a solver consisting of a continuous-time Newton's method augmented with the $\mathcal{L}_1$ Adaptive Optimizer ($\mathcal{L}_1$-AO). While MHE is inherently time-varying, conventional approaches treat it as a sequence of independent, time-invariant problems and employ iterative solvers at each time step, which can be both inaccurate and computationally burdensome. In contrast, time-varying solvers track the optimal solution with fewer iterations by exploiting the temporal evolution of the problem, thereby reducing the computational load. In this research, we enhance both the performance and efficiency of MHE through a time-varying solver with an $\mathcal{L}_1$-AO augmentation that compensates for the prediction inaccuracy, which is common in practice due to noisy sensors and the lack of prior knowledge of the system. Simulation results on a quadrotor platform show that the $\mathcal{L}_1$-AO-augmented approach solves the MHE optimization problem more efficiently than the baseline time-invariant solver and achieves higher estimation accuracy under challenging conditions, compared with both the Extended Kalman Filter and the standard MHE.

</details>


### [125] [SeA-RVINS: Semantic-Aware Tightly Coupled RTK-Visual-Inertial System with Correlation-Preserving Robust Estimation for Urban Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30814)
*Wang Hu,Bo Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SeA-RVINS 是一个面向城市环境的固定滞后因子图 RTK 视觉惯性里程计系统，通过语义感知的学习型立体前端、针对双差 GNSS 的鲁棒协方差缩放以及混合模糊度延续策略，实现高可用、低误差的全局位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市环境中全局位姿估计面临两类核心威胁：一是 GNSS 观测受多径效应污染，产生离群测量；二是视觉惯性传感虽能提供局部运动约束，但错误的视觉关联会污染整个估计器。更关键的是，在紧耦合估计器中，离群测量与错误时序关联可能长期残留在状态中，持续破坏估计结果。因此，如何在测量进入图优化之前进行有效筛选，并在 GNSS 层面构建鲁棒的抗差模型，是提升城市绝对定位可靠性的关键问题。

Method: 系统为固定滞后（fixed-lag）因子图框架下的 RTK 视觉惯性紧耦合估计器。方法包含三部分：（1）语义感知的学习型立体前端，在持久地标进入图之前拒绝不可靠的跟踪轨迹，从源头抑制错误视觉关联；（2）针对双差 GNSS 测量引入动态协方差缩放（Dynamic Covariance Scaling），提供可配置的批量（batch）、标量（scalar）与隐枢轴（latent-pivot）三种鲁棒化形式，同时保留共享枢轴（shared-pivot）的相关性结构，即在不破坏测量间相关性的前提下进行抗差处理；（3）提出混合模糊度延续策略，在短弧段内共享同一模糊度状态并验证其连续性，同时通过随机游走因子软性关联相邻弧段，从而在模糊度固定与连续性之间取得平衡。

Result: 在公开 TEX-CUP 数据集约 20 km 的路线（含约 50% 深度城市行驶场景）上进行评估。隐枢轴（latent-pivot）配置实现了 100% 的可用性（availability），最大水平误差为 1.6 m；其中 96.16% 的历元水平误差低于 1.0 m，99.90% 低于 1.5 m。结果表明显式建模 GNSS 共享枢轴相关结构的鲁棒化形式（尤其隐枢轴配置）在深度城市场景中具备最优的可用性与精度。实现已开源。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：将语义过滤前端与 GNSS 鲁棒协方差缩放、混合模糊度延续统一到一个固定滞后因子图 RTK 视觉惯性系统中，并在深度城市长距离路线上验证了 100% 可用性与亚 1.6 m 的最大水平误差，同时开源实现。潜在局限包括：仅在单一 TEX-CUP 数据集与特定城市路线上验证，泛化性有待更多场景确认；学习型立体前端的语义泛化能力与推理开销可能影响实时性；固定滞后窗口下长期漂移与模糊度软关联的精度上限仍需进一步讨论。

Abstract: Reliable absolute pose estimation in urban environments is undermined by outlier measurements and incorrect temporal associations that can persist in tightly coupled estimators. Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) observations provide globally referenced measurements but are prone to multipath effects. Visual-inertial sensing supplies local motion constraints, but false visual associations can corrupt the estimator. We present SeA-RVINS, a fixed-lag factor-graph Real-Time Kinematic (RTK) visual-inertial system for robust urban pose estimation. A semantic-aware learned stereo frontend rejects unreliable tracks before persistent landmarks enter the graph. For double-differenced GNSS measurements, SeA-RVINS applies Dynamic Covariance Scaling through configurable batch, scalar, and latent-pivot robust formulations while retaining the shared-pivot correlation structure. We propose a hybrid ambiguity-continuation strategy that shares one ambiguity state over short arcs with verified continuity and softly links successive arcs through random-walk factors. On an approximately 20-km route from the public TEX-CUP dataset, including about 50\% deep-urban driving, the latent-pivot configuration achieves 100\% availability and a 1.6-m maximum horizontal error, with 96.16\% and 99.90\% of epochs below 1.0 and 1.5 m, respectively. The implementation is released as open-source software

</details>


### [126] [Evaluation Is All You Need for Multi-Modal Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30818)
*Zeyu He,Shiqi Liu,Ke Chen,Yun Yan,Jinzi Wu,Dianqiao Lei,Sirui Wang,ShuRui Peng,Tao Chen,Zhuo Huang,Yu Wu,Yadong Shao,Zhichao Li,Ke Sun,Yang Guan,Keqiang Li,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 iDriveVLA，一个面向自动驾驶的多模态规划框架，通过"改进候选轨迹空间 + 增强轨迹评估可靠性"双管齐下，缓解多模态规划中的"生成-评估不对称"问题，在 NAVSIM v1 上以 94.95 PDMS 刷新 SOTA，超过人类专家参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶在模糊场景与长尾场景中需要表示多种合理行为，多模态规划因此成为主流范式。现有工作主要聚焦于提升轨迹的多模态性、优化轨迹表示或重塑候选分布，但作者发现一个被忽视的"生成-评估不对称"（generation-evaluation asymmetry）问题：尽管规划器具备很强的 oracle 性能（即候选集合中往往已包含优质轨迹），实际选择机制却常常无法可靠地挑出最优候选，导致大量规划潜力未被释放。该问题的意义在于，它揭示了多模态规划的性能瓶颈更多来自评估/选择环节而非生成环节。

Method: iDriveVLA 的核心是同时优化候选轨迹空间与评估过程。其一，构建统一的轨迹评估器，由两部分组成：Safety-aware Scorer 负责对候选轨迹进行质量与风险估计；VLM-guided Modulator 借助视觉语言模型（VLM）实现场景自适应的评估准则权重调节，从而提升上下文感知的选择能力。其二，设计 oracle-aligned 渐进式训练策略，分三阶段：候选模仿预训练（candidate imitation pretraining）、候选空间精炼（candidate space refinement）、语义排序对齐（semantic ranking alignment），使训练目标与 oracle 选择偏好对齐。

Result: 论文在公开的 NAVSIM v1 排行榜上进行评测，采用 PDMS（Planning Divergence Metric Score）作为主要评价指标。iDriveVLA 取得 94.95 PDMS 的最新 SOTA 成绩，并超过人类专家参考基线的表现，验证了通过改善评估可靠性来释放多模态规划潜力的有效性。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：（1）识别并形式化了多模态规划中的生成-评估不对称问题；（2）提出集成 Safety-aware Scorer 与 VLM-guided Modulator 的统一轨迹评估器；（3）设计 oracle 对齐的渐进式训练策略。方法在 NAVSIM v1 上达到 SOTA 并超越人类专家，显示出显著的实用价值。潜在局限包括：评测主要基于 NAVSIM 单一仿真基准，真实道路场景泛化性有待检验；引入 VLM 可能带来推理开销与实时性挑战；对 oracle 候选质量的依赖可能限制在极端长尾分布下的表现。

Abstract: Multi-modal planning is promising for autonomous driving by representing multiple plausible behaviors in ambiguous and long-tail scenarios. Existing methods mainly focus on improving trajectory multi-modality, enhancing trajectory representations, or reshaping the candidate distribution. Nevertheless, we identify a pronounced generation-evaluation asymmetry in multi-modal planning: despite strong oracle performance, existing planners often fail to reliably select the best available candidate, leaving substantial planning potential unrealized. To address this challenge, we propose iDriveVLA, a multi-modal planning framework that improves the candidate trajectory space while enabling more reliable and context-aware trajectory evaluation. Specifically, iDriveVLA introduces a unified trajectory evaluator comprising a Safety-aware Scorer for quality and risk estimation, together with a VLM-guided Modulator for scene-adaptive criterion weighting. We further develop an oracle-aligned progressive training strategy consisting of candidate imitation pretraining, candidate space refinement, and semantic ranking alignment. On the public NAVSIM v1 leaderboard, iDriveVLA achieves a new state-of-the-art performance of 94.95 PDMS, surpassing the human-expert reference.

</details>


### [127] [HIRE: History-Conditioned Interaction Reasoning and High-Rate Execution for Visually Aliased Precision Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30828)
*Rongji Li,Wenhao He,Cewu Lu,Xingyu Chen,Xu-Yao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HIRE：一个跨速率（cross-rate）框架，通过历史条件化的交互状态推理（ISR）与高速率交互流形执行器（IME）的耦合闭环，解决历史依赖型精密操作中的视觉歧义与执行精度问题，在真实机器人任务中实现所有评估阶段≥90%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触关键型精密操作具有历史依赖性：视觉上相似的观测可能对应不同的潜在交互状态，因而需要不同动作；而微小执行误差也会改变任务结果。仅依赖当前视觉观测的策略无法消解这种歧义。现有方法存在割裂：力感知与记忆增强方法虽能补充物理或时间上下文，反应式高速率策略虽能改善局部接触响应，但长时程时间推理与精密执行在现有方法中大多相互解耦，导致在视觉混淆（visually aliased）的精密操作中难以可靠推进任务。作者将此问题形式化为"历史依赖的部分可观测性"下的精密操作问题。

Method: 提出HIRE（History-Conditioned Interaction Reasoning and Execution），一个跨速率框架，由两个组件构成：
1）ISR（Interaction-State Reasoner，交互状态推理器）：历史条件化、低速率模块，使用时间力/力矩编码器（temporal wrench encoder）对有序的wrench历史进行编码，并结合Force Perceiver，将力信息作为持久的物理证据，以实现状态一致的动作生成。
2）IME（Interaction-Manifold Executor，交互流形执行器）：高速率模块，将接触关键运动结构化分解为"内在进度（intrinsic progress）"与"横向修正（transverse correction）"两部分，以实现精确执行。
3）跨速率闭环（cross-rate loop）：高速率执行产生的物理轨迹（physical traces）反哺后续推理，使推理与执行相互耦合而非解耦。核心创新在于统一了长时程时间推理与精密接触执行，并利用物理交互本身既实现任务意图、又揭示潜在状态证据。

Result: 在真实机器人（real-robot）实验中进行评估，覆盖表面交互（surface）、插入（insertion）与旋转（rotational）三类接触任务。结果显示：在所有评估的任务阶段中，HIRE均达到至少90%的完成率，并在交互状态消歧、执行精度和泛化能力方面均取得提升。论文摘要未给出具体数据集名称、评价指标细节或与基线的定量对比数值。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出HIRE这一跨速率推理—执行框架，通过历史条件化的力感知推理与流形结构化高速执行器的闭环耦合，统一了历史依赖型精密操作中的长时程推理与精细执行，并在真实机器人任务上实现≥90%的稳定完成率。其更广泛意义在于为"历史依赖部分可观测性"下的精密操作提供了统一的推理—执行视角，即物理交互既是任务意图的实现手段，也是揭示潜在状态证据的来源。潜在局限：摘要中未披露具体基线对比、消融实验、定量指标与失败情况分析；方法依赖力/力矩（wrench）传感，未讨论在缺乏力觉反馈场景下的适用性；真实机器人实验的规模与任务多样性也需正文进一步验证。代码将在发表后放出。

Abstract: Precision manipulation with contact-critical interactions is often history-dependent: visually similar observations can correspond to different latent interaction states and therefore require different actions, while small execution errors can alter task outcomes. Policies relying on the current visual observation alone cannot resolve such ambiguity; force-aware and memory-augmented methods enrich physical or temporal context, while reactive high-rate policies improve local contact response, yet long-horizon temporal reasoning and precision execution remain largely decoupled in existing methods, limiting reliable progression in visually aliased precision manipulation. To bridge this gap, we introduce History-Conditioned Interaction Reasoning and Execution (HIRE), a cross-rate framework comprising a history-conditioned Interaction-State Reasoner (ISR) and a high-rate Interaction-Manifold Executor (IME). ISR encodes ordered wrench history with a temporal wrench encoder and Force Perceiver as persistent physical evidence for state-consistent action generation, while IME structures contact-critical motion into intrinsic progress and transverse correction for precise execution; their cross-rate loop allows the resulting physical traces to inform subsequent reasoning. In real-robot experiments across surface, insertion, and rotational interactions, HIRE achieves at least 90% completion across all evaluated task stages while improving interaction-state disambiguation, execution precision, and generalization. More broadly, HIRE provides a unified reasoning--execution perspective on precision manipulation under history-dependent partial observability, where physical interaction both realizes task intent and reveals latent-state evidence for future decisions. Code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [128] [Fast Plans, Faithful Actions: Closing the Planning-Execution Gap in Hierarchical Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30833)
*Chuanliang Xie,Boyu Ma,Gen Li,Yizhou Liu,Houwang Chen,Xinyu Zhou,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对层次化视觉-语言-动作(VLA)系统中高层规划器与低层动作专家之间的错位问题，提出航点对齐的块自回归解码(Block-AR)与归一化目标调制(NGM)，在保持成功率的同时将VLM前向次数从57降至8，规划延迟降低8.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次化VLA系统由高层视觉-语言规划器与低层动作专家组成。这一设计要具有实用价值，必须满足两点：(1)规划器生成计划的速度足够快以满足实时控制需求；(2)生成的计划确实对动作生成有贡献。作者以从π_{0.5}改编而来的航点层次化流水线为研究对象，发现这两点都未满足：基线依赖token级自回归解码(Token-AR)生成航点计划，需要57次极其昂贵的VLM前向。更关键的是，作者发现擦除航点端点几乎不影响任务成功率，揭示出规划器-执行器的双重错位——规划器输出粒度过细，而执行器对计划作为控制条件的利用不足。这一发现说明当前层次化设计存在严重的计算浪费与架构冗余。

Method: 针对延迟问题，作者提出航点对齐的块自回归解码(Block-AR)，以航点为对齐单元进行块级解码，从而大幅减少VLM前向次数。针对计划利用不足的问题，提出归一化目标调制(NGM)：在层级间构建逐层目标路径，由相位门控(phase gating)约束，并采用反捷径训练(anti-shortcut training)加以正则化，使航点能够真正影响动作生成，同时保持其他信号的传播。作者将Block-AR与NGM结合，并统一在LIBERO基准与真实双臂机器人(Rokae dual-arm robot)上进行验证。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，方法将最大VLM前向次数从57降至8(包含一次前缀prefill)；在Rokae双臂机器人上实现8.7倍规划延迟降低。引入NGM与反捷径训练后，Block-AR在LIBERO-Long上的成功率从91.0%提升至96.2%，四个套件的平均成功率从95.85%提升至98.45%。在该机器人上的三个双臂任务中，各方法成功率保持相当。评测指标为任务成功率，并附带规划延迟与前向次数。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于：(1)实证揭示了层次化VLA系统中规划器-执行器的双重错位(输出粒度过细、计划利用不足)；(2)提出Block-AR减少自回归解码开销；(3)提出NGM配合相位门控与反捷径训练，使计划真正参与动作生成。方法在显著降低计算与延迟的同时提升或保持成功率，对实时机器人控制具有实际意义。潜在局限包括：实验主要基于一个改编自π_{0.5}的航点层次化流水线及LIBERO与单一机器人平台，泛化性有待验证；反捷径训练等机制可能引入额外超参；真实机器人任务在成功率上未显示优势，性能提升主要体现在仿真基准上。

Abstract: Hierarchical vision-language-action (VLA) systems consist of a high-level vision-language planner and a low-level action expert that generates continuous actions. This hierarchical design has practical value only if the planner can generate plans fast enough to meet real-time control requirements, and the resulting plans actually contribute to the generation of action. We study one such system, a waypoint hierarchy pipeline adapted from $π_{0.5}$, and find that neither requirement is satisfied. This baseline relies on token-level autoregressive decoding (Token-AR) to generate a waypoint plan, requiring 57 very expensive vision-language model (VLM) forward passes. However, we find that erasing the waypoint endpoints has little effect on task success. Two findings reveal the misalignment of planner-executor: the planner generates outputs at an excessively fine granularity, and the executor underuses plans as a control condition. We address the latency issue with waypoint-aligned block-autoregressive decoding (Block-AR), and plan underuse issue with normalized goal modulation (NGM), a layer-wise goal path constrained by phase gating and anti-shortcut training so that the waypoint influences action generation maintaining other signals. Our method reduces the maximum number of VLM forward passes from 57 to 8 on LIBERO, including one prefix prefill, and achieves an $8.7\times$ reduction in planning latency on a Rokae dual-arm robot. With normalized goal modulation and anti-shortcut training, Block-AR's success rate on LIBERO-Long increases from 91.0% to 96.2%, while its average success rate across the four suites increases from 95.85% to 98.45%. On three bimanual tasks with this robot, success rates remain comparable across methods.

</details>


### [129] [Impedance Cloning: Learning Equilibrium Point Parameters for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30842)
*Hayato Takahashi,Ryoga Oishi,Yuki Kasuga,Toshiaki Tsuji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 Impedance Cloning（阻抗克隆），即不模仿可观测的运动轨迹，而是模仿产生运动的生物力学先验——刚度（stiffness）与平衡点（equilibrium point），从而被动吸收接触不确定性。该方法通过粒子滤波从双臂遥操作示范中提取阻抗参数（无需力矩传感器），在两个 CRANE-X7 机械臂上验证，在擦拭、抓放、抓取任务中均优于轨迹基线和固定阻抗基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富（contact-rich）的操作任务要求机器人对表面施加并调节力，而真实表面几何往往与训练条件存在不可预测的偏差。基于轨迹的模仿学习复现的是可观测的输出（动作序列/位置），在分布偏移下会失效：当接触条件变化时，复现的轨迹无法自适应地维持合适的接触力。论文的动机在于模仿"意图"而非"结果"——因为刚度与平衡点等阻抗参数编码的是生成运动的意图，而非具体轨迹结果，因此天然对表面几何变化具有泛化能力，从而能被动吸收接触不确定性。这一问题对机器人在非结构化环境中的鲁棒操作具有重要意义。

Method: 核心思路是学习阻抗控制参数而非动作轨迹。具体地：1）将机器人操作建模为阻抗控制，用刚度矩阵与平衡点（equilibrium point）描述交互行为；2）通过粒子滤波（particle filter）从双边遥操作（bilateral teleoperation）示范中估计这些阻抗参数，且不使用力/力矩（F/T）传感器（仅依赖位置等信息）；3）学习得到的参数化表示可作为模仿学习策略的输出目标，即策略预测刚度与平衡点，再由阻抗控制器执行。论文还验证了该表示与 ILBiT、Mamba 两类骨干网络的兼容性，说明其与具体架构无关，具有通用性。

Result: 在两个 CRANE-X7 机械臂上评估。擦拭任务（joint-space 动作）：轨迹基线在低于 -6 cm 时失去接触，而所提方法在 -6 cm 以上维持稳定的 4–5 N 接触力，低于 -6 cm 时力逐渐减小；Cartesian-space 动作下，所提方法在 0 到 +8 cm 区间内力的高度斜率（force-height slope）为 0.13 ± 0.03 N/cm，固定阻抗基线为 0.34–0.83 N/cm，表明所提方法对高度偏移更不敏感。抓放任务（10 种不同杯子，100 次试验）：所提方法成功 84/100，优于固定阻抗基线（74/100），与可变阻抗控制基线（82/100）相当，但仅需一次示范而非十次。抓取任务中该表示降低了力矩跟踪误差，且在 ILBiT 和 Mamba 两种骨干上均成立。

Conclusion: 主要贡献：提出 Impedance Cloning 范式，用生物力学先验（刚度与平衡点）替代轨迹作为模仿学习目标；提出基于粒子滤波、无需 F/T 传感器的阻抗参数提取方法；在多项接触丰富任务上验证了鲁棒性、数据效率和架构通用性。意义在于为接触不确定性下的模仿学习提供了更具泛化性的意图级表示。局限：评估限于特定机械臂平台与有限任务类型；依赖双边遥操作示范；对强动态或非阻抗可建模的接触交互是否适用尚不明确。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires robots to regulate force against surfaces whose geometry deviates unpredictably from training conditions. Trajectory-based imitation learning, which reproduces observable outputs, breaks down under such shifts. We propose Impedance Cloning, which instead imitates the biomechanical priors that generate motion -- the stiffness and equilibrium point -- and thereby passively absorbs contact uncertainty. Because these parameters encode intent rather than outcome, they generalize across surface geometries where trajectory reproduction does not. We extract them from bilateral teleoperation demonstrations via a particle filter without force/torque sensors and evaluate the framework on two CRANE-X7 manipulators. In a wiping task with joint-space actions, the trajectory-based baseline loses contact below -6 cm, whereas the proposed method maintains a consistent 4-5 N contact force above -6 cm, with a gradual decrease below; with Cartesian-space actions, its force-height slope over 0 to +8 cm is 0.13 +/- 0.03 N/cm, versus 0.34-0.83 N/cm for fixed-impedance baselines. In a pick-and-place task with 10 diverse cups (100 trials), the proposed method succeeds in 84 trials, outperforming the fixed-impedance baseline (74/100) and performing comparably to a variable impedance control baseline (82/100) with one demonstration instead of ten. In a grasping task, the representation reduces torque tracking error with both ILBiT and Mamba backbones, confirming its generality across architectures.

</details>


### [130] [VLaRL: Augmenting Vision-Language-Action Models with Simulation-Trained Latent-Conditioned Residual RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30868)
*Namiko Saito,Kinam Kim,Heecheol Kim,Katsushi Ikeuchi,Yasuyuki Matsushita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLaRL 提出一种基于冻结 VLA 内部视觉-语言隐表示的条件化残差强化学习框架，在仿真中训练、无需真实世界 RL 或在线自适应即可迁移到真实机器人，通过轻量级 latent mapper 对齐仿真与真实的隐表示分布，在四项接触密集型操作任务上一致提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型虽能提供广泛的、由指令条件化的操作行为，但在接触密集型交互中物理执行往往不够精确。残差强化学习（residual RL）可以在冻结 VLA 的前提下纠正此类误差，但在真实机器人上做 RL 成本高且安全风险大。因此核心问题在于：如何让残差 RL 仅在仿真中训练，却能跨仿真-现实视觉差异迁移部署到真实机器人。传统 sim-to-real 依赖像素级视觉对应，在视觉差距下难以成立，而本文试图绕开这一要求。

Method: VLaRL 的关键在于不再要求像素级视觉对齐，而是复用冻结 VLA 的**内部视觉-语言隐表示（vision-language latent）**作为两个环节的接口：（1）作为残差控制策略的条件输入；（2）作为仿真到现实（sim-to-real）迁移的桥梁。具体地，残差策略在仿真中训练，输入为 VLA 隐表示；同时学习一个**轻量级 latent mapper**，将仿真中得到的隐表示变换到真实隐表示分布，以弥合视觉域差异。这样避免了真实机器人上的 RL 训练与在线自适应，VLA 本身保持冻结。

Result: 在**四项接触密集型操作任务**与**两种 VLA 骨干网络**的组合上，VLaRL 均提升了真实世界成功率（all task-backbone combinations）。通过受控消融实验，作者验证了**latent conditioning（隐表示条件化）**与**latent alignment（隐表示对齐）**两者对于迁移仿真训练残差控制均不可或缺。摘要未给出具体数值、数据集名称或评价指标细节。

Conclusion: VLaRL 的主要贡献是提出以冻结 VLA 隐表示为接口的残差 RL 框架，使仿真训练的残差策略无需真实世界 RL 或在线自适应即可改善真实接触密集型操作，并通过隐表示对齐替代像素级视觉对应来缓解 sim-to-real 差距。其意义在于降低了真实机器人 RL 的成本与安全风险，同时保留 VLA 的指令条件化泛化能力。局限在于效果依赖 VLA 内部隐表示的可迁移性与 latent mapper 的仿真-真实分布对齐质量，摘要未报告具体成功率、任务定义或跨域泛化边界。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide broad, instruction-conditioned manipulation behaviors, but their physical execution can remain imprecise during contact-rich interaction. Residual reinforcement learning (RL) can correct such errors while keeping the VLA frozen, but real-robot RL is costly and safety-critical. We propose VLA Latent-Conditioned RL (VLaRL), which enables residual RL for frozen VLAs to be trained in simulation and deployed on real robots without real-world RL or online adaptation. The key challenge is transferring the learned residual policy despite the visual gap between simulation and reality. Rather than requiring pixel-level visual correspondence, VLaRL uses the VLA's internal vision-language latent representation to condition residual control and as the sim-to-real transfer interface, and learns a lightweight mapper that transforms simulation-derived latents toward the real latent distribution. Across four contact-rich manipulation tasks and two VLA backbones, VLaRL improves real-world success in all task-backbone combinations, while controlled ablations demonstrate the importance of both latent conditioning and latent alignment for transferring simulation-trained residual control.

</details>


### [131] [A Second Torque Port for Series Elastic Actuators: Parallel-Integrated Design and Time-Scale Torque Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30873)
*Donghao Jia,Xiubo Xia,Junhang Liu,Zeyu Zhu,Xiaoyu Geng,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Pi-SEA（Parallel-Integrated SEA），一种在负载侧并联一个无框直驱微型电机的双力矩端口串联弹性执行器。通过按时间尺度而非滤波器设计来分配指令力矩，并利用微电机力矩环构成的快子系统使闭环具有奇异摄动结构，从而无需力矩传感器即可从弹簧形变和微电机电流读取输出力矩。相比传统 SEA，Pi-SEA 在小振幅下将可跟踪频带拓宽一个数量级，并降低关节对环境施加的残余力矩一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统串联弹性执行器（SEA）只有一个力矩端口，存在两个根本缺陷：一是减速电机必须带动自身反射惯量穿过弹簧，当电机饱和后其可输出力矩幅值随指令频率 ω 以 ω^{-2} 衰减；二是低于传动系统静摩擦（breakaway friction）的指令根本无法传递到负载。这两个问题严重限制了 SEA 在小振幅、宽频带场景下的力矩跟踪能力与环境柔顺性，对于需要精细力控与高透明度交互的机器人关节（如协作机器人、外骨骼、腿足机器人）尤为重要。

Method: 核心思路是在负载侧开启第二个力矩端口，将一个无框直驱微型电机与固定刚度弹簧并联，构成 Pi-SEA。其技术要点包括：(1) 无需力矩传感器——输出力矩由弹簧形变和微电机电流读取；(2) 按时间尺度划分两个通道而非依赖滤波器交叉设计：微电机力矩环作为快子系统，使整个闭环呈奇异摄动（singularly perturbed）结构，将两通道所需的分离条件从"需调参的交叉频率"转变为"需检验的界限（bound to check）"；(3) 引入带泄漏的中值调节积分器（leaky mid-ranging integrator），使稳态负载回归由弹簧承担，微电机专注于高频/小振幅成分；(4) 给出闭式选型规则：将幅值上限反向读取，得到匹配弹簧、减速电机与微电机，使其适应应用所需的振幅与频率范围的定量设计准则。

Result: 论文以传统 SEA 作为对比基线，评估指标聚焦于两个方面：可跟踪频带宽度（在小振幅条件下）以及关节对环境施加的残余力矩。实验结果表明，Pi-SEA 在这两项指标上均较 SEA 改善约一个数量级——即在小振幅下显著拓宽了有效跟踪频带，同时大幅降低了强加的残余力矩。文中未在摘要中给出具体数据集或数值，实验主要基于执行器硬件平台而非公开视觉/机器人数据集。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：提出并联双力矩端口 SEA 架构，通过时间尺度通道分离与奇异摄动分析替代传统滤波器交叉调参，利用泄漏中值调节积分器实现稳态负载回归，并给出闭式选型规则；在频带拓宽与残余力矩降低两方面取得约一个数量级的改进。意义在于提升力控关节的透明度与柔顺性。潜在局限包括：摘要未给出定量指标与实验条件细节；奇异摄动分离假设、静摩擦建模及微电机力矩能力在实际负载与动态工况下的鲁棒性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: A series elastic actuator has a single torque port and pays for it twice: the geared motor must swing its own reflected inertia through the spring, so the amplitude it delivers collapses as $ω^{-2}$ in the command frequency $ω$ once it saturates, while commands below the transmission's breakaway friction never arrive at all. This letter opens a second torque port on the load side, placing a frameless direct-drive micro motor in parallel with a fixed-stiffness spring -- a parallel-integrated SEA, or Pi-SEA, whose delivered torque is read from spring deflection and micro current without a sensor -- and dividing the commanded torque between the two channels by time scale rather than by filter design. The micro torque loop is the fast subsystem, which makes the closed loop singularly perturbed and turns the separation the channels need into a bound to check rather than a crossover to tune; a leaky mid-ranging integrator returns the steady load to the spring; and the amplitude ceiling, read backwards, becomes a closed-form sizing rule that matches spring, geared motor and micro motor to the amplitudes and frequencies an application asks for. Against SEAs, the Pi-SEA widens the tracked band at small amplitudes and lowers the residual the joint imposes on its environment, each by an order of magnitude.

</details>


### [132] [PHASE: Compliance-Enabled Tactile Phase Retrieval for Few-Shot Insertion Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30889)
*Jeremy Siburian,Cristian C. Beltran-Hernandez,Tatsuya Matsushima,Yusuke Iwasawa,Masashi Hamaya,Mai Nishimura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: tldr: PHASE（PHase-Aware Segmentation and REtrieval）是一个面向接触密集型装配任务的“合规使能触觉相位检索”框架，利用柔顺手腕维持接触所产生的触觉/力信号自动揭示插入任务的相位结构，实现变长相位分割与相位一致的检索增强模仿学习。tldr: 在真实 peg-in-hole 插入任务上，PHASE 相较最强非相位感知基线整体成功率提升 13 个百分点，在未见初始位置场景下提升 30 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: motivation: 接触密集装配（如 peg-in-hole 插入）从少量演示中学习仍非常困难。检索增强模仿学习（retrieval-augmented imitation learning）通过用相关先验数据增强目标演示提供了可行方向，但其在接触密集操作中的适用性尚未被充分探索。接触式插入本身包含搜索（search）到插入（insert）等多个阶段，而如何从先验数据中有原则地检索“相位特定”的经验仍是开放问题。作者的洞察是：柔顺手腕使机器人能在整个执行过程中维持接触，从而产生丰富的触觉和力信号，这些信号天然地揭示插入过程的相位结构，并可用于指导“应该检索什么”。

Method: method: PHASE 将合规控制、触觉相位感知与检索增强策略学习整合为一个框架，核心包含三个组件：(1) 多模态接触感知表征学习，融合触觉/力等接触信号学习接触感知表征；(2) 基于触觉信号的变长相位分割，从接触信号中自动推断插入任务的相位边界（区别于固定长度或人工标注切分）；(3) 相位一致的检索（phase-consistent retrieval），依据当前所处相位从先验数据中检索相位匹配的经验用于策略学习。整体在共享的策略架构下与多种 SOTA 检索策略进行对比，强调以“交互定义的接触相位”对齐检索过程，而非在整段轨迹层面做无相位区分的检索。

Result: result: 在真实世界 peg-in-hole（轴孔插入）任务上进行评估，覆盖五种 peg 几何形状（五种轴几何），并在统一的策略架构下与来自 SOTA 方法的检索策略进行对比。评估指标为任务成功率，并额外考察对未见初始位置的泛化表现（few-shot 插入学习设定）。主要结果：PHASE 相比最强非相位感知基线（strongest non-phase-aware baseline）整体成功率提升 13 个百分点；在未见初始位置（unseen initial positions）条件下性能提升 30 个百分点，表明相位对齐检索显著提升了几何泛化与位姿鲁棒性。

Conclusion: conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于首次系统性地将检索增强模仿学习应用于接触密集型插入任务，并提出以柔顺手腕提供的持续接触触觉/力信号来自动定义任务相位，进而实现变长相位分割与相位一致的检索，使检索与交互结构语义对齐。实验证明该方法在少样本真实插入任务中、尤其对未见初始位置具有显著鲁棒性提升。可能的局限：评估仅限 peg-in-hole 插入任务与较小规模的 peg 几何集合；依赖柔顺手腕与可靠触觉/力信号；相位分割依赖接触信号质量，对于无法持续保持接触或缺乏力传感的平台，方法的可迁移性尚待验证。

Abstract: Contact-rich assembly tasks such as peg-in-hole insertion remain difficult to learn from limited demonstrations. While retrieval-augmented imitation learning, which augments target demonstrations with relevant prior data, offers a promising direction, its applicability to contact-rich manipulation remains largely unexplored. Contact-rich insertion unfolds over multiple phases from search to insert, and retrieving phase-specific experience from prior data in principled ways remains an open question. Our key insight is that a compliant wrist enables the robot to sustain contact throughout execution, producing rich tactile and force signals that naturally reveal the phase structure of insertion and inform what should be retrieved. Based on this insight, we present PHASE (PHase-Aware Segmentation and REtrieval), a framework for compliance-enabled tactile phase retrieval that integrates multimodal contact-aware representation learning, variable-length phase segmentation from tactile signals, and phase-consistent retrieval for policy learning. We evaluate PHASE on real-world peg-in-hole insertion across five peg geometries, comparing against retrieval strategies drawn from state-of-the-art methods under a shared policy architecture. PHASE improves the overall success rate by 13 percentage points over the strongest non-phase-aware baseline, and improves performance under unseen initial positions by 30 percentage points. These results demonstrate that aligning retrieval with interaction-defined contact phases substantially improves robustness in few-shot insertion learning.

</details>


### [133] [Causeway: Restoring Task Accessibility for Instruction Switching in VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30913)
*Qingzi Wang,Kaixi Feng,Guangyao Shi,Xiyang Wu,Ang Li,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究VLA（视觉-语言-动作）策略在"指令切换"场景下的失效问题，提出"任务孤岛（task islands）"概念：目标任务从标准初始状态可完成，但从其他任务产生的状态却无法进入。作者提出Causeway——一种无需训练的推理时干预方法，通过在冻结解码过程中反传并施加状态定向写入，使VLA自行解码返回运动，从而恢复任务可达性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略通常从标准初始状态评估，但在真实机器人连续作业中，新指令往往在前一任务执行中或执行后下达，此时机器人物理状态已被改变。作者观察到一个关键现象：某些任务从自身标准初始状态可稳定完成，却无法从其他任务遗留的状态进入，形成"任务孤岛"。这种状态相关的不可达性直接限制了VLA在多任务连续执行与指令切换中的实用性，是实际部署中被忽视但重要的鲁棒性问题。

Method: Causeway是一种免训练（training-free）的推理时干预方法。给定当前状态与目标任务的"重入位姿（re-entry pose）"，方法在冻结的VLA解码计算中执行反向传播，并在动作流表示（action-stream representation）内施加一个"状态定向写入（state-directed write）"。该干预不更新模型参数、不引入新动作头、也不依赖外部动作生成模块，而是让VLA本身解码出返回运动。核心创新在于将不可达状态的恢复视为对动作流潜在表示的定向操控问题，通过梯度信息定位需写入的方向，实现跨任务状态的可达性恢复。

Result: 在LIBERO-Goal上，作者构建了71组跨物体任务对、3种切换时机，并测试了3种VLA架构。裸切换（bare-switch）成功率仅3-26%，Causeway将其提升至47-65%，同时将到达"交接邻域（handoff neighborhood）"的比例提升42-72个百分点。在LIBERO-Object及真实xArm平台上，恢复效果同样得到验证，说明该方法不仅限于LIBERO-Goal主设置，且在仿真与真实机器人上均可泛化。

Conclusion: 论文的贡献在于：（1）识别并提出"任务孤岛"这一VLA指令切换中的状态相关失效模式；（2）提出免训练、无需参数更新的推理时干预Causeway，使VLA自行解码返回运动；（3）在多种架构、切换时机、两个仿真基准及真实xArm上验证了显著提升。局限方面，方法依赖重入位姿的提供，且主要面向返回/交接恢复运动，对更复杂长时程连续切换的可扩展性尚待进一步验证。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies can execute many tasks from standard initial states, yet a new instruction may fail after another task has altered the robot's physical state. We study instruction switching, where a new task is issued during or after the execution of a different one. We observe that a target task that is reliably completed from its standard initial states can become inaccessible from states produced by a preceding task. We call such states task islands. We propose Causeway, a training-free inference-time intervention. Given the current state and a re-entry pose for the target task, Causeway back-propagates through the frozen decoding computation and applies a state-directed write within the action-stream representation. The VLA decodes the return motion itself, without parameter updates, a new action head, or external action generation. Across 71 cross-object pairs, three switch timings, and three VLA architectures on LIBERO-Goal, Causeway raises bare-switch success from 3-26% to 47-65% and increases the rate of reaching the handoff neighborhood by 42-72 percentage points across models. Additional experiments on LIBERO-Object and a real xArm platform show that the recovery extends beyond the main LIBERO-Goal setting, both in simulation and on a robot.

</details>


### [134] [Bundled Contact Gradients: Stabilizing Differentiable Simulation for Deployable Dynamic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30951)
*Dyuman Aditya,Jin Cheng,Clemens Schwarke,Quan Nguyen,Gaurav Sukhatme,Stelian Coros,Gabriele Fadini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"捆绑接触梯度（Bundled Contact Gradients, BCG）"，一种面向可微机器人仿真的接触局部随机平滑框架，在检测到刚性接触时通过对局部随机扰动轨迹束的梯度进行聚合并降低方差，从而支持高接触刚度下的稳定一阶策略优化，并将动态人形动作零样本迁移到真实 Unitree G1 人形机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微仿真通过机器人动力学的解析梯度实现高效的一阶策略优化，但穿过刚体接触的平滑且信息丰富的梯度通常依赖于"软化"接触模型（低刚度、低阻尼等效），这会牺牲物理保真度，使学到的策略难以在真实世界生效。对于动态人形运动而言，精确的接触动力学对真机迁移尤为关键。若提高刚体仿真的接触刚度以提升交互保真度，动力学会对微小状态扰动极度敏感，导致梯度方差急剧升高，破坏一阶策略学习的稳定性。因此，论文要解决的核心矛盾是：物理保真度（高接触刚度）与可学习性（低梯度方差）之间的权衡。

Method: 论文提出 BCG，一种接触局部的随机平滑（randomized smoothing）框架，用于可微策略学习。其核心机制是：当系统检测到刚性接触（stiff contact）时，在刚性接触构型周围评估一组"局部扰动 rollout 束（local bundle of randomized perturbation rollouts）"，并将这些 rollout 的梯度信号聚合，以降低方差。这一设计只在高刚度接触的局部区域引入随机平滑，而非全局软化接触模型，因此保留了整体动力学的物理保真度。方法建立在可微仿真提供解析梯度、进而支持一阶（first-order）策略优化的基础上，属于将随机平滑与可微物理梯度结合的策略学习算法。

Result: 实验以动态人形运动为任务场景，采用 Unitree G1 真实人形平台进行验证。主要结果是：使用 BCG 成功训练动态动作策略，并实现零样本（zero-shot）从仿真到真机的迁移，即在无真实世界微调的情况下直接部署。论文未在摘要中给出具体量化指标（如成功率、跟踪误差等），相关视频与补充材料见项目主页。总体表明在高接触刚度、高梯度方差条件下，BCG 相比直接一阶优化能稳定训练。

Conclusion: 贡献在于提出一种面向可微仿真的接触局部随机平滑梯度聚合方法 BCG，缓解了高接触刚度下梯度高方差导致的一阶策略学习不稳定问题，从而在保持物理保真度的同时实现动态人形动作的零样本真机迁移，展示了可微仿真中"放弃软化接触"路线的可行性。潜在局限包括：摘要未给出定量对比与统计结果，方法在大规模并行 rollout 下的计算开销、以及是否依赖特定任务/机器人平台仍有待评估。

Abstract: Differentiable simulation provides analytic gradients of robot dynamics, enabling fast and sample-efficient first-order policy optimization. However, obtaining smooth and informative gradients through rigid-body contact typically requires softened contact models, often at the expense of physical fidelity and thereby limiting learned policies largely to simulation. This trade-off becomes particularly consequential for dynamic humanoid motions, where accurate contact dynamics are critical for transferring policies to the real world. Increasing contact stiffness in rigid-body simulation improves the fidelity of interactions, but also makes the dynamics increasingly sensitive to small state perturbations, producing high-variance gradients that can destabilize first-order policy learning. To address this, we propose \emph{Bundled Contact Gradients (BCG)}, a contact-local randomized smoothing framework for differentiable policy learning. When stiff contact is detected, our method evaluates a local bundle of randomized perturbation rollouts around the stiff contact configuration and aggregates their gradient signal thereby reducing gradient variance. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our method by successfully training and transferring dynamic motions zero-shot onto a real-world Unitree G1 humanoid platform. Videos and supplementary information can be found at https://bundledcontactgradients.github.io/

</details>


### [135] [VisTacAlign: Co-Training Dexterous Policies on Tactile Human and Robot Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30959)
*Julien Poffet,Matthew Strong,Ankush Dhawan,Baiyu Shi,Shalika Neelaveni,Yujia Yuan,Zhenan Bao,Monroe Kennedy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 VisTacAlign，一个在人类演示与机器人演示上协同训练 3D 视觉-触觉灵巧操作策略的框架。其核心在于从视觉、触觉与运动学三个模态同时"闭环"人-机差距：人手动作用 17-DoF 触觉机器人手重定向并做一次性指尖校正；将人手从双目视图中擦除并用机器人手网格替换、贴上机器人录制像素后重跑实时立体基础模型，使人点云带上与机器人点云相同的立体误差与可见性；再用信号空间对齐将电容触觉手套映射到机器人指尖传感器，得到统一可解释的逐指力表征。策略为扩散 Transformer，消费点云、本体感知与逐指触觉 token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示是廉价且易扩展的灵巧操作数据来源，但协同训练（co-training）机器人策略的前提是弥合人-机之间的"模态鸿沟"——若每个被策略消费的模态（视觉点云、触觉信号、手部运动学）都来自不同分布，人类数据反而会引入分布偏移而非增益。现有工作多只处理单一模态的差异（如仅做动作重定向或仅做视觉域适配），而力敏感任务（装配、采摘、工具操作）恰恰同时依赖精确的视觉几何与触觉力反馈。因此需要一个在视觉、触觉、运动学上系统性对齐的协同训练框架，使人类演示能真正提升需要精细力控的真实世界任务表现。

Method: 1) 运动学对齐：将手套追踪的人手运动重定向到 17-DoF 触觉机器人手，仅需一次性指尖校正（one-time fingertip correction）。2) 视觉对齐：将人手从双目视图中擦除，替换为已摆姿态的机器人手网格，并为其"上色"——用机器人实拍记录中的像素渲染，从而在合成图像上重新运行实时立体基础模型。这样生成的人手点云继承了与机器人点云一致的立体估计误差与遮挡/可见性模式，实现视觉观测分布的对齐（而非简单的域随机化或风格迁移）。3) 触觉对齐：将电容触觉手套的信号在信号空间中对齐到机器人指尖传感器，得到单一、可解释的逐指力表征，避免触觉输入的双分布问题。4) 策略架构：扩散 Transformer（diffusion transformer）作为策略主干，输入由点云 token、本体感知 token 与逐指触觉 token 构成，输出操作动作序列。

Result: 在三个需要精确施力的真实世界任务上评估：乐高装配（Lego assembly）、采摘不同尺寸的草莓、以及按下并举起电钻。结果表明，在已有机器人数据的基础上加入经过对齐的人类演示，性能优于仅用机器人数据的策略（robot-only baseline）。消融实验进一步显示触觉输入与视觉对齐二者均为必需——去掉任一环节都会导致性能下降，说明增益并非单纯来自数据量增加，而是来自各模态的对齐。

Conclusion: 贡献上，VisTacAlign 首次（在所述范围内）系统性地在视觉、触觉与运动学三个模态上同时闭合人-机差距，并通过在合成图像上重跑立体基础模型这一巧妙设计，使人手点云与机器人点云共享同一误差结构，为人类演示在 3D 视觉-触觉策略中的协同训练提供了可复用的对齐范式；配合扩散 Transformer 策略，在真实力敏任务上验证了有效性。可能的局限包括：对齐依赖于手套追踪与一次性指尖校正的精度，视觉替换流程与实时立体基础模型的计算开销可能限制部署，评测仅覆盖三个任务且需已有机器人数据作为"锚点"分布。

Abstract: Human demonstrations are a cheap source of data for dexterous manipulation, but co-training a robot policy on them requires closing the human--robot gap in every modality the policy consumes. We present VisTacAlign, a framework for co-training 3D-visual-tactile dexterous policies on human and robot demonstrations. Glove-tracked human hand motion is retargeted to a 17-DoF tactile robot hand with a one-time fingertip correction. The human hand is then erased from both stereo views and replaced by a posed robot-hand mesh painted with pixels from robot recordings, and a real-time stereo foundation model is re-run on the composite, so the human point clouds carry the same stereo errors and visibility as the robot ones. Finally, a capacitive tactile glove is aligned to the robot's fingertip sensors in its signal space, giving one interpretable per-finger force representation. A diffusion transformer consumes point-cloud, proprioceptive, and per-finger tactile tokens. On three real-world tasks requiring precise force -- Lego assembly, plucking strawberries of varying size, and activating and lifting a power drill -- adding aligned human demonstrations to existing robot data improves over robot-only policies, and ablations show that both tactile input and visual alignment are necessary. Project page: https://vis-tac-align.github.io

</details>


### [136] [FRAM: Trajectory-Guided Visual Feature Selection for Compact Language-Conditioned Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30965)
*Hiroshi Ito,Hyogo Hiruma,Yoshiki Kanai,Takahiro Yoshida,Akira Kanazawa,Hiroki Yamada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FRAM（Future Representation Action Model）是一个仅138.7M参数的小型机器人操作策略，通过将预测的未来末端执行器轨迹映射为图像坐标作为"空间指针"，从当前图像中读取与运动相关的局部视觉特征，将动作生成所需信息组织为"位置（Where）+ 视觉状态（What）+ 未来运动（Future）"三要素，在LIBERO四个标准套件上达到92.2%的平均成功率，接近3.3B参数π₀的94.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision-Language-Action（VLA）模型在机器人操作中表现优异，但普遍参数量庞大（如π₀的3.3B），带来高计算与部署成本。现有方法倾向于将整幅图像的全局视觉特征输入策略网络，缺乏对"未来运动"这一动作本质的显式建模，导致冗余信息引入、泛化与鲁棒性受限。作者认为，动作生成真正需要的是与未来运动相关的局部视觉信息，而非全局图像表征，因此希望以显式的未来轨迹引导视觉特征选择，从而在极小参数量下兼顾性能与鲁棒性。

Method: FRAM的核心思想是以未来末端执行器轨迹作为视觉信息选择的"空间指针"。具体而言：1）模型预测未来末端执行器轨迹，并取其图像坐标作为指针；2）以这些坐标在当前图像上采样对应位置的局部视觉特征，得到与运动相关的稀疏视觉表征；3）将信息显式解耦为参考位置（Where）、视觉状态（What）和未来运动（Future）三部分，再据此生成动作。轨迹标签由演示数据结合相机几何自动生成，无需人工标注。模型总参数量为138.7M，其中语言编码器为冻结状态，仅训练轻量策略部分，从而大幅降低训练与推理开销。这种"以未来运动检索局部视觉特征"的设计是区别于全局特征输入VLA的关键创新。

Result: 在LIBERO的四个标准任务套件上，FRAM取得92.2%的平均成功率，接近参数量约为其24倍的π₀（3.3B，94.2%）。在无需额外训练的情况下，FRAM在LIBERO-Plus上达到67.3%的平均成功率，体现出较强的泛化能力。消融实验表明，未来轨迹信息与局部视觉特征二者均对性能和鲁棒性有正向贡献。在真实双机械臂UR5e平台上，FRAM仅依靠腕部相机完成叠杯子任务，并能在左右臂之间进行选择与切换。

Conclusion: FRAM的主要贡献在于提出了一种以未来运动为引导的视觉信息选择机制，将动作生成的输入显式拆解为位置、视觉状态与未来运动，从而以138.7M的小体量达到接近亿级参数大模型的性能，并具备跨套件泛化与真机双臂操作能力。其意义在于验证了"基于未来运动选择视觉信息"是小型机器人策略实现高性能与鲁棒性的有效途径，同时轨迹标签自动生成降低了标注成本。潜在局限包括：依赖相机几何自动生成轨迹标签，可能对相机标定精度较敏感；实验规模相对集中（LIBERO系列与单一UR5e平台），在更复杂、长时序或动态场景中的可扩展性仍待验证；局部特征采样策略对轨迹预测误差的鲁棒性也需进一步分析。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models achieve strong performance in robot manipulation, but often require large numbers of parameters. In this work, we propose the Future Representation Action Model (FRAM), a small policy that explicitly links the future end-effector trajectory to the current visual input. FRAM uses the image coordinates of the predicted trajectory as spatial pointers and reads local visual features related to the motion from the current image. This organizes the information for action generation into the reference position (Where), the visual state (What), and the future motion (Future). Trajectory labels are generated automatically from demonstrations and camera geometry, so no manual annotation is needed. With 138.7M parameters, including a frozen language encoder, FRAM reaches an average success rate of 92.2% over the four standard LIBERO suites, close to the 94.2% of $π_0$ with 3.3B parameters. Without extra training, it also reaches an average of 67.3% on LIBERO-Plus. Ablations confirm that both the future trajectory and the local visual features improve performance and robustness. On a real dual-arm UR5e, FRAM stacks cups using only wrist cameras, including choosing and switching between the left and right arms. These results show that selecting visual information based on future motion is an effective way to obtain both high performance and robustness in a small robot policy.

</details>


### [137] [TACTIC: Understanding Tactile Encoders and Conditioning for Contact-rich Robot Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30969)
*Seongjin Bien,Débora Oliveira Makowski,Carlo Kneissl,Reihaneh Mirjalili,Pankhuri Vanjani,Rudolf Lioutikov,Gitta Kutyniok,Florian Walter,Wolfram Burgard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对基于视觉的触觉传感器（vision-based tactile sensors）中的触觉编码器与视觉-触觉融合策略进行了系统性实证研究，在真实机器人平台上覆盖多种接触密集型操作任务，通过超过2000次真实世界rollout的受控对比实验，得出核心结论：不存在普适最优的触觉表征或融合策略，最优编码器骨干与融合方案强烈依赖于具体任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉信息对机器人接触密集型操作任务至关重要。基于视觉的触觉传感器（如GelSight类传感器）的一大优势是可将触觉信号表示为图像，从而直接复用计算机视觉领域成熟的编码器来构建端到端操作策略。然而这一便利也导致研究社区涌现出大量异构的架构、训练数据集与评估协议，彼此之间缺乏可比性，使得"哪种设计最能有效编码触觉"这一基础问题始终悬而未决。已有的对比研究多局限于仿真环境，而仿真性能未必能迁移到真实场景，且真实机器人实验成本高、样本量小，难以获得可靠统计结论。因此，本文的动机是在统一的真实世界实验条件下进行受控、大规模的评测，厘清触觉编码与融合的设计选择。

Method: 作者构建了一个统一的训练与评估流程（pipeline）与实验平台，以确保所有模型在完全相同的条件下进行对比，消除架构、数据、评估协议之间的混杂因素。研究维度主要包含两部分：（1）多种触觉编码器骨干网络（复用计算机视觉中的图像/时序编码器来编码基于视觉的触觉图像）；（2）多种视觉-触觉融合策略。所有模型在相同的接触密集型操作任务集合上训练，并通过真实机器人rollout进行评测。核心的方法学贡献不在于提出新架构，而在于建立了一个可复现、可扩展且以真实世界数据统计为基础的触觉表征基准框架，累计超过2000次真实环境rollout，以保证统计可靠性。

Result: 实验在真实世界环境中进行，覆盖多种接触密集型操作任务，累计超过2000次机器人rollout，并对所有编码器与融合方案施加统一的训练/评估流程以保证公平对比。评估以真实任务性能为依据（而非仿真指标）。主要发现是：不存在普遍最优的触觉表征或融合策略；最优的编码器骨干网络与融合方案显著依赖于具体任务。这一结论有别于仅比较仿真性能的既有研究，强调了真实世界大规模评测对于获得可靠统计与可信结论的必要性。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于提供了一个统一、受控、以真实世界大规模rollout为基础的触觉编码与融合对比研究，明确指出触觉表征与融合策略的任务依赖性，为后续研究者的设计选择提供了实证依据，也间接质疑了仿真指标的可迁移性。其意义在于推动触觉感知研究从"追求单一通用架构"转向"面向任务选择合适编码与融合方案"。局限可能包括：结论依赖于所选的传感器类型（基于视觉的触觉传感器）与任务集合，其任务依赖性结论未必能推广到其他触觉模态（如电容、压阻式触觉）或完全不同类型的操作任务；此外，尽管2000次rollout规模可观，但在特定任务子集上的样本量仍可能有限。

Abstract: Tactile information is essential for contact-rich manipulation tasks in robotics. Vision-based tactile sensors make it particularly easy to design end-to-end manipulation policies with tactile sensing, as they enable the use of existing encoders from computer vision. However, this has led to a huge variety of architectures, training datasets, and evaluation protocols, making it difficult to determine which design choices best encode touch. In this work, we address this gap and present a comprehensive study of tactile encoders and fusion strategies across various contact-rich manipulation tasks in real-world experiments. To enable a controlled comparison, we train and evaluate all models under the same pipeline and experimental setup, comprising more than 2000 real-world rollouts. Our results go beyond other studies that only compare simulation performance, which does not necessarily translate to real-world settings, where large-scale evaluations are needed to obtain reliable statistics. Our key finding is that there is no universally optimal representation or fusion strategy for encoding visual-tactile. Instead, the best encoder backbone and fusion scheme depend strongly on the task.

</details>


### [138] [Quadruped Obstacle Avoidance and Footstep Planning with Distributed Low-cost Time-of-Flight Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31008)
*Giammarco Caroleo,Timothée Mahamoodally,Matteo Manzardo,Jin Jin,Marco Pontin,Matias Mattamala,Renato Vidoni,Perla Maiolino,Maurice Fallon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨使用分布式低成本飞行时间(ToF)传感器替代深度相机，为ANYmal四足机器人提供近场地形感知与行走所需的空间几何信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人通常依赖深度相机和LiDAR进行局部环境建图，但这类传感器存在近距覆盖受限、成本高、功耗大的问题，并可能在机器人本体周围形成感知盲区。作者因此研究能否用廉价的分布式ToF传感器实现可靠的近场地形建图和局部导航，以降低硬件成本、能耗与系统复杂度，同时消除传统传感器的盲区。

Method: 论文为ANYbotics ANYmal四足机器人设计了一套分布式ToF感知架构，将多个ToF传感器分布在机器人本体周围，以覆盖传统传感器难以观测的近距区域。作者评估了该架构的环境重建精度，并将其与深度相机在地形建图和避障任务中进行基准对比，考察其在几何信息获取、盲区覆盖与近场感知方面的能力。

Result: 结果显示，尽管ToF传感器分辨率较低且测量噪声较大，分布式ToF方案仍能支持可靠的感知行走，实现厘米级的局部建图精度。该方案能够提供足够的几何信息用于近场避障与落脚点规划，同时相比深度相机在成本、能耗和系统复杂度方面具有明显优势。论文未在摘要中给出具体的定量指标、数据集或测试场景细节。

Conclusion: 论文表明分布式ToF感知可作为深度相机的低成本替代方案，为四足机器人近场地形建图和局部导航提供可行路径，具有降低能耗与硬件复杂度的实际意义。主要局限在于ToF传感器分辨率低、噪声大，且结论依赖ANYmal平台的实验验证，其在大范围建图、远距离感知或复杂动态环境中的泛化能力尚待进一步考察。

Abstract: Quadruped robots typically rely on depth cameras and LiDAR sensors to map their local environment. However, these sensors have limited close-range coverage, are relatively expensive, and consume significant power. This study investigates whether distributed Time-of-Flight (ToF) sensors can serve as a low-cost alternative to depth cameras for near-field terrain mapping for locomotion and local navigation. We designed a distributed ToF sensing architecture for the ANYbotics ANYmal quadruped, assessed its environment reconstruction accuracy, and benchmarked it against depth cameras for terrain mapping and obstacle avoidance. Distributing these sensors around the robot can also avoid the blind spots of traditional sensors. Our results show that, despite their low resolution and higher measurement noise, distributed ToF sensors can support reliable perceptual locomotion with centimeter-level local mapping accuracy. The proposed sensing strategy provides sufficient geometric information for near-field obstacle avoidance and footstep planning, at substantially lower cost, energy consumption, and system complexity than depth cameras.

</details>


### [139] [Co-design of trajectory and morphology for a vertical jump-climbing robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31014)
*Christopher Y. Xu,Elliot W. Hawkes*

Main category: cs.RO
